01IR 解谱速通（3 步 + 10 个锚点）

Step 0
先认题型（你们最常见的）
连线题
几张谱 ↔ 几个分子（先抓“独一无二峰”秒配对）
Band assignment
Band1–Band6 选项（只要把每个 band 归到官能团区间）
判断题
OH/NH、C=O、是否共轭/氢键、是否有芳环
Step 1
先扫一眼“独一无二峰”（10 秒定大类）
问① 有无 超宽带？
答

3200–3600 宽峰（像一坨云） → 醇/酚 O–H stretch

2500–3300 更宽更拖尾（像一整片雾） → 羧酸 O–H（强特征）

问② 有无 1700 左右的大刀峰？
答
  • 1650–1750 强尖峰 → C=O（羰基）
    • ~1740 常见酯
    • ~1715 常见酮
    • ~1700 左右可能共轭/酸/醛（要结合其他峰）
问③ 有无 2250 左右的尖峰？
答

~2250 → C≡N（腈）

~2100–2260 → C≡C（炔）（通常弱一点）

口诀：宽云=OH；1700大刀=C=O；2250尖钉=C≡N。

Step 2：再看“背景区”（确认烃骨架类型）

④ C–H stretching（2800–3100）

2850–2960：sp³ C–H（CH₂/CH₃）→ 饱和烷基

~3030：sp² C–H → 芳香/烯烃

~3300 细尖：sp C–H（末端炔）→ 终端炔加分点

⑤ 芳香环“铁证区”（1500–1600 + 700–900）

~1600 & ~1500：芳香 C=C 骨架

700–900：芳香 C–H 面外弯曲（还能看取代型，考试一般只要写“aromatic”）

Step 3：最后用“指纹区”做“排除/确认”（不求背全表）

指纹区（1500–600）你不用背细节，只要会抓 2–3 个常见确认峰：

C–O（醇/醚/酯）：1000–1300（常强）

C–N（胺）：1020–1250（中等）

NO₂：两个强峰 ~1550 + ~1350

CH₂ rocking（长直链）：~720（超好用！直链烷基“指纹”）

你必须背的“10个锚点”（背完就能做你们90%的IR题）

1
O–H（醇）：3200–3600，宽
2
O–H（羧酸）：2500–3300，超宽拖尾
3
N–H（胺）：3300–3500（伯胺常“双峰”，仲胺常“单峰”）
4
C=O：1650–1750 强
5
C≡N：~2250
6
sp³ C–H：2850–2960
7
sp² C–H：~3030
8
芳香 C=C：~1600 & ~1500
9
C–O：1000–1300（常强）
10
直链 CH₂ rocking：~720

考场写答案模板（照抄就像标准答案）

**CN：**谱图出现（特征峰1）→ 推断官能团；出现（特征峰2）→ 进一步确认；缺少（特征峰3）→ 排除某类结构；因此最可能为……

**EN：**Presence of (feature 1) indicates …; (feature 2) supports …; absence of (feature 3) rules out …; therefore the compound is most consistent with …

最后给你一个“连线题秒杀策略”

当有 3–4 张谱要配 3–4 个分子时：

1
先找唯一峰（羧酸OH/羰基/腈）直接锁一张谱
2
剩下的再用 **OH vs NH（宽 vs 窄、单峰 vs 双峰）**区分
3
最后用 **芳香环区（3030 + 1600 + 700–900）**收尾

02NMR 课堂笔记

NMR spectroscopy

答

*degewaraoss?? (equal energy) in absence of magnetic field

*non-degenaratea (=/= energier??) submitted to B0 magnetic?? field

一、 NMR 的基本原理 (The Principle)

NMR 的核心原理基于原子核的自旋 (Spin) 及其在磁场中的行为。

1
原子核的磁性 (Magnetic Properties of Nuclei)

并不是所有的原子核都能进行 NMR 分析。只有自旋量子数 (Spin Quantum Number, I) 不为零的原子核才具有磁性。

最常用的原子核
1H (氢谱) 和 13C (碳谱)。

这些原子核就像微小的“磁铁”。

2
外加磁场的作用 (External Magnetic Field - B0​)
无磁场时
原子核的朝向是随机杂乱的。

放入强磁场 (B0​) 后： 原子核会根据磁场方向进行取向。对于 1H，主要有两种状态：

低能态 (α态): 与磁场方向一致（顺磁），能量较低。

高能态 (β态): 与磁场方向相反（逆磁），能量较高。

这两种状态之间产生了一个能级差 (ΔE)。

3
共振 (Resonance)

我们向样品发射特定频率的射频脉冲 (Radio Frequency, RF)。

当射频能量等于能级差 (ΔE) 时，处于低能态的原子核吸收能量，跃迁到高能态。这种现象就叫**“共振”**。

4
信号检测 (Signal Detection)停止射频脉冲后，原子核会从高能态回到低能态（这个过程叫弛豫, Relaxation）。

在此过程中，它们会释放出能量信号（自由感应衰减信号, FID）。

通过傅里叶变换 (Fourier Transform)，计算机将这个时间信号转换为我们看到的频率图谱（NMR 谱图）。

5
为什么能区分不同的原子？(Chemical Shift)

如果所有氢原子的共振频率都一样，NMR 就没用了。实际上，原子核外部的电子云会屏蔽一部分磁场。

电子云密度不同 → 感受到的实际磁场不同 → 共振频率不同。

这种频率的差异被称为化学位移 (Chemical Shift, δ)，单位是 ppm。它告诉我们该原子处于什么样的化学环境中（例如：是在苯环上，还是在甲基上）。

HISTORICAL INTRODUCTION 历史介绍

300 BC Theophrastus describes the regular shapes of crystals (Greece)

公元前300年
泰奥弗拉斯托斯描述了晶体的规则形状（希腊）。

50 Pliny the Elder (Gaius Plinius Secundus, AD 23/24–79) describes Quartz in his work 'Naturalis Historia'

公元50年
老普林尼（盖乌斯·普林尼乌斯·塞昆杜斯，公元23/24-79年）在其著作《自然史》中描述了石英。

1783 Romé de Lisle defines the law of constancy of angles

1783年
罗梅·德·利勒提出了角度恒定定律。

1801 Haüy introduces the law of "simple truncations"

1801年
阿于引入了“简单截断”定律。

1839 Miller uses Miller Indices to designate the faces of a crystal

1839年
米勒首次用米勒指数命名晶体的各个晶面。
讲解补充
米勒指数是一套用于标记晶体表面方向的符号体系，后续在晶体学中被广泛应用。 Miller indices are a notation system used for denoting crystal plane directions, which later became widely used in crystallography.

Key Additions from Audio Lecture 课堂录音补充重点：

角度问题在晶体学中的重要性，米勒引入米勒指数来区分不同晶面

The importance of angles in crystallography, and how Miller introduced Miller indices to distinguish between different crystal faces.

19世纪和20世纪初关于晶体面、晶体截断等法则的奠定，为后续晶体结构研究做了基础

The laws concerning crystal faces and truncations established during the 19th and early 20th centuries laid the foundation for subsequent crystal structure research.

FURTHER DEVELOPMENT OF CRYSTAL ANALYSIS 晶体分析技术的发展

1912 Friedrich, Knipping & Von Laue discover the diffraction of X-rays by crystals

1912年
弗里德里希、克尼平和冯·劳厄发现了X射线被晶体衍射的现象。

1913 W.H. & W.L. Bragg use X-ray diffraction to resolve the structure of NaCl (then diamond)

1913年
布拉格父子利用X射线衍射解析了氯化钠的结构（当时也包括金刚石）。

1916 Debye & Scherrer's experiments in powder diffraction

1916年
德拜和谢勒进行粉末衍射实验。

1936 Halaban & Preiswerk - Neutron diffraction by a crystal

1936年
哈拉班和普莱斯韦克研究中子衍射。

1980 Synchrotron radiation X-ray intensity × 10^12

1980年
同步辐射X射线强度提高到10^12的数量级。

Key Additions from Audio Lecture 课堂录音补充重点：

1912年X射线衍射发现极大促进了晶体结构研究

The discovery of X-ray diffraction in 1912 greatly promoted crystal structure research.

讲到20世纪同步辐射的技术进步，使实验灵敏度和分辨率大幅提高

Technical progress in synchrotron radiation in the late 20th century significantly increased experimental sensitivity and resolution.

处于紫外和伽马射线之间

The position of X-rays in the electromagnetic spectrum: between ultraviolet and gamma rays.

微波与无线电波依次分布在红外以后的区域

Microwaves and radio waves are arrayed in regions after infrared.

Key Additions from Audio Lecture 课堂录音补充重点：

老师强调了长波、短波、微波、射频波的基本波长范围和顺序

The lecturer emphasized the basic wavelength ranges and orders of long waves, short waves, microwaves, and radio frequency waves.

X射线作为一种重要区段，在晶体学中应用广泛

X-rays, as an important band, are widely used in crystallography.

Electromagnetic radiation, wave description 电磁辐射及其波动描述

电磁辐射由两个分量组成：

Electromagnetic radiation is made up of two components:

电场矢量E an electric field vector, E

磁场矢量B a magnetic field vector, B

E与B彼此垂直，并且都垂直于传播方向

E and B are perpendicular to each other and to the direction of propagation.

E与B按照正弦规律同相振荡（波动性质）

E and B oscillate in phase according to a sinusoidal law (wave nature).

E与B理论上可以在垂直于传播方向的平面内采取任何取向

E and B can in principle take all orientations in a plane perpendicular to the direction of propagation.

1905年爱因斯坦提出
光由“能量粒子”组成，即光子，可被原子与分子吸收或发射

In 1905, Albert Einstein proposed: Light consists of "grains of energy", i.e., photons, which can be absorbed or emitted by atoms and molecules.

Key Additions from Audio Lecture 课堂录音补充重点：

空间中的光传播可用波动描述，包括真空和介质情况

Propagation of light in space can be described by wave theory, including both in vacuum and in media.

如果x轴为传播方向，光的振动可分为两个正交分量

If the x-axis is the propagation direction, the light’s vibration can be split into two orthogonal components.

同时时提及E和B的所有取向，即偏振属性的基础

The possibility for all orientations of E and B underlies the fundamental property of polarization.

电磁辐射的传播与衍射仪Electromagnetic Radiation Propagation and Diffractometer

1
真空中线性偏振电磁辐射的传播Propagation of Linearly Polarized Electromagnetic Radiation in Vacuum
电磁波在真空中传播时，电场　和磁场(B)相互垂直，并且两者都垂直于传播方向。
答案 E
The electric field 　 and magnetic field (B) are perpendicular to each other and both are perpendicular to the direction of propagation when electromagnetic waves travel in vacuum.
答案 E

E场通常在y轴方向，B场在z轴方向，波沿x轴传播。

The E field is usually along the y axis, B field along the z axis, and the wave propagates along the x axis.

线性偏振时，电场和磁场的变化满足正弦函数表达式：

For linear polarization, the variations of electric and magnetic fields are described by sinusoidal functions:

E = Ey = E0 sin(2πνt - 2πνx/c)

E = Ey = E0 sin(2πνt - 2πνx/c)

B = Bz = B0 sin(2πνt - 2πνx/c)

B = Bz = B0 sin(2πνt - 2πνx/c)

振幅(amplitude)表示波的强度，波的相位(phase)随着时间和空间变化。

The amplitude represents the wave's strength, and the phase varies with time and position.

老师补充
光的传播始终用波动方式描述，同时也能用x轴方向和两个正交分量来表示光的偏振属性。
Teacher's addition
Light propagation is always described by wave fashion, and polarization can be described by two orthogonal components along the propagation (x) direction.
2
电磁辐射与光子的定义Electromagnetic Radiation or Photon
辐射的传播速度
在真空中，光速c = 2.997925108 × 10^8 m/s ≈ 3.108 × 10^8 m/s。

Speed of radiation propagation: In a vacuum, the speed of light c = 2.997925108 × 10^8 m/s ≈ 3.108 × 10^8 m/s.

在均匀介质中传播速度v < c。

In a homogeneous medium, the propagation speed v < c.

折射率n = c/v > 1。

The refractive index n = c/v > 1.

微观上（量子观点），辐射也可视为能量包、能量子（quanta），或特定情况下称为光子（photons），均以光速移动。

Microscopically (in quantum view), radiation can be seen as energy packets (quanta), or, in specific contexts, photons, all moving at the speed of light.

老师补充
1905年爱因斯坦提出光子理论，光子为携带能量的微粒。
Teacher's addition
In 1905, Einstein proposed the photon theory, regarding photons as particles of energy.
3
波动-粒子二象性及普朗克-爱因斯坦关系Dual Wave and Particle (Corpuscular) Descriptions & Planck-Einstein Relation

电磁辐射同时具有波动性和粒子性，这由普朗克-爱因斯坦关系式统一描述：

Electromagnetic radiation exhibits both wave and particle nature, which are unified in the Planck-Einstein relation:

E = hν = hc/λ

E = hν = hc/λ

其中
E为每个能量子的能量（焦耳），ν为波的频率（Hz 或 s⁻¹），λ为波长（米），h为普朗克常数（6.6256 × 10⁻³⁴ J·s）。
Where
E is the energy of each quantum (Joule), ν is frequency of the wave (Hz or s⁻¹), λ is the wavelength (meters), h is Planck's constant (6.6256 × 10⁻³⁴ J·s).
4
衍射仪Diffraction Instrument (Diffractometer)

衍射仪用于检测和分析晶体样品对X射线的衍射情况。

A diffractometer is used to detect and analyze how a crystal sample diffracts X-rays.

原理：X射线从射线源（Source RX）射向样品并被衍射，探测器在不同角度（2θ）收集信号。

Principle
The X-ray from the source (Source RX) is directed towards the sample and diffracted; the detector collects signals at different angles (2θ).

采集衍射角与X射线强度，可以得到衍射谱图（如下图下方），其中每一个峰代表特定晶面反射。

By collecting the diffraction angle and X-ray intensity, a diffraction pattern (as shown in the lower image) is obtained; each peak corresponds to reflection from a specific crystal plane.

老师补充
样品中有多重结晶方向，可以通过衍射峰的角度和强度分析晶体结构特征。
Teacher's addition
There are multiple crystallization directions in the sample; the crystal structure can be analyzed by the position and intensity of diffraction peaks.

该方法允许直接观察晶体内部的有序排列，对研究物质结构意义重大。

This method allows direct observation of the ordered arrangement inside crystals, which is significant for understanding the structure of materials.

同步辐射及其应用Synchrotron Radiation and Its Applications

同步辐射的定义与基本原理Definition and Principle of Synchrotron Radiation

同步辐射是由在加速器中做圆周高速运动的高能电子或正电子受到离心加速度而发出的电磁辐射。 Synchrotron radiation is electromagnetic radiation emitted by high-energy electrons or positrons moving in a circular accelerator under centripetal acceleration.

这些辐射以光子形式表现，表现为能量极高的电磁波。 The radiation is in the form of photons and represents high-energy electromagnetic waves.

同步辐射的速度接近光速。 The velocity of synchrotron radiation is close to the speed of light.

其基本原理基于麦克斯韦电磁波理论。 The basic principle is based on Maxwell's theory of electromagnetic waves.

SOLEIL同步加速器SOLEIL Synchrotron

SOLEIL同步加速器是法国国家级大型同步辐射装置。 SOLEIL Synchrotron is a national large-scale synchrotron radiation facility in France.

它具有极高的光度，约为太阳光的1万倍。 It has extremely high brilliance, about 10,000 times brighter than sunlight.

可覆盖极宽的光谱范围，是“白光”源，从红外（几百μeV）到高能X射线（100 keV）。 It covers a very wide spectral range as a "white" source, from infrared (a few hundred μeV) to hard X-rays (100 keV).

可以生成多种偏振状态（线性、圆偏振等）。 It can generate various polarization states (linear, circular, etc.).

具有脉冲式发射特性。 It operates as a pulsed source.

同步辐射的主要应用Main Applications of Synchrotron Radiation

1
光谱学SPECTROSCOPY

用于高分辨率分析材料、分子或原子结构和组分。 Used for high-resolution analysis of materials, molecular or atomic structures, and compositions.

2
成像IMAGING

对样品进行非破坏性成像，可获得内部精细结构信息。 Non-destructive imaging of samples, obtaining detailed internal structural information.

3
衍射DIFFRACTION

研究晶体结构及有序排列，测定原子空间分布。 Used to study crystal structures and ordered arrangements, and to determine atomic spatial distributions.

4
散射DIFFUSION

用来分析材料的微观不均匀性和复合结构。 Analyzes microscopic heterogeneity and composite structures of materials.

课堂补充讲解要点Key Points from Teacher’s Explanation

老师强调同步辐射是20世纪80年代后期推动X射线及材料分析进步的关键工具，灵敏度和分辨率大幅提升。 The teacher emphasized that since the late 1980s, synchrotron radiation has been a key tool boosting X-ray and material analysis, greatly increasing sensitivity and resolution.

同步加速器只需微小体积样品即可进行多种测试，大大提高了研究效率。 Only tiny amounts of sample are needed for various tests, greatly improving research efficiency.

其高光度和宽波段使其在生命科学、材料科学和纳米科技等领域应用极为广泛。 Its high brilliance and wide spectral range enable extensive applications in life sciences, materials science, nanotechnology, and more.

为什么X射线（和中子）散射重要Why X-ray (and Neutron) Scattering Important——朗缪尔单分子膜实例Example of Langmuir Monolayers

朗缪尔单分子膜用于研究分子层次的结构组织。 Langmuir monolayers are used to study structural organization at the molecular level.

左图
表面压力-分子面积等温线显示不同相的存在，包括气态、膨胀液相、倾斜凝聚相等。 The left figure: The surface pressure-area isotherm shows different phases including gaseous, expanded liquid, and tilted condensed phases.

等温线上水平段代表一阶相变的相共存区，转折点代表连续相变。 Horizontal sections on the isotherm indicate phase coexistence of first-order transitions, and kinks indicate continuous phase transitions.

右图
相图揭示了不同温度和表面压力下单元结构的变化，基于X射线衍射数据。 The right figure: The phase diagram reveals changes in unit cell shapes at different temperatures and surface pressures based on X-ray diffraction data.

通过掠入射X射线衍射（GIXD），可以对分子排列和晶格相的具体结构进行定量识别。 Using Grazing Incidence X-ray Diffraction (GIXD), specific structures of molecular packing and lattice phases can be quantitatively identified.

掠入射X射线技术应用于
1）分子尺度结构表征；2）定量相态判定。 Grazing Incidence X-ray techniques are applied for: 1) Molecular-level structure characterization; 2) Quantitative phase identification.
老师补充
GIXD对于检测薄膜和界面上的微观有序排列非常敏感，是研究软物质结构的重要手段。 Teacher's note: GIXD is highly sensitive for detecting micro-order on films and interfaces, and is an important tool for soft matter structure studies.

X射线散射/衍射——X射线与物质相互作用X-ray Scattering/Diffraction——X-ray Interaction with Matter

问问题：X射线如何与物质发生相互作用？ Question: How do X-rays interact with matter?
答

有两种理论方法： There are two theoretical approaches:

动力学理论——更加全面，可用于模拟复杂散射与反射等现象（如GISAXS、扩散散射、反射率等）。 Dynamical Theory: A more comprehensive approach, useful for simulating complex scattering and reflectivity phenomena (e.g., GISAXS, diffuse scattering, reflectivity, etc.).

运动学理论——简单的解析方法，足以理解散射基本规律。 Kinematical Theory: A simpler analytic approach, sufficient for understanding basic scattering principles.

按Thompson公式，对单一电荷q，在距离R处的散射电场为： According to the Thompson formula, for a single charge q, the scattered electric field at distance R is:

电子的散射贡献远超质子。 Scattering by electrons dominates (vs. protons).

老师补充：实际分析中，大多数X射线与电子散射相关，因此主要从电子分布理解散射信号。 Teacher's note: In practice, X-ray interactions are mostly with electrons, so scattering signals are mainly understood from electron distribution.

X射线散射/衍射——电荷密度与散射公式X-ray Scattering/Diffraction——Charge Density and Scattering Formula

对于电荷密度ρρ，运动学近似下各个电荷独立散射，单位体积微元dVdV内的微分电场为： For charge density ρρ, under kinematical approximation each charge scatters independently, the differential field in a volume element dVdV is:

全部散射场通过体积分得到： The total scattered field is obtained by spatial integration:

其中Q=kd−kiQ=kd​−ki​代表波矢转移，积分部分为电子密度的傅里叶变换。 Where Q=kd−kiQ=kd​−ki​ is the wave vector transfer, and the integral is the Fourier transform of the electron density ρ(r)ρ(r).

实际实验中，测量的是散射强度，为电场强度的模平方： In experiments, the measured quantity is the scattered intensity, which is the modulus squared of the electric field:   I(R)=∣Ed(R)∣2=∣F(ρ)∣2∣Ez∣2I(R)=∣Ed​(R)∣2=∣F(ρ)∣2∣Ez​∣2

即X射线散射实验本质上测到的是电子密度的傅里叶变换模方。 Thus, X-ray scattering experiments essentially measure the squared modulus of the Fourier transform of the electron density.

实验需要注意：1）相位信息丢失，2）仪器分辨率限制会引入卷积效应。 Experimental concerns: 1) Phase information is lost; 2) Instrumental resolution causes convolution effects.

老师补充：这种理论可推广，用于界面、纳米材料等复杂体系的结构分析，关键在于理解电子密度分布。 Teacher's addition: This theory can be extended to structural analyses of interfaces and nanomaterials, with the key being the understanding of electron density distribution.

晶体的衍射Diffraction by a crystal

晶体结构与电子密度Crystal Structure and Electron Density

晶体由周期性重复的基本结构单元（晶胞）组成。 Crystals consist of periodically repeated unit cells as their basic structural units.

通过格矢a⃗1a1​、a⃗2a2​、a⃗3a3​，可以表示晶胞沿三个方向的平移。 The lattice vectors a⃗1a1​, a⃗2a2​, and a⃗3a3​ represent the translation of the unit cell along three directions.

晶体总电子密度ρ(r⃗)ρ(r)为各单位晶胞电子密度ρ0ρ0​逐格平移的总和： The total electron density ρ(r⃗)ρ(r) of a crystal is the sum of electron densities ρ0ρ0​ of each unit cell translated over the lattice:

晶体的周期性决定了其衍射性质。 The periodicity of a crystal determines its diffraction properties.

晶体衍射的数学描述Mathematical Formulation of Crystal Diffraction

晶体的散射幅可表示为电子密度的傅里叶变换F(ρ)(r⃗)F(ρ)(r)。 The scattering amplitude of a crystal is expressed as the Fourier transform F(ρ)(r⃗)F(ρ)(r) of its electron density.

公式展开为晶格和结构因子的乘积： The formula expands into the product of the lattice sum and the structure factor:

其中，Lattice Sum（晶格和）S(q⃗)S(q​)反映系统有序性，Structure factor（结构因子）F(q⃗)F(q​)反映化学组成。 Here, the lattice sum S(q⃗)S(q​) reflects system order, and the structure factor F(q⃗)F(q​) encodes chemical composition.

晶格和（Lattice sum）的一般表达式为： The general expression for the lattice sum is:

衍射峰与Q空间Diffraction Peaks and Reciprocal Space

在QQ空间（q⃗=k⃗d−k⃗iq​=kd​−ki​，散射矢量），当Q⃗⋅a⃗j=2πnQ​⋅aj​=2πn时，散射强度达最大，形成衍射峰。 In QQ-space (q⃗=k⃗d−k⃗iq​=kd​−ki​, the scattering vector), the scattered intensity reaches a maximum, forming diffraction peaks when Q⃗⋅a⃗j=2πnQ​⋅aj​=2πn.

这对应倒易空间的节点（衍射峰只在倒易晶格点出现）。 This corresponds to nodes of the reciprocal space (diffraction peaks appear only at reciprocal lattice points).

倒易晶格基底为a⃗1∗,a⃗2∗,a⃗3∗a1∗​,a2∗​,a3∗​，定义为： The reciprocal lattice is based on a⃗1∗,a⃗2∗,a⃗3∗a1∗​,a2∗​,a3∗​, defined as:   a⃗1∗⋅a⃗1=2πa1∗​⋅a1​=2π，a⃗2∗⋅a⃗2=2πa2∗​⋅a2​=2π，a⃗3∗⋅a⃗3=2πa3∗​⋅a3​=2π*

米勒指数与晶面Miller Indices and Crystal Planes

满足Q⃗⋅a⃗1=2πhQ​⋅a1​=2πh，Q⃗⋅a⃗2=2πkQ​⋅a2​=2πk，Q⃗⋅a⃗3=2πlQ​⋅a3​=2πl的hh、kk、ll即为米勒指数，表示不同晶面。 hh, kk, and ll which satisfy Q⃗⋅a⃗1=2πhQ​⋅a1​=2πh, Q⃗⋅a⃗2=2πkQ​⋅a2​=2πk, and Q⃗⋅a⃗3=2πlQ​⋅a3​=2πl are called Miller indices and represent different crystal planes.

不同的米勒指数对应不同的晶面取向。 Different Miller indices correspond to different crystal plane orientations.

课件举例说明了如何通过OA、OB、OC等几何量确定具体晶面。 The slides provide examples showing how to determine specific planes using geometric quantities such as OA, OB, and OC.

Bravais点阵Bravais Lattices

全部晶体可归为14种Bravais点阵类型，包括三斜、单斜、正交、四方、三方、六方和立方等。 All crystals can be classified into 14 types of Bravais lattices, including triclinic, monoclinic, orthorhombic, tetragonal, trigonal, hexagonal, and cubic systems.

每种Bravais点阵有不同的对称性和参数a,b,ca,b,c及角度α,β,γα,β,γ。 Each Bravais lattice has distinct symmetry and parameters a,b,ca,b,c and angles α,β,γα,β,γ.

衍射强度的决定因素Factors Determining Diffraction Intensity

散射强度I(q⃗)=∣S(q⃗)∣2∣F(q⃗)∣2I(q​)=∣S(q​)∣2∣F(q​)∣2，其中S(q⃗)S(q​)表征有序性，F(q⃗)F(q​)表征单位晶胞的化学组成。 The scattered intensity I(q⃗)=∣S(q⃗)∣2∣F(q⃗)∣2I(q​)=∣S(q​)∣2∣F(q​)∣2; S(q⃗)S(q​) indicates order, F(q⃗)F(q​) shows the unit cell’s chemical composition.

粉末样品与单晶样品主要区别是取向无序，导致所有可能的衍射信号均被采集。 The main difference between powder samples and single crystals is the random orientation of grains in powders, enabling detection of all possible diffraction signals.

衍射信号还受分辨率卷积、极化因子、Ewald球截取等影响。 Diffraction signals are also affected by resolution convolution, polarization effects (cos⁡2θcos2θ), and Ewald sphere interception.

老师补充说明：结构因子决定了哪些衍射峰会因调制度和相互抵消而消失（峰熄灭）。 Teacher adds: The structure factor modulates peak presence, leading to extinction of certain diffraction peaks by cancellation.

表面衍射Surface diffraction

表面衍射关注表面二维周期结构而非三维体结构。 Surface diffraction focuses on the two-dimensional periodic structure at the surface, rather than the three-dimensional bulk structure.

电子密度公式：

，第三个方向（a⃗3a3​）不参与周期性叠加。 Electron density formula: with the third direction (a⃗3a3​) excluded from periodic stacking.

这种处理描述了表面层、薄膜或单分子层等低维结构，有别于体材料三维周期。 This treatment describes low-dimensional structures like surface layers, thin films, or monolayers, unlike bulk materials’ three-dimensional periodicity.

晶格和及其物理意义Lattice Sum and Physical Meaning

∣S(q⃗)∣2=∏j=12∣sin⁡(NjQ⃗⋅a⃗j2)sin⁡(Q⃗⋅a⃗j2)∣2∣S(q​)∣2=∏j=12​​sin(2Q​⋅aj​​)sin(2Nj​Q​⋅aj​​)​​2

这种表达式意味着在倒易空间形成二维杆状阵列。 This expression means a 2D array of rods appears in reciprocal space.

倒易点阵和截断杆Reciprocal Lattice and Truncation Rods

二维周期结构在倒易空间表现为杆状分布，每条杆沿垂直表面的方向无限延伸。 The 2D periodic structure appears as rods in reciprocal space, each rod extending infinitely along the direction perpendicular to the surface.

物理上，这些杆（truncation rods）反映了表面结构的有限厚度和表面截断效应。 Physically, these rods (truncation rods) reflect the finite thickness and truncation effect of the surface structure.

老师补充：只有在低维体系中可观察到截断杆，体材料则呈离散衍射点。 Teacher’s note: Truncation rods are only observable in low-dimensional systems; bulk materials show discrete diffraction spots.

散射强度公式及结构因子Scattering Intensity Formula and Structure Factor

散射强度公式：

Scattering intensity formula: I(q⃗)=∣S(q⃗xy)∣2∣F(q⃗xy,qz)∣2I(q​)=∣S(q​xy​)∣2∣F(q​xy​,qz​)∣2

结构因子FF体现表面内部的原子排列和化学组成，并决定截断杆的调制。 The structure factor FF characterizes inner atomic arrangement and chemical composition of the surface, modulating truncation rods.

如何实现表面选择性衍射How: Grazing Incidence Geometry（掠入射几何）

常规情况下，表面分子密度ds=1016ds​=1016分子/厘米²，体材料密度cv=1016cv​=1016分子/厘米³，导致表面散射IS≪IVIS​≪IV​，难以区分信号。 In normal geometry, surface molecular density ds=1016ds​=1016 molecules/cm² versus bulk density cv=1016cv​=1016 molecules/cm³, making surface scattering IS≪IVIS​≪IV​, thus hard to detect.

传统反射/入射等角度下，体散射信号淹没表面信息。 At conventional incident/reflected angles, bulk scattering overwhelms surface information.

X射线用于掠入射时，利用其在物质中折射率n=1−δn=1−δ，δ∼10−6δ∼10−6，空气中n≈0n≈0。 With X-rays at grazing incidence, the refractive index in materials is n=1−δn=1−δ, δ∼10−6δ∼10−6, while n≈0n≈0 in air.

发生全外反射的条件为θin<θcθin​<θc​，θc=arccos(1−δ)≈0.1∘θc​=arccos(1−δ)≈0.1∘（2 mrad）。 Total external reflection happens when θin<θcθin​<θc​ and θc=arccos(1−δ)≈0.1∘θc​=arccos(1−δ)≈0.1∘ (2 mrad).

全反射下，无穿透波，仅有沿界面传播的消逝波（evanescent wave）。 Under total external reflection, there is no transmitted wave; only an evanescent wave travels along the interface.

消逝波特点与穿透深度Evanescent Wave Properties and Penetration Depth

消逝波沿界面传播，其强度随深度指数衰减。 The evanescent wave travels along the interface, with intensity decaying exponentially with depth.

该深度与典型朗缪尔单分子膜厚度同级，表明可高灵敏测量表面结构。 This is of the same order as the thickness of a typical Langmuir monolayer, indicating high sensitivity for surface structure.

界面任意层都会散射或衍射消逝波，实现界面专属性探测。 Any layer at the interface will scatter or diffract the evanescent wave, enabling interface-specific probing.

对实验的实际要求Practical Experimental Requirements

由于需要高亮度、低发散的X射线，以保证信号可被测量，需采用同步辐射X射线源。 Due to the need for high brilliance, low divergence X-ray to ensure measurable signal, synchrotron radiation X-ray sources are essential.

只有高通量和高准直度可实现掠入射全反射和有效表面探测。 Only high flux and high collimation enable grazing incidence total reflection and effective surface probing.

同步辐射源下掠入射技术Grazing Incidence Techniques @ Synchrotron Source

同步辐射源简介Introduction to Synchrotron Radiation Source

高能电子在储存环内以相对论速度运动并经历加速，产生同步辐射。 Electrons at relativistic speeds in a storage ring experience acceleration and generate synchrotron radiation.

荷电粒子在磁场中被加速时会辐射光，这就是同步辐射效应。 Charged particles emit light when accelerated in a magnetic field, known as the synchrotron effect.

同步辐射源提供极高强度和准直性的光子（高通量，低发散性），非常适合表面与薄膜X射线实验。 Synchrotron sources provide extremely intense and well-collimated photons (high flux, low divergence), ideal for surface and thin film X-ray experiments.

比常规X射线源更优：可实现小于100微弧度的低发散性（远优于2毫弧度的入射要求）。 Superior to conventional X-ray sources: achieves divergence lower than 100 μrad (far better than the required 2 mrad incidence angle).

同步辐射光束通过光束线传递到样品进行实验。 The synchrotron light beam is transmitted through beamlines to the sample for experiments.

老师补充说明：只有如此高亮度和低散射性的光源，才能进行掠入射实验并获得可靠的数据结果。 Teacher’s explanation: Only with such high-brightness, low-divergence sources can grazing incidence experiments be performed and yield reliable data.

SIRIUS线站实验布局与关键光学部件SIRIUS Beamline @ SOLEIL: Experimental Layout and Key Optical Components

螺旋波荡器（Helicoidal Undulator HU36）产生能量范围为1.1~13 keV的高强度准直辐射，即实验所需的X射线。 The helicoidal undulator HU36 generates intense, collimated radiation with photon energies 1.1–13 keV, which is used as the X-ray source for experiments.

M1反射镜（球面或平面，用于谐波抑制）、M2/M3/M4反射镜（分别为垂直和水平弯曲反射镜，以及偏折镜）用于聚焦和定向光束。 M1 mirror (spherical or flat, for harmonic rejection), M2/M3 (vertical/horizontal benders), and M4 deflector mirrors are used to focus and steer the beam.

多层光栅单色仪、双晶单色仪用于选择所需波长和控制极化状态。 Multilayer grating monochromators and double crystal monochromators are used to select the desired wavelength and control polarization.

光束监测采用金刚石二极管（50或3μm厚），实现对不同能量区域的探测与校准。 Beam monitoring is performed with diamond diodes (thickness 50 μm for >4 keV, 3 μm for <4 keV), for detection and calibration across energy ranges.

老师补充强调：每个光学元件都在高精度光束准直、单色和调控过程中起决定性作用。 Teacher’s note: Every optical element plays a crucial role in achieving high-precision beam collimation, monochromatization, and control.

掠入射几何与GIXD实验设计Grazing Incidence Geometry & GIXD/Surface Diffraction Setup

实验采用掠入射模式，即X射线入射角θ_in小于材料的临界角θ_c。 The experiment uses grazing incidence geometry, where incident angle θ_in is less than the critical angle θ_c of the material.

掠入射下X射线在样品表面入射，只有极薄的表面层被激发，实现表面/界面专属性探测。 Under grazing incidence, X-rays impinge on the surface, exciting only an ultra-thin layer and thus enabling surface/interface-specific probing.

表面衍射收集样品表面周期性结构信息，常用参数包括qxy=4πλsin⁡2θ2qxy​=λ4π​sin22θ​，qz=2πλ[sin⁡θz+sin⁡θin]qz​=λ2π​[sinθz​+sinθin​]。 Surface diffraction collects periodic structure information; key parameters: qxy=4πλsin⁡2θ2qxy​=λ4π​sin22θ​, qz=2πλ[sin⁡θz+sin⁡θin]qz​=λ2π​[sinθz​+sinθin​].

通过扫描2θ，实际上就是扫描矢量q，获得衍射图谱反映分子/原子排列规律。 By scanning 2θ, one effectively scans vector q, acquiring diffraction patterns that reveal molecular or atomic arrangement.

课堂补充：掠入射几何极大抑制了体材料散射噪音，使得微弱的表面结构信号得以检测。 Teacher’s supplement: Grazing incidence geometry suppresses bulk scattering noise, allowing the detection of weak surface structure signals.

仿生膜模型体系与多种研究表征技术Bio-mimetic Model Systems vs. Investigation Techniques

常见模型包括：单分子膜、支撑/漂浮/自持双层、油滴包裹双层和脂质体，模拟生物膜结构与功能。 Common models include: monolayers, supported/floating/free-standing bilayers, droplet interface bilayers, and liposomes, to mimic biological membrane structure and function.

多种表征手段协同应用： Various characterization methods are used in combination:

热力学方法（如相图、吸附动力学） Thermodynamics (e.g., phase diagrams, adsorption kinetics)

光学与局部探针显微（如BAM、荧光） Optical and local probe microscopies (e.g., BAM, fluorescence)

光谱学方法 Spectroscopies (molecular conformation, π-stacking, etc.)

X射线、中子散射（定量研究分子有序、结构演化） X-ray/neutron scattering (quantitative study of molecular order, structural evolution)

组学注释：不同技术适用于不同膜结构、体系和物理问题，X射线、中子优于解析分子有序性。 Annotation: Each technique suits different membranes, systems, and physical problems; X-ray/neutron techniques excel in parsing molecular ordering.

课堂补充：标准膜模型实验平台可以通过掠入射X射线技术达成表面分辨探测，是研究生物物理过程的重要手段。 Teacher emphasizes: Standard membrane model platforms, when combined with grazing incidence X-ray techniques, achieve surface-resolved probing, essential for advanced biophysical studies.

能带理论（1）Band theory (1)

能带理论是凝聚态物理的核心，描述了固体中电子的允许能级，并阐释了电导率的本质。 Band theory is fundamental in solid-state physics, describing the allowed energy levels of electrons in a solid and explaining the origin of electrical conductivity.

固体中的能级被分为价带、导带，两者之间为禁带（能隙 EgapEgap​）。 In solids, energy levels are divided into the valence band, the conduction band, and the forbidden energy gap (EgapEgap​) between them.

绝对零度（0K）时，电子全部处于费米能级以下，禁带内无电子。 At absolute zero (0 K), all electrons are below the Fermi level, and there are no electrons in the band gap.

温度升高后，部分电子跃迁到能级更高的导带区，从而具备电导性。 With increasing temperature, some electrons gain enough energy to jump into the conduction band, enabling electrical conductivity.

费米分布函数 f(E)f(E) 给出在特定温度下电子占据特定能级的概率，表达式为： The Fermi function f(E)f(E) gives the probability that a given electron energy state is occupied at a particular temperature; it is expressed as:

其中 EFEF​ 是费米能级，kk 是玻尔兹曼常数，TT 是绝对温度。 Where EFEF​ is the Fermi level, kk is Boltzmann constant, and TT is the absolute temperature.

老师补充：能带图反映了温度、能隙与电子布居之间的定量关系，是区分金属、半导体和绝缘体的基础。 Teacher’s note: Band diagrams quantitatively reflect the relationship among temperature, band gap, and electron occupation, forming the basis for distinguishing metals, semiconductors, and insulators.

能带理论（2）Band theory (2)

固体的能带结构决定其电学特性，能带之间存在可允许带（如价带、导带）和禁带。 The band structure of solids determines their electrical properties, with permitted bands (valence band, conduction band) separated by forbidden gaps.

金属：费米能级处于价带和导带重叠区，能隙为零，具有良好的导电性。 Metals: The Fermi level lies where the valence and conduction bands overlap (gap = 0), thus metals are good electrical conductors.

绝缘体：价带和导带之间有大于5eV的能隙，常温下电子难以跃迁，基本不导电。 Insulators: There is a large band gap (>5>5 eV) between the valence and conduction bands, so electrons cannot jump across at normal temperatures, resulting in almost no conductivity.

半导体：能隙较小（通常1–3eV），掺杂可进一步减小能隙。掺杂后导电性增强。 Semiconductors: They have a small band gap (typically 1–3 eV); doping can reduce the gap further, thereby increasing conductivity.

能带的电子填充遵循费米-狄拉克统计规律，电子为自旋1/2费米子。 Electron filling in bands follows Fermi-Dirac statistics—electrons are spin-1/2 fermions.

老师提示：能隙宽度直接影响材料用途，决定其在微电子、光电子等领域的应用。 Teacher’s remark: The band gap width directly determines the application of a material in fields such as microelectronics and optoelectronics.

隧穿效应The tunnel effect

隧穿效应描述了电子面对势垒时能以一定概率穿透（即使其能量低于势垒），是STM等扫描探针技术的基础。 The tunnel effect describes how electrons can cross a potential barrier with a certain probability even if their energy is lower than the barrier; this is the physical basis of scanning probe techniques like STM.

STM实验中，金属尖端与样品表面间距约为几个埃，能级重叠区允许隧穿电流产生。 In STM experiments, the metallic tip is placed a few angstroms above the sample surface, and the overlap of energy levels allows tunneling current to flow.

通过测量隧穿电流II，反映样品表面在费米能级处的局部态密度 ρSρS​ ： By measuring the tunnel current II, one probes the local density of states ρSρS​ at the Fermi level of the sample surface:

其中 ρtρt​ 为金属探针的态密度。 Where ρtρt​ is the density of states in the metallic tip.

老师补充：只有电子波函数在势垒间重叠时才有隧穿电流，这对表面分析（如原子分辨成像）极为重要。 Teacher’s note: Tunneling current arises only when electron wavefunctions overlap across the barrier, which is crucial for atomic resolution in surface analysis.

STM中的隧穿效应The tunnel effect in STM

负极性(-V)时，尖端费米能级的电子隧穿到样品表面的HOMO能级，此时主要探测被占据态。 In negative polarity (-V), electrons from the tip’s Fermi level tunnel into the sample’s highest occupied molecular orbital (HOMO), so the STM probes mostly the occupied states.

此时图像对表面固体态密度不敏感。 Under this condition, the image is insensitive to the density of solid states of the surface.

正极性(+V)时，尖端的电子可隧穿到样品的空态（LUMO），映射了空能级的信息。 With positive polarity (+V), tunnel electrons cross from the tip into the empty states of the sample (LUMO), enabling probing of empty energy states of the surface.

调节外加电压，实现被占据态与空能级的分辨，是STM解析表面电子结构的关键。 By varying the applied voltage, STM distinguishes between occupied and empty states, which is key for analyzing the electronic structure of surfaces.

老师补充：STM可以通过选择不同极性，分别成像材料的电子分布与能带结构，广泛应用于表面科学和纳米材料研究。 Teacher’s supplement: STM can image both the electron distribution and band structure by selecting voltages of different polarity, making it widely used in surface science and nanomaterials studies.

STM原理与操作模式STM Principle and Operating Modes

AFM原理与操作模式及其应用AFM Principle, Operating Modes and Applications

AFM原理示意Principle of AFM

AFM利用极尖锐的探针头（通常为硅微加工探针）结合柔性悬臂梁对样品表面进行扫描成像。 AFM uses an extremely sharp tip (typically a micromachined silicon probe) mounted on a flexible cantilever to scan and image sample surfaces.

探针通过压电陶瓷驱动器实现精确位移，表面形貌通过栅格方式一行行扫描获得。 The tip is moved with nanometer precision by a piezoelectric scanner, and the surface morphology is obtained by raster scanning line by line.

探针靠近样品产生作用力，导致悬臂梁发生微小弯曲，弯曲被激光反射并由位置灵敏探测器检测。 When the tip approaches the sample, tip-sample forces cause cantilever bending, which is detected by the reflection of a laser onto a position-sensitive photodetector.

图像可以记录表面的高度变化（拓扑）或反映局部物理化学性质。 The resulting image reflects surface height (topography) or local physicochemical properties.

AFM关键结构及检测方式Key AFM Components and Detection

纳米级锐利探针固定在微小悬臂梁末端，整体为弹性弹簧结构。 The nanoscale sharp tip is attached to the end of a micromachined spring-like cantilever.

探针与样品作用力产生悬臂梁的偏折，激光束照射悬臂梁并反射到分束光电探测器，精确检测偏折量。 Tip-sample interactions induce cantilever deflection, which is measured by reflecting a laser beam onto a position-sensitive photodetector.

反馈回路根据检测到的偏折信号，自动调整探针高度以稳定作用力，实现高分辨率成像。 A feedback loop adjusts tip height in real time based on the detected deflection, maintaining constant interaction force for high-resolution imaging.

AFM操作模式AFM Operating Modes

接触模式（Contact Mode）：探针头持续与样品表面接触并拖曳，主要受到短程斥力（泡利原理）影响。 Contact mode: The probe tip is in continuous contact with the surface and scanned across, dominated by short-range repulsive forces (Pauli exclusion principle).

反馈系统监测并调节梁的偏折，实现力控扫描。 The feedback system monitors and adjusts cantilever deflection to maintain constant force during scanning.

非接触与间断接触（轻敲/tapping模式）：探针以固定频率和振幅振荡，主要受到范德华力和静电力作用。 Non-contact and intermittent contact (tapping mode): The probe oscillates at fixed frequency and amplitude, mainly subject to van der Waals and electrostatic forces.

反馈回路可控制探针的振幅、相位或频率，输出振幅像和相位像等。 The feedback loop can control amplitude, phase, or frequency, thus generating amplitude and phase images.

接触模式特点Contact Mode Features

用于测量拓扑、偏折和摩擦等表面物理信息，采集速度快，但易损伤软样品。 Suitable for topography, deflection and friction measurements; fast acquisition but potential to damage soft samples.

斥力作用源于泡利不相容原理——同种自旋的费米子不能处于同一量子态。 Repulsive force arises from the Pauli exclusion principle—identical fermions (same spin) cannot occupy the same quantum state.

振幅调制AFM（AM-AFM）模式特性Amplitude Modulation AFM (AM-AFM) Mode Features

探针以受控方式持续振荡，主要感受吸引力（如范德华力）及静电力。 The tip oscillates in a controlled fashion, subject to mainly attractive (vdW) and electrostatic forces.

振幅和相位随样品位置/表面特性变化，反馈调节保持振幅稳定。 Amplitude and phase shift depend on sample properties; feedback loop ensures stable oscillation amplitude.

AFM结构与仪器Atomic Force Microscope Structure and Equipment

现代AFM包含高精度压电扫描器、激光测量系统、分区式光电探测器以及数据采集/反馈控制单元。 Modern AFMs comprise a precise piezo scanner, laser measuring system, segmented photodetector, and data acquisition/feedback unit.

不同仪器样式适应多种研究场景（如大型仪器/光学耦合/环境控制等）。 Various configurations are adapted for research needs (e.g., stand-alone or integrated with optical microscopes, environmental control).

AFM成像与典型案例AFM Imaging and Typical Cases

通过不同操作模式在云母片表面成像，展示核酸、胶体颗粒等纳米对象的排列。 Imaging of cleaved mica and other substrates in various modes demonstrates arrangement of nanomaterials such as DNA, colloidal particles.

通过对比不同模式，可观察拖拽造成的表面结构破坏（如接触模式下颗粒移位）。 Comparing modes, one can observe structural disruption (e.g., particle displacement in contact mode).

轻敲模式更适合软材料或生物样品，可获得高度分辨结构且对样品损伤小。 Tapping mode is preferred for soft or biological samples, providing high-resolution imaging with minimal sample damage.

AFM原子级分辨能力Atomic Resolution by AFM

AFM能在空气甚至溶液中实现石墨（HOPG）、云母等材料的原子级分辨。 AFM can achieve atomic resolution imaging of substrates such as HOPG and mica under ambient or liquid conditions.

采用恒幅、恒频扫描，实现对表面原子周期性的直接观测。 Constant amplitude and frequency scanning enables direct observation of surface atomic periodicity.

老师课堂补充Teacher’s Supplement

着重强调了接触模式下反馈回路、探针与样品之间力的控制，以及不同样品所需成像模式的选择逻辑。 Teacher emphasized the feedback loop and force control in contact mode, and how mode selection depends on sample types.

实际演示了AFM在溶液（如水）中成像纳米球、分辨云母原子周期的经典实验。 Demonstrated classic experiments of AFM imaging nanospheres in water and resolving atomic lattice of mica.

相位成像模式及其应用Phase Imaging Mode and Its Applications

相位成像模式原理与特点Principle and Features of Phase Imaging Mode

相位成像模式指的是测量悬臂梁激发信号与实际由光电二极管检测到信号之间的相位差。 Phase imaging mode measures the phase shift between the cantilever excitation signal and the measured signal on the photodiode.

该模式能够获得样品局部的机械信息，包括弹性、刚性、粘弹性、摩擦、黏附力以及化学成分等。 This mode provides access to local mechanical properties of the sample, including elasticity, rigidity, viscoelasticity, friction, adhesion, and chemical composition.

相位信号对AFM探针与样品表面之间的接触面积和相互作用高度敏感。 The phase signal is highly sensitive to the tip contact area and the interaction between tip and sample surface.

相位成像信号与测量Interpretation of Phase Imaging Signal

通过比较激励信号与响应信号的相位差，可识别表面的不同力学属性区。 Comparing the excitation and response phase shift helps distinguish regions with different mechanical characteristics.

相位成像使表面微区的物理化学特性成像成为可能，对材料异质性分布尤为敏感。 Phase imaging allows mapping of local physicochemical properties, being especially sensitive to material heterogeneity.

典型应用如LPS单分子层样品，可以同时获得高度与相位的图像。 A typical application is the LPS monolayer, where both height and phase images can be obtained simultaneously.

相位成像对材料性质的表征Characterization of Material Properties by Phase Imaging

弹性与粘弹性区域在相位图中表现为明暗对比，摩擦力和黏附变化也会导致相位变化。 Regions with different elasticity or viscoelasticity appear as contrasting areas in the phase image; friction and adhesion also modulate the phase.

化学成分的改变会引起相位信号的差异，有助于识别材料组分分布。 Changes in chemical composition result in phase signal differences, assisting in mapping the distribution of components.

相位成像图库与案例Phase Images Gallery and Examples

在高取向石墨（HOPG）上可以直接观察到烷烃类（C18H38、C36H74、C60H122）分子的有序排列。 Ordered arrangement of alkanes (C18H38, C36H74, C60H122) can be observed directly on highly oriented pyrolytic graphite (HOPG).

脂质双层（如DOPC/SM/Chol 1:1:1）在高度像、振幅像以及相位图中都显示出丰富的形貌信息。 Lipid bilayers (e.g., DOPC/SM/Chol 1:1:1) display detailed morphology in height, amplitude (error signal), and phase shift images.

聚苯乙烯和聚丁二烯等聚合物材料的相位图揭示了纳米尺度的结构和分散情况。 Phase images of polymers like polystyrene and polybutadiene reveal nanoscale structure and domain dispersion.

AFM光谱模式简介及在液体环境下的应用Introduction to AFM Spectroscopic Mode and Application in Liquid

AFM光谱模式通过力-距离曲线分析样品的力学响应，可以分别获得刚度、杨氏模量、接触点、粘附功等力学参数。 AFM spectroscopic mode analyzes force–distance curves to extract mechanical parameters like stiffness, Young’s modulus, contact point, and work of adhesion.

在液体环境下测量，尖端与样品间的粘附力被极大降低，使操作更温和并能连续获取力曲线。 In liquid medium, adhesion forces between tip and sample are almost eliminated, making measurements less invasive and enabling continuous force–distance curves.

成像时，每个像素点快速采集一组力曲线，便于高通量分析。 During imaging, a rapid succession of force–distance curves is acquired at each pixel, allowing high-throughput analysis.

AFM的实际成像与测量实例Practical Examples of AFM Imaging and Measurement

细菌视紫红质单分子层在缓冲液下的高度像能够分辨分子排列。 Height images of bacteriorhodopsin monolayers in buffer resolve molecular arrangements.

AFM可以直接成像活细胞表面蛋白，为细胞膜蛋白的动态研究提供依据。 AFM can directly image S-layer proteins on live cell surfaces, aiding dynamic membrane protein studies.

小鼠星形胶质细胞的高度、局部放大、杨氏模量分布展示了细胞力学和形态的细致信息。 Mouse astrocytes’ height, zoomed features and Young’s modulus maps showcase detailed mechanical and morphological cell information.

AFM纳米力学测试在软骨老化及组织工程中的应用AFM Nanomechanical Tests in Cartilage Aging and Tissue Engineering

利用AFM开展软骨组织力学测试，可评估不同时期或处理后的力学性能变化，包括分子-细胞-组织多尺度力学响应。 AFM-based mechanical tests on cartilage can assess property changes at different stages/treatments, revealing hierarchical responses from molecule to cell to tissue.

不同细胞类型、培养条件下的杨氏模量分布揭示了软骨工程材料的生物力学性能差异。 Distributions of Young’s modulus for different cell types/culture conditions illustrate the biomechanical variability in cartilage engineering.

AFM结合荧光成像应用于神经元轴突生长锥研究AFM Combined with Fluorescence Imaging for Neuronal Growth Cone Study

全内反射荧光（TIRF）和AFM成像可同步分析神经元轴突生长锥的形态与微管分布。 TIRF and AFM imaging can jointly investigate axon growth cone morphology and microtubule distribution in neurons.

AFM轻敲模式下获得的生长锥区域高度像，尺度条10微米，清晰显示了微结构。 Tapping mode AFM provides detailed height images of growth cone regions (scale bar 10 μm), clarifying local nano-/micro-architecture.

课堂补充与要点归纳Teacher’s Supplements and Summary

老师强调：相位成像模式对软硬异质样品尤其有效，是生物、材料科学表征不可或缺的方法。 The teacher emphasized that phase imaging mode is especially effective for heterogeneous soft/hard samples and is indispensable in bio- and material sciences.

AFM纳米机械和光谱模式不断扩展其应用边界，推动组织工程等交叉学科领域发展。 AFM nanomechanical and spectroscopic modes are expanding application boundaries, fueling advances in interdisciplinary fields like tissue engineering.

其他实例与表面分析方法结合Other Examples and Combination of Surface Analysis Techniques

胶原纤维与生物样品AFM成像Collagen Fibers and Biomolecular AFM Imaging

云母表面胶原纤维成像：AFM能清晰观测到胶原纤维在云母基底上的网状排列，展现纤维结构及交织状态。 On mica, AFM reveals a clear meshwork of collagen fibers, showing fiber arrangements and cross-linking.

I型胶原蛋白的骨组织成像：AFM应用于人体骨骼可观测到I型胶原的带状周期性结构，体现骨内纳米尺度的有序排列。 AFM imaging of human bone with type I collagen displays periodic banded structures, representing nanoscale collagen organization in bone matrix.

DNA分子吸附云母的成像：AFM能分辨单链或环状DNA在云母上的吸附形态，包括分子链长度和卷曲状态。 AFM can distinguish single-stranded or circular DNA adsorbed on mica, providing details on chain length and conformation.

生物素-链霉亲和素连接的DNA定位：利用生物素链霉亲和素强连接将DNA固定于云母表面，AFM可精确定位分子末端，实现单分子级别分析。 DNA fixed to mica via strong biotin-streptavidin coupling allows AFM to precisely locate molecular termini, enabling single-molecule analysis.

液晶受限结构与多界面观测Liquid Crystals Frustrated by Multiple Interfaces

8CB/石墨烯型二维材料（如MoS2）/空气三相界面：液晶8CB在两界面形成层状排列，与MoS2衬底晶格产生有序互补。 At the air/8CB/MoS2 interface, layered liquid crystal 8CB forms ordered structures, matching with the MoS2 lattice.

AFM非接触模式：能在空气中对大面积样品（50微米²）进行高分辨成像，观察到液晶条带及台阶结构。 Non-contact AFM mode enables high-resolution imaging of large areas, revealing stripes and step structures of the liquid crystal.

STM成像：在空气中也能显示液晶分子局部排列，明确可见周期结构。 STM in air also resolves local 8CB arrangements, with clear periodicity visible.

高度剖面分析：通过截取线剖面，可定量液晶层间距和表面起伏。 Height profile analysis across the image allows quantitative measurement of layer spacing and surface undulations.

8CB单分子层在MoS2上的吸附和结构8CB Monolayer Adsorbed on MoS2

MoS2表面STM成像：捕捉到裸MoS2表面原子级台阶和缺陷。 STM captures atomic steps and defects on bare MoS2 surfaces.

8CB单分子层STM成像：分子层在MoS2上呈带状有序排布，沿晶格特定[100]方向定向排列。 8CB monolayer shows ribbon-like, highly ordered arrangement along the [100] crystal direction of MoS2.

结构与MoS2基底共轭，分子平铺呈头尾连接。 The structure is commensurate with the MoS2 substrate; molecules lie flat in a head-to-tail orientation.

表面分析技术的结合与互补Combination of Surface Analysis Techniques

多厚度薄膜的AFM成像：AM-AFM（振幅调制-原子力显微镜）展示不同厚度膜（如300nm、70nm、10nm）上液晶条带与薄膜表面差异。 AM-AFM illustrates how stripe patterns and surface features change across films with varying thickness (e.g., 300nm, 70nm, 10nm).

X射线衍射：揭示多层膜的取向与有序度，归一化强度-角度曲线反映结构信息。 X-ray diffraction discloses multi-layer orientation and order; normalized intensity versus angle curves provide structural insights.

光学显微镜辅助：通过偏光或干涉增强对表面微区形貌的宏观辨识，与AFM、XRD实现多尺度互补。 Optical microscopy, using polarization or interference, helps visualize surface domains at larger scale, complementing AFM and XRD.

8CB体相结构及厚薄膜差异8CB Bulk Structure and Thin/Thick Film Differences

厚膜（400nm）：在MoS2/空气界面形成稳定层状排列，各层堆叠规整。 Thick films (400nm) at MoS2/air interface form regular, stable lamellar structures.

薄膜（50nm）：边缘处出现缺陷、层错现象，更易出现复杂的界面畸变。 Thin films (50nm) show more defects and dislocations at the interface, with increased interfacial distortion.

AFM探针功能化与分子间作用力测量AFM Tip Functionalization and Force Measurement

通过在AFM探针头修饰特定配体（如生物素），可特异性检测受体（如链霉亲和素）之间的结合力。 By functionalizing the AFM tip with specific ligands (e.g., biotin), binding forces between receptors (e.g., avidin/streptavidin) can be measured.

探针-样品一旦形成单分子连接，通过拉伸—断裂过程可量化分子间粘附力。 Upon single-molecule bond formation between tip and sample, pulling and rupture events yield quantitative adhesion force measurements.

该方法推动了分子识别与生物相互作用测量的定量化和高灵敏度分析。 This approach advances the quantitative, highly sensitive study of molecular recognition and biomolecular interactions.

课堂补充与要点归纳Teacher’s Supplements and Key Points

老师强调：AFM和STM强大的表面成像与分析能力适用于从纳米材料、液晶到生物大分子的多尺度表征。 Teacher emphasized that the powerful surface imaging and analysis abilities of AFM/STM suit multi-scale characterizations—from nanomaterials and liquid crystals to biomacromolecules.

多方法结合（AFM/XRD/光学）有助于全面理解界面结构、力学及功能特性。 Combining multiple techniques (AFM/XRD/optical) supports holistic understanding of interface structures, mechanics, and functions.

AFM分子识别及力谱学应用（以SbpA-StreptagI为例）

AFM Molecular Recognition and Force Spectroscopy Applications (Case Study: SbpA-StreptagI)

AFM探针功能化与SbpA-StreptagI力谱学实验AFM Tip Functionalization and Force Spectroscopy on SbpA-StreptagI

AFM探针通过化学方法功能化，使其末端结合特定分子，如Ethanolamine、Aldehyde-PEG800-NHS、Streptavidin，实现特异性分子识别。 AFM tips are chemically functionalized to attach specific molecules such as ethanolamine, aldehyde-PEG800-NHS, and streptavidin, enabling specific molecular recognition.

PEG链作为柔性连接臂，提高了分子识别和力测量的灵敏度与可控性。 The PEG chain acts as a flexible linker, enhancing sensitivity and control during molecular recognition and force measurement.

利用这一机制，探针与功能化的SbpA-StreptagI层实现受控接触，可记录分子结合及断裂过程的力学响应。 Through this mechanism, controlled contact is established between the probe and the functionalized SbpA-StreptagI layer, allowing the mechanical response of binding and unbinding events to be recorded.

AFM图像显示表面单分子层的有序结构，典型尺度约100纳米，并可辨别局部高低起伏。 AFM images reveal the ordered monolayer structure on the surface at a typical scale of 100 nm, and local topographic variations can be distinguished.

SbpA-StreptagI结合力测量及受生物素阻断的影响Measurement of SbpA-StreptagI Binding Force and Effect of Biotin Blocking

在rSbpA-StreptagI系统中，通过力谱学统计大量解离力，得到对应的概率密度分布。 In the rSbpA-StreptagI system, force spectroscopy is used to collect a large number of unbinding force events, producing a probability density distribution.

未加生物素前，探针与表面分子产生特异性结合，表现为较高概率和典型解离力峰值。 Before biotin blocking, the tip establishes specific binding with surface molecules, resulting in a higher probability and distinct unbinding force peak.

加入2%生物素后，结合位点被竞争性占据，特异性结合事件显著减少，解离力概率分布降低。 Addition of 2% biotin competes for binding sites, which significantly reduces specific binding events and lowers the force probability distribution.

实验表明，AFM可有效区分特异性与非特异性分子相互作用。 The experiment demonstrates that AFM can effectively distinguish between specific and non-specific molecular interactions.

基于AFM分子力谱的分子识别原理Principle of Molecular Recognition via AFM Force Spectroscopy

分子识别过程：探针表面功能化蛋白与样品表面的单分子层结合，AFM可检测结合事件。 The molecular recognition process involves the interaction of protein-functionalized AFM tips with monolayers on the sample surface, and AFM detects such binding events.

拉近探针并逐步后撤的过程记录粘附-断裂曲线，出现特征性的解离力突变，表明分子键断裂。 During approach and retraction, adhesion–rupture curves are recorded, and characteristic force jumps indicate molecular bond breakage.

结合力的统计分布可据此绘制概率密度函数（pdf），并对比不同实验条件下的变化。 The statistical distribution of bond strengths allows probability density function (pdf) plots and comparison across different conditions.

Bell-Evans模型常用于分析解离力随加载速率的变化，推导有效速率常数和分子间能垒。 The Bell-Evans model is commonly used to analyze the dependence of rupture forces on loading rate, yielding effective rate constants and energy barriers of molecular interactions.

AFM分子力谱学的核心功能与拓展应用Core Applications and Further Utilities of AFM Molecular Force Spectroscopy

AFM不仅能成像表面拓扑，还能量化单分子间的机械结合力，实现高灵敏度分子识别。 AFM provides not only topographic imaging but also quantitative measurement of single-molecule binding forces, achieving highly sensitive molecular recognition.

该方法应用于生物分子、受体-配体、生物素-链霉亲和素等特异性交互作用研究，在生物学、药物研发等领域具有重要价值。 This approach is widely used in studies of biomolecular interactions, receptor–ligand and biotin–streptavidin binding, with significant value in biology and pharmaceutical research.

AFM能提供的主要研究内容Main Research Aspects Provided by AFM

表面形貌成像与结构分析，实时研究生长、腐蚀、氧化等动力学过程。 Surface topography imaging and structure analysis; real-time studies of kinetics of growth, dissolution, corrosion, oxidation.

表面电性、磁性、导电性，以及物理/化学/生物活动原位观测。 Electrochemical, electrical, magnetic, and biological activities at the surface; in-situ observations.

分子及颗粒间结合强度测量，摩擦与粘弹性等纳米力学性能分析。 Measurement of bond cohesion between molecules and particles, evaluation of surface friction and viscoelastic properties.

多环境适应性研究、无损检测。 Adaptability to diverse environments like variable humidity, temperature, and non-destructive testing.

纳米压痕及纳米生物摩擦学测量。 Nanoindentation and nanotribology, including friction and viscoelasticity analysis on nanoscale.

课堂补充与要点归纳Teacher’s Supplement and Key Takeaways

老师举例强调，AFM分子力谱不仅可测量蛋白质等生物大分子的单分子力学性质，也能通过功能化探针实现对病毒外壳、细胞表面等复杂体系的力学分析。 The teacher highlighted that AFM force spectroscopy measures the mechanics of single biomolecules (e.g., proteins) as well as more complex systems like viral capsids or cell surfaces via probe functionalization.

实验设计中，探针与分子层间的可控连接与解离为分子识别和动力学提供了坚实的物理依据。 Controlled bonding and rupture between the tip and molecular layers provide robust physical bases for molecular recognition and kinetics studies.
