光谱法和分析成像的范围

Scope of Spectral Methods and Analytical Imaging

本课程涉及多种成像和分析技术，包括：

The course covers a range of imaging and analytical techniques, including:

SEM（扫描电子显微镜）、AFM（原子力显微镜）、IR（红外光谱）、RAMAN（拉曼光谱）、近红外（near IR），以及化学计量学基础等。

SEM (Scanning Electron Microscopy), AFM (Atomic Force Microscopy), IR (Infrared Spectroscopy), RAMAN (Raman Spectroscopy), Near IR, and basics of chemometrics.

这些技术可大致分为显微成像和光谱技术。

These techniques can be broadly divided into microscopic imaging and spectral techniques.

显微成像的实际图像举例

Examples of Real Microscopic Images

SEM成像常见的样品有
花粉、氧化锌四足体、蚊子头部、血细胞、熔喷纤维等。

Common SEM imaging samples include: pollen, zinc oxide tetrapods, mosquito heads, blood cells, and melt blown fibers.

这些图像通常为黑白色，展示样品表面的三维形貌细节。

These images are usually in black and white, showing detailed 3D morphology of sample surfaces.

显微成像与光谱技术的结合，让我们能同时获得结构和成分等多方面信息。

The combination of microscopic imaging and spectral techniques enables us to obtain both structural and compositional information.

显微镜技术种类

Types of Microscopy Techniques

主要显微镜技术包括：

The main microscopy techniques include:

SEM
扫描电子显微镜，用于表面形貌和成分分析。
SEM
Scanning Electron Microscopy, for surface morphology and composition analysis.
TEM
透射电子显微镜，揭示样品的结构信息（电子穿过样品）。
TEM
Transmission Electron Microscopy, provides structural information as electrons pass through the sample.
SPM
扫描探针显微镜，进一步细分为AFM（原子力显微镜）和STM（扫描隧道显微镜）。
SPM
Scanning Probe Microscopy, further includes AFM (Atomic Force Microscopy) and STM (Scanning Tunneling Microscopy).

多种显微技术对比及优势

Comparison and Advantages of Multiple Microscopy Techniques

不同技术可用于同一样品（如硅），获得互补信息。

Different techniques can be applied to the same sample (e.g. silicon), providing complementary information.

SEM主要分析样品表面的形貌（morphology）和成分（composition）。

SEM is used mainly for analyzing the sample's surface morphology and composition.

TEM主要获取样品结构信息，电子束穿透样品，可以看到原子排列。

TEM provides structural information by allowing the electron beam to pass through the sample, revealing atomic arrangements.

AFM测量样品表面拓扑（topography），提供纳米级表面高度信息。

AFM measures surface topography, providing nanoscale surface height information.

各技术成像尺度和信息类型不同，因此常常互补使用。

Each technique offers different scales and types of information, so they are often used complementarily.

教师补充内容重要点

Key Additional Points from Teacher

显微成像常见为黑白色，不同技术标注不同的尺度线，如一条线代表1微米或5纳米。

Microscopic images are often black and white, with scale bars indicating dimensions such as 1 micron or 5 nanometers.

三大显微镜（SEM、TEM、SPM）各具特色、互为补充。

The three major microscopy techniques (SEM, TEM, SPM) each have unique features and are complementary.

SEM图像来源于样品与电子束相互作用，产生二次电子或背散射电子等多种信号。

SEM images originate from the interaction between the electron beam and the sample, producing secondary electrons, backscattered electrons, etc.

各成像技术的参数调节与优化，将进一步影响成像质量和分析结果。

The adjustment and optimization of each imaging technique's parameters further impact image quality and analytical results.

扫描电子显微镜的技术原理
SEM不同组成部分Technical Principle: different components of SEM

基本结构与原理 Basic Structure and Principle

SEM由一束高能电子束扫描样品表面成像，需要高真空环境（最低10−310−3 Pa甚至10−810−8 Pa）。 SEM uses a focused beam of high-energy electrons to scan and image the surface of a sample, requiring high vacuum (minimum 10−310−3 Pa or even down to 10−810−8 Pa).

核心组成部分分为电子枪、专用真空立柱（包含透镜与扫描系统）、样品腔和探测器。 The core components include the electron gun, a dedicated vacuum column (with lenses and scanning systems), sample chamber, and detectors.

主要部件解析 Main Components Analysis

电子枪Electron Gun

电子枪产生电子束，是整个系统的发射源。 The electron gun generates the electron beam, serving as the emission source of the system.

老师强调三种常见电子枪类型
钨发丝（Tungsten hairpin）、六硼化镧（Lanthanum hexaboride, LaB6）、场发射（Field emission）。 The lecturer emphasized three main types of electron guns: tungsten hairpin, lanthanum hexaboride (LaB6), and field emission.

真空柱Column

真空柱需维持高真空环境，保证电子束传播无干扰，主要由以下三大部分组成： The column must be kept under high vacuum to allow undisturbed propagation of the electron beam, mainly consisting of three key parts:

1
聚光透镜（Condenser lens）——控制电子束的发散程度与束斑直径。 Condenser lens—controls the divergence and diameter of the electron beam.
2
扫描线圈（Scanning coils）——使电子束以栅格方式扫描样品表面。 Scanning coils—raster the beam across the sample surface.
3
物镜透镜（Objective lens）——精准聚焦电子束到样品表面，提高分辨率。 Objective lens—precisely focuses the beam on the sample surface, improving resolution.

探测器Detectors

样品与电子束相互作用后会产生多种电子信号（如二次电子、背散射电子），分别被不同的探测器收集，用于图像形成和成分分析。 After the interaction between the electron beam and the sample, various electron signals (e.g., secondary electrons, backscattered electrons) are generated and collected by corresponding detectors for imaging and compositional analysis.

电子路径控制 Electron Path Control

核心控制手段包括
The core control methods include:

电磁透镜（Electromagnetic lenses）——决定电子束尺寸和最终分辨率。 Electromagnetic lenses—define beam size and ultimate resolution.

扫描线圈（Scanning coils）——电子束按栅格扫描样品。 Scanning coils—raster the electron beam over the sample.

物镜透镜（Objective lens）——聚焦电子束，实现精准成像。 Objective lens—focuses the beam for precise imaging.

电子枪的类型与性能对比 Types and Performance Comparison of Electron Guns

传统热发射电子枪Thermionic Emission Guns

钨丝与LaB6常用于此类，结构简单、成本低，对真空要求较低。 Tungsten filament and LaB6 types belong here, with simple structure, low cost, and lower vacuum requirement.

钨丝电子枪使用寿命较短（约40-100小时），亮度低，束斑较大（30-100微米）。 Tungsten guns have shorter lifespan (about 40–100 hrs), lower brightness, and larger spot size (30–100 microns).

场发射电子枪Field Emission Guns

金属尖端极细，半径小于100纳米，可获得更高分辨率的电子束，适用于高分辨成像。 Metal tips with a radius below 100 nm provide a much finer, brighter beam, suitable for high-resolution imaging.

场发射电子枪需要极高真空，亮度极高，束斑极小（<5μm），能量扩散低，电流稳定性好。 Field emission guns require ultra-high vacuum, have extremely high brightness, very small spot size (<5 μm), low energy spread, and excellent current stability.

主要参数比较，如下表：

钨丝（Tungsten）：亮度 105105，寿命 40-100小时，束斑 30-100μm，能量扩散 1-3eV，要求真空10−510−5 Torr。

LaB6
亮度 106106，寿命 200-1000小时，束斑5-50μm，能量扩散1-2eV，真空10−710−7 Torr。

热场发射（Thermal FEG）：亮度 108108，寿命>>1000小时，束斑<<5μm，能量扩散 1eV，真空10−1110−11 Torr。

冷场发射（Cold FEG）：亮度 108108，寿命大于1000小时，束斑<<5μm，能量扩散0.3eV，真空10−1110−11 Torr。

Main parameter comparison (see the table below):

Tungsten
brightness 105105, lifetime 40–100 hrs, spot size 30–100 μm, energy spread 1–3 eV, vacuum 10−510−5 Torr.
LaB6
brightness 106106, lifetime 200–1000 hrs, spot size 5–50 μm, energy spread 1–2 eV, vacuum 10−710−7 Torr.
Thermal FEG
brightness 108108, lifetime >>1000 hrs, spot size <<5 μm, energy spread 1 eV, vacuum 10−1110−11 Torr.
Cold FEG
brightness 108108, lifetime >>1000 hrs, spot size <<5 μm, energy spread 0.3 eV, vacuum 10−1110−11 Torr.

实际操作与影响因素 Practical Operation and Influencing Factors

维持高真空对保证图像质量和系统运行极为重要。 Maintaining high vacuum is crucial for image quality and proper operation.

电子源选择和系统参数直接影响最终分辨率与成像效果。 The choice of electron source and system parameters directly affects final resolution and imaging quality.

SEM中二次电子/背散射电子探测器成像SEM: Images from secondary/back scattered electrons detectors

二次电子与背散射电子的来源与深度 Sources and depths of secondary and backscattered electrons

二次电子信号主要来自于样品表面（1-10纳米），能量低于50电子伏特，能细致反映样品表面形貌，分辨率高。 Secondary electrons are mainly emitted from the very surface of the sample (1–10 nm), with low energy (<50 eV), providing detailed surface information and high resolution.

背散射电子主要来自较深层次（约0.1-0.5微米），对材料原子序数差异高度敏感，原子序数越高，图像越亮。 Backscattered electrons originate from deeper regions (about 0.1–0.5 μm) and are highly sensitive to differences in atomic number — the higher the atomic number, the brighter the material appears.

主要信号类型及信息对比 Key signal types and information comparison

二次电子带来表面形貌与细节分辨；背散射电子强调成分差异。 Secondary electrons provide surface topography and detail, while backscattered electrons highlight compositional contrasts.

以太阳能电池SEM图为例，白色区域为银（Z=47），黑暗区域为硅（Z=14），清晰区分不同元素区域。 For example, in a SEM image of a solar cell, the white area represents silver (Z=47) and the dark area represents silicon (Z=14), clearly distinguishing between different elemental regions.

成像模式对比与应用举例 Imaging mode comparisons and practical examples

FeO2颗粒
左图为背散射电子（BSE）成像，右图为二次电子（SE）成像。BSE突出成分对比，SE揭示拓扑结构与表面细节。 FeO2 particles: the left image is BSE, the right image is SE. BSE emphasizes compositional contrast, SE reveals topography and surface details.
Ni/Au纳米棒
SE成像突出表面粗糙度和细节，BSE成像使高原子序元素区域更明亮。 Ni/Au nanorods: SE imaging highlights surface roughness and detail, BSE imaging makes high atomic number elements appear brighter.
Ti线缠绕Ni线
SE成像展示结构和形态细节，BSE成像显示高原子序元素（Ni，Z=28）更亮。 Ti wires wrapped around Ni wire: SE reveals detailed structure and morphology, BSE shows regions with greater atomic mass (Ni, Z=28) as brighter.

成像深度对分辨率的影响 Effect of imaging depth on resolution

背散射电子的逃逸深度大于二次电子，因此对表面微细特征的分辨能力低于二次电子成像。 The escape depth of backscattered electrons is greater than that of secondary electrons; consequently, surface topographical resolution is lower in BSE imaging than in SE imaging.

SE和BSE对同一光刻胶的成像显示，SE图像边缘、细节更锐利，而BSE图像整体对比及深层结构更清晰。 SE and BSE imaging of photoresist: SE image is sharper at edges with clearer surface detail, BSE image provides stronger contrast and reveals deeper structure.

老师讲课补充重点内容 Supplementary key points from teacher's lecture

二次电子和背散射电子成像所用探测器不同，成像效果和信息类型各有侧重，因此实际研究中常需结合两者获得全貌。 Different detectors are used for SE and BSE, each focusing on specific information types; combining both is important for comprehensive analysis.

老师强调，判断亮区与暗区的方法：原子序数越大，BSE图像区域越亮。因此通常可用仿真（如Monte Carlo计算）预测图像分布和对比。 The teacher emphasized that in BSE, regions with higher atomic number are brighter; simulation (e.g., Monte Carlo) can predict image distribution and contrast.

详细观察同一样品的SE与BSE图，能发现表面结构和成分分布的互补特征，这对于材料科学分析非常重要。 By comparing SE and BSE images of the same sample, one observes complementary features of surface morphology and composition, which is crucial for material analysis.

audio

SEM中背散射电子/能谱X射线探测器成像SEM: Images from back scattered electrons / Energy dispersive X-Rays detectors

背散射电子与X射线的信号来源及物理机制 Origins and physical mechanisms of backscattered electrons and X-ray signals

入射电子束轰击样品后，与原子核弹性散射产生背散射电子（Backscattered electrons）；与内层电子非弹性散射激发出特征X射线（Characteristic X-rays）。 When an incident electron beam bombards the sample, elastic scattering with atomic nuclei produces backscattered electrons (BSE), and inelastic scattering with inner-shell electrons excites characteristic X-rays.

背散射电子对样品的平均原子序数极其敏感，原子序数越高区域越亮，可用于区分不同元素区域。 BSE signals are highly sensitive to the mean atomic number of the sample; higher atomic number regions appear brighter, enabling mapping of elemental composition.

特征X射线反映元素种类和丰度，通过能谱仪（EDS、EDXS、EDX等）可实现微区元素分析与分布测绘。 Characteristic X-rays indicate both elemental type and abundance. Using spectrometers (EDS, EDXS, EDX, etc.), micro-area elemental analysis and mapping become possible.

不同信号产生过程及结构示意 Generation processes and structural schematic of different signals

背散射电子是在电子束与原子核大角度弹性散射后脱离样品； BSE are emitted when incident electrons are elastically scattered at large angles by atomic nuclei and escape from the sample.

特征X射线源于电子轰击造成的内层电子空位，由其他高能级电子跃迁补空发射； Characteristic X-rays originate from vacancies created by incident electrons knocking out inner-shell electrons, followed by electron transitions from higher to lower energy levels emitting X-ray photons.

例如，铜元素的Kα、Kβ、L等X射线跃迁清晰反映在能谱图谱中。 For example, copper atoms exhibit characteristic X-ray transitions (Kα, Kβ, L, etc.) clearly visible in X-ray spectra.

实际成像与能谱分析举例 Practical imaging and spectroscopic analysis examples

Al/Cu样品SEM图像中，白色区为铜（Z=29），黑色区为铝（Z=13），直观反映原子序数与BSE信号的对应关系。 In a SEM image of an Al/Cu sample, the white area indicates copper (Z=29), the dark area indicates aluminum (Z=13), directly showing the relationship between atomic number and BSE signal intensity.

钙磷酸盐结晶在不同倍率下的BSE图像显示，通过EDX进行成分分析，可归属峰值到具体Ca、P、O等元素，实现空间分布映射。 Backscattered images of calcium phosphate crystals at different magnifications can be analyzed by EDX to assign spectrum peaks to specific elements like Ca, P, O, enabling elemental mapping.

EDX能谱映射界面，可同时显示样品图像和元素丰度分布，并按光谱峰判断材料组成。 The EDX mapping interface presents both sample images and elemental abundance maps, and identifies materials based on spectrum peaks.

元素能量表与谱线判读 Energy table for elements and spectrum interpretation

能量表（见课件第三页）列出各元素的特征X射线发射能量，可用于判定SEM-EDX/EDS分析中的元素归属与分辨。 The periodic energy table (see slide 3) lists the characteristic X-ray energies for all elements, providing a reference for accurate element identification in SEM-EDS/EDX analysis.

不同元素的检测下限受激发电压、原子序数及样品密度影响。 Detection limits for each element depend on excitation voltage, atomic number, and sample density.

老师补充内容与使用建议 Teacher's additional points and operational advice

老师强调，不同信号产生区深度不同，X射线来自更大体积，可实现样品内部成分分析。 The teacher stressed that different signals originate from distinct depths: X-rays arise from a larger volume, enabling analysis of internal composition.

背散射电子成分对比敏感，适合辨别多元素样品区分，配合EDX可实现高效定性和类似准定量分析。 BSE images are composition-sensitive and suitable for distinguishing multi-element zones; combining with EDX enables effective qualitative and semi-quantitative analysis.

实验时，合理设定激发电压和时间，有助于优化信号分辨率和灵敏度。 Setting optimal excitation voltage and collection time enhances signal resolution and sensitivity during experiments.

总结 Summary

背散射电子与能谱X射线结合，实现SEM成像的成分分布与元素分析功能，是结构与成分双重表征的重要手段；利用能谱峰与能量表，可高效判断样品组成。

The combination of BSE and EDS/X-ray analysis in SEM enables comprehensive imaging of compositional distribution and detailed elemental analysis, serving as a key dual approach for structural and compositional characterization; reference to characteristic peaks and energy tables ensures efficient and accurate identification of sample components.

调节与优化参数Adjustment and optimization parameters

一、图像干扰及成因 Image disturbances and their causes

图像不清晰和对比度低、图像不稳定、整体质量差、噪音大、锯齿边缘等，常见成因包括：加速电压影响、束流电流和束斑直径、样品充电、工作距离与物镜光阑、像散影响。 Common image issues like lack of sharpness and contrast, unstable images, poor overall quality, noisy images, jagged edges are often caused by: influence of accelerating voltage, probe current and spot size, charge-up effect, working distance & objective aperture, astigmatism.

不寻常的图像对比或变形，可能由电子束损伤或污染导致。 Unusual-contrast or deformed images may result from specimen damage by electron beam or contamination.

老师补充，很多初学者刚用SEM会遇到对比度不足或细节模糊，其根本原因多与上述参数设定有关。 The instructor added that beginners often encounter poor contrast or blurry details with SEM, mostly due to misadjustment of these parameters1。

二、加速电压的影响 Influence of accelerating voltage

加速电压(kV/keV)即灯丝与阳极间的电压差，直接决定入射电子束能量。 Accelerating voltage (kV/keV) is the voltage difference between the filament and the anode, determining the energy of incident electrons.

改变电压（0-30kV），电子束穿透深度随之变化，电压越高穿透越深。高电压使更多电子参与反应，产生更多次级信号，可能掩盖表面结构细节、降低图像对比度。 Changing the voltage (0-30 kV) alters the penetration depth of the electron beam—the higher the voltage, the deeper the beam penetrates. Higher voltage can generate more secondary signals, which may obscure surface features and reduce image contrast.

通过示意图可见，低原子序或高原子序材料下，低电压时电子体积分布浅，高电压时体积分布深。 As shown in diagrams, both low and high atomic number materials show shallower interaction volumes at low voltage and deeper volumes at high voltage.

不同加速电压条件下成像示例
10 kV条件下表面结构不清，降低至3 kV或1 kV时表面细节明显增强。 In example images, at 10 kV surface structures are less clear, while at 3 kV or 1 kV, surface details emerge more distinctly.

加速电压高 → 分辨率高、充电效应增强、样品损伤加重；电压低 → 分辨率低、损伤小、充电效应弱。 High accelerating voltage = higher resolution, more charge-up and damage; low voltage = lower resolution, milder charge-up and damage.

keV应用范围
低电压（1-5 kV）适合较脆弱或未镀膜样品；中电压（5-10 kV）用于镀膜生物样品；高电压（10-30 kV）多用于物理科学样品。 keV application ranges: 1-5 kV for delicate/uncoated specimens; 5-10 kV for coated biological samples; 10-30 kV for physical science samples.

老师强调，电压设置需根据实际材料性质、期望观测深度合理选择。电压过高会损伤样品且丢失表面特征，电压过低则分辨率下降。 Instructor stressed, voltage should be set according to sample type and desired observation depth. Over-high voltage damages samples and loses surface details; too low reduces resolution1。

三、束流电流与束斑直径影响 Influence of probe current and probe diameter

束流通过样品，其横截面（束斑直径）决定每次扫描的“点”大小，直接影响图像分辨率和亮度。 As the beam scans the sample, its cross-sectional diameter (spot size) determines the size of each scanned point, directly affecting image resolution and brightness.

束斑大时
分辨率下降、细节丢失，图像变平滑但不清晰；束斑小时：细节丰富、分辨率高，但图像颗粒感增强，亮度降低。 Large spot size: lower resolution, loss of fine details, smooth but unclear images; small spot size: rich details, higher resolution, but more graininess and reduced brightness.
束流大时
亮度和细节↑，但同步损伤也增多，分辨率反而下降。束流小时：分辨率↑、损伤↓，但图像偏粗糙、亮度低。 Large probe current: increases details and brightness, but also increases sample damage and deteriorates resolution. Small probe current: higher resolution, less damage, but grainier and dimmer images.

示例图显示，束斑越大，特征分辨能力越差，表面细节变模糊。 Example images show that as the spot size increases, ability to resolve features decreases and surface appears blurred.

老师补充，束流电流和束斑选择需权衡成像清晰度与样品保护。在实际实验中常需多次调节以兼顾这两方面。 The instructor noted, optimal probe current and spot size depend on balancing clear imaging and protecting the sample; practical operation often requires multiple adjustments

工作距离影响与样品损伤和污染Adjustment and optimization parameters: Influence of working distance & Specimen damage and contamination

一、工作距离（Working Distance, WD）的影响 Influence of Working Distance (WD)

工作距离定义为SEM柱底端到样品顶部的距离。 Working distance is defined as the distance from the bottom of the SEM column (pole piece) to the top of the sample.

工作距离以毫米为单位进行测量。 The working distance is measured in millimeters.

工作距离的大小影响成像深度和分辨率
The size of working distance affects both depth of field and resolution:

较大工作距离（Large WD）：景深加大，分辨率降低，适合整体结构观察、对焦范围宽广。 Large WD: Greater depth of field, lower resolution, suits overview and broad focus areas.

较小工作距离（Small WD）：分辨率更高，景深变小，适合高倍率、高精度细节成像。 Small WD: Higher resolution, smaller depth of field, suits high magnification and detail imaging.

深景深（大WD）可以降低图像模糊，使更多区域保持清晰，但整体分辨率受限。 Greater depth of field (large WD) reduces image blurriness, keeping more regions sharp, but overall resolution is limited.

示意图和实际SEM图像显示，10毫米工作距离时只有部分螺旋清晰，38毫米工作距离时前后景物均清楚但细节损失。 As shown in the schematic and SEM images, 10 mm WD yields sharpness in part of the coils, while 38 mm WD captures more in focus but with detail loss.

α为大工作距离时的会聚角，β为小工作距离时的会聚角，且有α＜β。 α is the convergence angle at large WD, β at small WD, with α < β.

老师强调，实际应用中应根据样品结构和成像目的选择合适工作距离，平衡景深和分辨率。 The instructor emphasized that, in practice, WD should be chosen based on sample structure and imaging purpose, balancing depth of field and resolution4。

二、样品损伤与污染 Specimen Damage and Contamination

充电效应（Charging）：低导电性样品易积累电子，导致图像移动、分辨率降低。需包覆导电材料，降低电压、电流并调整角度，将样品固定于导电支架。 Charging: Samples with low conductivity easily accumulate electrons, causing image drift and loss of resolution. Solutions include coating with conductive material, reducing voltage/current and tilt, and fixing the sample on a conductive stub.

污染（Contamination）：电子束附近残余气体分解并沉积，降低图像对比和锐度，多由长时间扫描小范围引起。 Contamination: Residual gas near the beam decomposes and deposits, reducing contrast and sharpness, usually caused by scanning a small region for a long time.

损伤（Damage）：高电子束功率导致局部热积累、物理损伤。应降低加速电压、扫描面积和扫描时间，同时考虑样品包覆导电材料。 Damage: High electron beam power causes local heating and physical damage, requiring reduction of accelerating voltage, scan area/time, and consideration of conductive coating.

配套示意和实际SEM图像说明电压越高/扫描越久，损伤和污染越明显。 Supporting diagrams and SEM images show that higher voltage or longer scanning lead to more obvious damage and contamination.

老师补充，操作时需密切注意成像参数，及时调整，充分保护样品结构与图像质量。 The instructor added that during operation, close attention must be paid to imaging parameters for timely adjustment, thereby protecting both sample structure and image quality46。

三、总结 Summary

工作距离对SEM成像的景深和分辨率有直接影响，需根据实际需求平衡；样品充电、污染和损伤是常见问题，可通过参数调整和包覆导电层等操作减少。

Working distance directly affects SEM image depth of field and resolution and must be balanced according to actual needs; sample charging, contamination, and damage are common issues that can be minimized by parameter adjustment and conductive coating.

样品损伤与污染Specimen damage and contamination

一、充电效应Charging effect

低导电性样品易产生充电，导致图像漂移和分辨率降低。 Samples with low electrical conductivity easily accumulate charge, resulting in image drift and reduced resolution.

需包覆导电材料以避免充电。 Coating with a conducting material is necessary to avoid charging.

应减少加速电压、束流电流和样品倾角。 Reducing the accelerating voltage, probe current, and tilt angle helps minimize charging.

固定样品于导电载台上。 Fix the sample onto a conductive stub.

二、污染Contamination

主要由电子束区域残余气体分解并沉积在样品表面引起。 Contamination mainly results from residual gas in the electron probe region decomposing and depositing on the sample surface.

污染减弱了图像对比度和清晰度。 It leads to reduced image contrast and loss of sharpness.

通常发生在长时间扫描小区域时。 Usually caused by scanning a small region for a prolonged period.

三、损伤Damage

损伤来源于照射点的热积累。 Damage comes from heat generation at the irradiated point.

电子束能量过高会造成样品局部结构受损。 Excessively high electron beam energy can damage the local structure of the sample.

应减小加速电压、扫描面积和时间，并考虑包覆导电层。 The accelerating voltage, scan area, and scan time should be reduced; coating with a conducting material is recommended.

四、老师课堂补充Teacher’s Additional Comments

老师强调，高电压或长时间扫描时，表面损伤和污染显著，明显影响生物样品等脆弱材料的真实结构观察。 The teacher emphasized that high voltage or prolonged scanning causes obvious surface damage and contamination, significantly affecting the observation of true structures, especially in fragile materials like biological specimens。

充电产生的图像移动不仅影响分辨率，还会使拍摄图像模糊，需优先采取样品导电处理。 Charging-induced image drift reduces resolution and makes captured images blurry, so conductive coating is a priority treatment.

污染在成像范围缩小时尤为明显，可在大倍率下用肉眼观察到束斑痕迹。 Contamination becomes especially apparent when imaging small areas, where beam traces are visible to the naked eye at high magnification.

五、总结Summary

样品损伤、污染和充电均可导致SEM图像质量下降。通过调整加速电压、束流电流、扫描面积和包覆导电材料，可有效降低不良影响，获取更真实可靠的显微图像。 Specimen damage, contamination, and charging all cause deterioration in SEM image quality. By adjusting accelerating voltage, probe current, scan area, and using conductive coating, these negative effects can be effectively minimized, resulting in more authentic and reliable microscopic images.

032025-11-20

质谱法 Mass Spectrometry

质谱法简介 Introduction to Mass Spectrometry

质谱法是一种用于测定离子的质荷比（m/z）的分析技术。

Mass spectrometry is an analytical technique used to measure the mass-to-charge ratio (m/z) of ions.

技术功能 Functions of the Technique

可获取分子的结构信息。

Allows access to structural information.

可确定混合物的成分组成。

Enables establishment of the composition of a mixture.

可进行定量分析。

Permits quantification.

技术特点 Characteristics of the Technique

灵敏度高。

High sensitivity.

信息特异性强。

High specificity of information.

广泛应用于多个领域。

Widely used in many fields of application.

离子实体的检测 Detection of Ionic Entities

样品可以直接引入系统，或通过色谱法（如液相色谱LC、气相色谱GC）引入。

Sample introduction can be direct, or via chromatography such as LC (liquid chromatography) or GC (gas chromatography).

首先将样品转化为气相离子。

First, sample is converted to gas-phase ions.

接下来根据其质荷比（m/z）对离子进行称量。

Then, ions are weighed according to their mass-to-charge ratio (m/z).

离子通过计数与信号放大进行检测。

Ion detection is realized through ion counting and signal amplification.

最终通过信号处理与谱图可视化，将结果输出为质谱图。

Finally, signal processing and spectrum visualization output the results as a mass spectrum.

离子化的不同方式 Different Ways of Ionization

正离子化（正离子的形成） Positive Ionization (Formation of Positive Ions)

中性分子（M）失去1个电子，形成正自由基离子[M]^+，称为分子离子。

Neutral molecule (M) loses 1 electron to form a positive radical ion [M]^+, called the molecular ion.

中性分子（M）加上带正电的X^+（如H^+、Na^+、NH4^+），形成加合物[M+X]^+，称为准分子离子。

Neutral molecule (M) adds a positive X^+ (e.g., H^+, Na^+, NH4^+), forming an adduct [M+X]^+, called a pseudomolecular ion.

高能模式下分子断裂，产生正碎片离子[F]^+。

Under energetic ionization, molecules fragment, forming positive fragment ions [F]^+.

负离子化（负离子的形成） Negative Ionization (Formation of Negative Ions)

中性分子（M）获得1个电子，形成负自由基离子[M]^-。

Neutral molecule (M) gains 1 electron, forming a negative radical ion [M]^-.

中性分子（M）加上负离子X^-（如-H^+、Cl^-、HCOO^-），形成加合物[M+X]^-，称为准分子负离子。

Neutral molecule (M) adds a negative ion X^- (e.g., -H^+, Cl^-, HCOO^-), forming an adduct [M+X]^-, called a pseudomolecular negative ion.

分子断裂亦可形成负碎片离子[F]^-。

Fragmentation also produces negative fragment ions [F]^-.

质谱图的解读 Interpreting a Mass Spectrum

质谱图的x轴代表质荷比m/z（汤姆森单位），y轴代表离子强度（绝对值或相对丰度）。

The x-axis of a mass spectrum represents mass-to-charge ratio (m/z, in Thomson units), while the y-axis shows ion intensity (in absolute or relative abundance).

通过分析不同m/z值的离子峰，可以获得分子的分子离子、准分子离子及碎片离子的数据信息。

By analyzing the ion peaks at different m/z values, one obtains data about molecular ions, pseudomolecular ions, and fragment ions.

教师补充讲解要点 Additional Points from the Lecture

通过质谱可以获得分子的结构信息，但如果只需获得目标分子的分子量，有时解读可能较复杂。

Mass spectrometry provides structural information, but if the main information you seek is the molecular mass, interpretation can sometimes be tricky.

根据不同离子化源，能得到分子离子或准分子离子，其m/z为分子最完整的质量信号。

Depending on the ionization source, you can observe molecular ions or pseudomolecular ions, representing the entire molecule.

质谱中常见术语
分子离子、准分子离子和碎片离子。

Common terms in mass spectrometry include molecular ions, pseudomolecular ions, and fragments.

既能生成正离子，也能生成负离子，同理也能有负碎片离子。

Both positive and negative ions can be generated, as well as corresponding fragment ions.

质谱图的形状取决于所用离子源和方法。

The shape of the mass spectrum depends on the ionization source and method used.

离子源Ion Sources

离子源的类型 Types of Ion Sources

常用的离子源包括电子轰击（EI）、化学电离（CI）、电喷雾（ESI）、大气压化学电离（APCI）、基质辅助激光解吸（MALDI）。

Commonly used ion sources include Electron Impact (EI), Chemical Ionization (CI), Electrospray (ESI), Atmospheric Pressure Chemical Ionization (APCI), and Matrix Assisted Laser Desorption Ionization (MALDI)。

离子源的选择依据 Selection Criteria for Ion Sources

离子源的选择取决于分析分子的物理化学性质，如极性和分子量。

The choice of ionization source depends on the physicochemical properties of analytes, such as polarity and molecular weight。

分析物的极性和分子量决定了哪种离子源最适合。

The polarity and molecular weight of analytes determine the most suitable ionization source。

ESI和APCI等主要用于极性/大分子，常见于LC/MS系统；EI和CI主要用于小分子或非极性分子，常见于GC/MS系统。

ESI and APCI are mainly for polar/large molecules, common in LC/MS systems; EI and CI are mainly for small or nonpolar molecules, common in GC/MS systems。

图表中显示，ESI适用于高极性高分子量分子，GCMS适用于低极性低分子量分子，APCI和APPI覆盖一定范围的极性和分子量。

The diagram shows ESI is suitable for highly polar/high molecular weight compounds, GCMS is suitable for low polarity/low molecular weight compounds, and APCI and APPI cover a range of polarities and molecular weights。

电子轰击（EI）Electron Impact (EI)

电子轰击离子源工作于高真空环境，通过加热金属丝产生电子。

The electron impact ion source operates under high vacuum, generating electrons by heating a metal filament。

电子在70 eV电位差下加速，与分子相撞，把部分动能传递给分子。

The electrons are accelerated by a 70 eV potential difference, collide with molecules, and transfer some kinetic energy。

分子的一个电子被撕裂出去，形成分子离子（[M]^•+）。

One electron is ejected from the molecule, forming the molecular ion ([M]^•+)。

该过程通常导致分子内部能量激增，引发大量碎片离子的形成，是一种高能离子化方式。

This process leads to a sharp increase in the internal energy of the molecule, causing extensive fragmentation; it is a highly energetic ionization method。

方程式
M + e^- (70 eV) → M^•+ + 2e^-
Equation
M + e^- (70 eV) → M^•+ + 2e^-
EI离子源特点
分子离子少，碎片离子多，用于结构分析。
Features of EI
few molecular ions, many fragment ions, suitable for structural analysis。

化学电离（CI）Chemical Ionization (CI)

CI是一种软电离技术，分三步进行：反应气体的电离→分子离子的反应→质子转移。

CI is a soft ionization technique, performed in three steps: ionization of the reagent gas → reaction with the analyte molecule → proton transfer。

反应气体（G）通过更高电位（约100 eV）由EI电离。

The reagent gas (G) is ionized by EI at higher potential (~100 eV)。

反应气体的离子与分子发生电荷交换或质子转移反应，生成分子离子或准分子离子。

The reagent gas ions interact with the analyte molecule via charge exchange or proton transfer, producing molecular or pseudomolecular ions。

主要反应如下
G^•+ + M → G + M^•+（电荷交换）；GH^+ + M → G + [MH]^+（质子转移）。
Main reactions
G^•+ + M → G + M^•+ (charge exchange); GH^+ + M → G + [MH]^+ (proton transfer)。
CI离子源特点
分子离子、准分子离子多，碎片离子少，更适合分子量测定。
Features of CI
more molecular and pseudomolecular ions, few fragments, better for molecular weight determination。

教师讲解补充 Additional Lecture Points

市场上有多种离子源和分析仪，实验室可根据实际需求进行组合选用。

There are multiple ion sources and analyzers available on the market; laboratories can combine them as needed。

不同离子源产生的碎片和分子离子分布不同，这会影响到最终质谱的形状和解读方式。

Different ion sources produce varying distributions of fragments and molecular ions, affecting the final mass spectrum’s appearance and interpretation。

接下来课程会具体介绍各种离子源的具体原理和适用性。

The following part of the course will introduce the specific principles and applications of each ion source。

文拉法辛在EI和CI中的质谱图

Venlafaxine mass spectra in EI and CI

电子轰击电离（EI）下，文拉法辛的分子离子峰（M⁺·，m/z = 277）缺失，表现为大量碎片离子，主峰为小分子碎片，结构信息丰富但失去分子量信息。 Under electron impact ionization (EI), the molecular ion peak (M⁺·, m/z = 277) of venlafaxine is missing, and the spectrum is dominated by fragment ions. The main peaks correspond to small molecular fragments, providing rich structural information but losing clear molecular weight information.

EI的优点包括
操作简单、方法灵敏、碎片有助于结构识别、可检索指纹图谱。 The advantages of EI include: simple operation, high sensitivity, fragmentation aids in molecular identification, and library-searchable fingerprint spectra.
EI的缺点为
分子必须易挥发且热稳定、广泛碎片化导致数据难以解释、分子离子峰常缺失、适用质量范围小于1000 Da。 The disadvantages of EI are: the molecule must be volatile and thermally stable, extensive fragmentation makes interpretation difficult, the molecular ion peak is often missing, and the useful mass range is below 1000 Da.

化学电离（CI，使用异丁烷）下，文拉法辛的准分子离子[MH]⁺（m/z = 278）清晰可见，主峰为准分子离子，容易获得分子量信息。 Under chemical ionization (CI) with isobutane, the pseudomolecular ion [MH]⁺ (m/z = 278) of venlafaxine is clearly observed, with the main peak being the pseudomolecular ion, making molecular weight determination straightforward.

CI具有分子量信息明确、选择性好、灵敏度高的特点。 CI provides clear molecular weight information, is highly selective, and possesses high sensitivity.

通过对比EI和CI的质谱图，EI生成大量碎片、分子离子弱或缺失，常用于结构鉴定；CI则易获得高强度的[M+H]⁺离子，更适合分子量的精准测定。 By comparing the EI and CI mass spectra, EI produces numerous fragments with weak or missing molecular ions, making it suitable for structural identification; CI, however, easily yields intense [M+H]⁺ ions and is better for accurate molecular weight determination.

老师补充，复杂生物样品中若样品具有相似碎片，单靠EI无法确定具体分子量，因此常需借助CI或其他软电离方式辅助确认。 As the lecturer explained, in complex biological samples where compounds may share similar fragments, EI alone may not determine the precise molecular weight, so CI or other soft ionization techniques are often necessary for confirmation.

质谱数据常需结合数据库和标准对比判定分子结构和分子量。 Mass spectrum data often need to be compared with databases and standards for determining molecular structure and molecular weight.

电离源Ionization source：电喷雾（ESI）Electrospray (ESI) 及大气压化学电离（APCI）Atmospheric Pressure Chemical Ionization (APCI) 以及基质辅助激光解吸电离（MALDI）Matrix Assisted Laser Desorption Ionization (MALDI)

电喷雾电离（ESI）原理与流程Electrospray Ionization (ESI) Principle and Process

ESI是一种常压下进行的软电离技术，适于联用液相色谱。 ESI is a soft ionization technique operating at atmospheric pressure, suitable for combination with liquid chromatography.

雾化器产生带电微小液滴，随后逐步溶剂蒸发并体积缩小。 The nebulizer produces tiny, charged droplets which gradually shrink as the solvent evaporates.

带电液滴在电场作用下朝毛细管入口运动，外部有加热氮气流以加快溶剂挥发。 Charged droplets are attracted toward the capillary by the electric field, and a stream of heated nitrogen promotes solvent evaporation.

随着液滴逐渐减小，表面的静电（库仑）斥力超过内聚力，液滴发生爆裂，形成更小更高电荷的液滴，最终释放出分析物离子进入气相。 As the droplets shrink, the electrostatic (Coulombic) repulsion overcomes cohesion, causing the droplets to explode into smaller, more highly charged droplets, and eventually releasing analyte ions into the gas phase.

这一循环反复，直到分析物离子最终解吸到气相，被电场吸引进入质谱仪。 The process repeats until analyte ions are ultimately desorbed into the gas phase and enter the mass spectrometer.

ESI应用与离子类型ESI Applications and Ion Types

适用于从小极性分子到大生物分子（如蛋白质、寡核苷酸等）的分析。 Applicable to analysis of small polar molecules up to large biomolecules (such as proteins, oligonucleotides, etc.).

能产生单电荷和多电荷加合物，常有准分子离子（如[MH]+），碎片离子极少，适合分子量测定。 Can generate singly and multiply charged adducts, commonly pseudomolecular ions (like [MH]+), with few fragments—suitable for molecular weight determination.

当分析大分子（如蛋白）时，可得到多种带不同电荷数的离子，需通过公式计算真实分子质量： When analyzing large molecules (e.g., proteins), ions with different charge numbers are produced and the true molecular mass is calculated as: 分子质量 = (观测m/z × 电荷数) – (电荷数 × 质子质量) Molecular mass = (observed m/z × charge state) – (charge state × proton mass)

ESI适合高极性或带电大分子，是蛋白质组学及生物分析的主流技术。 ESI is ideal for highly polar or charged large molecules, and is a core technique in proteomics and bioanalysis.

老师补充讲解Additional Points from Lecture

ESI适合极性化合物，特别是蛋白、多肽等高分子物质，并可用于基础代谢物、药物、小分子研究。 ESI is suitable for polar compounds, especially for proteins, peptides, and is also used for metabolites, pharmaceuticals, and small molecules.

离子往往在溶液中已带电，常见为正离子模式，也可以产生负离子形式。 Ions are often pre-charged in solution, typically in the positive mode, but negative ions can also be produced.

碎片离子较少，有利于分子量的直接判定和复杂体系样品的定量分析。 Fewer fragment ions facilitate direct molecular weight determination and quantitative analysis in complex samples. 45

大气压化学电离（APCI）原理与特点Atmospheric Pressure Chemical Ionization (APCI) Principle and Features

APCI为气相化学电离过程，样品须先加热完全汽化（约400–500°C），适用于中低极性且可气化物质。 APCI is a gas-phase chemical ionization method, requiring samples to be vaporized at high temperature (about 400–500°C), suitable for mildly or non-polar and vaporizable compounds.

加热后的气体与反应气体（通常为流动相气体）在等离子区（Corona放电）发生电离反应，将电荷转移至分析物。 The heated gas reacts with reagent gas (usually mobile phase gas) in a corona discharge region to ionize and transfer charge to the analyte.

APCI主要生成单电荷离子（[MH]+）及加合物，碎片离子很少，有利于分子量确认。 APCI mainly produces singly charged ions ([MH]+) and adducts, with few fragments, making molecular weight identification simple2.

APCI应用范围及老师补充Usage Scope and Additional Comments

APCI适用于低至中等极性的分子，包括热稳定且易于汽化的小分子、药物及某些天然产物。 APCI is suited for low-to-moderate polarity molecules, including thermally stable, volatile small molecules, drugs, and some natural products.

不能用于热不稳定或大分子如蛋白质的电离，但可覆盖ESI覆盖不到的目标物。 Not suitable for thermally unstable or large molecules like proteins, but complements the range ESI can't cover.

与ESI一样属于软电离技术，常常与液相色谱联用，有助于复杂体系分析物的高灵敏检测。 Like ESI, it's a soft ionization technology and is often coupled to LC for sensitive detection in complex matrices. 2

基质辅助激光解吸电离（MALDI）原理与应用Matrix Assisted Laser Desorption Ionization (MALDI) Principle and Application

MALDI是软电离技术，样品需与合适基质充分混匀并共结晶于金属板上。 MALDI is a soft ionization technique; samples are mixed with a compatible matrix and co-crystallized on a metal plate.

激光照射样品点，将样品和基质共同解吸进入气相并实现电离；分子可通过加质子或失质子方式带电。 Laser irradiation desorbs both the sample and matrix; the molecules are ionized by gaining or losing protons.

适用于大分子（蛋白、肽、DNA、多糖和聚合物等），可生成单或多电荷离子，碎片离子极少。 Suitable for large molecules (proteins, peptides, DNA, polysaccharides, polymers, etc.), producing single or multiple charged ions with minimal fragmentation.

老师录音补充和重点Teacher’s Supplement & Highlights

MALDI常用于超高分子量分析，也广泛应用于组织成像和生物分子研究。 MALDI is widely used for ultra high molecular weight substances, as well as tissue imaging and biomolecule analysis.

与LC不易直接联用，适合直接点样分析，尤其在蛋白组学和多糖及聚合物分析领域极具优势。 Not readily coupled with LC; best for spot sample analysis, especially in proteomics and biopolymer research.

小结Summary

三种离子源（ESI、APCI、MALDI）分别适用于不同极性和分子量范围化合物，均是常用的软电离技术，各有侧重和优势。 The three ion sources (ESI, APCI, MALDI) are suited for compounds of varying polarity and molecular mass, all are widely used soft ionization techniques, each with its own focus and advantages.

质谱分析器及其特性Mass Analyzer and Its Characteristics

常见质谱分析器类型Common Types of Mass Analyzer

四极杆 Quadrupole

离子阱 Ion trap

飞行时间 Time of Flight

轨道阱 Orbitrap

分析器性能指标与定义Analyzer Characteristics and Definitions

质量电荷比限制
分析器能够检测的m/z范围（最小值和最大值），通常以Thomson（Th）或质量单位（u或amu）表示，z=1时适用。 Limit in mass/charge ratio: Minimum and maximum values of measurable m/z range, expressed in Thomson (Th) or in mass unit (u or amu), applicable when z = 1.
分辨率
区分两组非常接近信号的能力，是识别复杂样品的重要指标。 Resolution: Ability to distinguish two closely spaced signals, which is crucial for complex sample analysis.
质量准确度
所测得m/z的准确度，极大依赖分析器的稳定性和分辨率，常用百分比或百万分之一（ppm）表示。 Mass accuracy: Accuracy of measured m/z ratios, highly dependent on analyzer stability and resolution, commonly expressed in % or ppm.
扫描速度
单位时间内可检测的质量范围，通常用amu/秒或/ms表示。 Speed scanning: The measurable mass domain per unit of time, expressed in amu per second or millisecond.

分辨率及其表示Resolution and Its Expression

分辨率定义公式
R=MΔMR=ΔMM​，其中ΔMΔM为可区分的最小质量差，MM为目标峰的质量。 Resolution formula: R=M/ΔMR=M/ΔM, where ΔMΔM is the smallest distinguishable mass difference, and MM is the mass of the target peak.

FWHM（全宽半高）：常用来衡量峰的宽度。 FWHM (Full Width at Half Maximum): Commonly used to measure peak width.

分辨率越高，代表分析器能更好地区分相似质量的离子，提高定性和定量准确性。 The higher the resolution, the better the analyzer can distinguish ions with similar masses, improving both qualitative and quantitative accuracy.

不同分析器分辨率与m/z范围对比Comparison of Analyzers by Resolution and m/z Range

四极杆
分辨率约2000，m/z范围。 Quadrupole: Resolution around 2000, m/z range 4000.
离子阱
分辨率2000-5000，m/z范围。 Ion trap: Resolution of 2000-5000, m/z range 6000.
飞行时间
分辨率2万-6万，m/z范围最大可达50万。 Time of Flight (TOF): Resolution of 20,000-60,000, m/z range up to 500,000.
轨道阱
分辨率10万-50万，m/z范围。 Orbitrap: Resolution of 100,000-500,000, m/z range 6000.

傅里叶变换离子回旋共振（FT-ICR）：最高分辨率达100万-1000万，m/z范围3万。 FT-ICR (Fourier Transform Ion Cyclotron Resonance): Resolution of 1,000,000-10,000,000, m/z range 30,000.

老师补充解读与实际应用Teacher’s Supplement and Practical Notes

选择哪种分析器要结合样品复杂度、对分辨率和质量准确度的需求等因素。 The choice of analyzer depends on sample complexity, requirements for resolution and mass accuracy, etc.

通常实验室运用最多的是四极杆、离子阱和飞行时间，轨道阱和FT-ICR适合高分辨需求。 In practice, quadrupole, ion trap, and TOF are most widely used; orbitrap and FT-ICR are for high-resolution needs.

分析器性能提升有助于复杂组分混合物的解析，如蛋白质组学与代谢物组学中的高精度检测。 Improved analyzer performance enables better separation and measurement in complex mixtures, such as in proteomics and metabolomics.

在实际应用中，分辨率和扫描速度往往需要权衡选择，确保数据采集既快速又精确。 In real-world applications, a balance is often struck between resolution and scan speed for fast yet accurate data collection.

分析器技术进步是现代质谱数据高通量、高准确性和高灵敏度的基础。 Advances in analyzer technology are the foundation of modern mass spectrometry’s high throughput, accuracy, and sensitivity.

四极杆质谱分析器的结构与优势Structure and Advantages of Quadrupole Mass Analyzer

四极杆基本结构与原理Quadrupole: Basic Structure and Principle

四极杆由四根平行金属杆组成。 Quadrupole consists of four parallel metal rods.

对置的两根杆带有相同的电性，相邻的两根杆电性相反。 Opposite rods have the same polarity, while adjacent rods have the opposite polarity.

同时施加恒定电压（直流, DC）和变化电压（射频, RF），在四极区内形成特殊的四极电场，使离子发生选择性稳定、被聚焦或排斥。 Both a constant voltage (direct current, DC) and a variable voltage (radio frequency, RF) are applied, forming a specific quadrupolar electrostatic field to focus or repel ions selectively.

每根杆同时承载直流电　和射频电压（RF），通过调节DC和RF电压，实现对不同质荷比（m/z）的离子扫描。 Each rod simultaneously carries DC and RF voltages; by varying these voltages, ions with different mass-to-charge ratios (m/z) can be scanned.
答案 CD

某一特定的DC/RF电压组合只允许特定m/z的离子轨迹稳定通过，其他离子则因轨迹不稳定被排斥出去。 A specific combination of DC/RF voltages stabilizes only ions with one selected m/z value, while all others with unstable trajectories are expelled.

只有选定质荷比的离子能够在射频场中保持正确的振荡轨迹，成功通过四极杆到达检测器。 Only ions with the selected mass-to-charge ratio will have the correct oscillatory pathway in the RF field and reach the detector.

图示原理与离子轨迹说明Diagram Principle and Ion Pathway Explanation

稳定离子（如图中绿色轨迹）顺利穿越四极杆，进入检测器。 Stable ions (green path) travel through the quadrupole and arrive at the detector.

不稳定离子（如图中橙色轨迹）偏离轨道，最终被四极杆结构排斥出去，无法检测。 Unstable ions (orange path) deviate and are ejected from the quadrupole, thus not detected.

四极杆的分辨率与选择性Resolution and Selectivity of Quadrupole

四极杆属于低分辨率分析器，适合快速全扫描和常规分析。 Quadrupole is a unitary (low resolution) analyzer suitable for fast full scans and routine analyses.

对于复杂样品，四极杆通过调节电压组合，实现对单一或有限m/z范围的优先检测。 For complex samples, quadrupole can selectively detect specific m/z values by tuning voltage combinations.

四极杆的主要优势Main Advantages of Quadrupole

结构简单，制造及维护成本低。 Simple structure, inexpensive to manufacture and maintain.

可实现快速质谱全扫描。 Capable of fast, full scan mass spectrometry.

优异的离子选择性，适合定量分析和目标化合物筛查。 Excellent ion selectivity, ideal for quantitative analysis and targeted compound screening.

课堂讲解补充Teacher’s Lecture Supplement

四极杆作为最常用的质谱分析器之一，因其稳定、价格低廉、易于与各种进样和离子源联用被广泛应用。 As one of the most commonly used analyzers, quadrupole is widely applied due to its stability, low cost, and easy integration with diverse sample inlets and ion sources.

适合用于多种检测模式，包括全扫描模式和选择反应监测（SRM/MRM）。 Suitable for various detection modes, including full scan and selected reaction monitoring (SRM/MRM).

四极杆的局限在于分辨率较低，但在药物分析、环境监测等常规应用中表现优异，对高通量实验极为友好。 The limitation of quadrupole is its lower resolution, but it performs excellently in pharmaceutical, environmental analysis, and other routine applications, and is very friendly for high-throughput experiments.

子阱质量分析器的结构和优势Ion Trap Mass Analyzer Structure and Advantages

离子阱由一个环电极和两个端盖电极构成。 The ion trap consists of one ring electrode and two endcap electrodes.

原理与四极杆极为相似，通常充入氦气作为惰性碰撞气体以稳定离子轨迹。 The principle is very similar to the quadrupole, usually filled with helium gas as an inert collision gas to stabilize the ion trajectory.

离子通过射频（RF）和直流　电场共同作用被困在陷阱内。 Ions are trapped inside by the combined action of radio frequency (RF) and direct current (DC) fields.
答案 CD

离子在阱内沿三维“8”字形轨迹运动，各自的质荷比决定其分布与能级。 Ions move along a three-dimensional "figure-eight" trajectory within the trap, with each m/z value having a distinct spatial and energetic distribution.

为了测定不同m/z的离子，通过逐步改变电极上的RF与DC电压，将一个特定m/z的离子释放出来送往检测器，再依次扫描全部离子。 To analyze different m/z values, the RF and DC voltages on the electrodes are tuned stepwise to release a specific m/z ion to the detector and then sequentially scan all ions.

惰性气体（主要为氦气）能够降低离子动能，进一步加强离子聚集和稳定性。 The inert gas (mainly helium) helps reduce ion kinetic energy and enhances ion confinement and trajectory stability.

离子阱体积小，整体结构紧凑，便于集成与实验室应用。 The ion trap is compact in size with an overall tight structure, which is easy for integration and laboratory use.

离子阱的分辨率属于低分辨率（unitary，~2000-5000），但灵敏度高，可进行全扫描。 The ion trap provides low (unitary) resolution (about 2000–5000), but has high sensitivity and allows full scan MS.

主要优势Main Advantages

结构紧凑、造价低廉，适合多种实验场景。 Compact structure and low cost, suitable for diverse experimental scenarios.

灵敏度高，适用于全扫描质谱分析，能够检测到极低丰度的离子信号。 High sensitivity, ideal for full scan mass spectrometry, capable of detecting even very low-abundance ion signals.

可以实现多级串联碎裂（MSⁿ），尤其适合结构分析和复杂分子的深入研究。 Enables successive (multistage) fragmentation (MSⁿ), making it especially suitable for structure analysis and in-depth study of complex molecules.

由于离子在阱内可被重复操控和激发，离子利用率高，信息采集丰富。 Since ions can be repeatedly manipulated and excited within the trap, ion utilization is high and information acquisition is abundant.

课堂补充要点Classroom Supplement Key Points

离子阱本质上类似一个“盒子”，离子在三维八字轨道中运动通过调控电压释放不同m/z离子。 The ion trap is essentially like a "box", ions traverse 3D figure-eight trajectories and are released by tuning the voltage for each m/z.

离子释放通过调整射频电压，仅允许特定m/z离子在某一时刻出来检测，逐步释放所有离子。 Ion ejection is achieved by adjusting the RF voltage, allowing only a specific m/z ion to exit for detection at each moment, then proceeding sequentially.

离子阱具有极高重复激发和多级碎裂能力，这是结构解析和复杂组分定性的有力工具。 Ion traps have exceptional capabilities for repeated excitation and MSⁿ fragmentation, making them powerful tools for structure elucidation and qualitative analysis of complex mixtures.

操作灵活，尤其适用于小型化设备和高通量分析，但在分辨率方面不及TOF和轨道阱等新型分析器。 Highly flexible operation, especially suitable for miniaturized devices and high-throughput analysis, but the resolution is not as high as that of TOF and Orbitrap analyzers.

虽然部分应用已被新型高分辨率质谱仪替代，但离子阱在经济性、多级碎裂和灵敏度上仍具独特优势，被广泛应用于药物代谢、蛋白组和复杂天然产物研究。 Although partially replaced by high-resolution MS instruments, the ion trap still offers unique advantages in economy, MSⁿ fragmentation, and sensitivity; it is widely used in drug metabolism, proteomics, and natural product research.

总结Summary

离子阱分析器以结构紧凑、操作灵活、灵敏度高和多级碎裂等特点，广泛用于全扫描和结构鉴定，为复杂样品提供了强有力的定性分析手段。 The ion trap analyzer, featuring compact design, flexible operation, high sensitivity, and multistage fragmentation, is widely used for full scans and structure elucidation, providing a powerful qualitative analysis tool for complex samples.

飞行时间质谱分析器与轨道阱质谱分析器Mass Analyzers: Time of Flight (TOF) & Orbital Trap (Orbitrap)

飞行时间（TOF）质谱分析器原理与过程Time of Flight (TOF) Mass Analyzer: Principle and Process

飞行时间分析器根据离子的动能决定速度，轻的离子速度快，重的离子速度慢。 The TOF analyzer determines ion velocity based on kinetic energy: lighter ions move faster, heavier ones slower.

所有离子获得相同的动能，在真空飞行管道中向检测器飞行。 All ions are given the same kinetic energy and travel through the vacuum flight tube toward the detector.

由于速度差异，质量较小（m/z低）的离子先到达检测器，质量较大（m/z高）的离子后到达。 Due to velocity differences, ions with smaller mass-to-charge ratios (m/z) reach the detector first; higher m/z ions arrive later.

通过精确测量各个离子到达检测器的时间，计算其m/z值。 By precisely measuring the arrival times of ions at the detector, their m/z values are calculated.

提高分辨率通常通过延长飞行管道或加入反射器（reflectron）实现，有效拉开不同m/z离子的到达时间差。 Resolution is improved by extending the flight tube or adding a reflectron, which increases the separation of arrival times for different m/z ions.

飞行时间分析器拥有结构简单、分析速度极快的优点。“全扫描”时间为微秒级，适合复杂样品快速分析。 The TOF analyzer features simple construction and extremely fast analysis (“full scan” takes microseconds), making it ideal for rapid complex sample analysis.

分析仪能够覆盖极宽的m/z范围，适合分析较大分子的单电荷离子（如蛋白质等）。 TOF instruments can scan a very wide m/z range and are suitable for analyzing singly-charged large molecules (such as proteins).

典型应用包括高分辨率需求场景和大分子分析，如蛋白质谱和聚合物表征。 Typical applications include high-resolution requirements and large molecule analysis, such as proteomics and polymer characterization.

分辨率高于四极杆和离子阱，通过精确飞行时间测量实现数字级（4位数）质量分辨。 TOF delivers higher resolution than quadrupole and ion trap, achieving four-digit mass resolution through precise time-of-flight measurement.

轨道阱（Orbitrap）质谱分析器原理与特点Orbitrap Mass Analyzer: Principle and Features

轨道阱是一种高分辨率质谱分析器，概念于1999年提出，2005年商业化。 The Orbitrap is a high-resolution mass analyzer; the concept was proposed in 1999 and commercialized since 2005.

采用傅里叶变换（FT）技术，通过记录离子在特殊电场中的运动电流，间接获得m/z信息。 It uses Fourier Transform (FT) technology, indirectly deriving m/z information by recording the current from ion motion in a special electric field.

结构上由中央电极和同轴圆筒壳组成，离子围绕中央电极作轴向振荡。 Structurally, the Orbitrap consists of a central spindle electrode and coaxial outer shell; ions oscillate axially around the central electrode.

直流电压施加于电极，规定离子轨道形状，使不同m/z离子具有不同的振荡频率。 DC voltage is applied to the electrodes to define ion trajectories, so different m/z ions have distinct oscillation frequencies.

检测时，设备记录离子引起的电流信号，通过傅里叶变换将频率转换为各m/z比和信号强度。 During detection, the device records ion-induced current; the Fourier Transform converts oscillation frequencies into m/z ratios and signal intensities.

轴向振荡频率ω=zmkω=mz​​k，完全由离子的m/z决定，实现高度精确辨析。 The axial oscillation frequency ω=z/m kω=z/m​k depends solely on the ion’s m/z, enabling highly accurate distinction.

轨道阱具备极高分辨率（常用4位小数，甚至更高）和出色的m/z准确度，适用于定量和结构研究。 The Orbitrap offers outstanding resolution (typically 4-digit, or even higher) and excellent m/z accuracy, suitable for quantitative and structural research.

设备体积相对紧凑，价格适中，适合高通量组学和复杂样品的高分辨分析。 The instrument is relatively compact and moderately priced, ideal for high-throughput omics and high-resolution analysis of complex samples.

老师讲解补充Teacher’s Supplement Highlights

TOF通过给所有离子相同的动能，将离子的质量与到达检测器所需时间直接相关联，分辨率和准确度远高于四极杆等“单位分辨率”分析器。 TOF links ion mass directly to their detector arrival time by equalizing kinetic energy; this yields much higher resolution and accuracy than “unit resolution” analyzers like quadrupole.

轨道阱无需像传统离子阱一样逐个释放离子，而是同步检测所有离子，通过电流信号解算出所有m/z的丰度，效率更高，混杂少。 Unlike traditional ion traps that sequentially release ions, Orbitrap synchronously detects all ions and resolves their abundances from current signals, providing higher efficiency and less signal overlap.

两种分析器在现代质谱研究和实际应用中均处于主流，尤其是需要高分辨率和复杂样品解析时不可或缺。 Both analyzers are mainstream in modern MS research and applications, especially in scenarios demanding high resolution or complex mixture analysis.

轨道阱能适配多种前端分离技术，用于精确分子定量、组成鉴定和微量组分鉴别。 Orbitrap can be interfaced with various separation techniques for precise quantitation, composition identification, and trace component discrimination.

质谱仪总结与分析器组合Mass Spectrometers: To Sum Up & Analyzers Combination

一、经典质谱仪结构与离子源类型Summary of Classic Mass Spectrometer Structure and Ion Sources

传统质谱仪由离子源（S）、分析器　和检测器（D）三部分组成。 A traditional mass spectrometer consists of three parts: Source (S), Analyzer (A), and Detector (D).
答案 A
常见离子化方式包括
电子轰击、电喷雾、化学电离、大气压化学电离、基质辅助激光解吸。 Common ionization methods include: Electron Impact (EI), Electrospray Ionization (ESI), Chemical Ionization (CI), Atmospheric Pressure Chemical Ionization (APCI), and Matrix Assisted Laser Desorption (MALDI).

二、分析器类型与单级质谱仪Analyzer Types & Simple Mass Spectrometers

主要分析器有
四极杆、离子阱、飞行时间和轨道阱。 Main analyzers are: Quadrupole, Ion Trap, Time of Flight (TOF), and Orbitrap.

经典单级（simple）质谱仪结构为 S–A–D，即离子源–一个分析器–检测器。 The typical single-stage (simple) MS setup is S–A–D: source–one analyzer–detector.

每种离子源和分析器有其适用的样品和分析需求。 Each ion source and analyzer fits different samples and analytical demands.

老师补充
在选择离子源时，必须考虑分子的理化性质，尤其是极性。例如，高极性分子多用ESI，低极性分子更适合GC-MS及电子轰击。 Teacher’s note: When selecting an ion source, you must consider the physico-chemical properties of your molecule, especially polarity. For example, highly polar molecules are often analyzed with ESI; less polar ones are suitable for GC-MS and electron impact.

三、串联与组合质谱仪Tandem and Hybrid Mass Spectrometers

串联质谱仪(MS/MS)可实现多级分析，常见结构如：S–A1–A2–D。 Tandem mass spectrometers (MS/MS) allow multistage analysis, commonly structured as S–A1–A2–D.

可以两种分析器串联（如四极杆-飞行时间，Q-TOF），或同一类型多段组合（如三重四极杆，QQQ）。 Two analyzers can be combined (e.g., Quadrupole-TOF, Q-TOF) or multiples of the same type (e.g., Triple Quadrupole, QQQ).

混合型（hybrid）仪器，如四极杆与轨道阱搭配，能兼顾多级碎裂、定量与高分辨分析。 Hybrid instruments, such as quadrupole and Orbitrap combinations, can provide both multi-stage fragmentation, quantitation, and high-resolution analysis.

通过分析器串联，能够精准筛选母离子、碎裂产物并检测，有助于结构解析和复杂样品定性。 By tandem configuration, precursor and fragment ions can be precisely selected and detected—crucial for structure elucidation and qualitative analysis of complex samples.

老师补充
串联与混合质谱实现了检测灵活性和应用范围的极大拓展，尤其适用于复杂矩阵、生物分子和代谢物的研究。 Teacher’s note: Tandem and hybrid MS greatly extend flexibility and application range, especially for studies of complex matrices, biomolecules, and metabolites.

四、总结与核心要点总结Summary & Key Points

质谱分析流程可快速切换离子源和分析器以适合不同应用。 The MS workflow allows rapid switching of sources and analyzers to fit diverse applications.

现代质谱常采用高效离子化（ESI、MALDI等）和多样化分析器组合以拓展检测能力。 Modern MS commonly uses efficient ionization (ESI, MALDI, etc.) and diverse analyzer combinations to expand detection capability.

串联及混合仪器广泛用于组学、药物分析、天然产物和代谢研究。 Tandem and hybrid instruments are widely used in omics, drug analysis, natural product, and metabolism research.

获取模式与MS/MS分析Acquisition Modes and MS/MS Analysis

1
获取模式简介Acquisition Modes Overview
质谱仪主要有三种数据获取模式
全扫描模式、单离子监测/记录、离子碎裂。 Mass spectrometers mainly use three acquisition modes: Full scan, Single Ion Monitoring/Recording, and Ion Fragmentation.
2
全扫描模式 Fullscan

核心原理为扫描直流和射频电压，依次检测全部 m/z 离子，获得在某一时间所有来源离子的总览图谱。 The key principle is to scan both DC and RF voltages to sequentially detect all m/z ions, providing an overview spectrum of all ions generated at a given time.

该模式下，源光谱可作为样品的“清单”，即样品组成的整体指纹。 In this mode, the source spectrum acts as an "inventory" or overall fingerprint of all sample components.

全扫描适用于初步筛查新化合物、组分复杂样本定性，但灵敏度与选择性较差，低丰度组分信号易被掩盖。 Full scan is suitable for initial screening of unknown compounds and qualitative analysis of complex mixtures, but has poor sensitivity and selectivity, and low-abundance signals may be masked.

典型举例
电子轰击下碎片较多，ESI下主峰多为[M+H]+及加合物。 Example: Under electron impact, many fragments appear; under ESI, main peaks are usually [M+H]+ and adducts.
老师补充
全扫描获得的“指纹”仅适用于本台仪器和特定参数；与其他实验条件全同的数据库比对才有意义，否则不同设备/方法存在差异不具可比性。 Teacher's note: The "fingerprint" obtained in full scan applies only to the specific instrument and parameters; database comparison is meaningful only under identical conditions since variations exist across methods/instruments 1.
3
单离子监测/记录 SIM/Single Ion Monitoring/Recording（SIR）

在此模式下，分析器用特定的直流与射频电压设定，仅允许某一m/z离子通过。 In this mode, the analyzer applies specific DC and RF voltages so that only a given m/z ion can pass through.

可通过轮换电压设定，依次检测多个特定离子。适合目标物定量和高灵敏度监测。 By rapidly switching voltage settings, multiple selected ions can be sequentially detected. This mode is useful for targeted quantification and high-sensitivity monitoring.

高灵敏度得益于干扰离子的排除，但选择性有限，无法消除同分异构体等干扰。 High sensitivity comes from exclusion of unwanted ions; however, selectivity is limited and cannot eliminate interference from isomers, etc.

老师补充
SIM常用四极杆、离子阱，适合已知目标物定量，选择性监测某一离子或其碎片，适合含量极低样品检测。 Teacher's note: SIM is often used with quadrupole and ion trap analyzers for quantification of known targets, monitoring specific ions/fragments, and is ideal for low-level analytes 1.
4
离子碎裂 Ion Fragmentation

通过碰撞气激发离子，在分析器间碎裂（通常为串联质谱两分析器，在碰撞池内完成）。 By colliding the ion with neutral gas between analyzers (usually requiring tandem MS with two analyzers and a collision cell), fragmentation is achieved.

碎裂信息可以揭示结构，有助于解释未知物、测定官能团等。 Fragmentation data reveal molecular structure, aiding in interpretation of unknowns and functional group identification.

支持选择性与高灵敏定量，同时保留结构信息，可用于痕量检测并剔除复杂基质干扰。 Enables selectivity and highly sensitive quantitation while retaining structural info, suitable for trace detection and removal of complex matrix interference.

老师补充
碎裂结合定量分析技术是现代复杂样品测定的核心，既保证定性又能实现准确定量。 Teacher's note: Fragmentation plus quantitation is the core of modern complex sample analysis, ensuring reliable structure elucidation alongside accurate quantitation 1.
5
串联质谱MS/MS分析操作流程 MS/MS Analysis Workflow

除了离子阱外（可单体内完成），一般需两台分析器。 Except for ion traps (which can perform all steps internally), typically two analyzers are required.

基本步骤包括
Steps include:

第一步，通过A1分析器（如四极杆）选择母离子，常用SIM模式；

1
Select the precursor ion in A1 analyzer (often a quadrupole) via SIM.

第二步，母离子与碰撞气在碰撞池反应被激发碎裂； 2. Activate and fragment precursor ion by collision with gas in collision cell.

第三步，产生碎片离子（子离子）； 3. Generate fragment (daughter) ions in the collision cell.

第四步，用A2分析器扫描所有碎片离子，获得碎片谱。 4. Scan all fragment ions in A2 to obtain fragmentation spectrum.

优点：灵敏度高，选择性强，结构信息丰富，定量能力出色。 Advantages: high sensitivity, strong selectivity, rich structural information, and outstanding quantification performance.

老师补充
MS/MS串联质谱是复杂样品分析、生物大分子结构测定、痕量分析的金标准技术。 Teacher’s note: MS/MS tandem mass spectrometry is the gold standard for complex sample analysis, structural elucidation of biomolecules, and trace analysis 1.
6
综合小结 General Summary

三种采集模式分别适应不同分析目标和灵敏度需求，全扫描用于整体把握，单离子监测针对定量，碎裂/串联则用于定性与痕量检测。 The three acquisition modes serve different analytical goals and sensitivity levels: full scan for overall profiling, SIM for quantification, and fragmentation/tandem for qualitative and trace analysis.

随着复杂性提升，采用串联和高选择性分析成为主流。 As complexity increases, tandem and highly selective analyses are increasingly mainstream.

MS/MS分析与获取模式MS/MS Analysis & Acquisition Modes

MS/MS分析基本流程 MS/MS Analysis Workflow

串联质谱通常需两个分析器（除了离子阱可自内完成），用于母离子筛选与碎片分析。 Tandem mass spectrometry usually requires two analyzers (except that an ion trap can perform all steps internally) for precursor ion selection and fragment analysis.

步骤1
在A1中用SIM方式选择母离子（常用四极杆）。 Step 1: Selection of a precursor ion by SIM in A1 (often a quadrupole).
步骤2
母离子在碰撞池与惰性气体碰撞被激发。 Step 2: Activation by collisions between the selected ion and a collision gas in the collision cell.
步骤3
碰撞诱发母离子碎裂，产生活性“子离子”或碎片离子。 Step 3: Fragmentation of the precursor ion in the collision cell, generating daughter or fragment ions.
步骤4
A2对生成的全部碎片离子进行扫描与检测，获得碎片离子谱。 Step 4: Scanning of fragment ions in A2 to obtain the fragmentation spectrum.

MS/MS分析综合实现高灵敏度、高选择性、丰富结构信息与精准定量。 MS/MS delivers high sensitivity, high selectivity, abundant structural information and precise quantification.

结构信息主要通过对碎片谱的详细解析获得。 Structural information is mainly obtained by detailed interpretation of the fragmentation spectra.

教师补充
MS/MS是复杂样品定性、结构分析及痕量定量的金标准。 Teacher supplement: MS/MS is the gold standard for qualitative, structural analysis and trace quantification in complex samples 。

MS/MS分析在结构解析中的应用 MS/MS for Structural Analysis

结构解析时，首先需获得目标分子的全扫描MS谱。 For structural elucidation, acquisition begins with a fullscan MS spectrum of the target molecule.

例如催产素（Oxytocin）分子，MS全扫描可见主峰为[M+H]+（如1007.443）。 For example, for oxytocin, the full scan MS spectrum shows a main [M+H]+ peak (e.g., 1007.443).

选择主离子（如氧催产素[M+H]+），经碰撞诱导碎裂后获取MS/MS碎片谱。 Select the main ion (e.g., [M+H]+ of oxytocin), induce fragmentation, then obtain the MS/MS spectrum.

各碎片离子特征峰反映分子结构局部，如不同氨基酸序列。 The fragment ion peaks reveal partial structure, such as specific amino acid sequences.

对照全扫描谱与碎裂谱可确认化合物结构，探索分子特征位点。 Comparing the full scan with the MS/MS spectrum confirms compound structure and identifies characteristic sites 。

串联四极杆定量分析模式 Quantitative Analysis in Tandem Quadrupole

串联四极杆可执行三种主要扫描模式
全扫描、碎片扫描、MRM。 Tandem quadrupole can perform three main scan modes: full scan, fragmentation scan, and MRM (Multiple Reaction Monitoring).
全扫描 Fullscan
Q1不设过滤，所有离子检测，获得母离子全谱。 Fullscan: Q1 allows all ions to pass and detects all, acquiring the precursor spectrum.

碎片扫描 Fragmentation（MS/MS Spectrum）： Q1选定目标母离子，Q2检测所有碎片离子。 Fragmentation (MS/MS Spectrum): Q1 selects a specific precursor ion, Q2 detects all fragment ions.

多反应监测MRM
Q1只允许目标母离子通过，Q2只监测特定碎片离子，实现超高灵敏定量。 MRM: Q1 allows only the precursor ion of interest, Q2 detects only specific fragment ion(s), achieving ultra-sensitive quantification.

MRM模式与灵敏定量 MRM Mode and Sensitive Quantification

MRM可在复杂生物样品中实现高选择性、高灵敏度的目标分子定量。 MRM enables highly selective and sensitive quantification of target molecules in complex biological samples.

通过锁定“母离子→子离子”特定转变，极大降低背景干扰，只检测目标物。 By focusing on specific "precursor→fragment" transitions, it minimizes background noise and detects only the target.

老师强调
MRM能同时定量上百个分子，是法医、药物分析和兴奋剂检测等领域常用方法。 Teacher’s highlight: MRM allows simultaneous quantification of hundreds of molecules, and is commonly used in forensic, drug, and doping analyses 。

获取模式与选择性数据采集 Acquisition Modes and Selective Data Collection

全扫描模式适合成分筛查，定性为主；MRM聚焦目标反应，适合痕量分析和精准定量。 Full scan mode is mainly for screening/qualitative analysis; MRM focuses on target reactions and is suited for trace/quantitative analysis.

离子碎裂与串联质谱协同选用，提高结构解释力和定量能力。 Ion fragmentation and tandem MS are chosen synergistically, enhancing structural elucidation and quantification.

质谱分析总体步骤及记忆要点 Mass Spectrometry: Summary and Key Points

主要步骤包括
（1）色谱分离与样品挥发；（2）分子电离；（3）m/z分离与碎裂（可选高分辨/低分辨）；（4）m/z检测与信号记录。 Main steps: (1) Chromatographic separation and vaporization; (2) Molecule ionization; (3) m/z separation and fragmentation (low/high resolution optional); (4) m/z detection and intensity recording.

质谱的核心优势是结构信息和高效定量。 The core advantages of mass spectrometry are access to structural information and effective quantification.

教师总结
灵活应用多模式与串联分析是现代质谱准确、高通量分析的基础。 Teacher concludes: Flexible use of multi-mode and tandem analysis is fundamental for accurate and high-throughput modern MS 。

紫外-可见吸收光谱法Ultraviolet–Visible Absorption Spectroscopy

内容提要Contents

紫外-可见吸收光谱法的四个核心内容包括：简介、比尔定律、吸收物种、应用。 The four main contents of UV–Visible absorption spectroscopy are: Introduction, Beer’s Law, Absorbing Species, Applications.

A. 简介
电磁波谱区段A. Introduction: Regions of the Electromagnetic Spectrum
紫外-可见区波长划分
深紫外10-200 nm，近紫外200-400 nm，可见光400-800 nm。 UV–Visible regions by wavelength: Deep UV 10–200 nm, Near UV 200–400 nm, Visible 400–800 nm.

常用于分析的是近紫外（200–400 nm）和可见光（400–800 nm）区。 The commonly used analytical ranges are the near UV (200–400 nm) and visible (400–800 nm) regions.

此范围光子的能量约为100千卡，能够引发分子的电子跃迁。 Photons in this range have energies around 100 kcal, sufficient to induce electronic transitions in molecules.

电子跃迁是紫外-可见吸收光谱的核心原理。 Electronic transitions constitute the central mechanism of UV–Vis absorption spectroscopy.

录音补充
光谱学定义这些区段时需考虑溶剂及空气在深紫外下的吸收，因此深紫外多在真空或特殊条件下研究。 Audio supplement: When defining these regions spectroscopically, one must consider strong absorption by solvents and air in the deep UV, so deep UV studies require vacuum or special conditions 2.
A. 简介
透射率与吸光度A. Introduction: Transmittance and Absorbance

光通过样品的过程中，入射光强为P₀，出射光强为P。 When light passes through a sample, incident intensity is P₀, transmitted intensity is P.

透射率（T）定义为T = P / P₀。 Transmittance (T) is defined as T = P / P₀.

吸光度　定义为A = log₁₀(1/T) = log₁₀(P₀ / P)。 Absorbance (A) is defined as A = log₁₀(1/T) = log₁₀(P₀ / P).
答案 A
录音补充
实际操作中，更倾向于讨论吸光度，因为其与浓度呈线性关系，便于后续定量分析。 Audio supplement: In practice, absorbance is more commonly used since it shows a direct linear relationship with concentration, enabling straightforward quantitation 2.

英文（English）

Absorbance is a quantitative measure of how much light a sample absorbs at a specific wavelength. It is defined as the logarithm of the ratio between the incident light (I₀) and the transmitted light (I):

A = log₁₀(I₀ / I).

Absorbance reflects the concentration of light-absorbing molecules in a solution.

中文（Chinese）

吸光度是指样品在特定波长下对光的吸收程度，是一个定量指标。其定义为入射光强度（I₀）与透射光强度（I）之比的以 10 为底的对数：

A = log₁₀(I₀ / I)。

吸光度反映溶液中吸光物质的浓度。

A. 简介
吸光度的测量A. Introduction: Measurement of Absorbance

吸光度测量时需排除各种干扰，如溶液-壁、空气-壁的反射损失，容器壁吸收，和溶液中大分子的散射。 Measuring absorbance requires correction for various interferences such as reflection losses at the solution–wall and air–wall interfaces, container wall absorption, and scattering by large molecules in solution.

对于溶液样品，常用参比池进行对照，最终吸光度公式为A = –logT = log(P_solvent / P)。 For solutions, a reference cell (solvent only) is used for comparison, leading to the absorbance formula: A = –logT = log(P_solvent / P).

这种比值测量真实反映了样品溶液对入射光的吸收能力。 This ratio accurately reflects the true absorption of the incident light by the sample solution.

录音补充
实验中常利用参比减少系统误差，只有分析物吸收部分被计入吸光度。 Audio supplement: Using a reference reduces systematic errors, ensuring only absorption by the analyte is measured in absorbance 2.
A. 简介
吸收光谱A. Introduction: Absorption Spectra

吸收光谱是吸光度随波长变化的曲线。 Absorption spectra are plots of absorbance as a function of wavelength.

以对乙酰氨基酚为例，其溶液吸收谱呈宽展带宽，主要因分子在溶液中的相互作用。 For example, paracetamol in solution shows broad absorption bands, mainly due to intermolecular interactions in solution.

气相光谱及高分辨仪器可得到窄带和细结构，但常规分析多采用宽带的溶液光谱。 Gas-phase spectra and high-resolution instruments can resolve narrow bands and fine structure, but routine measurements often use broad-band solution spectra.

吸收带的存在与分子的某些官能团、结构特征密切相关。 The presence and shape of absorption bands are closely related to specific functional groups or structural features in the molecule.

录音补充
宽带特性是分子与溶剂间微观环境变化的反映，有时用于分辨不同分子或构象。 Audio supplement: The broad-band nature reflects variations in microenvironment between molecule and solvent and is sometimes used to distinguish different molecules or conformers 2.

小结Summary

紫外-可见吸收光谱法基于电子跃迁、吸光度与浓度的关系以及溶液实验的测量特点。 UV-visible absorption spectroscopy is based on electronic transitions, the absorbance–concentration relationship, and specific features of solution-phase measurements.

常用溶液测量，注意实验中的误差来源及结果解读。 Measurements are mostly done in solution phase, with attention to experimental errors and result interpretation.

本法广泛用于定性、定量与结构分析，是最常见的分子分析方法之一。 This spectroscopy is widely used for qualitative, quantitative, and structural analysis, being one of the most common analytical techniques.

比尔定律Beer's Law

比尔定律表达溶液中吸收物质的吸光度与其浓度和光程长度成正比。 Beer's law states that the absorbance of a solution is directly proportional to the concentration of the absorbing species and the path length of the cell.

公式表达为
A = α l C The equation is given as: A = α l C

其中A为吸光度，α为吸收系数，l为光程长度，C为吸收物浓度。 Here, A is the absorbance, α is the absorptivity, l is the path length, and C is the concentration of the absorber.

基本参数说明 Key Parameter Explanation

光程长度l
通常为1厘米。 Path length l: typically 1 cm.
吸收物浓度C
可以用mol·L⁻¹或g·L⁻¹表示。 Concentration of absorber C: can be expressed in mol·L⁻¹ or g·L⁻¹.
吸收系数α
单位为L·g⁻¹·cm⁻¹，对应的C单位为g·L⁻¹。 Absorptivity α: unit is L·g⁻¹·cm⁻¹, C should be in g·L⁻¹ in this case.
摩尔吸收系数ε
单位为L·mol⁻¹·cm⁻¹，此时C单位为mol·L⁻¹。 Molar absorptivity ε: unit is L·mol⁻¹·cm⁻¹, and C is in mol·L⁻¹ for this formulation.

课堂及录音要点补充Class and Audio Key Points Supplement

通常实验中，光程长度l用标准比色皿，固定为1 cm，便于浓度和吸光度的直接换算。 In routine practice, the path length l is usually fixed at 1 cm using standard cuvettes, allowing for direct calculation between absorbance and concentration.

吸光度与浓度呈线性关系是构建定量分析标准曲线的基础，适用浓度范围应确保线性。 The linear relationship between absorbance and concentration provides the basis for quantitative standard curves; the concentration range must stay within the linear regime for accuracy.

α和ε用于不同单位的表达，ε（摩尔吸收系数）在科学文献和高准确度定量分析中更常用。 α and ε serve for different units; ε (molar absorptivity) is more commonly used in academic literature and for accurate quantitative work.

实际测定时需确保测量参数（光程长度、溶液浓度）精确控制，才能保证比尔定律的适用性与结果的可靠性。 In practice, precise control of measurement parameters (path length, solution concentration) is essential to ensure the validity of Beer’s law and the reliability of results.

录音强调
实验常考吸光度定义及对比尔定律公式的应用，理解其与浓度成正比关系对于分析科学非常关键。 The audio highlights that exam questions often cover definitions of absorbance and the application of Beer’s law formula; understanding the proportionality with concentration is key in analytical science.

小结Summary

比尔定律简化了浓度与吸光度之间的关系，是紫外-可见分光光度法定量分析的核心基础。 Beer’s law simplifies the relationship between concentration and absorbance, forming the core of quantitative UV-Vis spectrophotometry.

吸光度 Absorbance

光程长度 Path Length

浓度 Concentration

吸收系数/摩尔吸收系数 Absorptivity/Molar Absorptivity
