吸收物种
分子的能级及跃迁C. Absorbing species: Energy states and electronic transitions of molecules
1
分子的能级结构Energy States of Molecules
分子的总能量由三部分组成
电子能（E_el），振动能（E_vib），转动能（E_rot）。 The total energy of a molecule consists of three parts: electronic energy (E_el), vibrational energy (E_vib), and rotational energy (E_rot)。
能量分布关系
E_tot = E_el + E_vib + E_rot。 Energy distribution: E_tot = E_el + E_vib + E_rot。

电子能变化通常约100 kcal，振动能约1 kcal。 Electronic energy changes are typically around 100 kcal, and vibrational energy is about 1 kcal。

老师补充
紫外-可见光谱法主要探测分子中电子能级的跃迁，但每个电子能级下细分为多个振动能级，每个振动能级下又包含多个转动能级，这决定了谱带的展宽和结构。 Audio supplement: UV-Vis spectroscopy mainly detects electronic transitions, but each electronic level contains many vibrational levels, and each vibrational level contains many rotational levels, which determines the bandwidth and structure of the spectrum。

S₀、S₁、S₂为不同的单重态电子能级，吸收时分子从基态S₀跃迁到激发态S₁或S₂。 S₀, S₁, S₂ represent different singlet electronic states; during absorption, molecules transition from ground state S₀ to excited states S₁ or S₂。

光谱带宽体现为分子不同振动能级、转动能级的叠加。 Bandwidth in spectra results from combinations of various vibrational and rotational levels。

2
分子的电子跃迁类型Types of Electronic Transitions in Molecules

紫外-可见区域检测的本质是分子轨道上的电子跃迁，涉及成键、反键及非键轨道。 The essence of UV-Vis detection is electronic transitions on molecular orbitals, involving bonding, anti-bonding, and non-bonding orbitals。

常见分子轨道类型有
σ（成键、bonding）、π（成键、bonding）、n（非键、non-bonding）、π*（反键、anti-bonding）、σ*（反键、anti-bonding）。 Common molecular orbital types: σ (bonding), π (bonding), n (non-bonding), π* (anti-bonding), σ* (anti-bonding)。
主要可观测的重要跃迁及能量排名为
Main observable transitions and their order of energy required:

n→π* < π→π* < n→σ* < σ→σ*

n→π跃迁能量最小，σ→σ最大。 n→π* requires the least energy, σ→σ* requires the most。

教师补充
π→π和n→π跃迁常在有共轭双键或含杂原子的有机分子中观察到，这些跃迁能量位于紫外-可见区，因此尤为重要。 Audio supplement: π→π* and n→π* transitions are typically observed in conjugated systems or organic molecules containing heteroatoms; these fall in the UV-Vis region and are particularly important。
甲醛的能级跃迁实例Example
Electronic Transitions in Formaldehyde

甲醛分子能级示意包括σ、π、n、π*、σ等轨道，底层为基态，激发单重态为高能级。 The formaldehyde energy diagram includes σ, π, n, π, and σ* orbitals, with the ground state at the bottom and the excited singlet states at higher energies。

HOMO和LUMO分别是最高占据和最低未占据分子轨道。 HOMO and LUMO refer to the highest occupied and the lowest unoccupied molecular orbitals。

甲醛可实现以下跃迁
Formaldehyde allows the following transitions:

σ→σ*（高能） σ→σ* (high energy)

n→σ* n→σ*

π→π* π→π*

n→π*（低能） n→π* (low energy)

各跃迁对应不同能量，决定其在紫外-可见光谱中的吸收波长区间。 Each transition corresponds to a different energy, thus determining its absorption wavelength in the UV-Vis region。

4
电子跃迁的特征与波长范围Characteristics & Wavelength Ranges of Electronic Transitions

各电子跃迁的基本特征及典型波长/摩尔消光系数如下： Principal features and typical wavelengths/molar absorptivity for transitions:

σ→σ* 跃迁能量高，常见于λ<150 nm。 σ→σ* transition: high ΔE, excitation at λ<150 nm。

n→σ* 能量稍低，激发波长150–250 nm可见。 n→σ* transition: lower ΔE, possible at 150–250 nm。

π→π* 和 n→π* 能量更低，波长分布在180–700 nm，且摩尔吸光系数分别较高和较低。 π→π* and n→π* transitions: lowest ΔE, λ=180–700 nm, ε higher for π→π* (1000–10000), lower for n→π* (10–100)。

教师强调
π→π和n→π跃迁才是大多数有机物，尤其是含共轭体系、有孤对电子基团分子的主要分析对象。 Audio highlight: π→π* and n→π* transitions are the main analytical focus for most organics, especially conjugated and heteroatom-containing molecules。
5
有机化合物的吸收特性与色团Organic Compounds: Absorption and Chromophores

所有有机物含单键，理论上在λ<185 nm可吸收，但应用中限定于少数特定结构（色团）能在λ>200 nm发生跃迁。 All organics have single bonds and can absorb at λ<185 nm, but only specific structures (chromophores) can be excited at λ>200 nm。

σ→σ跃迁，如甲烷（125 nm）、乙烷（135 nm）；n→σ跃迁见于含杂原子单键（如H₂O: 167 nm, ε=1480）。 σ→σ* transition: e.g. methane (125 nm), ethane (135 nm); n→σ* transition: single bond with heteroatoms (e.g. H₂O: 167 nm, ε=1480)。

n→π和π→π为色团典型跃迁，共轭双键、羰基等基团显著吸收紫外-可见光。 n→π* and π→π* are typical chromophore transitions; conjugated systems and carbonyls significantly absorb in UV-Vis。

不同化合物λ_max和最大消光系数ε_max不同，具体如表所示。例如：六碳烯（177 nm, ε=13000, π→π*）；丙酮（186 nm, ε=1000, n→σ*）；甲醛（280/180 nm，n→π* / n→σ*）。 λ_max and ε_max vary for each compound (see table): e.g. hexene (177 nm, ε=13000, π→π*); acetone (186 nm, ε=1000, n→σ*); formaldehyde (280/180 nm, n→π*, n→σ*)。

色团（chromophores）使相关有机物具可见/紫外特征吸收，是分析中识别与定量的重要基础。 Chromophores provide characteristic UV/Vis absorption, forming the basis for identification and quantitation in analysis。

关键词Keywords

能级分布 Energy level distribution

电子跃迁 Electronic transitions

成键/反键轨道 Bonding/anti-bonding orbitals

非键轨道 Non-bonding orbital

色团 Chromophore

λ_max, ε_max λ_max, ε_max (molar absorptivity/消光系数)

有机物的色团与共轭效应Chromophores and Conjugation Effects in Organic Compounds

共轭效应对π→π跃迁的影响Influence of Conjugation on π→π Transitions

共轭双键体系（如C=C与C=C-C=C）能量跃迁分析显示，随着共轭程度增加，π→π跃迁的能级间距ΔE减小。 The energy transition analysis of conjugated double bond systems (e.g., C=C and C=C–C=C) shows that as conjugation increases, the energy gap ΔE for π→π transitions decreases.

能级图中，简单双键只有一个π和π能级，而在长共轭体系中（如丁二烯），能级数增多，最低π→π能级间距下降。 In the diagram, a simple double bond has only one π and π* level, but in a longer conjugated system (e.g., butadiene), there are more π levels and the lowest π→π* gap decreases.

因ΔE减小，吸收波长（λ_max）红移，典型红移幅度为30-50nm。 As ΔE decreases, the absorption maximum (λ_max) shifts to longer wavelengths (red shift), typically by 30-50 nm.

有机分子的吸收特性与摩尔消光系数Absorptive Properties and Molar Absorptivity of Organic Molecules

有机化合物的λ_max与ε_max因结构不同而显著变化，特别是共轭系统。 The λ_max and ε_max of organic compounds vary significantly with structure, especially in conjugated systems.

典型数据
五碳共轭烯（CH₂=CHCH₂CH=CH₂，λ_max = 217nm, ε_max = 21000），六碳共轭烯（λ_max = 250nm, ε_max = 20000），具有羰基的共轭系统（如CH₂=CHCH₂CH₂(C=O)CH₃，λ_max = 282nm, ε_max = 27），表明λ_max随着共轭长度增加而显著红移。 Typical data: pentadiene (CH₂=CHCH₂CH=CH₂, λ_max = 217 nm, ε_max = 21000), hexadiene (λ_max = 250 nm, ε_max = 20000), conjugated carbonyl systems (e.g., CH₂=CHCH₂CH₂(C=O)CH₃, λ_max = 282 nm, ε_max = 27), showing marked red shift of λ_max as conjugation length increases.

右侧谱图显示，随着共轭长度n的增加，光谱明显红移且ε显著增大。 The spectral graph on the right shows that as conjugation number n increases, the spectrum shifts to longer wavelengths and ε increases markedly.

苯环及其衍生物的紫外吸收特性UV Absorption of Benzene and its Derivatives

苯分子的吸收谱主要表现为π→π跃迁，具有明显的E带、K带与B带结构。 The absorption spectrum of benzene features π→π transitions, with distinct E, K, and B bands.

不同苯环取代基对红移影响显著，取代基的电负性越高，红移越明显。 Substituents on the benzene ring notably affect the red shift; the higher the electronegativity, the more pronounced the red shift.

典型苯系化合物的吸收带数据如表所示
如苯E₂带λ_max=204nm, ε_max=7900，B带λ_max=256nm, ε_max=200；苯酚E₂带λ_max=211nm, ε_max=6200，B带λ_max=270nm, ε_max=1450。 Typical aromatic compounds' band data include: benzene E₂ band λ_max=204 nm, ε_max=7900, B band λ_max=256 nm, ε_max=200; phenol E₂ band λ_max=211 nm, ε_max=6200, B band λ_max=270 nm, ε_max=1450.

多环芳香烃（如萘、蒽、菲）随着环数增加，吸收进一步红移并增强。 With increased ring number in polycyclic aromatics (like naphthalene, anthracene, phenanthrene), absorption shifts further to the red and intensifies.

色团（Chromophores）与分析应用Chromophores and Analytical Implications

色团是指能在200nm以上区域显著吸收的有机结构单元，比如共轭烯、芳香环、羰基等。 Chromophores refer to organic structural units that absorb significantly above 200 nm, such as conjugated alkenes, aromatic rings, carbonyls, etc.

共轭结构和特定取代基可通过调节吸收带实现分子的识别与定量分析。 Conjugation and specific substituents can tune the absorption bands, allowing for molecular identification and quantitative analysis.

总结Summary

共轭效应导致π→π跃迁吸收波长显著红移，且ε上升。 Conjugation causes a prominent red shift in π→π transition absorption wavelengths and an increase in ε.

芳香化合物和多环体系具有复杂吸收结构，取代基显著影响红移及吸收强度。 Aromatic and polycyclic systems have complex absorptions, and substituents significantly affect the red shift and absorption intensity.

这些规律为紫外-可见光谱在有机物结构分析中的应用提供理论基础和方法保证。 These principles form the theoretical and methodological basis for UV-Vis spectroscopy in organic structural analysis.

溶剂效应对吸收物种的影响及定性应用Solvent Effect on Absorbing Species and Qualitative Applications

1
溶质/溶剂作用对分子能级的影响Solute/Solvent Interactions Affect Molecular Energy Levels

溶剂极性变化会影响分子中n轨道和π轨道的能量位置，尤其对于含羰基（C=O）的化合物更为明显。 Changes in solvent polarity affect the energy of the n and π orbitals in molecules, especially for compounds containing carbonyl groups (C=O).

在极性溶剂中，n和π能级均降低，但降低幅度有差异。 In polar solvents, both n and π energy levels are lowered, but the extent of lowering differs.

溶剂类型的比较
非极性溶剂、偶极-偶极作用、氢键作用，氢键作用对能级降低效果最强。 Types of solvents compared: non-polar solvent, dipole-dipole interactions, and hydrogen bonding; hydrogen bonding has the strongest effect on lowering energy levels.

结果： Result:

n→π跃迁在极性溶剂中需更高能量，发生蓝移（谱带向较短波长移动）。 The n→π transition requires higher energy in polar solvents, causing a blue shift (band moves to shorter wavelength).

π→π跃迁在极性溶剂中所需能量降低，发生红移（谱带向较长波长移动）。 The π→π transition requires less energy in polar solvents, causing a red shift (band moves to longer wavelength).

谱带普遍展宽。 General band broadening occurs.

2
谱带位置、强度与形状的变化Modification of the Position, Intensity, and Shape of Absorption Bands

n→π跃迁能量增加（蓝移），π→π跃迁能量减少（红移）。 For n→π* transitions, energy increases (blue shift); for π→π* transitions, energy decreases (red shift).

极性溶剂增加时会导致吸收峰宽度增加，谱带展宽。 Increased solvent polarity results in broader absorption peaks and band broadening.

3
代表性实例说明Representative Examples

乙醛（Acetaldehyde）： Acetaldehyde:

在气相和低极性溶剂（戊烷）中，吸收峰更锐利且波长较长。 In the gas phase and in low-polarity solvents (heptane), the absorption peak is sharper and at longer wavelengths.

在极性溶剂（如水、醇）中，n→π吸收峰向短波长移动并展宽。 In polar solvents (water, alcohol), the n→π absorption peak shifts to shorter wavelengths and broadens.

苯及其衍生物（Benzene Derivatives）： Benzene and derivatives:

苯蒸汽的紫外吸收出现大量细致结构（振动精细结构），在低极性有机溶剂中（己烷）这种结构变宽且变少。 Benzene vapor in the UV spectrum shows rich fine (vibrational) structure; in low-polarity organic solvents (hexane), these structures broaden and decrease.

芳烃的π→π吸收带在溶剂中普遍展宽，进一步证实溶剂作用能显著影响紫外-可见吸收光谱的定性特征。 The π→π absorption bands of aromatics generally broaden in solution, further confirming that solvent effects significantly influence the qualitative features of UV-Vis spectra.

4
课堂与补充讲解听课要点Key Points from Lecture and Audio

老师强调，不同溶剂条件下能级变化和跃迁能量变化，对于定性分析（如区分有机物类型或推断分子结构）具有重要意义。 The instructor emphasized that changes in energy levels and transition energies in different solvents are critical for qualitative analysis (e.g., distinguishing types of organic compounds or inferring molecular structure).

溶剂效应不仅改变能级排列，也影响吸收峰强度和带宽，解释了为什么实际测量中谱图形态明显不同。 Solvent effects change not only the energetic ordering but also the intensity and width of absorption peaks, explaining the clear differences in spectra under real measurement conditions.

课堂特别指出极性溶剂中n→π和π→π跃迁位置变化的具体方向和原因，并通过乙醛和芳烃的案例图谱加深理解。 The lesson specifically pointed out the direction and reason for the position changes of n→π* and π→π* transitions in polar solvents, and deepened understanding with case spectra of acetaldehyde and aromatics.

关键词Keywords

溶剂效应 Solvent effect

能级降低 Energy level lowering

蓝移 Blue shift

红移 Red shift

谱带展宽 Band broadening

n→π跃迁 n→π transition

π→π跃迁 π→π transition

定性分析 Qualitative analysis溶剂效应对吸收物种的影响及定性应用Solvent Effect on Absorbing Species and Qualitative Applications

溶剂效应概述Solvent Effect Overview

溶质/溶剂相互作用会影响分子的能级结构，尤其是含羰基（C=O）等官能团。 Solute/solvent interactions affect the energy levels of molecules, especially those with functional groups such as carbonyl (C=O).

在极性溶剂中，n轨道和π轨道的能级均降低，但下降幅度不同，n轨道受溶剂极性影响更明显。 In polar solvents, both n and π energy levels are lowered, but the n orbital is more significantly affected by polarity.

三种典型情形包括非极性溶剂、偶极-偶极相互作用、以及氢键作用，其中氢键作用导致能级降幅最大。 Three typical cases are non-polar solvent, dipole-dipole interaction, and hydrogen bonding, where hydrogen bonding causes the greatest decrease.

跃迁类型与溶剂调节Transition Types and Solvent Modulation

n→π跃迁
极性溶剂中需要更高能量，导致吸收峰出现蓝移（向短波长移动）。 n→π transition: higher energy required in polar solvents, absorption peak blue-shifts (moves to shorter wavelength).
π→π跃迁
极性溶剂中能量需求下降，吸收峰红移（向长波长移动）。 π→π transition: lower energy required in polar solvents, absorption peak red-shifts (moves to longer wavelength).

通常伴随吸收谱带展宽，尤其在氢键溶剂存在时。 Band broadening usually accompanies these shifts, especially in hydrogen-bonding solvents.

具体实例讲解Specific Case Study

乙醛（Acetaldehyde）：

气相和戊烷等低极性溶剂中，n→π吸收带波长长且结构较清晰。 In gas phase and low-polarity solvents such as heptane, the n→π absorption band has a longer wavelength and clearer structure.

水、醇等高极性溶剂中，吸收带明显蓝移且展宽。 In polar solvents like water and alcohol, the absorption band significantly blue-shifts and broadens.

苯及其衍生物（Benzene derivatives）：

苯蒸汽的π→π吸收有清晰的振动结构，溶液中（如己烷）结构展宽，双苯（联苯）进一步展宽。 Benzene vapor shows clear vibrational structure in the π→π absorption; in solution (e.g., hexane), the structure broadens, and biphenyl shows even more band broadening.

谱带修饰机制与应用Band Modification Mechanism and Applications

n→π跃迁蓝移、π→π跃迁红移，谱带展宽，是溶液紫外-可见吸收光谱判别分子环境的重要依据。 n→π* transitions blue-shift, π→π* transitions red-shift, and band broadening serve as important evidence for judging the molecular environment in UV-Vis absorption spectra.

此类溶剂效应广泛应用于有机分子的定性鉴别和微环境分析。 Such solvent effects are widely used for qualitative identification and microenvironment analysis of organic molecules.

补充课堂要点Audio and Lecture Highlights

教师特别强调通过吸收峰位置和形态的变化可推测被测分子的环境极性和特殊相互作用（如氢键）。 The instructor particularly emphasized that changes in peak position and shape allow inference of the polarity and specific interactions (such as hydrogen bonding) of the sample’s environment.

溶剂引入的新相互作用不仅移动能级，还可能增强或削弱某类跃迁强度，导致吸收带定性特征变化。 The introduction of new interactions by solvents not only shift energy levels but may also strengthen or weaken transition intensity, altering the qualitative spectral feature.

以实验谱图实例强化溶剂效应的实际意义，尤其在结构和定性分析中的判别能力。 Demonstrations with actual spectral examples underscore the practical significance of solvent effects—especially their power in structural and qualitative analyses.

关键词Keywords

溶剂效应 Solvent effect

能级能量变化 Energy level shift

蓝移 Blue shift

红移 Red shift

n→π跃迁 n→π transition

π→π跃迁 π→π transition

谱带展宽 Band broadening

定性应用 Qualitative application

应用——定性与定量分析D. Applications—Qualitative and Quantitative Applications

一、色团检测的定性应用Detection of Chromophoric Groups—Qualitative Applications

在近紫外和可见光区的吸收表明体系中存在不饱和基团或元素（如硫、卤素）。 Absorption in the near UV and visible regions indicates the presence of unsaturated groups or atoms such as sulfur or halogens.

可通过将未知物的光谱与已知色团的标准光谱（需用相同溶剂）进行对比，达到溶质结构的初步识别。 Comparison of the spectrum of an unknown with those of known simple chromophores (using the same solvent) enables preliminary solute identification.

然而，由于许多有机物的吸收带缺乏微细结构，并且环境依赖性强，具体定性能力有限。 However, most organic spectra show a lack of fine structure and are highly dependent on environment, thus limiting precise qualitative identification.

主要还是用于检测官能团，辅助推断分子结构类型。 The main qualitative value is detecting the presence of functional groups, providing clues about the type of molecular structure.

老师补充
若吸收出现在近紫外区说明分子具有不饱和键（如C=C、芳香环），在可见区有吸收则说明存在高度共轭色团。 Additional point from lecture: Absorption in the near UV implies unsaturation (e.g., C=C, aromatic ring); absorption in visible region indicates highly conjugated chromophores.

如果样品与标准色团光谱吻合但缺少微细结构，说明是同类色团，但无法进一步区分具体结构。 If the sample’s spectrum matches that of the chromophore standard but lacks fine structure, it confirms the same type of chromophore, but cannot further distinguish specific structure.

二、常见有机化合物的典型吸收Typical Absorption of Organic Compounds

不同有机分子（如β-胡萝卜素、咖啡因、阿司匹林、丙酮）在紫外-可见区的最大吸收波长和摩尔吸收系数差异很大。 Different organic molecules—such as β-carotene, caffeine, acetylsalicylic acid (aspirin), and acetone—show markedly different UV-Vis λ_max values and molar absorptivities.

β-胡萝卜素（高度共轭）吸收波长大，吸收系数高，色泽鲜明。 Beta-carotene (highly conjugated) absorbs at longer wavelengths with high molar absorptivity, yielding pronounced color.

咖啡因、阿司匹林等分子的吸收峰低于β-胡萝卜素，吸收系数也较小，因结构中含有较少的共轭体系。 Caffeine and aspirin absorb at shorter wavelengths with lower absorptivity due to fewer conjugated systems.

丙酮吸收峰更偏紫外短波区，摩尔吸光系数最低，属于n→π跃迁。 Acetone peaks in the lower UV range with the lowest molar absorptivity, typical of n→π transitions.

老师强调
通过比较样本吸收峰的位置、强度和形状，可以快速判断其有机骨架的主要特征（如是否有共轭、芳香环、羰基等）。 Instructor emphasized: By examining the position, intensity, and profile of absorption peaks, one can rapidly infer key structural features such as conjugation, aromaticity, and functional groups.

三、定量分析的紫外-可见吸收应用Quantitative Applications of UV-Vis Absorption

紫外-可见吸收法是最常用的定量分析技术之一，因其适用范围广、检测灵敏度高（LOD一般为10⁻⁴至10⁻⁵ mol/L）、选择性较高、准确性好、数据采集便捷。 UV-Vis absorption is one of the most widely used quantitative techniques, with broad applicability, high sensitivity (typical LOD: 10⁻⁴~10⁻⁵ M), moderate to high selectivity, good accuracy, and easy data acquisition.

λ选择非常重要。通常选择主吸收峰（如Band A），以获得最大灵敏度和良好的线性关系。 Wavelength (λ) selection is critical. Typically, the main absorption peak (such as Band A) is chosen for highest sensitivity and optimal linearity.

曲线左图显示同一物质的两个吸收带，选择光强更大的带有更佳的灵敏度。 The left figure shows two absorption bands for the same compound; selecting the stronger band yields greater sensitivity.

右图说明主吸收峰同时具有良好的线性关系，便于定量分析。 The right figure demonstrates that the major peak gives good linearity, facilitating accurate quantitation.

老师补充
选择波长要兼顾灵敏度、线性和基线干扰，最佳选取是主吸收峰顶点，尤其在分析复杂样品时。 Supplementary lecture content: When choosing λ, consider sensitivity, linearity, and baseline interference; the best choice is typically the apex of the main absorption band, especially for complex samples.

A. 引言
发光现象 A. Introduction: Luminescence

发光（Luminescence）是指物质在被激发后，发射紫外、可见或红外光的现象。 Luminescence refers to the emission of ultraviolet, visible, or infrared light from an electronically excited species.

光致发光（Photoluminescence）是一类发光，其激发方式为光的吸收。 Photoluminescence is a type of luminescence where the excitation is caused by the absorption of light.

光致发光主要分为两类
荧光（Fluorescence）和磷光（Phosphorescence）。 Photoluminescence is mainly divided into two categories: fluorescence and phosphorescence.

荧光与磷光通常发生在紫外或可见光区域。 Both fluorescence and phosphorescence typically occur in the UV or visible regions.

老师录音要点补充 Key Points from Lecture Audio

老师在课上开篇强调
荧光光谱是吸收光谱以外非常重要的分子分析技术，后续课程将系统讲解荧光的物理过程、表现特性与分子结构的关系，以及如何在实际分析化学和生命科学中应用。 The instructor emphasized at the beginning: Fluorescence spectroscopy is a crucial molecular analysis technique beyond absorption spectroscopy. The subsequent lectures will systematically introduce the physical processes, characteristics, structure-dependence, and practical analytical and life science applications of fluorescence.
录音中指出
荧光与磷光都是光致发光现象，两者的区别在于能态间的“跃迁时间”，荧光为快速发射之一，而磷光为较慢发射。 In the audio, it was noted: Both fluorescence and phosphorescence are photoluminescence phenomena, but the key difference is the transition time between states: fluorescence is a fast emission, while phosphorescence is much slower.

荧光分子常用于示踪、生物标记、环境检测等领域，因其高灵敏度和特异性。 Fluorescent molecules are widely used in tracing, bio-labeling, and environmental sensing due to their high sensitivity and specificity.

荧光的特征 Perrin-Jablonski图Characteristics of fluorescence Perrin-Jablonski diagram

1
Perrin-Jablonski图与基本能级过程Perrin-Jablonski Diagram and Basic Energy Processes

分子吸收光子后，从基态S₀跃迁到激发单线态S₁或S₂（A：吸收，A:hνA:hν） After absorbing a photon, a molecule transitions from ground state S₀ to excited singlet states S₁ or S₂ (A: absorption, A:hνA:hν) ⬇️

振动弛豫（VR）使分子快速回到最低振动态，随后可能发生内部转化（IC），能量在不同电子态间非辐射转移。 Vibrational relaxation (VR) rapidly returns the molecule to the lowest vibrational level, followed by possible internal conversion (IC), i.e., nonradiative energy transfer between electronic states. ⬇️

S₁态分子可通过发射光子的形式回到S₀（F：荧光，F:hν′F:hν′），荧光在纳秒内完成。 Molecules in S₁ can return to S₀ by emitting a photon (F: fluorescence, F:hν′F:hν′), which typically occurs within nanoseconds. ⬇️

部分分子由S₁态发生系间窜跃（ISC）进入三线态T₁；T₁到S₀的光发射即为磷光（P：hν′′hν′′），持续时间远长于荧光。 Some molecules undergo intersystem crossing (ISC) from S₁ to triplet state T₁; photon emission from T₁ to S₀ is phosphorescence (P: hν′′hν′′), much longer-lived than fluorescence. ⬇️

2
各能级过程的特征时间及定义Characteristic Times and Definitions of Processes
吸收　：10−1510−15 s。 Absorption (A): 10−1510−15 s. ⬇️
答案 A

振动弛豫（VR）：10−1210−12 到 10−1010−10 s。 Vibrational Relaxation (VR): 10−1210−12 to 10−1010−10 s. ⬇️

S₁激发单线态寿命
10−1010−10 到 10−710−7 s。 Excited singlet state S₁ lifetime: 10−1010−10 to 10−710−7 s. ⬇️

内转换（IC）：10−1110−11 到 10−910−9 s。 Internal Conversion (IC): 10−1110−11 to 10−910−9 s. ⬇️

系间窜跃（ISC）：10−1010−10 到 10−810−8 s。 Intersystem Crossing (ISC): 10−1010−10 to 10−810−8 s. ⬇️

T₁激发三线态寿命
10−610−6 到 1 s。 Excited triplet state T₁ lifetime: 10−610−6 to 1 s. ⬆️

荧光（F）：上面S₁态回S₀，纳秒级，过程极快。 Fluorescence (F): S₁ to S₀ transition, nanosecond scale, very fast. ⬆️

磷光（P）：T₁态回S₀，寿命长，发射持续。 Phosphorescence (P): T₁ to S₀ transition, longer lifetime and emission duration. ⬆️

3
单线态与三线态多重性Spin Multiplicities of Singlet and Triplet States
S₀、S₁为单线态
自旋反平行，M=2(12−12)+1=1M=2(21​−21​)+1=1； S₀, S₁ are singlet states: spins antiparallel, M=2(12−12)+1=1M=2(21​−21​)+1=1; ⬇️
T₁为三线态
自旋平行，M=2(12+12)+1=3M=2(21​+21​)+1=3。 T₁ is triplet state: spins parallel, M=2(12+12)+1=3M=2(21​+21​)+1=3. ⬇️
4
吸收与荧光光谱原理Principle of Absorption and Fluorescence Spectra
吸收光谱
样品吸收λEλE​波长的光，吸收度A=log⁡(I0(λE)/IT(λE))A=log(I0​(λE​)/IT​(λE​))。 Absorption spectra: sample absorbs light of λEλE​, absorbance A=log⁡(I0(λE)/IT(λE))A=log(I0​(λE​)/IT​(λE​)). ⬇️
荧光发射光谱
样品在吸收λEλE​后，发射λFλF​的荧光，IF(λE,λF)IF​(λE​,λF​)为检测信号。 Fluorescence emission spectra: sample emits fluorescence at λFλF​ after absorbing λEλE​, IF(λE,λF)IF​(λE​,λF​) is the measured signal. ⬇️
5
讲解重点补充Key Supplemental Points from Lecture
老师强调
荧光和磷光均基于分子吸收能量后回到基态的不同路径和速率，荧光只发生于单线态，极快且高效；磷光需经自旋翻转，能级间跃迁慢，发射寿命更长。 Instructor emphasized: Both fluorescence and phosphorescence result from molecules returning to ground state after energy absorption but differ in transition paths and rates; fluorescence occurs only in singlet states (fast and efficient), while phosphorescence involves spin-flip and slower intersystem crossing, with longer lifetimes. ⬇️

实验室分析中，因荧光发射速度极快，可实现高灵敏的定量检测，且对背景干扰小。 In laboratory analysis, because fluorescence occurs extremely quickly, it enables highly sensitive quantitation with minimal background interference. ⬇️

Perrin-Jablonski图是解释光谱行为及区分各种发光过程的核心工具。 The Perrin-Jablonski diagram is a core tool for explaining spectroscopic behavior and distinguishing emission processes. ⬇️

荧光与化学结构 Fluorescence and Chemical Structure

发射
共轭π体系的扩展 Emission: Extension of electron π conjugation
1
π→π 跃迁与荧光 π→π Transitions and Fluorescence**

π→π跃迁的摩尔吸收系数通常比n→π跃迁高一到三个数量级。 Molar extinction coefficients for π→π* transitions are typically 100 to 1000 times higher than for n→π* transitions.

π→π跃迁态的寿命很短，约为 10−910−9 到 10−710−7 秒，而n→π跃迁的寿命相对更长，为 10−710−7 到 10−510−5 秒。 The excited state lifetime for π→π* transitions is around 10−910−9–10−710−7 s, while for n→π* it is longer, around 10−710−7–10−510−5 s.

π→π态的分子不易发生系间窜跃（ISC），因此荧光效率较高。 Inter-system crossing (ISC) is rare for π→π states, resulting in higher fluorescence efficiency.

2
荧光团的定义及结构特征 Definition and Structural Features of Fluorophores

荧光团通常为具有低能量π→π跃迁的芳香族化合物。 Fluorophores are typically aromatic compounds with low-energy π→π transitions.

结构上，如吡啶（pyridine）、吡咯（pyrrole）等因杂原子的n→π特征，往往不表现荧光；而喹啉（quinoline）、吲哚（indole）等多环芳烃则易于荧光发射。 Structurally, compounds like pyridine and pyrrole (with significant n→π character due to heteroatoms) are generally non-fluorescent, while polycyclic aromatics such as quinoline and indole are fluorescent.

3
芳香族共轭体系对发射波长的影响 Influence of Aromatic Conjugation on Emission Wavelength

延长分子的π共轭体系，会使发射波长向可见光长波方向移动，颜色依次由紫外、蓝、绿、红变化。 Extending the molecular π conjugation shifts fluorescence emission to longer wavelengths, changing color from ultraviolet to blue, green, and then red.

具体例子
Specific examples:

萘（naphthalene）：仅超紫外发射（Ultra violet） Naphthalene: emits in the ultra violet

蒽（anthracene）：发蓝光（Blue） Anthracene: emits blue

四苯（naphthacene）：发绿光（Green） Naphthacene: emits green

五苯（pentacene）：发红光（Red） Pentacene: emits red

4
录音内容要点补充 Key Points from Lecture Audio

老师特别强调，π→π跃迁因几率大、激发态寿命短，因此产生的荧光不仅强且高效，适合分析检测。 The instructor stressed that π→π transitions, due to higher transition probability and shorter excited state lifetime, lead to strong and efficient fluorescence ideal for analytical detection.

伴随π共轭体系的扩展，分子的HOMO-LUMO能隙缩小，发射波长红移，这是解释许多有机染料和荧光探针色彩变化的基础。 With the extension of π-conjugated systems, the HOMO-LUMO gap decreases, causing emission wavelength red-shift—this underpins the color tuning of many organic dyes and fluorescent probes.

含杂原子的芳环，多为n→π跃迁主导，因自旋禁阻及内转换等速率提升，导致无荧光或荧光极弱。 Aromatics with heteroatoms often have dominant n→π transitions, leading to spin-forbidden and fast non-radiative pathways, resulting in weak or absent fluorescence.

荧光团被广泛用于生物成像、示踪及环境分析等领域，正因其结构依赖性强且灵敏度高。 Fluorophores are widely used in bioimaging, tracing, and environmental analysis because of their strong structure dependence and high sensitivity.

荧光应用
定量分析与成像 D. Applications: quantitative analysis and fluorescence imaging

一、荧光定量分析的优缺点与吸收法对比 Comparison of Fluorescence Quantitative Analysis with Absorption Methods

相比吸收法，荧光法检测限更低（低10至1000倍）。 Compared to absorption, fluorescence has much lower detection limits (10 to 1000 times lower).

选择性更高，因为只有能发出荧光的分子才被检测到。 Selectivity is increased, since only molecules that emit fluorescence are detected.

应用范围有限，只能测定具备荧光特性的样品。 The applications are limited, as only fluorescent species can be analyzed.

二、激发和发射波长的选择 Selection of Excitation and Emission Wavelengths

可通过选择不同的激发波长（EX1、EX2、EX3），分别获得不同的发射波长（EM1、EM2、EM3）及其对应的强度。 By choosing different excitation wavelengths (EX1, EX2, EX3), various emission wavelengths (EM1, EM2, EM3) and their intensities can be recorded.

选择适当波长对提高灵敏度和特异性非常关键。 Selecting appropriate wavelengths is crucial for improving sensitivity and specificity.

荧光通常在激发光方向垂直方向检测，以减少激发光干扰。 Fluorescence is typically measured perpendicular to the excitation beam to reduce interference from the excitation light source.

三、荧光强度与浓度的关系 Relationship between Fluorescence Intensity and Concentration

当溶液吸光度极低（<0.05）时，荧光强度 IFIF​ 与浓度 CC 成正比： For low absorbance (<0.05), the fluorescence intensity IFIF​ is directly proportional to concentration CC:

当浓度继续升高，出现内滤效应，导致 IFIF​ 不再随 CC 线性增加，甚至下降。 As concentration increases further, the inner filter effect causes IFIF​ to deviate from linearity and eventually decrease.

因此，荧光法最适合稀溶液定量。 Fluorescence measurements are best suited for dilute solutions where linearity is preserved.

实验时常以标准曲线法定量分析，选择荧光强度与浓度呈线性关系的范围。 In practice, quantitation uses calibration curves within the linear range of fluorescence intensity versus concentration6.

四、荧光成像及细胞探针应用 Fluorescence Imaging and Cell Probe Applications

荧光成像结合了荧光光谱和光学显微镜，能实现复杂基质（如单细胞）中荧光团的成像。 Fluorescence imaging integrates fluorescence spectroscopy with optical microscopy for imaging fluorophores in complex matrices (such as single cells).

常用的细胞性探针包括
Typical cell imaging probes include:

膜和脂质探针 Probes for membranes and lipids

细胞器探针 Probes for organelles

钙、镁、锌离子指示剂 Indicators for Ca²⁺, Mg²⁺, Zn²⁺

pH指示剂 pH indicators

以SNARF-1为例，其用于pH成像，不同pH下呈现不同颜色，可分辨细胞酸化水平。 For example, SNARF-1 is used as a pH probe, showing different colors under different pH to distinguish cell acidification.

荧光图像实现了对细胞健康状态和代谢活动的实时、可视化监测。 Fluorescence imaging enables real-time visualization and monitoring of cellular status and metabolic activities6.

五、老师上课录音补充 Lecture Recording Key Points

老师强调
荧光法比吸收法灵敏度高，适用于复杂背景中的微量成分分析。 The lecturer emphasized that fluorescence has much higher sensitivity than absorption and is suitable for trace analysis in complex backgrounds.

只有一小部分分子能发射荧光，这种选择性提高了方法的特异性。 Only a small fraction of molecules emit fluorescence, but this selectivity improves analytical specificity.

由于广泛应用受限，非所有分子都能用荧光定量，但对于高要求微量/痕量分析具有明显优势。 Not all molecules are suitable for fluorescence quantitation, but for demanding trace analyses, fluorescence offers a clear advantage.

例如，生物样品中pH、离子浓度或某些活性分子的变化，可通过特定探针即时成像并定量。 For example, changes in pH, ion concentration, or specific bioactive molecules in biological samples can be imaged and quantified instantly with proper probes.

状态区域回顾（尤其针对高分子）Reminder on State Domains (Especially for Polymers)

玻璃态 ↔ 橡胶态 ↔ 熔融态 Vitreous (Glassy) ↔ Rubbery State ↔ Melted State

玻璃态特性
固态、刚性、大脆性、高弹模量 Glassy state: solid, stiff, brittle, high elastic modulus
橡胶态特性
有延展性（如橡胶）、适合无定形相、分子链为无规线团 Rubbery state: ductile (as rubber), amorphous phases are suitable, random coil

高分子状态变化的温度相关性 Temperature-Dependent State Transitions of Polymers

升温过程
玻璃态→软化→橡胶态→熔融 Heating Process: Vitreous (Glassy) → Softening → Rubbery State → Melting
降温过程
熔融态→结晶→橡胶态→玻璃化 Cooling Process: Melted State → Crystallization → Rubbery State → Vitrification (Glazing)

老师讲解补充内容 Teacher's Supplementary Explanation

用PVC作为例子，说明相同聚合物由于添加剂不同可表现出完全不同的物理性质（如硬的下水管和柔软的输血袋同为聚氯乙烯）。 Using PVC as an example, the teacher explained that the same polymer can show totally different physical properties (e.g., hard sewage pipes and soft blood bags) because of different additives.

塑料由聚合物和添加剂（如着色剂、防紫外、抗氧化剂、润滑剂等）组成，添加剂未与聚合物链结合，容易迁移，尤其在高温下会析出。 Plastics consist of polymers and additives (such as colorants, anti-UV, antioxidants, lubricants). These additives are not linked to the polymer chains and can migrate out, especially at high temperatures.

工业瓶建议低温储存，避免释放添加剂进入饮用水。 Industrial bottles are recommended to be stored at low temperature, to prevent additives from leaching into drinking water.

热分析原理
通过加热和冷却样品，观察高分子链的各种状态和转变，如玻璃化、软化、熔融、结晶与玻璃化等现象。 Principle of thermal analysis: Heating and cooling a sample to observe macromolecular chain states and transitions, such as glass transition, softening, melting, crystallization, and vitrification.
链的不同状态
无定形状态如熟透面条为无规线团，结晶状态则有远程有序排列。 Different chain states: In amorphous state, chains show a random coil (like overcooked spaghetti), whereas in crystalline state, chains are well ordered.

高分子有序性的回顾 Reminder on Orders in Macromolecules

1
高分子中两种有序状态 Two Ordered States in Macromolecules

无定形态 Amorphous state

晶态 Crystalline state

2
无定形态 Amorphous State

大距离上无有序结构 No long-range order.

分子链以无规线团构象存在 Molecular chains arranged as random coils.

缠结现象有无均可，缠结对性能有调节作用 Presence or absence of entanglement, which influences properties.

冷却时粘度上升 Viscosity increases upon cooling.

典型例子
PVC（聚氯乙烯）、PS（聚苯乙烯）、PMMA（聚甲基丙烯酸甲酯）、SAN（苯乙烯-丙烯腈共聚物） Typical examples: PVC (polyvinyl chloride), PS (polystyrene), PMMA (polymethyl methacrylate), SAN (styrene-acrylonitrile copolymer).
缠结示意图
无缠结与有缠结状态分别影响材料的柔韧性和力学性能 Diagram: compares without entanglement and with entanglements, affecting flexibility and mechanical properties.
3
晶态 Crystalline State

分子链有序排列 Molecular chains have a well-ordered arrangement.

链段在晶核中折叠形成致密结构 Compact design folded within crystal seeds.

呈现优先取向结构，如晶片层状排列 Preferential conformation as crystalline lamellae.

典型例子
PE（聚乙烯） Typical example: PE (polyethylene).

图示中分子链在排列整齐时形成晶态结构以及层状晶体 In illustrations, well-aligned chains form crystalline structures and lamellar crystals.

4
晶态结构的温度相关演变 Temperature-Dependent Evolution of Crystalline State
从熔融态到结晶态
From molten state to crystalline state:
熔融物质
高温下链条无序 Molten matter: at high temperature, disordered chains
结晶温度附近
链部分排列趋于有序 Near crystallization temperature: chains start to align in order
完全结晶
链条高度平行排列，形成结晶区域 Fully crystallized: chains highly aligned, form crystalline domains

温度降低时，粘度上升 Viscosity increases as temperature decreases

晶体形态的形成步骤
Process of crystalline morphology formation:

晶体成核与生长 Crystal nucleation and growth

层片、纤维、球晶等多级结构逐步出现 Formation of lamellae, fibers, spherulites, etc.

5
课堂讲解补充 Teacher’s Additional Notes

无定形态如熟透意面，链为无规缠结，无长程有序；晶态则有远程有序排列 Amorphous state is like overcooked spaghetti, chains randomly entangled and not ordered over long distance; crystalline state displays long-range order.

两态的比例影响材料的物理性能，如柔韧性与强度 Ratio between amorphous and crystalline phases impacts physical properties such as flexibility and strength.

热分析方法通过调节温度，可观察到无定形态向结晶态转变及其结构演化 By modifying temperature, thermal analysis techniques can reveal transition and evolution between amorphous and crystalline phases.

小结 Summary

高分子材料可呈现无定形和结晶两种极端有序状态，分别表现为无规缠结与高度有序排列，结构类型对材料性能起关键作用；冷却过程中粘度上升，结晶形成伴随有序结构的渐进建立。 Macromolecules show two extreme ordered states: amorphous (random entanglement) and crystalline (highly ordered arrangement). Structure type is crucial for material properties; viscosity increases upon cooling, and crystallization involves gradual setup of ordered structures.

高分子有序性的回顾——晶态结构与表面形貌分析 Reminder on Orders in Macromolecules—Crystalline State Structure and Surface Morphology Analysis

1
晶态结构的特点 Characteristics of the Crystalline State

高分子链长度远大于层状晶片（片层、lamellae）的厚度。 The length of macromolecular chains is much greater than the thickness of the crystalline lamellae.

晶态与无定形区域交替分布，形成多层结构。 Alternation of crystalline and amorphous areas generates multi-layered structures.

晶区之间常存在链的穿越、折迭，导致无定形连接区域产生。 Chains can cross from one crystallite to another and form loops through folding, resulting in inter-crystallite amorphous regions.

晶体区域的链折叠形式分为
I类（规则折叠）、II类（随机折叠）、III类（层状结构折叠）。 There are three folding modes of the crystalline areas: type I (regular folding), type II (random folding), and type III (lamellae folding).
相关符号
lc为晶片厚度，la为无定形区厚度，le为长周期长度。 Notations: lc denotes thickness of lamellae, la thickness of amorphous zones, le long period.
2
SEM表面形态分析及球晶结构 Surface Morphology by SEM and Spherulite Structure

电镜下放大视图（如×35000, ×95000）可清楚显示晶片层与晶区间无定形区。 SEM images at high magnification (such as ×35000, ×95000) clearly show crystalline lamellae and inter-crystallite amorphous areas.

球晶结构（spherulite）：多条辐射状晶片自异质成核点出发呈球状生长。 Spherulite structure: radiating crystalline lamellae grow spherically from heterogeneous nucleation sites.

晶区与无定形区交错分布是高分子晶态的基本特征。 Alternating arrangement of crystalline and amorphous domains is fundamental in polymer crystallinity.

有时，晶片内部还存在小范围的无定形区域（intra-crystallite amorphous area）。 Occasionally, intra-crystallite amorphous zones also exist within lamellae.

PLM（偏光显微镜）下可观察到典型PE球晶的多彩图像。 Polarised Light Microscopy (PLM) reveals characteristic colorful images of PE spherulites.

3
教师补充与讲解要点 Supplementary Explanation from the Instructor

晶态高分子材料内部，不同区域存在晶态和无定形态交错分布，晶片为规整堆叠，链段可以穿越不同晶区或形成环状结构。 Within crystalline polymers, crystalline and amorphous areas are intermixed; lamellae stack regularly, with chains crossing between crystallites or forming looped folds.

晶态常以球晶为基本结构，由中心成核点向外生长呈辐射状排列，类似“头发”形态。 Spherulites serve as the basic unit, growing radially from a nucleation center, resembling “hair-like” morphology.

通过扫描电镜和偏光显微镜可观测到这些微观结构特征。 These microstructures can be observed using SEM and PLM techniques.

高分子难以达到百分之百的结晶度，通常最大结晶度为85%至90%，实际材料总包含一定比例的无定形区。 Polymers rarely reach 100% crystallinity; typically, a maximum of 85%–90% crystallinity is found, with always some amorphous portion remaining.

4
小结 Summary

晶态高分子结构表现为链折叠、晶片排列与无定形区交错分布。 Crystalline polymers display folded chains, lamellar arrangement, and alternating amorphous regions.

球晶是最主要的结晶微观形态，异质成核点为核心，晶片辐射生长。 Spherulites are the principal crystalline morphology, growing outward from heterogeneous nucleation centers.

晶态与无定形区比例决定材料物理性能，电镜和光学方法助于深入理解与观察这一微观结构特征。 The crystalline/amorphous ratio defines physical properties, and SEM/PLM facilitate the investigation and visualization of these microstructural features.

不同热分析技术Different Thermal Analytical Techniques

1
课程大纲与热分析简介 Course Outline and Introduction to Thermal Analysis

本章介绍一组主要用于药物与高分子材料性质分析的热分析技术。 This section introduces the main thermal analytical techniques for characterizing properties of drugs and macromolecular materials.

热分析指的是在规定气氛下，通过控制样品温度的程序，监测其某一性质随时间或温度的变化。 Thermal analysis refers to techniques that monitor a property of the sample against time or temperature, as the sample is subjected to a programmed temperature in a specific atmosphere.

2
热分析技术的主要分类 Main Classes of Thermal Analytical Techniques

量热分析 Calorimetric Analysis

常见方法包括DTA（差热分析）、DSC（差示扫描量热法），用于测量温度和吸收/释放的热流。 Main methods include DTA (Differential Thermal Analysis) and DSC (Differential Scanning Calorimetry), measuring temperature and absorbed/desorbed heat flow.

这类分析特别适用于研究材料在加热、冷却过程中的物理化学转变（如熔融、结晶、玻璃化转变等）。 These analyses are especially suitable for studying physical and chemical transitions during heating/cooling, such as melting, crystallization, glass transitions, etc.

热重分析 Thermogravimetric Analysis (TGA)

主要用于监测加热或冷却过程中样品质量的变化。 Mainly used to monitor mass loss of the sample during heating or cooling.

TGA常用于分析分解、挥发性组分损失和热稳定性。 TGA is often used to study decomposition, loss of volatile content, and thermal stability.

3
常用扩展与其他热分析方法 Common Extensions and Other Thermal Analytical Methods

机械热分析 Thermomechanical Analysis (TMA, DMA)

TMA和DMA分别用于测量材料尺寸变化和刚度等力学性质。 TMA and DMA measure dimensional changes and mechanical stiffness of materials.

其他辅助热分析技术 Other Supporting Techniques

TSC（热刺激电流）：分析偶极分子 TSC (Thermally Stimulated Current): analyzes dipolar molecules

DEA（介电分析）：分析介电常数 DEA (Dielectric Analysis): studies dielectric permittivity

DOA（光学分析）：用于材料的光学性质分析 DOA (Dielectric Optical Analysis): for analysis of optical properties

4
相关标准与实际应用 Standards and Practical Applications

DSC作为欧洲药典中的参考方法，用于药品成分的鉴别和材料热特性分析。 DSC is referenced in the European Pharmacopeia for identifying pharmaceutical compounds and analyzing thermal properties.

实验设计时，常根据材料特性与研究目标选择合适的分析方法。 Selection of appropriate technique depends on material properties and study objectives.

5
课堂讲解补充 Teacher’s Supplementary Points

热分析包括量热型（如DSC，检测吸收/释放的热量）和重量型（如TGA，检测加热过程中的失重）。 Thermal analyses include calorimetric type (e.g., DSC, measuring absorbed/released heat) and gravimetric type (e.g., TGA, tracking weight loss during heating) 1.

药品、聚合物等材料常见的热分析应用包括相变研究、成分分析、热稳定性和物理结构变化等。 Typical applications for pharmaceuticals and polymers include phase transition study, component analysis, thermal stability, and monitoring of physical structure changes 1.

DSC、TGA操作程序主要涉及加热速率的设定、温度/热流与样品性质的同步监测，并结合相关标准数据作判断。 DSC and TGA procedures involve programming the heating rate, synchronously monitoring sample temperature/heat flow or mass, and comparing to reference data for interpretation 1.

6
小结 Summary

热分析技术通过测量样品在加热或冷却过程中热量变化或质量变化，评价其物理、化学和结构特性，是药物和材料科学中的重要表征工具。 By measuring heat or mass changes during programmed heating/cooling, thermal analytical techniques assess the physical, chemical, and structural features of samples—an essential toolbox in pharmaceutical and material sciences.

热量分析技术Calorimetric Techniques

1
课程大纲与技术简介 Course Outline & Technique Introduction
本节内容涵盖
态域回顾、高分子有序性、不同热分析技术、重点介绍热量分析（包括一阶转变、二阶转变、DSC设备与应用），以及热重分析（设备、校准、实例、联用技术）。 This part covers: state domains, orders in macromolecules, different thermal analytical techniques, a focus on calorimetric analysis (including first-order and second-order transitions, DSC equipment and uses), and gravimetric techniques (equipment, calibration, examples, coupled methods).
2
热量分析技术原理 Principle of Calorimetry

温度对材料状态变化、高分子重组与降解等产生显著影响。 Temperature strongly influences material state change, macromolecular reorganization, and degradation.

热量分析（CALORIMETRY）是指测量样品吸收（吸热现象）或释放（放热现象）热流的过程。 Calorimetry refers to measuring the flow of heat absorbed (endothermic) or released (exothermic) by the sample.

例如
加热时的吸热熔融，冷却时的放热结晶。 Examples: heat absorbed during melting (upon heating), heat released during crystallization (upon cooling).
3
热流与常见热过程 Heat Flow and Common Thermal Processes

热流随着温度变化而变化，不同阶段表现出不同特征。 Heat flow changes with temperature, different stages present unique features.

图示典型过程
玻璃化转变（Glass transition）、结晶（Crystallization）、熔融（Melting）、交联（Crosslinking）、氧化或降解（Oxidation/Degradation）。 Diagram shows stages: glass transition, crystallization, melting, crosslinking, and oxidation/degradation.

吸热过程（ΔH>0、向上为endothermic），放热过程（ΔH<0、向上为exothermic）。 Endothermic process (ΔH>0, upwards for endothermic), exothermic process (ΔH<0, upwards for exothermic).

4
热流和热容关系 Heat Flow and Heating Capacity
基本公式
ΔH = Cp × ΔT Basic formula: ΔH = Cp × ΔT
或者 用时间推导形式
d(ΔH)/dt = Cp × d(ΔT)/dt Or, in time-differential form: d(ΔH)/dt = Cp × d(ΔT)/dt

其中，Cp为热容（单位J/°C），T为温度（°C），H为热量（J），d(ΔH)/dt为热流（J/min）。 Where Cp is heat capacity (J/°C), T is temperature (°C), H is heat (J), d(ΔH)/dt is heat flow (J/min).

5
一阶与二阶热转变特征 First-order and Second-order Transitions
一阶转变
表现在热流曲线上为峰型（吸热或放热），对应焓变，其值与峰下面积成正比。例如熔融、结晶等。 First-order transition: peak in heat flow curve (endo- or exothermic), enthalpy change is proportional to area under the peak, e.g., melting, crystallization.
二阶转变
表现在热流曲线上为基线跳变，无明显焓变，但出现Cp（热容）变化，如玻璃化转变（Tg）、氧化等。 Second-order transition: step change in baseline (not a peak), no enthalpy change but heat capacity Cp changes, e.g., glass transition, oxidation.
6
课堂补充讲解 Teacher’s Additional Explanation

老师强调，加热或冷却程序可用来研究材料链的重组、状态变化甚至降解行为，我们实际测量的是样品吸放热流的变化。 The teacher emphasized that heating or cooling can be used to study chain reorganization, state change, or degradation; in practice, we measure changes in heat flow absorbed or released by the sample.

DSC是欧洲药典指定的参考方法，广泛用于药品和材料热特性分析。 DSC (Differential Scanning Calorimetry) is the pharmacopeia-referenced method widely utilized for pharmaceutical and materials thermal properties analysis.

7
小结 Summary

热分析尤其是热量分析，是材料科学和药物领域研究状态变化、转变过程和热特性的重要手段。 Thermal analysis, especially calorimetric techniques, are critical tools in materials science and pharmaceuticals for studying state transitions, processes, and thermal characteristics.

理解热流与温度、热容之间的关系，有助于分析各种物理化学转变。 Understanding the relation between heat flow, temperature, and heat capacity helps interpret various physical and chemical transitions.

热量分析技术中的一级转变Calorimetric Techniques—First-order Transitions

1
一级转变基本概念 Basic Concepts of First-order Transitions

一级转变指的是结晶态与无定形态（或液态）之间的可逆变化，如熔融和结晶。 First-order transitions refer to the reversible change between crystalline and amorphous (or liquid) states, such as melting and crystallization.

在DSC曲线上，这些转变表现为明显的峰形，代表热焓（ΔH）的变化。 On a DSC curve, these transitions appear as distinct peaks representing the enthalpy change (ΔH)2.

2
聚合物的两种区域与结构示意 Two Microstructural Domains in Polymers

高分子材料中同时包含有序的结晶区和无序的无定形区。 Polymer materials contain both ordered crystalline domains and disordered amorphous domains.

结晶区域链段规整堆积形成有序结构，无定形区域链段无序缠绕。 Crystalline areas are formed by regular stacking of chains, while amorphous areas consist of randomly entangled chains.

3
熔融与结晶的热行为 Thermal Behavior of Melting and Crystallization
熔融
结晶固态（有序）加热转变为液态（无序，熔融高分子）。 Melting: Upon heating, the ordered crystalline solid transforms into a disordered molten polymer state.
结晶
降温时，熔体链段自发有序排列形成结晶结构，是熔融的逆过程。 Crystallization: Upon cooling, melt chains spontaneously arrange into ordered crystalline domains—this is the reverse of melting.

结晶过程高度依赖降温速率，冷却过快可能导致结晶不完全。 The crystallization process strongly depends on the cooling rate; if cooled too quickly, crystallization may be incomplete2.

4
DSC图谱的解读 Interpretation of DSC Curves

熔融和结晶分别表现为吸热峰（向下/ΔH>0）和放热峰（向上/ΔH<0）。 Melting shows as an endothermic peak (downward; ΔH>0) while crystallization appears as an exothermic peak (upward; ΔH<0).

峰面积正比于对应的焓变，表示吸收或释放的热量。 The area under the peak is proportional to the enthalpy change, revealing the amount of absorbed or released heat.

有时还能观察到“冷结晶”现象，即在加热过程中发生的结晶。 Sometimes “cold crystallization” may be detected, which is crystallization occurring during heating2.

DSC熔融案例
聚乙烯与金属铟 Melting Case Study: Polyethylene and Indium (Pure Metal)

DSC曲线上，聚乙烯和金属铟的熔融过程分别为宽峰和尖锐窄峰。 On the DSC curve, polyethylene melting is a broad peak while indium (pure metal) melting is a sharp, narrow peak.

对纯金属而言，所有晶粒尺寸统一，熔融温度极为精确且窄；高分子材料因链长分布和结构不均匀，熔融区间较宽。 For pure metals, all grain sizes are uniform, so melting occurs at a precise, narrow temperature; for polymers, chain length distribution and structural heterogeneity lead to a broad melting range.

曲线主要参数包括起始温度To、峰值温度Tp、终止温度Te、以及熔融温度Tm，焓变以每克焦耳（J/g）计。 Curve key points: onset temperature (To), peak temperature (Tp), end temperature (Te), melting temperature (Tm), and enthalpy change in joules per gram (J/g).

6
教师重要补充与课堂说明 Key Teacher’s Additions and Explanations

聚合物熔融峰宽主要源自分子量分布广泛，不同长度的链依次熔化。 The broad melting peak in polymers is mainly due to wide molecular weight distribution and sequential melting of differently sized chains3.

通过示例对比聚乙烯与纯金属的DSC曲线，进一步理解有序度与分布对熔融行为的影响。 Comparing DSC curves of polyethylene and pure metal deepens the understanding of how order and distribution affect melting behavior2.

冷结晶现象在加热速率较高或先前未充分结晶样品中常见。 Cold crystallization is often observed in samples previously lacking full crystallinity or when heating rates are high2.

7
小结 Summary

一级热转变在高分子科学和材料分析中极为重要，可用于研究结构有序性、分子分布及其物理性能。 First-order thermal transitions are essential in polymer science and material analysis, valuable for investigating structural order, molecular distribution, and related physical properties.

DSC（差示扫描量热法）提供关键参数助于高分子的定量与定性分析。 DSC (Differential Scanning Calorimetry) provides critical parameters for both qualitative and quantitative analysis of polymers.

热量分析技术中的结晶与熔融以及结晶度分析Calorimetric Techniques: Crystallization, Melting, and Crystallinity Analysis

• PE-HD结晶分析与DSC曲线解读Analysis of PE-HD Crystallization and DSC Curve Interpretation

该图展示了高密度聚乙烯（PE-HD）结晶过程的DSC曲线。 This figure shows the DSC curve of the crystallization process for high-density polyethylene (PE-HD).

纵坐标为热流（Heat Flow），横坐标为温度（Temperature）。 The vertical axis is heat flow, and the horizontal axis is temperature.

结晶行为分为两个阶段
①结晶萌发（crystalline germination）；②晶体生长（growth）。 The crystallization process is divided into two stages: ① crystalline germination; ② growth.
主要参数
Tc为结晶温度（crystallization temperature）；Tn为成核温度（nucleation temperature）；Te为结束温度（end temperature）。 Key parameters: Tc = crystallization temperature; Tn = nucleation temperature; Te = end temperature.

在Tc = 152.62℃时，出现最大放热峰，表示结晶速率最快。 At Tc = 152.62℃, the largest exothermic peak appears, indicating the fastest crystallization rate.

曲线左侧起点（Te），是结晶过程开始温度；右阈值（Tn），是成核完成并结晶过程终止温度。 The curve’s left starting point (Te) is the beginning of crystallization; the right threshold (Tn) marks the end of nucleation and termination of crystallization.

• 结晶度的热分析法及应用Application of Thermal Analysis for Crystallinity

结晶度（crystallinity percentage）可用于评价高分子材料中有序相的比例，是衡量材料性能与结构的重要参数。 Crystallinity percentage assesses the proportion of ordered phase in polymers and is crucial for evaluating material properties and structure.

注意
样品需为纯聚合物（无共聚物或添加剂）；需已知100%结晶样品的熔融焓（ΔHlit.ΔHlit.​）。 Note: The sample must be pure polymer (no copolymers or additives). The melting enthalpy of a 100% crystalline sample (ΔHlit.ΔHlit.​) must be known.
结晶度计算公式
Crystallinity calculation formula: % crystallinity = 100 × ΔHfΔHf​ / ΔHlit.ΔHlit.​
示例
若PE的ΔHfΔHf​ = 191.7 J/g，ΔHlitΔHlit​ = 290 J/g，则结晶度为66%。 Example: For PE, if ΔHf=191.7ΔHf​=191.7 J/g and ΔHlit=290ΔHlit​=290 J/g, then crystallinity is 66%.

• 冷结晶计算及注意Cold Crystallization Calculation and Notes

若存在冷结晶，需要从熔融焓(ΔHfΔHf​)中减去冷结晶焓(ΔHcΔHc​)： If cold crystallization is present, subtract the cold crystallization enthalpy (ΔHcΔHc​) from the melting enthalpy: % crystallinity = 100 × (ΔHfΔHf​ - ΔHcΔHc​)/ΔHlit.ΔHlit.​

这样可精确反映真实结晶度，避免高估结果。 This accurately reflects true crystallinity and avoids overestimation.

• 聚乙烯与金属铟熔融对比Comparing Melting of Polyethylene and Indium

DSC熔融曲线展示了高分子材料和金属的熔融行为差异。 The melting DSC curves demonstrate the difference between polymeric materials and metals.

对聚乙烯，熔融区宽广，熔融起始温度To = 126.96°C，峰值温度Tp = 131.12°C，结束温度Te，熔融焓191.7 J/g。 In polyethylene, the melting range is broad, onset temperature To = 126.96°C, peak Tp = 131.12°C, end Te, enthalpy 191.7 J/g.

对纯金属（如铟），熔融峰极窄，To = Tp = Te = Tm = 157.85°C，焓变仅28.46 J/g。 For pure metals (e.g., indium), the peak is very narrow, To = Tp = Te = Tm = 157.85°C, with enthalpy only 28.46 J/g.

纯金属因粒径均一，熔融温度非常精确而窄；高分子因链长和分布不均造成区间宽广。 Pure metals have narrow melting due to uniform grains, while polymers show broad range due to chain length and heterogeneity.

• 冷结晶现象在DSC中的表现DSC Manifestations of Cold Crystallization

冷却不充分或加热速率过快，部分高分子可能在升温过程中发生冷结晶，呈现为先吸热再明显放热峰。 Inadequate cooling or rapid heating may cause cold crystallization in polymers during heating, seen as an initial endothermic shoulder followed by a strong exothermic peak on the DSC curve.

白板曲线显示不同程序（如“冷结晶—熔融”、“只熔融”等），温度和升降速率会影响冷结晶及熔融峰的出现。 Whiteboard diagrams show different programs (like “cold crystallization—melting,” “only melting”), where temperature range and ramp rates affect appearance of cold crystallization and melting peaks.

• PET实例分析与结晶度核算PET Example: Analysis and Crystallinity Calculation

DSC曲线
173°C出现30.1 J/g放热峰（冷结晶），257°C出现33.0 J/g吸热峰（熔融）。 DSC curve: Exothermic peak (cold crystallization) of 30.1 J/g at 173°C; endothermic peak (melting) of 33.0 J/g at 257°C.

已知100%结晶PET的焓ΔHlit.=140.1ΔHlit.​=140.1 J/g。 The literature value for 100% crystalline PET is ΔHlit.=140.1ΔHlit.​=140.1 J/g.

正确结晶度公式（冷结晶修正）：% crystallinity = 100×(33.0−30.1)/140.1=2%100×(33.0−30.1)/140.1=2%。 Correct formula with cold crystallization: % crystallinity =100×(33.0−30.1)/140.1=2%=100×(33.0−30.1)/140.1=2%.

若未修正，错误计算为100×33.0/140.1=23%100×33.0/140.1=23%，会高估结晶度。 Without correction, the erroneous calculation 100×33.0/140.1=23%100×33.0/140.1=23% would overestimate crystallinity.

白板和PPT均强调，要结合冷结晶放热量与样本真实物理信息，准确计算高分子的结晶度。 Both the whiteboard and PPT stress that the exothermic cold crystallization must be considered for accurately calculating polymer crystallinity.

• 教师课堂强调与补充Teacher’s Emphasis and Supplement

实验证明，只有纯聚合物样品且无明显添加剂时，结晶度计算才准确。 Experiments show that accurate crystallinity calculation only applies to pure polymer samples without significant additives.

冻结（淬火）处理的PET，初始结晶度极低，需用校正后公式计算。 For quenched PET, initial crystallinity is extremely low and must be calculated using the corrected formula.

理论与实际对比，DSC是判别结晶度、研究热历史、了解高分子结构性质的重要手段。 Theory and practice comparison: DSC is key for judging crystallinity, studying thermal history, and understanding polymer structure-property relationships.

一阶转变的应用实例——多晶型研究Examples of Application for First-order Transition — Polymorphism Study

多晶型定义 Polymorphism Definition

多晶型是指固体材料能够以两种或两种以上不同的晶体形式存在，这些晶体形式在晶格内具有不同的排列或构象。 Polymorphism refers to the ability of solid materials to exist in two or more different crystalline forms, which have distinct arrangements or conformations of the constituents in the crystal lattice.

多晶型对性质的影响 Effects of Polymorphism

影响物理化学性质，如溶解度、溶解速率、化学与物理稳定性、流动性及吸湿性。 It affects physicochemical properties such as dissolution, solubility, chemical/physical stability, flowability, and hygroscopicity.

影响药物的疗效、生物利用度，甚至毒性。 It affects drug efficacy, bioavailability, and even toxicity.

特定多晶型对某一特定性质起决定作用，这些性质可能其他晶型不具备。 A specific polymorph can be responsible for a particular property that may not be exhibited by other forms.

假多晶型的概念 Pseudo-polymorphism

若水分子被困在晶体结构中，则形成假多晶型。 Pseudo-polymorphism occurs when water molecules are trapped within the crystalline structure.

多晶型研究意义 Significance of Polymorphic Studies

多晶型分析在药物等固体材料领域至关重要，有助于筛选最佳晶型以确保性能与安全。 Polymorphic analysis is crucial in pharmaceuticals and other solid materials fields, aiding in selecting the optimal crystal form for performance and safety.

案例1
多晶型对药物性能的影响 Example 1: Impact of Polymorphism on Drug Performance

以氨苄青霉素为例，无水型（Anhydrous form）与三水合型（Tri-hydrate form）表现出不同的溶解速率与生物利用度。 Taking ampicillin as an example, the anhydrous and trihydrate forms exhibit different dissolution rates and bioavailability.

G-T图
无水型与三水合型的溶解对数随1/T变化显示溶解速率不同。 G-T diagram: The log of dissolution as a function of 1/T differs between forms.

尿排泄速率曲线显示两种形态吸收与排泄存在差异。 Urinary excretion rate curves demonstrate differences in absorption and excretion between the two forms.

结论：药物的生物利用度随所用晶型的不同而变化。 Conclusion: The drug’s bioavailability changes depending on the crystalline form used.

案例2
MHBA多晶型 Example 2: MHBA Polymorphism

对间羟基苯甲酸（MHBA）存在两种多晶型，分别为单斜晶型（单斜型，较稳定）和斜方晶型（正交型）。 There are two polymorphs of m-hydroxybenzoic acid (MHBA): monoclinic (more stable) and orthorhombic forms.

DSC热曲线表现为不同的熔融峰，反映了不同晶型的热行为。 DSC thermograms show distinct melting peaks, reflecting different thermal behaviors of each polymorph.

显微镜下，两种晶型的形貌与排列明显不同。 Under microscopy, the morphology and arrangement of the two crystal forms are clearly different.

一阶转变的小结 Summary on 1st Order Transition

熔融是一步现象，结晶分为萌发和生长两步。 Melting is a one-step phenomenon; crystallization involves two steps: germination and growth.

熔融焓可用于结晶度的测定。 Melting enthalpy can be used for crystallinity determination.

任何促进分子有序化的过程均会提高熔融温度。 Any process that increases molecular organization raises the melting temperature.

多晶型可通过DSC等量热法高亮和分析。 The phenomenon of polymorphism can be highlighted and analyzed by DSC and related calorimetric methods.

课堂补充与强调 Teacher's Supplement and Emphasis

多晶型直接影响药品品质及工业材料性能，DSC等方法能够定量区分及分析不同晶型。 Polymorphism directly influences pharmaceutical quality and industrial material performance; methods like DSC can quantitatively distinguish and analyze crystal forms.

水合态或溶剂夹杂态的“假多晶型”在药物与材料开发中需特殊关注。 Hydrated or solvent-included “pseudo-polymorphs” require special attention during drug and material development.

选择合适晶型对药品效果、安全性及专利保护具有重要意义，工业流程需严格控制材料的多晶型状态。 Choosing the right polymorph is vital for drug efficacy, safety, and patent protection; industrial processes must tightly control the polymorphic state of materials.

热分析技术中的二阶转变及其影响Calorimetric Techniques: Second-order Transition and Its Effects

1
课程大纲与二阶转变主题 Course Outline and Second-order Transition Focus

本部分属于热分析技术小节，重点讲解二阶转变（Second-order transition），包括玻璃化转变（glass transition）及其影响因素。 This section belongs to calorimetric techniques, focusing on the second-order transition, including glass transition and its influencing factors.

玻璃化转变是高分子材料结构与性能分析的关键指标。 Glass transition is a key parameter for analyzing the structure and properties of polymers.

2
二阶转变定义与DSC图谱特征 Definition of Second-order Transition and DSC Characteristics

二阶转变包括玻璃转变、交联反应及氧化/降解等现象，通常表现为DSC（差示扫描量热法）基线的“阶梯形跃变”，而非明显峰形。 Second-order transitions include glass transition, crosslinking, oxidation/degradation, etc., and typically appear as a “staircase jump” rather than distinct peaks on DSC curves.

二阶转变不会伴随系统总焓的显著变化，而体现为热容量（Cp）的突变，相关公式：Glass transition: ΔH≈0,but ΔCp≠0Glass transition: ΔH≈0,but ΔCp​=0Second-order transitions do not involve significant total enthalpy change, but present as a sudden change in heat capacity (Cp).

3
玻璃转变的物理意义与本质 Physical Meaning and Essence of Glass Transition

玻璃转变是高分子无定形区在加热时由硬脆玻璃态转变为粘性或橡胶态的可逆物理变化。 Glass transition is the reversible physical change of the amorphous phase of a polymer from a hard, brittle, glassy state to a viscous or rubbery state upon heating.

冷却时对应的过程相反，可由柔软橡胶态重新变为硬脆玻璃态。 During cooling, the process reverses, returning from the soft rubbery state to the brittle glassy state.

DSC热流-温度曲线上表现为基线突变（阶梯形变）。 This is displayed as a sudden step change in the DSC heat flow vs. temperature curve.

4
玻璃转变温度Tg的测定与影响因素 Determination and Influencing Factors of Glass Transition Temperature (Tg)

在DSC图谱中，Tg可由多个特征点表示：起点Tg（onset）、Cp跳跃中点Tg（at half Cp jump）、拐点Tg（inflection point）；这些Tg测定值具有一定范围。 In DSC curves, Tg can be represented by several characteristic points: onset Tg, Tg at half Cp jump, or inflection point Tg. Tg values vary by definition.

以PET为例，Tg（onset）=71°C，Tg（at half Cp jump）=76°C，Tg（拐点）=80°C。 For PET, Tg (onset)=71°C, Tg (at half Cp jump)=76°C, and Tg (inflection point)=80°C.

Tg容易受以下因素影响
Tg is affected by:

扫描速率（Scanning rate）

升温与降温历史（Heating and cooling history）

老化（Ageing）

增塑剂（Plasticizers）

杂质或填料含量（Charges）

结晶度（Crystallinity）

分子量（Molecular mass）

共聚参数（Copolymers）

氢键作用（Hydrogen bonds）

5
分子量和增塑剂对Tg的影响 Effects of Molecular Mass and Plasticizers on Tg

分子量越高，Tg越高，因为大分子链运动受限，结构更难松动。 Higher molecular mass leads to higher Tg since chain mobility becomes more restricted.

实例表明
104 g/mol时Tg为-138°C，170,000 g/mol时Tg为100°C。 For example: at 104 g/mol, Tg=-138°C; at 170,000 g/mol, Tg=100°C.

含量越高的增塑剂，Tg越低，因为增塑剂能分散链段，提升链的活动性。 More plasticizer reduces Tg, as plasticizers spread out chains and increase mobility.

聚酰胺水分含量从0.35%增至10.33%，Tg由94°C降至6°C。 For polyamide: moisture from 0.35% to 10.33% makes Tg drop from 94°C to 6°C.

6
教师重要讲解与补充 Key Teacher’s Explanations and Supplement

玻璃转变本质是高分子从无序缠结的“煮熟意面”状态（高黏度、无定形）向分子链局部运动自由的“橡胶态”转化。 The glass transition essentially is the change from a “cooked spaghetti” state (tangled, amorphous, high viscosity) to a rubbery state with locally mobile chain segments.

不同聚合物的Tg相差极大，依分子结构、组分和处理历史而变化，应精确标明测试条件。 Different polymers have widely varying Tg values, depending on structure, composition, and process history, so testing conditions must be clearly stated.

在实际应用中，塑料制品的工作性能、多用途领域与Tg密切相关，决定了其热稳定性、刚性、柔韧性等关键参数。 In practical applications, the working performance of plastics and their usability are closely related to Tg, determining key properties like thermal stability, rigidity, and flexibility.

7
小结与应用意义 Summary and Practical Significance

二阶转变（主要指玻璃化转变）是高分子热——力学特性与应用性能的核心指标。 Second-order transitions (mainly glass transition) are central to the thermomechanical properties and application performance of polymers.

DSC等热分析法可定量测定Tg及其影响因素，是高分子和材料科学的重要研究工具。 Thermal analysis techniques such as DSC enable quantitative measurement of Tg and its influencing factors, making them essential research tools in polymer and material science.

二、量热分析技术设备示例 Examples of Calorimetric Technique Instruments

常见DSC（差示扫描量热仪）包括PerkinElmer、Netzsch、TA Instruments等不同品牌与型号。 Typical DSCs (Differential Scanning Calorimeters) include various brands such as PerkinElmer, Netzsch, TA Instruments, and others.

设备为密闭腔体，常配有自动进样及温控系统，实现样品与参考对比测量。 Instruments usually feature a closed chamber with automatic sample loading and temperature control for comparative measurements between sample and reference.

实际分析时，可根据研究需求选择不同灵敏度与功能的DSC设备。 The choice of DSC device can be tailored to sensitivity and functionality needs of specific research.

三、DSC装置的结构与原理 Structure and Working Principle of DSC Device

DSC核心结构包括样品室、样品杯与参考杯、加热块及热电偶线圈（如铜铬-铝镍线）。 The main structure of DSC includes a sample chamber, sample capsule, reference capsule, heating block, and thermocouple wires (e.g. chromel-alumel).

测量时样品与参考物置于各自的样品杯，通过同步加热并比较二者温度差异测定热流。 During measurement, the sample and reference are placed in their respective capsules and synchronously heated; heat flow is determined by measuring the differential temperature between them.

通常采用惰性气体扫描环境，防止样品氧化并提升信号稳定性。 Inert gas scanning is often used to prevent oxidation and stabilize the signal.

仪器自动记录“样品温度—参考温度”差，即为“热流”，反映样品热事件。 Instruments automatically record the sample temperature minus the reference temperature, which yields the heat flow—this reveals thermal events in the sample.

四、DSC经典模式与调制模式 Compared: “Conventional” and Modulated DSC

传统DSC加热程序为线性模式
The conventional DSC uses a linear heating profile:

其中 bb 为平均升温速率。 Here, bb denotes the average heating rate.

调制DSC（Modulated DSC）技术则在基础线性升温上叠加正弦信号，便于区分重叠热事件与解析复杂热效应： Modulated DSC superimposes a sinusoidal variation on top of the basic linear temperature ramp, enhancing the ability to resolve overlapping or complex thermal events:

其中 AA 为调制幅度，ωω 为角频率，PP 为调制周期。 Here, AA is modulation amplitude; ωω is the angular frequency; PP is the modulation period.

调制DSC适用于分辨近似或重叠的转变温度，分析多阶段复杂热行为样品。 Modulated DSC is suitable for identifying closely situated or overlapping transition temperatures and analyzing samples with multiple complex thermal behaviors.

五、课堂和录音要点补充 Teacher’s Key Comments and Audio Highlights

DSC是热分析领域中最常用的量热分析技术，可实现对材料吸热与放热过程的高灵敏监测。 DSC is a widely used technique in thermal analysis, providing highly sensitive monitoring of endothermic and exothermic processes in materials1.

不同设备和扫描模式应根据实验目的（如相变、纯度分析、结晶度判定等）灵活选择。 Different devices and scanning modes should be flexibly chosen according to experimental aims (e.g., phase transitions, purity analysis, crystallinity assessment)1.

课堂特别指出，热分析实验条件（升温速率、大气环境、样品质量等）直接影响数据解读，要在记录时注明。 The experimental conditions (heating rate, atmospheric environment, sample mass, etc.) directly influence DSC data and should be clearly stated in records1.

六、小结与实际意义 Summary and Practical Significance

量热分析（特别是DSC）在探究药品、聚合物及健康产品热稳定性、物理结构以及工艺优化等领域具有核心作用。 Calorimetric analysis, especially DSC, plays an essential role in studying thermal stability, physical structure, and process optimization for pharmaceuticals, polymers, and health products1.

随着技术进步，调制DSC等高级模式在复杂材料的定量和定性表征中愈发重要。 With technological advances, modulated DSC and other advanced modes are increasingly vital for detailed characterization of complex materials

PET/ABS常规与调制DSC分析及DSC校准与样品制备PET/ABS Analysis by Conventional and Modulated DSC, DSC Calibration and Sample Preparation

一、常规DSC热流分析与双组分识别Conventional DSC Heat Flow Analysis and Binary Component Identification

图谱为PET/ABS样品的DSC首次升温扫描，横轴为温度，纵轴为热流（W/g，吸热向下，放热向上）。 The figure shows a conventional DSC first heating scan for PET/ABS, temperature on the x-axis, heat flow (W/g, endo down, exo up) on the y-axis.

67.38°C和70.262°C标记为玻璃转变温度(Tg)特征点，体现两个相的Tg，分别归属于PET和ABS。 The points at 67.38°C and 70.262°C indicate glass transitions (Tg), reflecting two distinct phases—PET and ABS.

120.92°C出现吸热峰，为冷结晶行为，表明加热过程中新结晶形成、吸收能量。 At 120.92°C, an endothermic peak appears corresponding to cold crystallization (formation of new crystallites during heating, absorbing energy).

235.36°C为熔融吸热峰，面积标记为22.63 J/g，代表结晶部分熔融的能量。 The 235.36°C peak is the melting endotherm with an area of 22.63 J/g—this is the energy absorbed for melting crystallites.

常规DSC测得的是所有热事件的总和，无法区分不同热效应的重叠。 Conventional DSC measures the sum of all heat flows; it does not separate overlapping thermal events.

二、重复升温——结晶度与热历史影响Second Heating—Effect of Crystallinity and Thermal History

冷却后以同样速率（10°C/min）再次升温，第二扫描图谱展出。 After rapid cooling (10°C/min), the second heating scan is performed, also shown in the figure.

再次升温时，111.82°C出现一个强烈吸热峰，相比第一次大幅向低温移动，吸热量（9.016 J/g）显著减小。 On the second heating, an endothermic peak appears at 111.82°C, which is shifted lower and much smaller in area (9.016 J/g) compared with the first scan.

249.75°C出现新的熔融峰（比第一次偏高），表明冷却过程中结晶状态改变、结晶度可能增加。 A melting peak at 249.75°C (higher than before) appears, indicating altered crystallinity due to the different cooling conditions; crystallinity may have increased.

两次升温对比体现热处理历史（如熔融、冷却速率）对聚合物结晶行为与热性质的显著影响。 Comparison of the two heating scans shows how thermal history (e.g. melting, cooling rate) strongly influences polymer crystallization and thermal properties.

三、调制DSC——分离可逆不可逆热事件Modulated DSC—Separation of Reversible & Non-Reversible Events

调制DSC可把总热流分解为可逆（蓝色，主要是玻璃转变相关，如Tg）和不可逆（红色，主要是结晶、熔融等）热流。 Modulated DSC separates total heat flow into reversible (blue, e.g. glass transition) and non-reversible (red, e.g. crystallization, melting) heat flows.

实测PET Tg为67.00°C，ABS Tg为104.45°C，及相关峰值、拐点温度。 Measured PET Tg is 67.00°C, ABS Tg is 104.45°C, with corresponding peak and inflection temperatures indicated.

可逆/不可逆热流分析有助于解析不同组分的重叠热事件，准确确定多相聚合物中每一相的特性。 Analysis of reversible/non-reversible heat flow enables separation of overlapping events and accurate identification of each phase’s characteristics in multiphase polymers.

四、DSC校准Calibration of DSC

需用标准物进行两方面校准
基线校准、热/温度校准。 Calibration requires two steps: baseline calibration and calibration of heat and temperature using certified standards.

热量校准常用标准（如苯甲酸、尿素、茚、蒽），温度校准涵盖多种相变点，如茚（156.60°C）、锡（231.95°C）、铅（327.46°C）等。 Heat calibration uses standards like benzoic acid, urea, indium, anthracene; temperature calibration includes phase change points such as indium (156.60°C), tin (231.95°C), and lead (327.46°C).

五、样品制备Sample Preparation

通常称样品3~20 mg，密封或非密封样品盘，压封固定。 Typically weigh 3–20 mg sample, seal into capsules (sealed/unsealed) using a crimping press.

样品盘材质多样（如铝、铜、金、石墨、石英等），不同材质对应不同耐温极限（如铝600°C、铜/石墨/金均为725°C等）。 Capsule materials vary (aluminum, copper, gold, graphite, quartz, etc.), each with different temperature limits (e.g., aluminum 600°C, copper/graphite/gold 725°C, etc.).

实际应根据样品性质及实验要求选择合适样品盘类型。 Choose capsule type according to sample nature and testing requirements.

六、课堂补充和要点归纳Additional Classroom Notes and Key Points

老师强调常规DSC对多相、高分子共混体系，容易出现多玻璃转变、结晶和熔融峰的重叠，需结合调制DSC等方法区分。 The instructor emphasized that in multiphase or blended polymer systems, conventional DSC may show overlapping Tg, crystallization, and melting peaks, so modulated DSC or other methods are needed for separation2.

样品质量、密封性、气氛、升温（冷却）速率等实验参数会显著影响测得的峰形和数值，应在实验设计与数据记录中详细注明。 Parameters such as sample mass, sealing, atmosphere, heating/cooling rates significantly affect peak shape and values; all must be carefully documented in design and reporting2.

利用标准样品校准是保证热分析数据准确可靠的基础。 Use of reference standards for calibration is critical to data accuracy and reliability2.

七、小结与实际意义Summary and Practical Significance

DSC是聚合物及药用高分子体系最常用的热分析手段，可用于定性定量分析Tg、结晶度、熔点等关键热参数。 DSC is the most widely used calorimetric analytical technique for polymers and pharmaceutical systems; it enables qualitative and quantitative assessment of Tg, crystallinity, melting point, and other key properties2.

灵活运用常规与调制DSC、规范校准与样品处理，才能获得真实、可比、可重复的热分析结果。 Appropriate use of conventional and modulated DSC, together with rigorous calibration and sample handling, ensures reliable and reproducible results2.

量热分析技术DSC实验参数及应用总结Calorimetric Techniques: Key DSC Experimental Parameters and Application Summary

一、样品制备和热交换Sample Preparation and Thermal Exchange

样品制备最重要的一点是实现样品与热电偶之间的最佳热交换。 The most important point for sample preparation is to achieve the best thermal exchange between the sample and the thermocouple.

二、样品要求Sample Requirements

样品应尽可能薄，以降低热阻，提高热交换效率。 The sample should be as thin as possible to decrease thermal resistance and enhance heat transfer.

样品与样品盘的接触质量对测试结果影响很大，需确保良好接触。 The quality of contact between the sample and the capsule is critical for reliable results and must be optimized.

若样品质量太低，灵敏度会明显下降。 Too low sample mass leads to a significant loss in sensitivity.

三、扫描速率影响Scanning Rate Effects

低扫描速率有更好的分辨率，适合分离相近热事件。 Lower scanning rates yield better resolution, making it easier to separate nearby thermal events.

高频扫描速率提高灵敏性，但分辨率较低，更适用于检测较弱信号。 Higher scanning rates offer improved sensitivity but at the cost of resolution; suitable for weak signals.

四、扫描气氛选择Scanning Gas Options

氮气具有较低的热导率，但灵敏度好，适用于常规分析。 Nitrogen provides low thermal conductivity but good sensitivity, and is commonly used for routine analyses.

氦气有更好的热导率和分辨率，适合对热事件细节要求高的实验。 Helium provides superior thermal conductivity and better resolution, making it ideal for high-detail thermal experiments.

经典DSC通常需要在灵敏度与分辨率之间权衡选择。 In classical DSC, a compromise is needed between sensitivity and resolution.

五、DSC主要应用总结Summary of DSC Applications

相变检测
Phase Change Detection:
固-固转变
玻璃化转变、多晶型变化、脱溶剂作用 Solid/Solid transitions: glass transition, polymorphism, desolvatation
固-液转变
熔融 Solid/Liquid transitions: melting
液-固转变
结晶 Liquid/Solid transitions: crystallization
化学组成变化检测
Chemical Composition Changes:

可用于测定特定条件下的焓变和温度，包括反应动力学、材料降解、脱溶剂等 Allows measurement of enthalpy change (ΔH) and temperature under specific conditions, such as reaction kinetics, degradation, and desolvation

相图绘制和工艺开发
Phase Diagram Establishment and Process Development:

对制剂前研究、冻干工艺尤为重要，指导工艺参数优化 Critical for pre-formulation studies and freeze-drying process optimization, supporting process parameter development

六、老师讲解补充Classroom Highlights and Teacher's Supplement

老师强调采样及操作规范对于获得可重复、准确结果极为重要，尤其是样品薄厚、质量、封装和接触质量。 The instructor emphasized that proper sample handling and standard operation are key to obtaining reproducible and accurate DSC results, especially regarding sample thickness, mass, sealing, and contact.

实验设计要针对灵敏度与分辨率在不同应用场景时的具体要求，合理选择扫描速率与气体。 Experimental design should take into account the practical requirements for sensitivity and resolution in different scenarios, and thus select appropriate scan rates and atmospheres.

DSC技术既可以分析物理变化（如玻璃化、结晶、熔融），也可监控化学变化（如反应、降解、脱水），兼具定量与定性功能。 DSC is suitable for both physical (glass transition, crystallization, melting) and chemical change monitoring (reaction, degradation, dehydration), with both quantitative and qualitative capabilities.

在药物和高分子材料制备、冻干、储存及性能评价等领域均有广泛应用价值。 It finds broad application in pharmaceutical and polymer preparation, freeze-drying, storage, and property evaluation.

七、小结Summary

样品与热电偶间最佳热交换、适宜的样品薄厚和质量、合理的扫描参数及气氛选择，是DSC实验成功的核心。 The best thermal exchange between sample and thermocouple, proper sample thickness and mass, and appropriate scan parameters and atmosphere are core to successful DSC experiments.

DSC可精准分析多种相变和化学反应，是药物与材料研究中不可或缺的热分析工具。 DSC precisely analyzes various phase changes and chemical reactions, making it indispensable for drug and material research.

二、量热分析
纯度测定原理Calorimetric Techniques: Principle of Purity Determination

通过测量熔融焓（ΔH）和熔点（Tm），可以获得杂质含量。 By measuring melting enthalpy (ΔH) and melting point (Tm), impurity content can be determined.

杂质存在会导致温度范围变宽（峰展宽）和热曲线变弯。 The presence of impurities leads to broadening of the melting range and curving of the thermogram.

这两者是检测样品杂质的灵敏标准。 Both are sensitive criteria for impurity detection.

三、DSC熔点与纯度分析实例DSC Melting and Purity Analysis Example

纯聚乙烯（PE）与金属铟（Indium）DSC曲线对比，纯金属熔点极窄且尖锐，而聚乙烯熔点区间较宽。 A comparison of the DSC curves for pure polyethylene (PE) and indium shows that pure metal has a sharp, narrow melting point, while PE has a broader melting range.

曲线上标注起始温度To、终止温度Tf、最大熔融温度Tp、以及曲线弯曲点Te。 The curve marks onset temperature To, final temperature Tf, peak melting temperature Tp, and transition points Te.

四、用DSC和Van't Hoff方程计算纯度DSC and the Van't Hoff Equation for Purity Calculation

用Van't Hoff方程可通过熔点降低计算杂质摩尔分数： The Van't Hoff equation uses melting point depression to calculate mole fraction of impurity: T = To - (R * To^2 / ΔHf) * x2

式中各量定义如下
The parameters are defined as:
To
纯物质的熔点（K） To: Melting point of a pure substance (K)
R
气体常数 R: Gas constant (J·K^-1·mol^-1)
ΔHf
熔化摩尔焓 ΔHf: Melting molar enthalpy (J)
x2
熔融杂质的摩尔分数 x2: Molar fraction of the molten impurity
F
熔融部分分数 F: Molten fraction
杂质摩尔分数x2计算方式
Calculation of impurity mole fraction x2: x2 = x / F 其中x为熔融杂质分子数，F为液相总分子数。 Where x is the number of impurity molecules in melt, F is the total number of molecules in the liquid phase.

五、Van't Hoff方程适用条件Validity Conditions for Van't Hoff Equation

主体组分纯度必须大于99.5%（摩尔比）。 The main component should have a purity greater than 99.5% (mole basis).

杂质一般是次级产物，可以通过HPLC等手段定性。 Impurities are generally secondary products and can be identified by HPLC, for example.

需制备含已知杂质含量的样品以校验计算精度。 It is necessary to prepare samples with known impurity levels to validate calculation accuracy.

六、DSC纯度分析数据及误差表DSC Purity Analysis: Data and Error Table

表格显示不同杂质添加量、测得杂质含量及计算误差（测得-添加，摩尔%）。 The table lists added impurity amount, measured impurity, and error (measured vs. added, in mol %).

七、DSC热图随纯度变化的表现Purity-Dependent Thermal Profiles in DSC

药典范例
对乙酰氨基酚（phenacetin）三种纯度热图，对比99.0%、99.5%、99.97%，纯度升高则熔点更集中、曲线更陡直。 Pharmacopeia example: Three purity levels of phenacetin show that higher purity corresponds to more concentrated, steeper melting peaks.

八、布洛芬DSC纯度测定实例DSC Purity Determination Example: Ibuprofen

必须使用较低升温速率（1°C/min）和高灵敏度热电偶。 Requires low scanning rate (1°C/min) and high sensitivity thermocouple.

结果包括
Results include:

纯度（98.06% mol） Purity (98.06% mol)

熔点（71.83°C） Melting point (71.83°C)

熔点下降（0.53°C） Melting point depression (0.53°C)

△H，分子量等 ΔH, molecular weight, etc.

九、老师讲课和录音要点补充Key Points from Teacher’s Lecture and Recording

老师强调纯度分析时，样品纯度越高，DSC熔点越窄越尖锐，杂质越多则区间宽大，尾部变得缓慢，与理论公式一致。 The teacher emphasized that higher sample purity results in sharper and narrower DSC peaks, while more impurities broaden the range and produce a more gradual tail, matching theoretical predictions.

用DSC和Van't Hoff方程进行纯度测定时，要保证主体成分高纯、杂质充分混匀，并进行低速升温以提高分辨率。 When using DSC and the Van't Hoff equation for purity measurement, ensure main component is highly pure, impurities are well mixed, and employ a low heating rate for increased resolution.

相关药典中的实际案例既可用于校准标准，也能验证测量准确性。 Pharmacopoeia case studies can be used for both standard calibration and accuracy validation.

十、小结Summary

DSC结合Van't Hoff方程可以定量分析样品纯度。 DSC, combined with the Van't Hoff equation, enables quantitative analysis of sample purity.

只要条件合适，是分析制药原料和健康产品纯度的重要方法。 If conditions are appropriate, this is an important method for analyzing the purity of pharmaceutical ingredients and health products.

二、热重分析法原理与应用概述Thermogravimetric Analysis (TGA) Principle & Applications

热重分析（TGA）是在温度变化下，给定气氛中连续测量样品质量随时间变化的一种热分析方法。 Thermogravimetry (TGA) is a thermal analysis method in which the mass of a sample is measured continuously as temperature changes under a controlled atmosphere.

通过热重分析，可以获得以下信息： TGA provides information on:

物理现象，如相变、吸附和解吸、吸收等； Physical phenomena, such as phase transitions, absorption, adsorption, and desorption;

化学现象，包括化学吸附、热分解、固-气反应（如氧化、还原）等； Chemical phenomena, including chemisorption, thermal decomposition, and solid-gas reactions (e.g., oxidation, reduction);

联用分析，可结合DSC、傅立叶变换红外光谱（FTIR）或质谱（MS）辨别挥发性产物。 Coupled analysis can combine TGA with DSC, FTIR, or MS to identify volatile products.

老师补充讲解
TGA方法常用于分析材料在受热条件下稳定性、分解温度、残余含量等，对于药物、聚合物及无机材料的热性质研究和产品开发至关重要。
Teacher’s addition
TGA is routinely used to assess thermal stability, decomposition temperatures, and residue in materials, which is vital for pharmaceuticals, polymers, and inorganic materials research and development.1

三、TGA典型仪器结构与测量关键点TGA Instrumentation & Measurement Essentials

仪器结构包括
气密腔、精密天平、样品托盘、加热器和温控系统。 The instrumentation includes: gas-tight enclosure, precision balance, sample pan, heater, and temperature controller.

实验中，气体流速、气氛（如氮气、空气、氧气）选择影响反应和分解行为。 During the experiment, gas flow, choice of atmosphere (e.g., nitrogen, air, oxygen) affect reaction and decomposition behavior.

样品被悬挂于天平上，随着温度程序变化自动记录质量。 The sample is suspended from the balance, and its mass is automatically recorded as the temperature program runs.

老师补充讲解
热重仪表需要高精度的天平与温度控制，尤其是在高分辨率与失重曲线细节要求严格的实验中。

Teacher’s supplement: TGA instruments require highly accurate balances and precise temperature control, especially for high-resolution and detail-demanding analyses.1

四、小结Summary

热重分析是热分析技术核心之一，配合DSC和联用技术可全面解析材料热行为。 Thermogravimetric analysis is a core thermal analysis technique, and when combined with DSC and hyphenated methods, it provides comprehensive information on a material’s thermal behavior.

在药物开发、材料改性、无机和高分子研究等领域应用广泛，实验设计需关注气氛、升温速率、样品状态等细节。 It is widely applied in drug development, material modification, as well as inorganic and polymer research. Correct experiment design should address atmosphere, heating rate, and sample condition.热分析课程纲要与热重分析原理Course Outline of Thermal Analysis & Principle of Thermogravimetric Analysis

二、热重分析原理与主要内容Principle and Main Points of Thermogravimetric Analysis

热重分析（TGA）是在温度变化和设定气氛下，实时测量样品质量随时间变化的热分析方法。 Thermogravimetry (TGA) is a type of thermal analysis in which the sample mass is measured in real-time as temperature changes under a defined atmosphere.

该方法可以获得以下信息
This method provides information on:
物理过程
相变、吸附/解吸、吸收等； Physical processes: phase transitions, adsorption/desorption, absorption, etc.;
化学过程
化学吸附、热分解、固-气反应（如氧化还原）； Chemical processes: chemisorption, thermal decomposition, solid-gas reactions (oxidation, reduction);
联用分析
可配合DSC、FTIR或质谱用于挥发性产物的鉴定。 Coupled analysis: can join with DSC, FTIR, or MS to identify volatile products.
老师强调
TGA对于分析物质的热稳定性、分解温度及残余含量极为关键，是药物成分和高分子材料质量控制的重要工具。
Teacher’s stress
TGA is vital for determining thermal stability, decomposition point, and residue, making it crucial in quality control for pharmaceuticals and polymeric materials.1

三、TGA仪器构成与操作要点TGA Instrumentation and Key Operation Points

TGA仪器一般包括气密腔体、高精度电子天平、样品托、加热炉和温度控制单元。 A TGA system generally consists of a gas-tight enclosure, precision balance, sample pan, furnace, and temperature control unit.

样品在自动加热程序中悬挂于天平下，升温过程中质量实时变化被连续记录。 The sample is suspended on the balance under programmed heating, with mass changes continuously recorded during temperature increase.

控制气体（如氮气、空气或氧气）的种类及流速可影响热分解和反应过程。 Control of atmosphere (nitrogen, air, or oxygen) and gas flow rate can affect decomposition and reactions throughout analysis.

老师补充说明
天平灵敏度和温控精度对热重分析结果有决定性影响，应根据分析目标优化参数设置。
Teacher’s note
Sensitivity of the balance and accuracy of temperature control are key factors for reliable TGA results; experimental parameters should be optimized as per analysis needs.1

四、小结Summary

热重分析是研究材料热稳定性、失重行为和反应过程的核心手段，配合DSC和联用手段可实现多维解析。 Thermogravimetric analysis is a core tool for studying material thermal stability, weight loss, and reactions; when combined with DSC or other coupling tools, it enables multifaceted insight.

广泛应用于药物、材料、无机与高分子领域，实验设计应关注气氛、升温速率和样品特性等细节。 TGA is widely used for pharmaceuticals, materials, inorganic and polymeric fields; robust experiment design must address atmosphere, heating rate, and sample characteristics.

二、热重分析仪（TGA）校准TGA Calibration

1
质量校准 Calibration of MASS

采用认证金球（静态校准） Certified gold bead (static calibration)

使用硫酸铜（动态校准） Copper sulphate (dynamic calibration)

2
温度校准 Calibration of TEMPERATURE

用磁性材料（居里点）或Alumel进行校准 Magnetic materials (Point de Curie) / Alumel

用在已知温度下发生转变的材料进行校准 Materials with a transformation at a known temperature

3
时间校准 Calibration of TIME
老师讲解要点
TGA校准须分别对天平（质量）、温度和程序时间进行。参考物质如金球和硫酸铜可确保称量准确性，磁性材料或相变物质则用于温度校正。
Key teacher point
TGA calibration must address the balance (mass), temperature, and program time. Reference materials like gold beads and copper sulphate ensure weighing accuracy, while magnetic or phase-transition materials are crucial for temperature calibration.1

三、TGA曲线类型Types of TGA Curves

1
无失重，无化学分解 No mass loss, no chemical decomposition
2
快速失重（蒸发） Fast mass loss (evaporation)
单步分解
反映热稳定性 One-step decomposition: thermal stability
4
多步分解 Several steps decomposition
质量增加
生成非挥发性产物/氧化 Mass increase: formation of a non-volatile product / oxidation
先增加后降低
先生成非挥发产物，后在较高温度下进一步降解 Mass increase and decrease: formation of a non-volatile product that is degraded at higher temperature
老师补充说明
TGA曲线形态可以直观反映样品的热行为，如蒸发、热分解、氧化等。轨迹的不同步骤代表着材料中不同成分的热反应，实验中应根据具体曲线判断热稳定性和分解路线。

Teacher's supplement: The shape of TGA curves directly reveals thermal behaviors such as evaporation, thermal decomposition, and oxidation. The steps on the curve correspond to different thermal processes and components; proper interpretation is essential for judging material stability and decomposition pathways.1
