热重分析法及实际应用举例Thermogravimetric Analysis and Practical Examples

一、课程内容提要Course Outline

各种热分析技术回顾，包含热分析的两大核心方向：量热分析和重量分析。 Review of various thermal analysis techniques, covering the two core directions: calorimetric and gravimetric analysis.

量热分析技术
主要涵盖一阶相变、二阶相变、DSC设备校准和样品准备、其他相关应用。 Calorimetric techniques: covers first-order/second-order transitions, DSC equipment calibration/sample prep, and other applications.
重量分析技术
包含仪器设备、校准、实际案例与联用（如与红外或质谱等）。 Gravimetric techniques: includes instrumentation, calibration, practical examples, and coupling with other techniques such as IR or MS.

本课程将结合具体实例与曲线解析，讲解热重分析在健康产品、药物和材料评估中的关键作用。 The course uses real examples and curve analysis to explain the key role of TGA in the evaluation of health products, drugs, and materials.

二、钙草酸盐的分解TGA曲线示例Example: Thermogravimetric Analysis of Calcium Oxalate Decomposition

1
分解曲线解读Analysis of Decomposition Curve

钙草酸盐水合物（CaC₂O₄·H₂O）在加热至900°C时，分三步失重：先失水（放水），后分解出CO，最后生成CO₂。 Calcium oxalate monohydrate (CaC₂O₄·H₂O) loses weight in three steps as heated to 900°C: initial water loss, CO release, and finally CO₂ formation.

失重平台和突变点分别对应H₂O、CO和CO₂的释放温区。 Weight plateaus and inflection points correspond to the temperature ranges for H₂O, CO, and CO₂ release.

操作条件
20°C升至900°C，升温速率10°C/min，样品放铝氧化物坩埚，载气为氦气。 Procedure: heating 20°C to 900°C at 10°C/min, alumina pan, helium atmosphere.
典型意义
可用于校准仪器，也可用于物质分解行为与成分测试。 Significance: Useful for instrument calibration and for analyzing decomposition behaviors and ingredient content4。

三、有机盐水分含量TGA鉴定实例Example: Determination of Moisture Content in Organic Salts

1
水分种类及分步失重Types of Water and Stepwise Weight Loss

TGA曲线可区分吸附水（自由水）、弱键合吸附水和结构水等多种状态。 TGA curves help differentiate free (adsorbed) water, weakly bound water, and structural water.

第一步失重（Loss 1，约59°C），对应自由吸附水的蒸发。 First weight loss (Loss 1, ~59°C): evaporation of free, adsorbed water.

第二步失重（Loss 2，约101°C），反映弱键合作用下吸附水脱除。 Second loss (Loss 2, ~101°C): removal of water weakly bound to the sample.

第三步（Loss 3，~131°C），为化合物晶体水或结构水进一步脱除。 Third step (Loss 3, ~131°C): further loss corresponds to crystalline or structural water.

总失重可定量计算有机盐总水分及其不同结合形式的比例。 Total weight loss allows quantification of overall moisture and fractions of each binding form.

老师补充
不同水分类型对材料性能和贮存稳定性影响巨大，TGA方法可指导药物和化工品常温水分管理。 Teacher’s addition: Different water types significantly affect material properties and storage stability, and TGA provides data crucial for water management in pharmaceuticals and chemicals.4

四、聚合物热稳定性对比TGA对不同聚合物热分解的分析TGA Comparison: Thermal Stability of Different Polymers

TGA曲线示例中，PVC、PMMA、HDPE、PTFE及PI的失重温区依次递增。 In the TGA example, PVC, PMMA, HDPE, PTFE, and PI show increasing decomposition onset temperatures.

曲线左侧为低温失重（如PVC、PMMA），右侧为高温高热稳定性（如PTFE、PI）。 Those on the left decompose at lower temperatures (PVC, PMMA); those on the right have higher thermal stability (PTFE, PI).

这体现了不同材料在加热过程中的分解顺序：PVC＜PMMA＜HDPE＜PTFE＜PI。 This indicates the order of decomposition: PVC < PMMA < HDPE < PTFE < PI.

失重梯度越平缓、残留量越多，代表热稳定性越高。 Flatter loss gradients and higher residue mean greater thermal stability.

老师强调
TGA不仅用于材料纯度检测，也是筛选高温工艺材料的重要依据，能辅助聚合物选择和生产。
Teacher’s emphasis
TGA is not only for purity but also critically supports high-temperature material selection and polymer processing decisions.4

五、小结Summary

热重分析能实现多种分解、水分与热稳定性的表征，是药品开发、高分子储存及材料学应用的核心工具。 Thermogravimetric analysis enables decomposition profiling, moisture determination, and thermal stability measurement—core tools in drug development, polymer storage, and material science applications.

快速判断样品失重区间与残余量，有助于理解材料结构、杂质含量和性能变化。 Rapidly identifies sample weight loss zones and residues, helping to understand structure, impurities, and property changes.

热重分析联用及其应用解读 Interpretation of Coupled Thermogravimetric Analysis and Its Applications

一、课程提纲Course Outline

本课程首先回顾了材料状态区间和高分子有序结构。 The course begins with a review of state domains and the ordered structure of macromolecules.

介绍了主流的热分析技术，包括两大方向：量热分析（如DSC）与重量分析（如TGA）。 It introduces mainstream thermal analytical techniques, divided into calorimetric (e.g., DSC) and gravimetric (e.g., TGA) analyses.

重点讲解量热分析技术中的一阶相变、二阶相变、DSC设备校准与样品准备，以及其应用。 Key points include first-order and second-order transitions, DSC equipment calibration/preparation, and broader applications.

对重量分析技术（TGA）的仪器设备、校准方法、典型应用和与其他分析手段的联用做了系统说明。 Gravimetric techniques (TGA) are discussed in terms of instrumentation, calibration, practical examples, and system coupling with other analytical tools.

特别强调TGA与DSC、质谱（MS）、傅里叶变换红外光谱（FTIR）等联用，扩展热分析的研究深度与精度。 Special emphasis is placed on combining TGA with DSC, mass spectrometry (MS), and FTIR to enhance analytical depth and precision.

二、TGA/DSC联用分析 Coupling TGA/DSC Analysis

TGA/DSC联用允许同时监测样品升温过程中的质量变化和热流（吸热/放热）变化。 Combining TGA/DSC permits simultaneous monitoring of mass loss and heat flow (endothermic/exothermic) during heating.

以粉末状苏打（Soda）为例，TGA曲线显示112℃失水，约占总质量的5.22%；同时DSC检测到失水的吸热峰。 Using powdered soda as an example, the TGA curve shows water loss at 112°C (about 5.22% of total mass), with DSC capturing an endothermic peak for water loss.

在851℃检测到熔融（melting）行为，对应DSC的显著吸热峰（180 J/g）。 At 851°C, melting is detected, with a prominent endothermic peak (180 J/g) on DSC.

该技术有助于理解水分挥发、样品组成及其高温物理转变。 This aids in understanding water evaporation, sample composition, and high-temperature physical transitions.

三、TGA/MS联用分析 Coupling TGA/MS (Mass Spectrometry) Analysis

TGA与MS联用可以识别热分解产物的挥发种类和具体分子。 Coupling TGA with MS identifies the types and molecular identities of volatile decomposition products.

以草酸钙分解为例，TGA曲线显示多步失重，MS同步检测激发气体（如H₂O、CO、CO₂）的信号。 For calcium oxalate decomposition, the TGA curve shows stepwise mass loss, while MS observes signal peaks from evolved gases (H₂O, CO, CO₂).

该方法应用于药品质量控制（如肾结石分析、药物杂质甄别），且可校准仪器性能。 This method is used in pharmaceutical quality control (e.g., kidney stone analysis, impurity identification) and instrument calibration.

四、TGA/FTIR联用分析 Coupling TGA/FTIR (Fourier-Transform Infrared Spectroscopy) Analysis

TGA/FTIR结合可实现热分解过程中气体产物的化学结构实时监测。 Combining TGA with FTIR enables real-time monitoring of gas-phase products’ chemical structures during decomposition.

以抗生素Ramoplanin分解为例，TGA揭示主失重区间，FTIR分析对应挥发性物质的红外吸收峰。 In decomposing the antibiotic Ramoplanin, TGA identifies the main weight-loss steps; FTIR then reveals infrared absorption bands of volatiles released.

多个温区内可同时检测H₂O、NH₃、HCl、CN、CO₂等物种，提升成分溯源与杂质分析能力。 Multiple gases such as H₂O, NH₃, HCl, CN, and CO₂ can be detected in different temperature regions, supporting component tracing and impurity analysis.

联用后常用于药物、聚合物、材料热稳定与分解过程复杂产物的精细鉴定。 These couplings are widely applied in drugs, polymers, and material studies for detailed identification of products formed during complex decomposition.

五、课程结论与注意 Conclusion and Key Takeaways

热分析（如TGA、DSC和联用技术）被欧洲药典推荐用于物质鉴别、纯度/成分评估和水分/残余溶剂测定。 Thermal analyses (TGA, DSC, and hybrid methods) are referenced in the European Pharmacopoeia for substance identification, purity assessment, and water or residual solvent determination.

通过与MS、FTIR等手段联用，TGA极大拓展了新材料、新药开发、杂质溯源等领域的分析手段。 Through couplings with MS and FTIR, TGA dramatically enhances analytical tools in areas such as new material and drug development, and impurity tracing.

随着仪器和数据处理能力提升，联用热分析技术成为复杂体系全面表征的重要趋势。 With advances in instrumentation and data processing, coupled thermal analysis is a key trend for comprehensive characterization of complex systems.

分析技术
包含分离步骤 Analytical Techniques containing a Separation Step
1
分析分离技术概览 Overview of Analytical Separation Techniques

分离技术在分析科学中起着核心作用，用以将样品中不同组分有效分离。 Separation techniques play a central role in analytical sciences to effectively separate different components in a sample.

分离技术通常需结合检测装置，才能实现分析功能。 Separation techniques are usually coupled with detection devices to enable analytical functions.

分离技术不包括
蒸馏、升华、结晶、沉淀等手段，这些仅用于物理分离、未与检测联用。 These techniques do not include: distillation, sublimation, crystallization, precipitation, etc., which are only for physical separation and not coupled with detection.
2
分析技术中的主要分离类别 Main Categories of Separation in Analytical Techniques
分离技术大体分为
色谱法（Chromatography）、固相萃取（Solid phase extraction）、电分离法（Electroseparations）、膜分离法（Membrane separations）及其他分离法（Other separations）。 The main categories include: Chromatography, Solid phase extraction, Electro-separations, Membrane separations, and Other separations.

色谱法进一步细分为气相色谱（Gas Chromatography）、液相色谱（Liquid Chromatography，如HPLC）、超临界流体色谱（Supercritical Fluid Chromatography）等。 Chromatography is further divided into Gas Chromatography, Liquid Chromatography (e.g., HPLC), Supercritical Fluid Chromatography, etc.

电分离法包括毛细管电泳（Capillary Electrophoresis）等；膜分离包括扩散、渗透、透析等。 Electro-separation includes Capillary Electrophoresis; membrane separations include diffusion, osmosis, dialysis, etc.

3
典型分析色谱仪器的组成 Components of an Analytical Chromatographic Instrument
典型色谱仪由以下部分组成
A typical chromatographic instrument consists of the following parts:   1）流动相来源（Source of mobile phase） 2) 进样器（Sample injector） 3) 分离单元/固定相（Separation unit / Stationary phase） 4) 检测器（Detector(s)） 5) 控制与数据处理系统（Control and data processing）
1
Source of mobile phase
2
Sample injector
3
Separation unit / Stationary phase
4
Detector(s)
5
Control and data processing system

系统通过流动相将样品带入分离单元，各组分因与固定相的亲和力不同而被分离，最终由检测器检测、记录并输出色谱图。 The system delivers the sample into the separation unit via the mobile phase; different components are separated due to different affinities to the stationary phase and are eventually detected and recorded by the detector, producing a chromatogram.

现代设备多通过计算机软件实现自动化控制和数据采集。 Modern systems typically use computer software for automated control and data acquisition.

4
补充讲解内容 Extra Information from Lecture

老师强调，我们关注的是色谱等与检测装置联用的分离技术，不包括单纯的结晶、蒸馏等。 The teacher emphasized that the focus is on separation techniques coupled with detectors, such as chromatography, not pure crystallization or distillation.

色谱分离的本质在于
样品各组分对固定相亲和力不同，导致分离。 The essence of chromatographic separation is: different sample components have different affinities for the stationary phase, leading to separation.

检测器负责记录各成分的响应信号，并将其转化为随时间变化的色谱图。 The detector records the response signals for each component and converts them into chromatograms over time.

5
简明结构梳理 Structural Overview

色谱分析通过连续流动的流动相和固定不动的固定相实现分离。 Chromatographic analysis achieves separation via a continuously flowing mobile phase and a stationary phase.

色谱仪的工作流程
样品注入 → 流动相带动 → 分离单元分离 → 检测器检测 → 结果输出。 Workflow in a chromatograph: sample injection → mobile phase transports → separation in separation unit → detection by detector → output of results.

色谱法发展历史 | History of Chromatography

1
色谱法的词源与定义 | Etymology and Definition of Chromatography

色谱法（Chromatography）一词源于希腊语：χρώμα（colour，颜色）+ γράφειν（writing，书写）。 The term Chromatography originates from Greek: χρώμα (colour) + γράφειν (writing).

该命名源于早期色谱实验中分离出的有色化合物在色谱柱上呈现出不同颜色带。 This naming comes from the colored bands of separated compounds observed in early chromatographic experiments.

色谱法的诞生
1900年 | Birth of Chromatography: 1900

1900年，俄罗斯植物学家Mikhail Tswet发明了色谱法，用于分离植物色素。 In 1900, Russian botanist Mikhail Tswet invented chromatography to separate plant pigments.

Tswet将植物色素样品加载到小柱顶部，随后不断添加溶剂，不同叶绿素成分因对固定相亲和力不同而被分离。 Tswet loaded plant pigment samples onto the top of a small column, then continuously added solvent; different chlorophyll components were separated due to different affinities for the stationary phase.

由于分离的化合物具有颜色，实验结果呈现出彩色条带，这也是色谱法命名的由来。 Because the separated compounds were colored, the experimental results showed colored bands, which is the origin of the term chromatography.

该实验结果发表在Tswet的原始文章中，图示展示了分离效果。 The experimental results were published in Tswet's original article, with figures showing the separation effect.

色谱法的理论预测
1941年 | Theoretical Prediction: 1941

1941年，Martin和Synge预测了高压和小颗粒固定相可实现高效分离。 In 1941, Martin and Synge predicted that high pressure and small particle size could achieve efficient separations.

这一理论为后续高效液相色谱（HPLC）的发展奠定了基础。 This theory laid the foundation for the subsequent development of High Performance Liquid Chromatography (HPLC).

HPLC的诞生与发展
1966-1967年 | Birth and Development of HPLC: 1966-1967

1966年，C. Horváth和S. Lipsky发表了利用HPLC分离核苷酸的研究。 In 1966, C. Horváth and S. Lipsky published research on nucleotide separation by HPLC.

1967年，Waters公司推出了第一台商业化HPLC仪器。 In 1967, the first commercial HPLC instrument was introduced by Waters.

HPLC的演进而非革命
1970-2000年代 | HPLC: Evolution Rather Than Revolution: 1970s-2000s

1972年，J. Waters和J. Little利用HPLC分离了维生素B-12合成中的中间体异构体。 In 1972, J. Waters and J. Little separated isomers of an intermediate in the synthesis of vitamin B-12 using HPLC.

1970-1985年
高压-高效颗粒技术发展，固定相颗粒尺寸为10-5微米，键合相技术成熟。 1970-1985: High Pressure-High Performance Particles developed, with stationary phase particle sizes of 10-5 micrometers, and bonded phase technology matured.
1980年
实现了对映异构体的分离。 1980: Separation of enantiomers was achieved.
1985-1990年
检测器技术进步，HPLC与质谱联用技术发展。 1985-1990: Detector technology advanced, and coupling of HPLC to mass spectrometry developed.
2000年后
超高效液相色谱（UHPLC）出现，颗粒直径小于2微米，压力达20000 psi。 After 2000: Ultra-High Performance Liquid Chromatography (UHPLC) emerged, with particle diameter less than 2 micrometers and pressure up to 20000 psi.

颗粒尺寸从大于20微米（不规则形状）逐步减小至小于2微米（均匀形状），分离效率显著提升。 Particle size gradually decreased from greater than 20 micrometers (irregular shape) to less than 2 micrometers (uniform shape), significantly improving separation efficiency.

技术进步的直观体现
分离时间缩短 | Visual Demonstration of Technological Progress: Reduced Separation Time
1980年代
色谱柱规格4.6 × 250 mm，颗粒5微米，分离时间8分钟。 1980s: Column specifications 4.6 × 250 mm, particle size 5 micrometers, separation time 8 minutes.
1990年代
色谱柱规格4.6 × 100 mm，颗粒3.5微米，分离时间3分钟。 1990s: Column specifications 4.6 × 100 mm, particle size 3.5 micrometers, separation time 3 minutes.
2000年代
色谱柱规格4.6 × 50 mm，颗粒1.8微米，分离时间1.5分钟。 2000s: Column specifications 4.6 × 50 mm, particle size 1.8 micrometers, separation time 1.5 minutes.
分离化合物包括
1-甲基黄嘌呤、1,3-二甲基尿酸、3,7-二甲基黄嘌呤和1,7-二甲基黄嘌呤（按洗脱顺序从左到右）。 Separated compounds include: 1-methylxanthine, 1,3-dimethyluric acid, 3,7-dimethylxanthine, and 1,7-dimethylxanthine (in order of elution, left to right).

现代UHPLC仪器体积更小、效率更高，实现了快速高效分离。 Modern UHPLC instruments are more compact and efficient, achieving rapid and efficient separations.

柱色谱中的洗脱 Elution in Column Chromatography → 分离优化 Improving Separation

一、柱色谱中的洗脱过程 Elution in Column Chromatography

样品A和B被一起加到填充色谱柱顶部。 Sample A and B are added together to the top of a packed chromatography column.

流动相（溶剂）自上而下不断流入色谱柱，将A、B两个组分向下淋洗。 The mobile phase (solvent) continuously flows from top to bottom through the column, eluting components A and B downward.

由于A和B与固定相的亲和力不同，在柱中迁移速度不同，逐渐分开形成带型。 Because A and B have different affinities for the stationary phase, they migrate at different speeds in the column and gradually separate into bands.

随着时间推进，A、B组分带不断稀释并扩散，在柱底分别到达检测器，形成各自峰值信号。 As time goes on, the bands of A and B become more dilute and diffuse, and they reach the detector at the bottom of the column separately, forming individual peak signals.

检测器连续记录信号变化，得到以时间为横坐标的色谱图。 The detector continuously records the signal changes, resulting in a chromatogram with time as the x-axis.

色谱图上的每个峰代表一种组分，峰面积与该物质的含量相关。 Each peak on the chromatogram corresponds to a component, and the area under the peak is related to its quantity.

洗脱过程伴随样品稀释（Solute dilution），峰宽反映为组分带的扩散。 Elution is accompanied by sample dilution, and the peak width reflects the spreading of the component band.

二、分离过程优化 Improving Separation

分离效果受组分之间分离度与峰形（带宽）影响。 The separation effect depends on the degree of separation between components and the shape/width of the peaks.

提高分离度可通过增加组分之间的间隔（Increase band separation）实现。 Increasing the separation can be achieved by increasing the distance between component bands (increase band separation).

减小峰宽，通过减少组分扩散（Decrease band width）可提高分辨率。 Reducing peak width (decrease band width) by minimizing the diffusion of components can improve resolution.

优秀的分离为
组分峰相互分开且各自较窄，避免重叠。 Good separation means component peaks are distinctly apart and each is narrow, avoiding overlap.

色谱优化目标是同时拉开组分间距并减小带宽，从而提升定性与定量分析的准确性。 The goal of chromatographic optimization is to increase band separation and decrease band width simultaneously, thereby improving both qualitative and quantitative accuracy.

三、老师补充讲解要点 Extra Points from the Lecture

洗脱是动态过程，流动相不断带动组分迁移直至全部洗脱出柱。 Elution is a dynamic process where the mobile phase constantly drives the migration of components until they are fully eluted from the column.

色谱分离效率的提升依赖多种参数，如固定相特性、流动相类型、颗粒大小等。 The improvement of chromatographic separation efficiency depends on factors such as stationary phase characteristics, mobile phase type, and particle size.

在仪器发展史中，使用更小、更均匀颗粒的色谱柱可以有效减小峰宽，缩短分离时间，提升分辨率。 In the history of instrument development, using columns with smaller and more uniform particles effectively reduces peak width, shortens separation time, and improves resolution

分配常数 KAKA​（或分配系数）描述了溶质A在两相（流动相和固定相）之间的分布平衡。 The distribution constant KAKA​ (or partition coefficient) describes the equilibrium distribution of solute A between the mobile and stationary phases.

VS,VM：固定相/流动相体积 (volume of stationary/mobile phase)

通常认为活度系数约为1，因而可简化表述。 The activity coefficient is usually nearly unity, thus this expression is simplified.

线性色谱（Linear chromatography）假定同样分析物在同样条件下保持常数的保留行为。 Linear chromatography assumes the retention behavior of the analyte remains constant under the same conditions.

六、选择性因子 αα（Selectivity Factor）

选择性因子定义为两种溶质B和A的分配常数/保留因子的比值： Selectivity factor is defined as the ratio of the distribution constants/retention factors for solutes B and A:

通常 α>1α>1，代表B比A被固定相保留得更多。 Generally, α>1α>1, indicating B is more strongly retained than A.

KB,kBKB​,kB​ 分别为溶质B的分配常数、保留因子；KA,kAKA​,kA​ 为A的相应值。 KB,kBKB​,kB​ are the distribution constant and retention factor for B; KA,kAKA​,kA​ are for A.

七、老师补充讲解要点 Extra Points from the Lecture

色谱分离的本质取决于溶质在两相中的分布平衡，分配常数KAKA​越大，A在固定相中滞留越久。 The core of chromatographic separation depends on equilibrium distribution of solute between two phases—the larger KAKA​, the longer A stays in stationary phase.

保留因子越高，组分滞留时间越长，峰向右移动。 The higher the retention factor, the longer the component is retained and the more its peak shifts rightward.

当比值αα接近1时，组分难以分开，因此选择性优化对于分离至关重要。 When selectivity factor αα approaches 1, separation is difficult, thus improving selectivity is crucial for resolution.

谱带展宽与色谱柱效率 Band Broadening and Column Efficiency

一、理论塔板数与塔板高度 Number of Theoretical Plates and Plate Height

色谱柱可等效为若干“理论塔板”，每一板上组分在流动相与固定相间达到分配平衡。 The chromatographic column can be regarded as several “theoretical plates”, where equilibrium of solute distribution between mobile and stationary phases is achieved on each plate.

理论塔板数 NN
Number of theoretical plates (N\

其中 LL 为柱长，HH 为塔板高度。 Where LL is the column length, and HH is the plate height.

塔板高度 HH
Plate height

其中 σ2σ2 表示色谱带的方差（带宽平方），反映峰的展宽程度。 Where σ2σ2 is the variance of the band (square of band width), indicating the extent of peak broadening.

理论塔板模型（Plate theory）：效率高的色谱柱塔板高度小，理论塔板数高，分离能力强。 Plate theory: Efficient columns have small plate heights and high plate numbers, leading to strong separation power.

色谱柱末端的分析物带型近似为高斯分布，带宽用标准差σσ描述。 The analyte band profile at the end of the column approximates a Gaussian distribution, and the width is described by the standard deviation σσ.

二、实验评价与计算 Experimental Evaluation and Calculation

色谱峰的展宽既可用距离（空间）描述，也可用时间（保留时间, tRtR​）来评价。 Peak broadening can be described by spatial length or by the retention time (tRtR​).

三、谱带展宽的影响因素 Factors Affecting Band Broadening

四、老师补充讲解要点 Extra Points from the Lecture

理论塔板概念来源于蒸馏理论，用于评价色谱柱效率，板数越多、板高越小，分离效果越好。 The idea of theoretical plates originates from distillation theory and is used to evaluate column efficiency: higher plate numbers and lower plate heights mean better separation.

色谱峰越窄，分离分辨率越高；而峰展宽则降低分离效果。 The narrower the peak, the higher the resolution; excessive band broadening decreases separation power.

实际分析中，常用高斯分布峰形对峰进行参数化，通过峰宽、半高宽等方式估算塔板数。 In practice, peak shape is usually approximated by a Gaussian, and plate number is estimated via peak width or width at half height.

多种物理过程同时影响柱内与柱外的展宽，因此提高仪器和操作条件的合理性是色谱分离优化的关键。 Multiple physical processes influence both column and extra-column broadening, so optimizing instrument and operational conditions is crucial for chromatographic separation.

影响色谱柱效率的动力学变量 Kinetic Variables Affecting Column Efficiency

1
Van Deemter方程与塔板高度 H Van Deemter Plate Height (H) Equation

Van Deemter方程描述了色谱柱理论塔板高度 HH 与流动相线速度 uu 的关系，是色谱分离动力学理论的核心。 The Van Deemter equation describes the relationship between the theoretical plate height HH of a chromatographic column and the linear velocity uu of the mobile phase, serving as the core of chromatographic kinetic theory.

公式如下
The equation is:
2
各参数含义解读 Explanation of Each Term
AA
多路径效应（涡流扩散系数），由填料颗粒不均影响，与流速无关。 AA: Multiple path effects (eddy diffusion coefficient), affected by irregular particle packing, independent of flow rate.
BB
纵向扩散系数，溶质分子沿柱方向自发扩散，流速越低影响越大。 BB: Longitudinal diffusion coefficient; describes spontaneous diffusion of solute molecules along the column, more significant at lower flow rates.
CSCS​
固定相传质阻力系数，溶质进出固定相耗时，高流速时组分难以快速达到平衡。 CSCS​: Mass transfer coefficient to and from stationary phase; reflects resistance of solute to equilibrate into or out of stationary phase, more pronounced at higher flow rates.
CMCM​
流动相传质阻力系数，溶质在流动相内传递的阻碍，流速越高影响越大。 CMCM​: Mass transfer coefficient in mobile phase; describes mass transfer resistance within the mobile phase, increases with higher flow rate.
uu
流动相线速度，即流动相在柱内的线性平均流速。 uu: Linear velocity of the mobile phase, referring to the average speed at which the mobile phase moves through the column.
3
Van Deemter曲线解析 Van Deemter Curve Interpretation

右侧图形展示了不同项对塔板高度 HH 的贡献与流动相线速度 uu 的依赖关系。 The graph on the right shows the contribution of different terms to plate height HH as a function of the linear velocity uu of the mobile phase.

B/uB/u 项随流速升高迅速减小（低流速主导峰展宽）； The B/uB/u term decreases rapidly with increasing flow velocity (it dominates band broadening at low flow rates);

CSuCS​u 与 CMuCM​u 项随流速升高线性增加（高流速时传质阻力主导峰展宽）； The CSuCS​u and CMuCM​u terms increase linearly with flow velocity (mass transfer resistance dominates at high flow rates);

AA 项为常数，始终存在。 AA is a constant and always present.

曲线存在一个“最低点”，此时 HH 最小，对应最佳柱效率。 There is a “minimum point” on the curve where HH is smallest, corresponding to optimal column efficiency.

4
柱效率优化与实践意义 Column Efficiency Optimization and Practical Implications

色谱柱效率最高时，选择恰当流动相线速度，使各种峰展宽来源的贡献综合最小。 The column efficiency is maximized when an appropriate flow rate is chosen so that the total contributions to band broadening are minimized.

实验应在Van Deemter曲线“低谷区”工作，以获得最优分辨率和最快分析速度的平衡。 In practice, one should operate near the “valley” of the Van Deemter curve to balance optimal resolution and analysis speed.

增加分离效率的方法包括改进填料均匀性、优化流速、降低固定相/流动相传质阻力等。 Methods to increase separation efficiency include improving packing uniformity, optimizing flow velocity, and reducing stationary/mobile phase mass transfer resistance.

老师补充指出
优化色谱条件不仅提高柱效率，还能大幅提升实验结果的重复性和准确性。 The instructor added: Optimizing chromatographic conditions not only enhances column efficiency but also significantly improves the reproducibility and accuracy of experimental results.
5
速率理论简述 Introduction to Rate Theory

速率理论（Rate theory）用以分析色谱分离过程中不同动力学过程对谱带展宽的影响。 Rate theory is used to analyze the effects of different kinetic processes on band broadening in chromatographic separation.

其核心在于平衡扩散与传质阻力，寻找最佳分离条件。 The key concept is to balance diffusion and mass transfer resistance to find optimal separation conditions.

一般洗脱问题 The General Elution Problem

1
一般洗脱问题的概念 What is the General Elution Problem

一般洗脱问题是指在色谱分析复杂混合物时，难以用固定的流动相组成同时优化所有组分的洗脱效果。 The general elution problem refers to the difficulty of achieving optimal elution for all components in a complex mixture using a fixed mobile phase composition.

当混合物组分极性和保留性差异较大时，采用等度（固定组成）洗脱，通常会出现部分组分分离效果好，其他组分分离及洗脱效果差的现象。 When mixture components have large differences in polarity and retention, isocratic (fixed composition) elution often results in good separation for some components but poor elution or separation for others.

2
不同等度洗脱条件下的谱图示例 Chromatogram Examples under Various Isocratic Conditions

第一张图展示了用LC等度洗脱分离六个组分，每次只能优化部分组分的分离。 The first figure shows LC isocratic elution of six compounds, where each run only achieves optimal separation for some of the components.

(a)：当1号和2号组分处于最优分离区间（1 < k1, k2 < 5）时，3~6号组分分离不理想并出现宽峰。 (a): When components 1 and 2 are in the optimal retention range (1 < k1, k2 < 5), components 3–6 show poor separation and broad peaks.

(b)：调整条件使5号和6号组分达到最佳范围（1 < k5, k6 < 5），此时前面组分几乎并肩出峰，难以分离。 (b): Adjusting conditions to optimize for components 5 and 6 (1 < k5, k6 < 5) causes early components to elute together with little separation.

(c)：针对3号和4号组分最优化（1 < k3, k4 < 5），其余部分分离或洗脱均不理想。 (c): Optimizing for components 3 and 4 (1 < k3, k4 < 5) leaves the rest poorly separated or eluted.

3
如何解决一般洗脱问题 How to Solve the General Elution Problem

实验中很难用单一等度洗脱条件兼顾所有组分，需采用改善手段。 In practice, it is difficult to use a single isocratic condition to suit all components; improvement strategies are needed.

一种办法是分别选择不同流动相条件，分组分析样品（如针对每类物质调配独特溶剂）。 One method is to run separate analyses with different mobile phase conditions, tailored to each group of compounds.

更高效的方法是在单次进样过程中采用“梯度洗脱”或“程序升温洗脱”。 A more efficient method is to use "gradient elution" or "temperature programming" within a single run.

4
梯度洗脱与程序升温的优势 Advantages of Gradient and Temperature-Programmed Elution

梯度洗脱是在分离过程中连续改变流动相的极性或比例，从而使所有组分都能在1 < k* < 5的最优区间内依次洗脱。 Gradient elution continuously changes the polarity or ratio of the mobile phase during separation, so all components are eluted within the optimal window 1 < k* < 5.

程序升温则用于气相色谱，通过逐步提高柱温，达到类似的分离优化效果。 Temperature programming in gas chromatography gradually increases the column temperature to achieve similar optimization.

下图中的(g)为梯度洗脱，能一次性分离全部14种组分，保留因子均为k*≈3，且洗脱时间合理，分离效果极佳。 In the figure, (g) shows gradient elution, successfully separating all 14 compounds with k*≈3, reasonable elution times, and excellent separation.

5
教师补充讲解要点 Instructor’s Key Supplementary Points

教师强调，用等度洗脱固定组成时，常见现象是前面组分分辨率太低，后面组分保留时间太长造成峰拖尾，甚至无法出峰。 The instructor emphasized that with isocratic elution using fixed composition, early peaks are poorly resolved and later peaks have excessive retention times and tailing, sometimes never eluting.

分析样品种类越复杂，一般洗脱问题越突出，实际难以完全通过单一条件分离。 The more complex the sample, the more significant the general elution problem becomes and single-condition separation becomes impractical.

梯度洗脱/程序升温既能保持分辨率又能缩短分析时间，是最有效的复杂混合物分离方式。 Gradient elution or temperature programming can maximize resolution and minimize analysis time, making them the most effective methods for separating complex mixtures.

老师还提到，实际操作可先进行等度条件的初筛，发现分离不足再采用梯度或温度程序优化。 The instructor added that in practice, one may begin screening under isocratic conditions and switch to gradient or temperature-programmed approaches if separation is insufficient.

6
结论及建议 Conclusion and Recommendation

对于复杂样品，建议优先考虑梯度洗脱（LC）或程序升温（GC），实现高效、全面洗脱所有目标组分。 For complex samples, it is recommended to prioritize gradient elution (LC) or temperature programming (GC) to efficiently elute all target components.

梯度洗脱 Gradient Elution

梯度洗脱的使用原因 Reasons of Use for Gradient Elution

当混合物中组分的保留因子范围超过 1≤k≤101≤k≤10（主要原因），需采用梯度洗脱。 When the retention factor range for mixture components exceeds 1≤k≤101≤k≤10 (main reason), gradient elution is required.

如果所有组分都能在 1≤k≤101≤k≤10 范围之内，则等度洗脱条件下通常能获得较好分离。但当有的组分 kk 很小、洗脱很快，而有的 kk 很大、洗脱极慢时，就会出现一般洗脱问题。 If all components fall within 1≤k≤101≤k≤10, isocratic elution can usually achieve good separation. However, when some components have very small kk and elute quickly, while others have large kk and elute very slowly, the general elution problem arises.

教师补充
梯度洗脱适用于待分离组分极性差异大、复杂样品，能兼顾前后组分分离和洗脱效率，是解决复杂混合物分离难题的有效技术。 Teacher’s note: Gradient elution is suitable for mixtures with a wide range of polarities and complex samples, balancing separation and elution efficiency for all components, and it is an effective solution to the separation of complex mixtures.

梯度洗脱形状 Gradient Elution Shapes

梯度洗脱通过控制流动相中某一成分（常为有机相%）的随时间变化方式，实现分离优化。 Gradient elution optimizes separation by controlling the time-dependent change of a mobile phase component (usually the percent organic).

常见梯度曲线主要有六种
The common types of gradient curves mainly include six shapes:
1
线性梯度（Linear）：%B随时间均匀上升。适用大多数情况，便于操作与重现。 Linear: %B increases uniformly with time; suitable for most cases, easy to operate and reproduce.
2
凸形梯度（Convex）：初期升高慢，后期增加快。前期保留组分充分展开。 Convex: slow increase at first, fast in the latter stage; allows early eluting compounds to separate well.
3
凹形梯度（Concave）：初期升高快，后期增加缓慢，有利于迅速洗脱强保留组分。 Concave: fast rise at first, slow later; benefits rapid elution of highly retained compounds.
4
分段梯度（Segmented）：由不同斜率线性部分组合，对分离复杂样品非常有效。 Segmented: composed of multiple linear segments with different slopes; highly effective for separating complex samples.
5
梯度延迟（Gradient Delay）：前段延迟后再开始梯度，上样后有预留平衡时间。 Gradient Delay: initial flat segment before gradient starts, allowing for equilibrium after sample injection.
6
阶梯梯度（Step Gradient）：流动相组成突变，多用于预先分组、粗分后细分。 Step Gradient: sudden changes in mobile phase composition, often for preliminary fractionation before finer separations.
教师补充讲解
Teacher’s supplement:

线性梯度最广泛应用，便于设置、结果易与文献对比。 Linear gradients are most widely used because of easy programming and reproducibility.

分段梯度可根据样品分布特性，针对性地优化每段分离效率，是复杂样品分离的高级方案。 Segmented gradients can be tailored to sample composition and maximize separation for each segment, being an advanced solution for complex samples.

凹形、凸形和阶梯梯度适用于特定分离目标，调整策略以平衡分辨率和洗脱时间。 Concave, convex, and step gradients are fit for particular separation goals, allowing for a balance between resolution and run time.

总结 Summary

梯度洗脱被广泛用于解决保留范围过宽、组分结构复杂混合物的分离难题。 Gradient elution is widely used to tackle the problem of broad retention range and complex mixture separation.

梯度类型多样，应根据目标样品成分、分离难度和所需效果合理选择与优化。 Various gradient types exist; selection and optimization depend on the sample, separation challenge, and required result.

色谱法的应用⟶Applications of Chromatographic Methods

1
定性分析 Qualitative Analysis

色谱定性分析基于溶质的保留时间进行判断。 Qualitative chromatographic analysis is based on the retention time of the solute.

能获得的结构信息有限，依赖于检测器的类型。 The amount of structural information is limited and depends on the type of detector.

可以确定混合物中某种成分的存在与否。 It can determine the presence or absence of a compound in a mixture.

在鉴定前先对溶质进行有效分离，提高鉴定可靠性。 Separation of the solute is performed before identification, increasing reliability.

2
定量分析 Quantitative Analysis

定量分析是色谱法的主要应用领域。 Quantitative analysis is the major application of chromatography.

通过对比峰面积或峰高与一个或多个标准的对应参数来进行含量测定。 Quantification is achieved by comparison of peak area or peak height with one or more standards.

峰面积更常用于定量分析，因为它不受带展宽影响，结果更可靠。 Peak areas are more frequently used because they are independent of band broadening, making the results more reliable.

3
教师补充要点 Instructor’s Supplementary Points
教师特别强调
色谱法在现代实验分析中是最重要的定量和定性工具之一。 The instructor highlighted that chromatography is one of the most important tools for both quantitative and qualitative analysis in modern laboratory work.

定性分析多与特征保留时间结合其他检测指标共同判别，不同类型的检测器结合可增强结构解析能力。 Qualitative analysis generally combines characteristic retention times with other detection features; using different types of detectors together can improve structural identification.

定量分析要求操作条件高度稳定，否则峰面积与峰高的可比性会受影响。 Quantitative analysis requires very stable operating conditions, as inconsistency affects comparability of peak area and height.

采用峰面积优于峰高，尤其在带宽显著变化时，面积法更能反映真实浓度。 Using peak area is superior to peak height, especially when peak width varies, as area better reflects true concentration.

高效液相色谱法（HPLC）High Performance Liquid Chromatography (HPLC)

色谱柱效率与带展宽 Column Efficiency and Band Broadening in LC

1
色谱柱中带展宽的来源 Sources of Band Broadening in Columns

色谱柱效率主要通过塔板高度 HH 衡量。 Column efficiency is mainly measured by the plate height (H\

色谱带展宽可分为柱内（Intra-column）和柱外（Extra-column）两部分。 Band broadening can be categorized as intra-column and extra-column.

2
Van Deemter方程 Van Deemter Equation

4
影响柱效的参数及其物理意义 Key Parameters and Physical Meaning

颗粒粒径的重要性与数值关系 Importance of Particle Size

流动相中质量转移阻力（CMCM​项）与填料粒径平方成正比。 Mass transfer resistance in mobile phase (CMCM​ term) is proportional to the square of particle diameter.

颗粒粒径减小，CMCM​项下降，塔板高度降低。 Smaller particle size results in decreased CMCM​, leading to a lower plate height.

实验证明
粒径从44.7 μm降至更小值，塔板高度显著降低，分离效率明显提高。 Experimentally, when particle size decreases from 44.7 μm to smaller values, plate height drops markedly and separation efficiency improves.
小颗粒与柱回压
性能与压力的权衡 Small Particles: Performance vs. Pressure

填料颗粒越小，可显著提高色谱分离性能，但同时导致柱压增加。 The smaller the packing particle size, the better the chromatographic performance, but column pressure increases.

小颗粒→高性能，但需高压系统（高效液相及超高效液相色谱系统）。 Smaller particles mean higher performance, but require higher pressure systems (HPLC, UHPLC).

教师讲解补充 Instructor’s Lecture Supplement

教师强调
各项带展宽贡献在不同线速度区间主导作用不同。降低粒径同时优化流速，可最大化分离效率。 Instructor stressed: Each type of band broadening dominates at different velocity ranges. Reducing particle size while optimizing flow rate maximizes separation efficiency.

小颗粒提高分辨率但增加能耗与设备要求，实际应用中需平衡性能和成本。 Smaller particles enhance resolution but increase energy consumption and equipment demands; a balance between performance and cost is needed in real applications.

色谱柱背压与流动相和溶剂对分离的影响 Column Backpressure & Effect of Mobile Phase and Solvent on Separation

一、色谱柱背压与颗粒粒径 Column Backpressure and Particle Size

色谱柱背压 ΔPΔP 指流动相推动通过色谱柱时产生的压力差。 Column backpressure (ΔPΔP) refers to the pressure drop as the mobile phase is pushed through the chromatographic column.

Darcy方程用于描述色谱柱背压的计算： The Darcy equation is used to calculate column backpressure:   ΔP=η u Φr Ldp2ΔP=dp2​ηuΦr​L​

ηη
流动相黏度（mobile phase viscosity） ηη: mobile phase viscosity
uu
流动相线速度（linear velocity of mobile phase） uu: linear velocity of mobile phase
ΦrΦr​
柱流阻系数（column flow resistance factor） ΦrΦr​: column flow resistance factor
LL
色谱柱长度（column length） LL: column length
dpdp​
填料颗粒直径（diameter of packing particles） dpdp​: particle diameter

颗粒越小，分离性能越高，但柱压显著升高。 The smaller the particle size, the higher the separation performance, but the column pressure increases significantly.

小颗粒带来高效分离，但要求色谱仪具备较高的承压能力。 Small particles deliver higher efficiency but require chromatographic instruments with greater pressure tolerance.

教师补充
随着粒径减小，塔板高度下降，柱效率上升，但系统更需高压泵（HPLC/UHPLC）。 Instructor supplement: As particle size decreases, plate height drops and column efficiency rises, but stronger high-pressure pumps are needed (HPLC/UHPLC systems) 112.

二、影响洗脱的分子间相互作用 Intermolecular Interactions Affecting Elution

分子间作用力决定溶质在固定相和流动相间的分配行为，是色谱分离的基础。 Intermolecular forces determine the distribution of solutes between stationary and mobile phases, forming the basis for chromatographic separation.

主要分子间作用力有
The main types of intermolecular interactions include:
1
色散力（Dispersion）：普遍存在，非特异性，多见于疏水作用。 Dispersion: universal and nonspecific, major in hydrophobic interactions.
2
极性-极性（Dipole-dipole）：发生在有永久偶极矩分子间，依赖偶极大小。 Dipole-dipole: between molecules with permanent dipole moments, depends on dipole strength.
3
氢键作用（Hydrogen bonding）：质子给体与受体之间（溶质或溶剂）。 Hydrogen bonding: between proton donors and acceptors (solute or solvent).
4
离子作用（Ionic/coulombic）：离子与极性溶剂或离子相互作用，随介电常数增加而增强。 Ionic (coulombic): between ions and polar solvents or other ions, increases with dielectric constant.
5
电荷转移作用（Charge transfer）：多见于芳香族、非饱和体系间。 Charge transfer: often between aromatic or unsaturated species.

这些相互作用决定了化合物在色谱条件下的保留与分离。 These interactions are responsible for compound retention and separation under chromatographic conditions.

三、常见溶剂性质对选择性的影响 Solvent Properties Affecting Selectivity

溶剂的酸性、碱性、偶极性等性质决定其对分离选择性的影响。 Solvent acidity, basicity, and dipolarity affect their impact on selectivity.

常用参数包括
Key parameters include:

氢键酸性 (α/Σα/Σ)（H-B Acidity） H-bond acidity ((\alpha/\Sigma\

氢键碱性 (β/Σβ/Σ)（H-B Basicity） H-bond basicity (β/Σβ/Σ)

偶极性 (π∗/Σπ∗/Σ)（Dipolarity） Dipolarity (π∗/Σπ∗/Σ)

极性指数（Polarity index, P′P′） Polarity index ((P^\prime\

介电常数（Dielectric constant, ϵϵ） Dielectric constant (ϵϵ)

不同溶剂（如水、乙腈、二甲基亚砜等）在这些参数上的差异显著影响分离表现。 Variation of these values among solvents (e.g., water, acetonitrile, DMSO) has a critical influence on separation results.

教师强调，正确选择溶剂和极性可有效调控组分的保留和分辨率。 The instructor highlighted that proper solvent and polarity selection can effectively regulate analyte retention and resolution.

四、溶剂选择性三角与方法调控 Solvent Selectivity Triangle & Method Control

非离子溶剂的极性相互作用可用“溶剂选择性三角”图进行分类与对比。 Polar interactions in non-ionic solvents can be classified and compared using the "solvent-selectivity triangle" diagram.

溶剂三角顶点分别代表氢键酸性、氢键碱性和偶极性三大极性。 The triangle vertices represent H-bond acidity, H-bond basicity, and dipolarity.

各类溶剂在三角图中的分布反映其选择性本质。 The distribution of solvents in the triangle reflects their intrinsic selectivity properties.

分析和方法开发时可利用三角图调节分离选择性，实现最佳分离。 During analysis and method development, the triangle can be used to fine-tune selectivity and achieve optimal separation.

教师补充
通过有针对性地选择流动相溶剂类型和比例，可对色谱方法的选择性实现有效控制。 Teacher’s note: By selectively adjusting the types and proportions of solvents in the mobile phase, method selectivity can be finely controlled.

电泳的定义与原理 Definition and Theory of Electrophoresis

电泳定义
电泳是分散粒子在空间均匀电场影响下相对于流体的运动。  → Definition: Electrophoresis is the motion of dispersed particles relative to a fluid under the influence of a spatially uniform electric field.

1948年Tiselius因蛋白质分离获得诺贝尔奖。  → In 1948, Tiselius won the Nobel Prize for protein separation.

电泳的基本原理（结合PPT与录音内容整理）：  → Basic principles of electrophoresis (integrating PPT and lecture audio):

当带电粒子（如分子或离子）处于溶液中并施加电场，粒子会向某一方向移动，称为电泳运动。   → When charged particles (such as molecules or ions) are in solution and an electric field is applied, the particles will move in a specific direction, known as electrophoresis.

持续的恒定速度下，保持粒子的恒速需要有两种力平衡：电场力和摩擦力（阻力/粘滞力）。   → To maintain constant velocity, two opposing forces must be balanced: electric force and friction force (drag/viscous force).

在电场作用下，电场力 F = qE，其中 q 为粒子电荷量，E 为电场强度。   → Under an electric field, the electric force F = qE, where q is the particle’s charge and E is the electric field strength.

颗粒在溶液中受到的摩擦力遵循斯托克斯定律 F’ = 6πηrv，其中 η 为溶液粘度，r 为粒子半径，v 为速度。   → The friction force follows Stokes’ law: F’ = 6πηrv, where η is the solution’s viscosity, r is the radius of the particle, and v is the particle’s velocity.

当两种力平衡时，粒子达到恒定速度，即电场力=qE=6πηrv。   → When the two forces are balanced, the particle reaches constant velocity: qE = 6πηrv.

电泳迁移率（μ）定义为单位电场下分子的速度：μ = v/E，所以 μ = q/(6πηr)。   → Electrophoretic mobility (μ) is defined as the velocity per unit electric field: μ = v/E, thus μ = q/(6πηr).

电泳迁移率受分子电荷（q↑）、分子大小（r↑）、溶液粘度（η↑）影响，分别正相关与反相关。   → Electrophoretic mobility is influenced by particle charge (q↑), particle size (r↑), and solution viscosity (η↑), being positive and inversely correlated respectively.

通过实验测定电泳迁移率，可以反推分子的电荷或大小。   → By measuring electrophoretic mobility experimentally, the charge or size of the molecule can be inferred.

电泳的种类
→ Categories of electrophoresis:

管内电泳（非常早期的版本）。   → In tubing (very old version).

支持介质上的区域电泳（常规使用）。   → Zone electrophoresis on support (commonly used).

等速电泳。   → Isotachophoresis.

电泳常见应用与类型（PPT简列，后续课件会展开）：  → Common applications and types of electrophoresis (PPT preview, will be detailed later):

1
理论基础 Theoretical   2. 琼脂糖凝胶电泳（针对DNA）Agarose gel electrophoresis (for DNA)   3. 聚丙烯酰胺凝胶电泳（针对蛋白）Polyacrylamide electrophoresis (for proteins)   4. 毛细管电泳 Capillary electrophoresis

课堂补充要点（录音补充）：  → Additional key points (from the lecture audio):

究竟有哪些力作用在运动的粒子上？主要是电场力和粘滞/摩擦力。   → What forces act on the moving particle? Mainly electrical force and viscous/frictional force.

粒子带电越多，受力越大，迁移率越高；粒子越大或溶液粘度越大，阻力越大，迁移率越低。   → The more charged a particle is, the greater the force and the higher the mobility; the larger the particle or the higher the viscosity, the greater the friction, and the lower the mobility.

平衡条件下粒子匀速运动，等式 qE = 6πηrv 成立。   → Under equilibrium, the particle moves at constant speed, and the equation qE = 6πηrv holds.

电泳分析常通过实验测定迁移率，然后推算分子的性质。   → In electrophoresis, properties of molecules are often inferred by experimentally determining their mobility.

核心公式（文字格式）：  → Key equations (plain text):

电场力
F = q E   → Electric force: F = q E

摩擦力（斯托克斯定律）：F' = 6 π η r v   → Friction force (Stokes' law): F' = 6 pi η r v

平衡条件
q E = 6 π η r v   → At equilibrium: q E = 6 pi η r v
电泳迁移率
μ = q / (6 π η r)   → Electrophoretic mobility: μ = q / (6 pi η r)

电泳支持介质类型Types of supports in electrophoresis

纸（吸附，高电渗作用）Paper (adsorption, elevated electroendosmosis)

纸电泳常用于基础实验，通过吸附作用和电渗流影响分离效果。

Paper electrophoresis is commonly used in basic experiments, where separation is affected by adsorption and electroendosmosis.

电场两端放置纸条，样品点在中间，阴离子和阳离子分别向负极和正极迁移。

Paper strips are placed between two reservoirs, sample is loaded in the middle; negative ions migrate to the cathode and positive ions migrate to the anode.

醋酸纤维素Cellulose Acetate

适用于小分子（如氨基酸、核苷酸）和带电的大分子（如脂蛋白、血红蛋白、糖蛋白、同工酶）。

Suitable for small molecules (such as amino acids and nucleotides) and charged macromolecules (such as lipoproteins, hemoglobins, glycoproteins, and isoenzymes).

凝胶Gels

聚丙烯酰胺凝胶用于分离蛋白质。

Polyacrylamide gel is used for protein separation.

琼脂糖凝胶用于分离核酸（DNA、RNA）。

Agarose gel is used for nucleic acid (DNA, RNA) separation.

淀粉凝胶主要用于高分子聚合物分离。

Starch gel is mainly for separation of high molecular weight polymers.

醋酸纤维素电泳Electrophoresis on cellulose acetate

每个葡萄糖单元含2-3个乙酰基。

There are 2-3 acetyl groups per unit of glucose.

吸附作用较低，条带更清晰锐利，优于纸电泳。

Less adsorption and sharper bands than paper electrophoresis.

蛋白质或亲水性材料无拖尾现象。

No tailing of proteins or hydrophilic materials.

可选用多样尺寸和不同层厚度。

Wide range of available sizes and layer thicknesses.

分辨率高，适合复杂样品分析。

High resolution, suitable for complex sample analysis.

可用较高电压，提升分离效率。

Possible to apply relatively high voltage to enhance separation.

琼脂糖Agarose

中性多糖，从琼脂（红藻）中提取，无毒。

Neutral polysaccharide extracted from agar (red seaweed), non-toxic.

制备简便，常用浓度0.5%-2%。

Simple preparation, typically used at 0.5%-2% concentration.

高温水中溶解，冷却后成凝胶。

Dissolve in hot water, then cool down to form a gel.

孔径较大（1%凝胶孔径约100-500纳米）。

Large pore sizes (1% gel: about 100 nm - 500 nm).

适合高分子量大分子的分离
如DNA（200-50000碱基对）、大型蛋白。

Suitable for separation of high molecular weight macromolecules: e.g., DNA (200 to 50,000 bp), large proteins.

大分子建议低浓度凝胶（0.15%-0.9%），小分子用高浓度凝胶，但运行时间较长。

For larger molecules, use low-concentration gels (0.15%-0.9%); for smaller molecules, use high-concentration gels, but longer run times may be required.

分辨率低，不及聚丙烯酰胺凝胶。

Low resolution, not as high as polyacrylamide gel.

琼脂糖凝胶DNA电泳(Agarose gel) DNA electrophoresis

线性DNA片段迁移速度与分子量对数成反比。

Linear DNA fragments migrate with mobility inversely proportional to the logarithm of molecular weight.

DNA在电泳时移向阳极（正极）。

DNA migrates toward the anode (positive electrode) during electrophoresis.

同样分子量情况下，环形DNA与线性DNA迁移行为不同。

With identical molecular weights, circular and linear DNA migrate differently.

实验步骤
加样、上电泳、电泳分离、紫外观察。
Experimental steps
sample loading, applying electric field, separation, UV visualization.

老师课堂补充要点Classroom Additional Points：

支持介质选择影响分离分辨率和适用样品类型。

Choice of support medium affects separation resolution and applicable sample types.

醋酸纤维素因吸附小、分辨高，常用于高精度临床或科研分析。

Cellulose acetate is suitable for high-precision clinical or research analysis due to low adsorption and high resolution.

琼脂糖凝胶适用于大分子（如长段DNA），常用于分子生物学实验。

Agarose gel is suitable for large molecules (such as long DNA fragments), commonly used in molecular biology experiments.

每种凝胶的孔径和性质决定了其用于分离蛋白质还是核酸，需结合实际样品选择合适材料。

The pore size and properties of each gel determine whether it is suitable for protein or nucleic acid separation; proper material is selected based on the actual sample.

DNA凝胶标记DNA gel labeling

DNA可以在电泳分离前或分离后进行标记，常用的方法是在凝胶中直接标记。

DNA can be labeled either before separation or after (in-gel), with in-gel labeling being common.

常用的DNA染色剂包括嵌插剂如溴化乙锭（Ethidium bromide, BET），碘化丙啶（Propidium iodide, PI）及噻氨（Cyanine）衍生物。

Common DNA stains include intercalating agents such as ethidium bromide (BET), propidium iodide (PI), and cyanine derivatives.

溴化乙锭（BET）Ethidium Bromide (BET)

溴化乙锭是一种常用的DNA嵌插性荧光染料，浓度约0.5 μg/mL。

Ethidium bromide is a commonly used intercalating fluorescent dye for DNA, typically at 0.5 μg/mL.

当暴露于紫外光下时，BET会发出红橙色荧光，与DNA结合后荧光强度是游离状态的20倍。

When exposed to UV, BET fluoresces red-orange, and is 20 times more intense when bound to DNA.

BET主要嵌插进双链DNA，对于不成对的RNA染色信号远低于DNA。

BET primarily intercalates into double-stranded DNA, and RNA shows much weaker staining at equal weight.

BET可在制胶时与琼脂糖混和，或电泳后将凝胶浸入BET溶液中实现染色。

BET can be mixed with agarose during gel preparation, or the gel can be stained post-electrophoresis by immersion in BET solution.

碘化丙啶（PI）Propidium Iodide (PI)

PI为荧光分子（分子量668,403 Da），每4-5个碱基对结合1个PI分子。

PI is a fluorescent molecule (molecular weight 668,403 Da), binding 1 PI molecule per 4-5 base pairs.

它可与DNA碱基结合，且几乎无序列特异性。

PI binds to DNA bases with little or no sequence specificity.

碘化丙啶也可与RNA结合，因此通常需核酸酶处理样品以消除假阳性结果。

PI can also bind to RNA, so RNase treatment is needed to degrade RNA and avoid false positives.

与核酸结合的PI分子其荧光强度是游离状态下的20-30倍。

Nucleic acid-bound PI is 20-30 times more fluorescent than free PI.

PI还常用于检测细胞活性，可标记细胞膜完整性丧失的细胞核。

PI is also used as a marker of cell viability, labeling nuclei in cells with compromised membrane integrity.

噻氨染料衍生物Cyanine Derivatives

噻氨类是一类属于多亚甲基基团的合成荧光标记物，可用于DNA标记。

Cyanines are a synthetic family of fluorescent markers with a polymethine group, used for DNA labeling.

不同的噻氨染料有不同的激发和发射波长，常见的有Cy2、Cy3、Cy3.5、Cy5、Cy7等。

Different cyanine dyes have distinct excitation and emission wavelengths, e.g., Cy2, Cy3, Cy3.5, Cy5, Cy7.

噻氨标记可多色同步检测不同样品或不同片段。

Cyanine labeling enables multiplex detection of different samples or fragments.

新型安全DNA染料
GelGreen New Safe DNA Dye: GelGreen

GelGreen是一种绿色荧光核酸染料，可标记dsDNA、ssDNA或RNA，灵敏度高，稳定性好。

GelGreen is a green fluorescent nucleic acid dye for staining dsDNA, ssDNA, or RNA, with high sensitivity and stability.

GelGreen相比溴化乙锭更安全，便于处理，极其稳定，易于操作，并兼容标准UV透射仪。

GelGreen is safer than ethidium bromide, easy to dispose of, extremely stable, simple to use, and compatible with standard UV transilluminators.

其灵敏度高于常规染料且在水解和加热条件下也很稳定。

It is more sensitive than conventional dyes and remains stable under hydrolytic and thermal stress.

老师讲解要点补充Classroom Key Points Supplement

常用DNA标记方法是通过荧光嵌插剂在电泳后直接对DNA进行染色，因操作简便且检测灵敏。

The standard method for DNA labeling uses fluorescent intercalators for direct post-electrophoresis staining due to simplicity and sensitivity.

不同染料选择主要依据样品是DNA还是RNA，以及实验目的来决定，若需专一检测DNA，需用核酸酶预处理去除RNA。

Choice of dye depends on whether the target is DNA or RNA and the experimental goal; nuclease pretreatment of samples can be used to remove RNA for DNA-specific detection.

检测步骤通常为电泳分离后，加入染色剂，再用紫外或可见光照射观察条带。

The detection step usually involves post-electrophoresis staining, followed by visualization under UV or visible light.

相较溴化乙锭，新型如GelGreen等染料更安全、灵敏且兼容现有成像设备，推荐在常规实验中优先选用。

Compared to ethidium bromide, new dyes like GelGreen are safer, more sensitive, and compatible with existing imaging systems, thus recommended for routine experiments.

SDS-PAGE及其原理与应用SDS-PAGE: Principle and Application

SDS-PAGE基础原理与步骤 Basic Principle and Procedure of SDS-PAGE

十二烷基硫酸钠（SDS）是一种阴离子型去污剂，能够包裹并包被蛋白质，形成胶束或复合物。

Sodium dodecyl sulfate (SDS) is an anionic detergent that wraps around proteins and forms micelles or complexes.

SDS可使所有蛋白带大量负电荷，并消除其原有的空间结构和电荷差异，使蛋白链变为近似线性的状态。

SDS imparts a strong negative charge to all proteins and removes the influence of their original shape and charge differences, transforming the protein chains into nearly linear conformations.

由于SDS结合比例固定（约每2个氨基酸结合1个SDS分子），蛋白质获得了相同的单位质量电荷密度。

Because the binding ratio of SDS is fixed (about one SDS molecule for every two amino acids), proteins acquire an identical charge per unit mass.

分离机制与意义 Separation Mechanism and Significance

蛋白质在聚丙烯酰胺凝胶中，仅依据分子大小进行分离，分子量越小迁移越快，分子量越大迁移越慢。

Proteins are separated in polyacrylamide gel solely by molecular size; smaller proteins migrate faster, larger ones more slowly.

SDS-PAGE可排除蛋白质天然电荷和结构的影响，实现蛋白质“单一变量”（分子量）分离。

SDS-PAGE eliminates the effects of native charge and structure, allowing “single variable” (molecular weight only) separation.

SDS层析的实验流程与图解 Experimental Procedure and Diagram

上样前蛋白需与SDS充分孵育（通常同时加还原剂如β-巯基乙醇，断裂二硫键，实现彻底变性）。

Proteins should be fully incubated with SDS before sample loading (and usually with a reducing agent like β-mercaptoethanol to break disulfide bonds and achieve complete denaturation).

蛋白SDS包被后加载至堆积胶（stacking gel）上，电场作用下蛋白进入分离胶（resolving gel）分离。

SDS-coated proteins are loaded onto the stacking gel; under an electric field, proteins migrate into the resolving gel for separation.

分离完成后染色（如考马斯亮蓝或银染）以显现条带。

After separation, staining (such as with Coomassie Brilliant Blue or silver stain) is used to visualize the bands.

实验数据通过泳动距离与对数分子量作图，以便分子量分析。

Experimental data are plotted as migration distance versus log(molecular weight) for molecular weight analysis.

SDS-PAGE的应用范围与不同浓度胶选择 Application and Gel Concentration Selection

根据蛋白分子量选择不同丙烯酰胺浓度的胶：

Different acrylamide concentrations are chosen according to the molecular weight of proteins:

5%
适合分离40-400 kDa蛋白。 7.5%: for 40-400 kDa proteins.
5%
适合分离20-100 kDa蛋白。 12.5%: for 20-100 kDa proteins.
20%
适合分离3-25 kDa蛋白。 20%: for 3-25 kDa proteins.

16.5%（Tris-Tricine）：适合分离2-25 kDa蛋白。 16.5% (Tris-Tricine): for 2-25 kDa proteins.

课堂补充要点 Classroom Key Points

SDS通过其疏水链包裹蛋白质主链，硫酸根暴露在外，使所有蛋白带大量负电荷。

SDS wraps around the protein backbone with its hydrophobic chain, leaving sulfate groups exposed and imparting strong negative charge to all proteins.

样品仅按大小分离，彻底消除电荷本身和结构对迁移的干扰。

Samples are separated solely based on size, completely eliminating interference from inherent charge and structure.

老师强调
SDS-PAGE特别适用于复杂蛋白混合物（如血清、细胞裂解液）的目标蛋白分离与分析，是生物医学和分子生物学研究中最常用的蛋白分析方法之一。

Instructor emphasizes: SDS-PAGE is especially suitable for target protein isolation and analysis from complex mixtures (such as serum or cell lysates), making it one of the most widely used protein analysis methods in biomedical and molecular biology research24.

公式表达 Formula Representation

迁移距离与分子量关系
log⁡(MW)∼distancelog(MW)∼distance Relationship between migration distance and molecular weight: log⁡(MW)∼distancelog(MW)∼distance

蛋白SDS变性后单位质量电荷密度相同，迁移速率仅由分子大小决定。

After SDS-denaturation, proteins have the same charge per unit mass, and migration rate is determined only by molecular size.

总结 Summary

SDS-PAGE是一种高分辨率蛋白分离技术，操作简便，结果清晰可靠，为蛋白定性和分子量分析提供重要工具。

SDS-PAGE is a high-resolution protein separation technique, easy to perform, with clear and reliable results, providing a key tool for protein characterization and molecular weight analysis.

PAGE及天然胶电泳PAGE and Native Gel Electrophoresis

电泳迁移依赖于蛋白质的形状、大小以及在工作pH下的电荷。 Migration depends on shape, size, and the charge of the protein at working pH.

天然胶电泳条件下无变性，无解离，蛋白质保持本来结构。 No denaturing and no dissociation occur under native gel conditions; proteins retain their native structure.

这种方法适合分析少量已知蛋白，尤其在需研究蛋白空间结构和功能状态时有独特优势。但混合样品分离复杂、不易解析各组分。 This method is suitable for analyzing a small quantity of known proteins, and is particularly valuable when studying protein conformation and functional state. However, separation of mixtures with many proteins can be complicated and hard to interpret.

蛋白样品加入凝胶孔后，依据本身电荷、结构和分子量，通过外加电场向正极或负极迁移，形成特定迁移速度和带型。 After sample loading into gel wells, proteins migrate towards the anode or cathode under an electric field, and migration speed/band pattern is determined by intrinsic charge, structure, and size.

课堂补充解析
蛋白质本身的电荷可因为pH不同而变化，因此在天然胶电泳中，迁移方向和速度与实际缓冲液pH密切相关。 Additional classroom explanation: Protein charge changes with pH, so in native PAGE, migration direction and speed are closely related to the buffer pH.

SDS-PAGE及其原理与应用 SDS-PAGE: Principle and Application

SDS即十二烷基硫酸钠，是一种阴离子型去污剂，可将蛋白质包裹并形成胶束或复合物。 SDS, sodium dodecyl sulfate, is an anionic detergent that forms micelles or complexes around proteins.

所有蛋白被SDS包裹后都带强烈负电荷，天然结构和原有电荷被消除，蛋白链趋近于线性，单位质量电荷密度一致。 Once coated with SDS, all proteins acquire a strong negative charge, and their native structure and original charge are eliminated, resulting in nearly linear chains with identical charge density per mass.

分离机制
蛋白质仅根据分子大小进行分离，分子量越小迁移越快，分子量越大迁移越慢，能彻底排除形状和固有电荷对迁移的影响。 Mechanism: proteins are separated solely by molecular size; smaller proteins migrate faster, larger migrate slower, with intrinsic charge and shape effects removed.
SDS-PAGE流程如下
蛋白样品与SDS及还原剂共同变性，上样，电泳分离，染色（如考马斯亮蓝或银染）分析。 SDS-PAGE procedure: samples are denatured with SDS and a reducing agent, loaded, separated by electrophoresis, and visualized by staining (Coomassie blue or silver stain).

对照分子量的标准品，泳动距离与log⁡(分子量)log(分子量)成线性关系，可用于蛋白分子量推算。 With molecular weight standards, migration distance relates linearly to log⁡(molecular weight)log(molecular weight), enabling MW estimation.

常用胶浓度选择
7.5%丙烯酰胺胶适合40–400 kDa蛋白，12.5%适合20–100 kDa，20%适合3–25 kDa，16.5%（Tris-Tricine系统）适合2–25 kDa。 Common gel concentrations: 7.5% acrylamide for 40-400 kDa, 12.5% for 20-100 kDa, 20% for 3-25 kDa, 16.5% (Tris-Tricine) for 2-25 kDa proteins.
课堂要点补充
SDS通过其疏水链包裹蛋白主链，硫酸根暴露在外，使所有蛋白最终带大量负电荷，实现统一迁移机制。 Classroom key point: SDS wraps around the protein's backbone via its hydrophobic chain, exposing sulfate groups for strong, uniform negative charge, ensuring unified migration.

PAGE胶电泳结果显色方法 Revelation of PAGE Gels: Detection Methods

考马斯亮蓝（Coomassie blue）：非特异性染色，灵敏度高（30–50 ng），与质谱兼容，主要与精氨酸、酪氨酸、色氨酸、苯丙氨酸、组氨酸等氨基酸结合，带蓝色。 Coomassie blue: non-specific staining, high sensitivity (30–50 ng), MS compatible, binds to arginine, tyrosine, tryptophan, phenylalanine, histidine; blue coloration.

硝酸银染色（Silver nitrate）：非特异，灵敏度高（0.5–1.5 ng），但定量范围窄，流程较长，更适用于微量蛋白检测。 Silver nitrate: non-specific, very high sensitivity (0.5-1.5 ng), narrow quantification range, long protocol, suitable for trace protein detection.

锌铜染料
提供负染（蛋白条带为浅色斑），易操作，价格低，灵敏度好。 Zinc/copper dye: negative stain (proteins as light spots), easy, low-cost, high sensitivity (6–12 ng).
荧光标记物
选择性强，易于操作但价格昂贵，能定量分析，且灵敏度高（如SYPRO ruby，1–2 ng）。 Fluorescent markers: highly selective, easy to use but costly, allow quantitative analysis, high sensitivity (e.g., SYPRO ruby, 1–2 ng).

PAGE电泳后免疫检测法 Immunodetection after PAGE: Western Blot

蛋白转膜（如硝酸纤维素膜），抗体杂交，底物显色或发光成像，常用于蛋白特异性检测与功能分析。 Protein transfer to membrane (e.g., nitrocellulose), antibody hybridization, substrate-based color or chemiluminescence imaging; frequently used for specific protein detection and functional studies.

课堂补充要点 Classroom Supplement Points

蛋白电泳条带强度与浓度成正比，可用于定量分析，但需标准品对照。 The intensity of protein bands is proportional to concentration and can be used for quantitation with standards.

染色剂选择取决于实验灵敏度需求、是否需后续分析（如MS）、样品量等因素。 Choice of stain depends on sensitivity needs, follow-up analysis (e.g., MS compatibility), and sample amount.

核心总结 Key Summary

PAGE和SDS-PAGE电泳是蛋白分离与分析的核心技术，根据变性与否、染色方法及检测灵敏度选择最佳方案。 PAGE and SDS-PAGE are core technologies for protein separation and analysis, with denaturing status, staining method, and detection sensitivity dictating optimal scheme selection.

电泳后染色结果不仅可确定蛋白条带位置，还可用于定量分析与进一步功能研究。 Post-electrophoresis staining identifies band position and allows quantitation and further functional studies.

Southern/Northern/Western杂交技术对比、等电聚焦(IEF)与二维电泳（2D-PAGE）Southern/Northern/Western Blot Comparison, Isoelectric Focusing (IEF), and 2D Electrophoresis

1
Southern/Northern/Western杂交技术对比 Comparison of Southern, Northern, and Western Blotting Techniques

检测分子种类 Molecule Detected Southern杂交检测DNA（双链），Northern检测mRNA（单链），Western检测蛋白质。 Southern blotting detects DNA (double-stranded), Northern detects mRNA (single-stranded), Western detects proteins.

电泳胶体 Gel Electrophoresis Southern用琼脂糖胶，Northern用甲醛变性琼脂糖胶，Western用聚丙烯酰胺凝胶。 Southern uses agarose gel, Northern uses formaldehyde-agarose gel, Western uses polyacrylamide gel.

凝胶预处理 Gel Pretreatment Southern需酸性、碱性处理及中和，Northern和Western一般无需预处理。 Southern requires depurination, denaturation, and neutralization; Northern and Western generally do not.

转膜方式 Blotting Method Southern和Northern常用毛细管转移，Western用电转移。 Southern and Northern use capillary transfer; Western uses electric transfer.

探针类型 Probes Southern用DNA探针（放射性或非放射性），Northern用cDNA/cRNA探针，Western用一抗（蛋白抗体）。 Southern employs DNA probes (radioactive or nonradioactive), Northern uses cDNA/cRNA, Western uses primary antibody.

检测系统 Detection System Southern/Northern可用自显影、化学发光和比色法，Western常用化学发光或比色法。 Southern/Northern can use autoradiography, chemiluminescence, or colorimetric detection; Western typically uses chemiluminescence or colorimetric methods.

2
等电聚焦IEF原理及特点 Principle and Characteristics of Isoelectric Focusing (IEF)

蛋白质为两性分子（含酸/碱性基团），有缓冲能力。 Proteins are amphoteric molecules (have acidic and basic groups) with buffering capacity.

碱性条件下蛋白带负电，酸性条件下蛋白带正电。 In basic conditions, proteins are negatively charged; in acidic conditions, positively charged.

等电点（pI）：是蛋白质净电荷为零时的pH值。蛋白达到pI时停止迁移。 The isoelectric point (pI) is the pH at which a protein's net charge equals zero; migration stops once the protein reaches its pI.

IEF在pH梯度中进行，分辨率极高。 IEF is conducted in a pH gradient and has high resolution.

示意图说明
凝胶纵向形成pH梯度，蛋白混合物在缓冲电场下迁移，直至各自pI点集中。 Illustration: a pH gradient is established across the gel; proteins migrate under the electric field until they reach their pI, where net charge is zero and they focus into sharp bands.
3
2D电泳Electrophoresis 2D的原理与应用 Principle and Application of 2D Electrophoresis

适合复杂混合物分离，常用于蛋白组学分析。 Suitable for separating complex mixtures, widely used in proteomics.

操作分两步
第一维为IEF分离（按电荷/pI）；第二维为SDS-PAGE分离（按分子量）。 Two-step process: first dimension is IEF for charge/pI separation; second is SDS-PAGE for separation by molecular weight.
2D-PAGE实验流程
2D-PAGE experimental workflow:
1
蛋白先在IEF凝胶条中分离；Each protein is separated along a pH gradient according to its pI.
2
将IEF分离的凝胶条贴在SDS-PAGE凝胶顶端；The IEF gel strip is applied to the top of the SDS-PAGE gel.
3
在SDS-PAGE凝胶中按分子大小进行再次分离，形成典型二维蛋白“地图”。Proteins are separated again by size in SDS-PAGE, resulting in a characteristic 2D protein "map".
优势
极高分辨率，可同时分离大量蛋白，能揭示复杂生物体系差异。 Advantages: extremely high resolution, simultaneous separation of numerous proteins, revealing complexity and differences in biological systems.
注意事项
实验流程长、操作繁琐，高丰度蛋白可能掩盖低丰度蛋白信号。 Notes: process is lengthy and complex; highly abundant proteins may mask signals of low-abundance proteins.
4
教师补充与要点总结 Key Classroom Supplements and Summary

蛋白等电聚焦时在pH梯度中迁移，针对蛋白混合样品可获得高分辨率分离。 During isoelectric focusing, proteins migrate in the pH gradient and achieve high-resolution separation for complex mixtures。

2D电泳核心顺序
先IEF后SDS-PAGE，因IEF后蛋白已失去净电荷，只有之后加SDS才能统一带负电做分子量分离。 The correct 2D order is IEF first, then SDS-PAGE; after IEF, proteins lose their net charge, so SDS is added so all proteins bear uniform negative charge for size-based separation。

2D电泳是目前复杂蛋白样品分离及疾病生物标志物筛查的重要方法。 2D electrophoresis is a leading method for separation of complex protein samples and screening of disease biomarkers。

5
关键术语解释 Key Term Explanations
等电点pI
pH值处，蛋白净电荷为零，不再迁移。 Isoelectric point (pI): the pH at which protein's net charge is zero and migration ceases.
IEF
运用pH梯度分离不同pI蛋白，实现微差分离。 IEF: employs a pH gradient for high-resolution separation of proteins by isoelectric point.

毛细管电泳原理与机理 Schematic and Mechanism of Capillary Electrophoresis

---

**1. 毛细管电泳装置示意 Schematic of Capillary Electrophoresis**

- 毛细管电泳利用细硅毛细管，两端分别连接阳极和阴极缓冲池，中间通过高压电源施加高电压（5-30kV）。

- Capillary electrophoresis uses a fused silica capillary connected to anode and cathode buffer reservoirs, with high voltage (5-30 kV) supplied between both ends.

- 装置包含铂电极、缓冲池、检测器及数据采集系统；毛细管内径10-100μm，长度20-100cm。

- The system includes platinum electrodes, buffer reservoirs, detector, and data acquisition; the capillary is 10-100 μm i.d., 20-100 cm long.

- 主要优点
焦耳热效应易于散失，系统电阻高，可用高电压，分析速度快。
- Main advantages
improved dissipation of Joule heating, high resistance, high voltage operation, and faster analysis.

---

**2. 迁移机理与速度 Migration Mechanism and Velocity**

-

---

**3. 电渗流与作用机理 Electroosmotic Flow (EOF) and Mechanism**

- 毛细管硅醇基（-SiOH）与电解液接触发生电离，赋予内壁负电荷。

- Silanol groups (–SiOH) inside capillary ionize in contact with electrolyte, rendering the surface negatively charged.

- 在强电场下，内壁吸附一层阳离子，作用像“电动带水流”——电渗流（EOF）。

- Under high voltage, a double layer of cations forms near the surface and drives electroosmotic flow (EOF), resembling "electric water flow".

- 当电压接通后，溶液中水分子及阳离子整体从阳极朝阴极平移，带动所有组分（包括阴离子和中性分子）沿电渗流方向迁移。

- When voltage is applied, the solution's water molecules with cations move from anode to cathode, carrying all analytes (including anions and neutrals) in the EOF direction.

- EOF具有独特平顶流型（非柱状流），且迁移速度快，带宽窄，提高分辨率和灵敏度。

- EOF exhibits a flat flow profile (not parabolic) and offers rapid, narrow band migration, boosting resolution and sensitivity.

- 电渗流速度 $V_\mathrm{eo} = \mu_\mathrm{eo} \cdot E$，其中$\mu_\mathrm{eo}$为电渗流迁移率。

- EOF velocity $V_\mathrm{eo} = \mu_\mathrm{eo} \cdot E$, where $\mu_\mathrm{eo}$ is EOF mobility.

- EOF大小受pH、缓冲液类型和浓度、毛细管材料/表面等影响。

- EOF depends on pH, nature/concentration of electrolyte, and capillary surface/material.

---

**4. 毛细管电泳中不同粒子迁移示意 Migration of Different Species in Capillary Electrophoresis**

- 在电渗流与电泳迁移叠加作用下，阳离子、阴离子、中性分子均可随EOF到达检测器。

- The combined effect of EOF and electrophoretic velocity enables cations, anions, and neutrals to all reach the detector.

- 阳离子
电泳迁移方向与EOF相同，速度最快最先到达。
- Cations
move with EOF, arrive first due to additive velocities.
- 中性分子
本身无电泳迁移，仅随EOF移动，到达时间介于阳离子和部分阴离子之间。

- Neutral molecules: not electrophoretic, move only with EOF, arrive after cations.

- 阴离子
电泳方向与EOF相反，需EOF强度大于其自身电泳速度，否则难以到达检测器。
- Anions
migrate against EOF; only reach detector if EOF overcomes their own electrophoretic movement.

---

**5. 主要应用模式 Major Capillary Electrophoresis Modes**

- 常见模式包括
毛细管区带电泳（CZE）、毛细管凝胶电泳、毛细管电色谱等。

- Main modes include: capillary zone electrophoresis (CZE), capillary gel electrophoresis, capillary electrochromatography, etc.

- 其它专用形式如非水溶液模式、手性、亲和力、电聚焦等，高度多样。

- Other specialized formats: non-aqueous, chiral, affinity, isofocusing, etc., with great versatility.

---

**6. 课堂补充与要点拓展 Classroom Supplements and In-depth Points**

- 毛细管电泳可高效自动化、微样本消耗，设备维护成本低，已广泛应用于医药、生化、法医等领域。

- Capillary electrophoresis offers high automation, minimal sample consumption, low maintenance cost, used in pharmaceutical, biochemical, forensic analysis.

- 关键优化参数有
毛细管长度/内径、电压、pH、缓冲强度与类型、温度及表面修饰。

- Key optimization factors: capillary length/diameter, voltage, pH, buffer strength/type, temperature, capillary surface modification.

- 检测方式多样，包括紫外、荧光、质谱及电导法，灵敏度高，适应分析需要。

- Multiple detection types: UV, fluorescence, MS, conductivity; high sensitivity for diverse analysis needs.

---

**7. 重要公式（文字格式，方便复制） Key Formulas (Plain Text for Easy Copying)**

---

**8. 结论总结 Summary**

- 毛细管电泳因其分辨率高、耗样少、快速、自动化发展快，成为现代分析科学的重要工具。

- Capillary electrophoresis is a vital modern analytical technique for its high resolution, low sample use, speed, and automation.

药物分析重要性与主要分析技术Why is Pharmaceutical Drug Analysis Important & Key Analytical Techniques

---

**药物分析为何重要Why is Pharmaceutical Drug Analysis Important**

- **保障安全与疗效 Ensuring Safety and Efficacy**

分析技术确保药物中活性成分正确、剂量准确且无有害杂质。

Analytical techniques ensure drugs contain correct active ingredients, at the right dosage, without harmful impurities.

- **质量控制 Quality Control**

在药物生产中，分析技术用于监控一致性和质量，确保批次间可重复性。

In pharmaceutical manufacturing, analytical methods monitor consistency and quality, ensuring reproducibility between batches.

- **合规监管 Regulatory Compliance**

各国监管机构（如EMA、FDA）要求药物上市前进行严格分析测试，以保证安全、增强公众信任。

Regulatory agencies (e.g., EMA, FDA) require rigorous analytical testing before drug approval to ensure safety and enhance public trust.

- **新药发现 Drug Discovery**

药物分析是新药化合物及其作用机制发现和鉴定的关键环节。

Drug analysis is crucial for the identification and mechanism elucidation of new pharmaceutical compounds.

---

**药物分析在实际应用中的典型场景Applications in Real-World Contexts**

- **制剂开发 Formulation Development**

在药物剂型研发阶段，分析用于评估和优化不同制剂的稳定性和可用性。

In formulation R&D, analysis is used to assess and optimize stability and bioavailability of different drug forms.

- **临床研究 Clinical Study**

通过分析生物液体中的药物浓度，监测药物的体内代谢和分布，确保疗效与安全。

By monitoring drug levels in biological fluids, analysis supports evaluation of metabolism, distribution, safety, and efficacy in vivo.

- **毒理研究 Toxicology**

检测和定量药物代谢产物，评估安全性，发现和预防潜在毒副作用。

Detecting and quantifying drug metabolites assesses safety, helping to identify and prevent potential toxic effects.

- **新药发现流程 Drug Discovery Process**

从初筛、药理分析、机制研究到临床开发，药物分析伴随整个研发流程，为每个环节提供数据支撑。

From early screening, pharmacological analysis, mechanism studies to clinical development, analytical techniques provide data support through the entire drug R&D process.

- **识别和表征新靶点 Identifying & Characterizing Potential Therapeutic Agents**

帮助发现、验证和表征新药靶点和治疗性分子。

Helps in discovering, validating, and characterizing new drug targets and therapeutic molecules.

---

**药物分析核心目标Main Objective of Drug Analysis**

- **目标**
通过定性和定量分析，验证药物及生物分子的身份、含量、纯度和稳定性。
**Objective**
To verify the identity, content, purity, and stability of drug substances, drug products, and biomolecules using qualitative and quantitative analysis.

---

**主要药物分析技术Key Techniques of Drug Analysis**

- **分离法 Separative Methods**

- 高效液相色谱（HPLC）、超高效液相色谱（UPLC）

High-Performance Liquid Chromatography (HPLC), Ultra-Performance Liquid Chromatography (UPLC)

- 气相色谱（GC）

Gas Chromatography (GC)

- 薄层色谱（TLC）、高效薄层色谱（HPTLC）

Thin-Layer Chromatography (TLC), High-Performance TLC (HPTLC)

- 超临界流体色谱（SFC）

Supercritical Fluid Chromatography (SFC)

- 毛细管电泳　
答案 CE
Capillary Electrophoresis 　
答案 CE

- **光谱法 Spectroscopic Methods**

- 质谱（MS）

Mass Spectrometry (MS)

- 紫外-可见光谱（UV-Vis）、红外与近红外（IR/NIR）

Ultraviolet-Visible Spectroscopy (UV-Vis), Infrared and Near-Infrared (IR/NIR)

- 拉曼光谱、核磁共振（NMR）

Raman Spectroscopy, Nuclear Magnetic Resonance (NMR)

- 电感耦合等离子体发射光谱（ICP）、原子吸收光谱（AAS）

Inductively Coupled Plasma (ICP), Atomic Absorption Spectroscopy (AAS)

- **联用技术 Hyphenated Techniques**

- 如液相色谱-质谱（LC-MS）、气相色谱-质谱（GC-MS）、ICP-MS等

Such as LC-MS, GC-MS, ICP-MS etc.

---

**课堂补充与要点总结Classroom Key Supplements and Summary**

- 药物分析贯穿从药品研发、生产、上市到临床使用的每一个环节，是药品质量和安全调控的基础。

Drug analysis is essential throughout drug development, manufacturing, approval, and clinical use, serving as the basis of quality and safety management.

- 不同分析方法各有适用场景，实验设计需根据所需分析的对象、灵敏度、特异性等因素选择适当技术。

Each analytical method fits specific scenarios; experiment design should consider target type, sensitivity, specificity, and other requirements.

- 新药研发、药品监管和社会健康密切依赖高效、可靠的分析科学。

Efficient, reliable analytical science is crucial for new drug development, regulatory affairs, and public health.

**药物分析核心目标Main Objective of Drug Analysis**

- **目标**
通过定性和定量分析，验证药物及生物分子的身份、含量、纯度和稳定性。
**Objective**
To verify the identity, content, purity, and stability of drug substances, drug products, and biomolecules using qualitative and quantitative analysis.

---

**主要药物分析技术Key Techniques of Drug Analysis**

- **分离法 Separative Methods**

- 高效液相色谱（HPLC）、超高效液相色谱（UPLC）

High-Performance Liquid Chromatography (HPLC), Ultra-Performance Liquid Chromatography (UPLC)

- 气相色谱（GC）

Gas Chromatography (GC)

- 薄层色谱（TLC）、高效薄层色谱（HPTLC）

Thin-Layer Chromatography (TLC), High-Performance TLC (HPTLC)

- 超临界流体色谱（SFC）

Supercritical Fluid Chromatography (SFC)

- 毛细管电泳　
答案 CE
Capillary Electrophoresis 　
答案 CE

- **光谱法 Spectroscopic Methods**

- 质谱（MS）

Mass Spectrometry (MS)

- 紫外-可见光谱（UV-Vis）、红外与近红外（IR/NIR）

Ultraviolet-Visible Spectroscopy (UV-Vis), Infrared and Near-Infrared (IR/NIR)

- 拉曼光谱、核磁共振（NMR）

Raman Spectroscopy, Nuclear Magnetic Resonance (NMR)

- 电感耦合等离子体发射光谱（ICP）、原子吸收光谱（AAS）

Inductively Coupled Plasma (ICP), Atomic Absorption Spectroscopy (AAS)

- **联用技术 Hyphenated Techniques**

- 如液相色谱-质谱（LC-MS）、气相色谱-质谱（GC-MS）、ICP-MS等

Such as LC-MS, GC-MS, ICP-MS etc.

**课堂补充与要点总结Classroom Key Supplements and Summary**

- 药物分析贯穿从药品研发、生产、上市到临床使用的每一个环节，是药品质量和安全调控的基础。

Drug analysis is essential throughout drug development, manufacturing, approval, and clinical use, serving as the basis of quality and safety management.

- 不同分析方法各有适用场景，实验设计需根据所需分析的对象、灵敏度、特异性等因素选择适当技术。

Each analytical method fits specific scenarios; experiment design should consider target type, sensitivity, specificity, and other requirements.

- 新药研发、药品监管和社会健康密切依赖高效、可靠的分析科学。

Efficient, reliable analytical science is crucial for new drug development, regulatory affairs, and public health.

药物分析重要性与主要分析技术Why is Pharmaceutical Drug Analysis Important & Key Analytical Techniques

---

**药物分析为何重要Why is Pharmaceutical Drug Analysis Important**

- **保障安全与疗效 Ensuring Safety and Efficacy**

分析技术确保药物中活性成分正确、剂量准确且无有害杂质。

Analytical techniques ensure drugs contain correct active ingredients, at the right dosage, without harmful impurities.

- **质量控制 Quality Control**

在药物生产中，分析技术用于监控一致性和质量，确保批次间可重复性。

In pharmaceutical manufacturing, analytical methods monitor consistency and quality, ensuring reproducibility between batches.

- **合规监管 Regulatory Compliance**

各国监管机构（如EMA、FDA）要求药物上市前进行严格分析测试，以保证安全、增强公众信任。

Regulatory agencies (e.g., EMA, FDA) require rigorous analytical testing before drug approval to ensure safety and enhance public trust.

- **新药发现 Drug Discovery**

药物分析是新药化合物及其作用机制发现和鉴定的关键环节。

Drug analysis is crucial for the identification and mechanism elucidation of new pharmaceutical compounds.

---

**药物分析在实际应用中的典型场景Applications in Real-World Contexts**

- **制剂开发 Formulation Development**

在药物剂型研发阶段，分析用于评估和优化不同制剂的稳定性和可用性。

In formulation R&D, analysis is used to assess and optimize stability and bioavailability of different drug forms.

- **临床研究 Clinical Study**

通过分析生物液体中的药物浓度，监测药物的体内代谢和分布，确保疗效与安全。

By monitoring drug levels in biological fluids, analysis supports evaluation of metabolism, distribution, safety, and efficacy in vivo.

- **毒理研究 Toxicology**

检测和定量药物代谢产物，评估安全性，发现和预防潜在毒副作用。

Detecting and quantifying drug metabolites assesses safety, helping to identify and prevent potential toxic effects.

- **新药发现流程 Drug Discovery Process**

从初筛、药理分析、机制研究到临床开发，药物分析伴随整个研发流程，为每个环节提供数据支撑。

From early screening, pharmacological analysis, mechanism studies to clinical development, analytical techniques provide data support through the entire drug R&D process.

- **识别和表征新靶点 Identifying & Characterizing Potential Therapeutic Agents**

帮助发现、验证和表征新药靶点和治疗性分子。

Helps in discovering, validating, and characterizing new drug targets and therapeutic molecules.

分析方法验证关键参数 Key Parameters in Analytical Method Validation

---

**核心参数一览与总述 Overview of Core Parameters**

- 分析方法验证是保证分析结果可靠性的基础，ICH 指南定义了八项关键性能参数：

Analytical method validation under ICH guidelines requires eight core performance parameters:

1
特异性/选择性 Specificity/Selectivity
2
线性 Linearity
3
范围 Range
4
准确度 Accuracy
5
精密度 Precision
6
检测限 Detection Limit (LOD)
7
定量限 Quantitation Limit (LOQ)
8
耐用性（健壮性）Robustness

---

**1. 特异性/选择性 Specificity/Selectivity**

- 特异性
指方法在杂质、降解产物或基体共存的情况下，能够明确无误地鉴别分析物的能力。

Specificity is the ability to unequivocally assess the analyte in the presence of impurities, degradation products, or matrix components.

- 选择性
指方法能够区分和定量分析物与其他成分（如结构类似物、异构体、共洗脱组分）之间的能力。

Selectivity is the ability to differentiate and quantify the analyte from other substances with similar properties.

- 实验性说明
一个理想的分析方法，能排除特定干扰物，但不一定能排除所有干扰。
Note
A selective method can differentiate from some interferents, but may not exclude all possible interference.

---

**2. 线性 Linearity**

- 线性指分析物浓度与检测响应之间成比例关系的区间。

Linearity is the range over which the analytical response is proportional to analyte concentration.

- 示意图
“线性范围”是响应随浓度线性增加区段，“动态范围”则包含所有可检测区间。
Diagram
The “linear range” is where response vs. concentration is proportional; “dynamic range” covers total measurable span.

---

**3. 范围 Range**

- 范围定义为在精密度、准确度和线性都能接受的浓度区间。

Range is the concentration interval where acceptable linearity, accuracy, and precision are maintained.

---

**4. 准确度 Accuracy**

- 准确度指结果与真实值的接近程度。

Accuracy is the closeness of the measured result to the true value.

- 计算公式（采用标准物质）：

Formula for certified reference material (CRM):

% recovery = (experimental measured / certified value) × 100

- 回收实验计算公式：

Formula for recovery studies:

% recovery = (experimental measured / theoretical amount spiked) × 100

---

**5. 精密度 Precision**

- 精密度指重复测量结果的一致性（包括重复性和再现性）。

Precision indicates the consistency (repeatability and reproducibility) of measurements.

- 分类解释：

- 系统精密度 System precision（仪器精密度）

System precision = Instrumental precision

- 可重复性 Repeatability（同一操作者、同一天、同一设备）

Repeatability = Intra-day, same operator and equipment

- 中间精密度 Intermediate precision（不同操作者、不同日期、不同仪器）

Intermediate precision = Inter-assay or inter-analyst, various days or instruments

- 再现性 Reproducibility（不同实验室间的一致性）

Reproducibility = Agreement between laboratories

- 制药行业常用的接受标准
RSD ≤ 2%

Industry acceptance: RSD ≤ 2%

---

**6. 检测限（LOD）Detection Limit**

- 检测限是可可靠区分于背景噪声的最低分析物浓度。

LOD is the lowest concentration reliably distinguished from background noise.

- 主要测定方法：

- 可视法 Visual approach（最低肉眼可分辨浓度）

- Lowest visually distinguishable concentration

- 信噪比法 Signal/Noise (S/N) approach（一般 S/N ≥ 2-3）

- S/N ≥ 2-3 is typical for LOD

- 统计法 Statistical approach（用回归曲线计算）

- LOD = $3.3 \times SD / S$，SD为空白或低浓度样中截距的标准差，S为校准曲线斜率

LOD = 3.3 × SD / S (SD: standard deviation of intercept; S: slope of calibration curve)

---

**7. 定量限（LOQ）Quantitation Limit**

- 定量限是可实现准确可靠定量分析的最低浓度，通常 S/N ≥ 10。

LOQ is the lowest concentration that can be quantitatively determined with acceptable precision and accuracy (typically S/N ≥ 10).

---

**8. 耐用性（健壮性）Robustness**

- 耐用性指方法在小幅操作条件变化（如pH、温度、流速等）下，依然能保持准确性和精密度。

Robustness is the ability of a method to remain unaffected by small variations in parameters (e.g., pH, temperature, flow rate), ensuring consistent accuracy and precision.

- 目的
发现对方法结果影响显著的变量，优化实验条件，指导日常故障排查。
Purpose
Identify critical variable influences, optimize conditions, and guide troubleshooting during daily use.

---

**课堂补充要点与实际应用 Classroom Supplements and Practical Points**

- 一套方法的验证标准用于确保药品分析从实验室到生产、到质量控制的全流程均符合国际法规要求。

Method validation standards ensure compliance from laboratory analysis to production and quality control across all pharmaceutical processes.

- 教师强调实际工作中验证标准如准确度、精密度、灵敏度是审核和注册药品时的重点考查内容。

The instructor emphasized that accuracy, precision, sensitivity, etc., are key regulatory review points in drug registrations and audits.

- 验证参数的选择和限值设定需结合分析对象、方法类型和法规指引。

Parameter selection and criteria must align with analyte type, methodological features, and regulatory guidelines.

---

**总结 Summary**

- 分析方法验证八大参数共同确保分析方法科学、可靠、可追溯，是制药领域质量控制的基石。

The eight key parameters together ensure scientific, reliable, and traceable analytical methods, providing the foundation for pharmaceutical quality control.

072025-12-08

**高效液相色谱法（HPLC）和超高效液相色谱法（UHPLC）及检测器原理笔记总结**

**Summary of High Performance Liquid Chromatography (HPLC), Ultra-High Performance Liquid Chromatography (UHPLC), and Detector Principles**

---

- **柱效与填料粒径**

- 在规则堆积的情况下，最佳柱高与颗粒直径的比值约为2；而对于超小颗粒，可以达到1.5。

- In regular packing, the optimal plate height to particle diameter ratio is about 2; for super small particles, it can reach 1.5.

- 这是关于柱效的章节内容补充。

- This completes the chapter about column efficiency.

---

- **色谱检测器类型**

- 液相色谱可在线连接各种检测器，最常用的是紫外-可见吸收检测器（UV-Vis），其价格低廉且易用。

- In chromatography, an online detection unit can be connected. The most commonly used is the ultraviolet-visible absorption detector (UV-Vis), which is cheap and easy to use.

- 近年来，质谱检测器（MS）也被采用，但价格昂贵且需要培训，并且操作成本较高。

- Recently, mass spectrometry detectors (MS) have been adopted, but they are expensive, require training, and have high operating costs.

- UV-Vis 只能检测有发色团（即能吸收紫外或可见光）的化合物，无法检测所有化合物。

- UV-Vis can only detect compounds with chromophores (groups absorbing UV or visible light), so it's not universal.

- MS不仅用于检测，还可提供分子结构等信息，有助于确认目标物身份。

- MS can provide molecular structure information, aiding in the identification of compounds.

- 还有蒸发光散射检测器和电晕放电检测器等通用型检测器，但它们响应非线性，无法提供结构信息。

- Other universal detectors include evaporative light scattering and corona charged aerosol detectors, but they have nonlinear response and give no structural information.

---

- **HPLC和UHPLC的区别**

- UHPLC（超高效液相色谱）是高效液相色谱（HPLC）的升级，近年来发展迅速，特别适用于研发场合。

- UHPLC is an upgrade of HPLC and has developed rapidly in recent years, suitable especially for research and development.

- HPLC常用颗粒直径约5微米；而UHPLC使用更小的颗粒（远小于5微米），从而提高分离效率和分析速度。

- HPLC usually uses particles about 5 μm; UHPLC uses much smaller particles, improving efficiency and speed.

- 更小的颗粒需要更高的压力，导致泵和仪器本身造价更高，且通常需要更小的柱内径和样品体积。

- Smaller particles require much higher pressure, resulting in more expensive pumps and instruments, and usually smaller column inner diameters and sample volumes.

- 尽管如此，UHPLC能在更短时间完成与HPLC相同的分析任务。

- Nevertheless, UHPLC can complete the same analysis as HPLC in a shorter time.

---

- **分配系数 log P 的定义与意义**

- 分配系数P是药物在辛醇（水相）和水之间分配的比值，log P为其对数值。

- Partition coefficient P is the ratio of drug distribution between octanol and water; log P is its logarithmic value.

- log P值越高，说明化合物越疏水、脂溶性越强；log P越低或为负，说明化合物越亲水。

- The higher the log P, the more hydrophobic (lipid-soluble) the compound is; a lower or negative log P means the compound is more hydrophilic.

- 用于比较不同化合物的极性和亲水/疏水性，并辅助分析色谱行为。

- It is used to compare the polarity and hydrophilicity/hydrophobicity of different compounds, and helps interpret chromatographic behaviors.

---

**总结（Conclusion）：**

这些内容涵盖了填料粒径对柱效的影响、常见检测器及其原理、HPLC与UHPLC的主要区别、分配系数log P的物理意义及其在色谱分析中的应用等知识点，便于研究和实际操作中的正确选择与理解。

These points cover the effect of particle size on column efficiency, types and principles of detectors, main differences between HPLC and UHPLC, the meaning of partition coefficient log P, and its application in chromatographic analysis, assisting in the correct selection and understanding in research and practice.

---

课上补充讲解 Supplementary Explanation from the Lecture

logP值反映了药物在辛醇和水两相之间的分配特性，是衡量分子疏水性的重要参数。

The logP value reflects the distribution property of a drug between octanol and water, which is an important parameter for molecular hydrophobicity.

logP值越高，说明分子越疏水、越易溶于脂类；logP为负值则分子更亲水，更易溶于水。

The higher the logP, the more hydrophobic and lipophilic the molecule is; a negative logP means the molecule is more hydrophilic and more soluble in water.

例如，这些镇痛药中，非那西丁（logP=1.41）最疏水，咖啡因（logP=-0.55）最亲水。

For example, among these analgesics, phenacetin (logP=1.41) is the most hydrophobic, whereas caffeine (logP=-0.55) is the most hydrophilic.

此次实验采用高效液相色谱法分析不同镇痛药在特定色谱条件下的保留和分离情况。

This experiment uses HPLC to analyze the retention and separation of different analgesics under specific chromatographic conditions.

色谱柱、流动相组成、流速、检测器类型等参数会直接影响药物分离效率和检测灵敏度。

Parameters such as the column, eluent composition, flow rate, and detector type directly affect the separation and detection sensitivity of the drugs.
