咖啡因 Caffeine1.750.071-0.55
2-乙酰氨基苯酚 2-acetamidophenol2.200.0720.91
乙酰苯胺 Acetanilide3.170.0861.21
乙酰水杨酸 Acetylsalicylic acid3.830.1161.24
非那西丁 Phenacetin4.770.1431.41

logP越大，表示化合物越疏水，保留时间越长；logP小或为负表示化合物越亲水，保留时间较短。

Higher logP means greater hydrophobicity and longer retention; lower or negative logP means greater hydrophilicity and shorter retention.

实验中，咖啡因（logP=-0.55）最亲水，先流出，非那西丁（logP=1.41）最疏水，最后流出。

In the experiment, caffeine (logP=-0.55) is most hydrophilic and elutes early, phenacetin (logP=1.41) is most hydrophobic and elutes last.

通过对比标准品混合物与药片溶液的色谱，可确认药片中仅含有部分成分。

By comparing the chromatograms of standard mixture and tablet solution, it is confirmed that only some components exist in the tablet.

3
HPLC类型与固定相/流动相选择 Type of HPLC and Phase Selection
教师讲解了HPLC分三种类型
常规相、反相和亲水作用色谱，核心是固定相与流动相极性的不同。

The instructor explained that HPLC is classified into three types: normal phase, reversed phase, and hydrophilic interaction chromatography, mainly based on the polarity differences between stationary and mobile phases.

常规相色谱
极性固定相，非极性流动相。

Normal phase chromatography: polar stationary phase, nonpolar mobile phase.

反相色谱
非极性固定相，极性流动相。

Reversed phase chromatography: nonpolar stationary phase, polar mobile phase.

亲水作用色谱
极性固定相，混合（水-有机）流动相。

Hydrophilic interaction chromatography: polar stationary phase, hydro-organic mobile phase.

本实验采用的是反相高效液相色谱（RP-HPLC），即用C8（非极性）固定相，甲醇-水磷酸溶液（极性）作为流动相，根据疏水作用实现分离。

This experiment uses reversed-phase HPLC (RP-HPLC), with a C8 (nonpolar) stationary phase and methanol-phosphoric acid aqueous solution (polar) as the mobile phase, achieving separation based on hydrophobic interactions.

反相色谱中，疏水性大的化合物与固定相作用强，保留更久，构成分离顺序的理论基础。

In reversed-phase chromatography, more hydrophobic compounds interact more strongly with the stationary phase and are retained longer, which forms the theoretical basis of the separation order.

4
课堂补充与老师强调 Teacher's Supplement and Emphasis

logP是预测色谱保留顺序的重要参考，但在化合物结构差异明显时可能存在特例。

logP is an important reference for predicting chromatographic retention order, but exceptions may occur when compound structure differs significantly.

药片色谱鉴定时可通过标准品的保留时间辅助判别，但需注意不同成分间的分子结构和相互作用影响。

For tablet chromatogram identification, the retention time of standards aids judgment, but attention should be paid to molecular structure and interaction effects between components.

此外，反相色谱广泛应用于药物分析，尤其适合极性差异较大的药物分离检测。

Moreover, reversed-phase chromatography is widely used in pharmaceutical analysis, especially suitable for separating drugs with significant polarity differences.

>

Question 1
HPLC 类型、固定相与流动相及保留机制 | Question 1: What is the type of HPLC used? (stationary phase, mobile phase, retention process)

色谱的类型主要有三种。

There are three main types of chromatography.

常规相色谱使用极性固定相与非极性流动相。

Normal phase chromatography uses a polar stationary phase and a nonpolar mobile phase.

反相色谱使用非极性固定相与极性流动相。

Reversed phase chromatography uses a nonpolar stationary phase and a polar mobile phase.

亲水作用色谱使用极性固定相和水-有机混合流动相。

Hydrophilic interaction chromatography uses a polar stationary phase and a hydro-organic mobile phase.

我们本实验条件为反相色谱。

Our experimental conditions fall under reversed phase chromatography.

反相色谱采用非极性固定相和极性流动相。

Reversed phase chromatography uses a nonpolar stationary phase and a polar mobile phase.

本实验用C8固定相，是非极性的。

The stationary phase in this experiment is C8, which is nonpolar.

流动相为甲醇/0.1%磷酸水溶液，是极性的。

The mobile phase is methanol/0.1% phosphoric acid aqueous solution, which is polar.

因此，此实验属于反相高效液相色谱。

Therefore, this experiment uses reversed phase high-performance liquid chromatography.

反相色谱分离机制以疏水性为主，非极性分子在固定相中保留时间更长。

The separation in reversed phase chromatography is mainly based on hydrophobicity, with nonpolar molecules being retained longer in the stationary phase.

解题思路：

Solution approach:

首先辨别实验所用色谱类型，通过固定相和流动相的极性判断。

First, identify the type of chromatography used in the experiment by judging the polarity of the stationary and mobile phases.

然后结合实验条件，明确固定相为C8（非极性），流动相为甲醇/磷酸（极性）。

Then, combine the experimental conditions: stationary phase is C8 (nonpolar), mobile phase is methanol/phosphoric acid (polar).

最后得出结论，属于反相高效液相色谱，保留机制为疏水作用。

Finally, conclude that this is reversed phase high-performance liquid chromatography, and the retention mechanism is hydrophobic interaction.

例题答案：

Sample answer:

本实验所用为反相高效液相色谱，固定相为C8（非极性），流动相为甲醇/0.1%磷酸（极性），分离机制为疏水作用。

The experiment used reversed phase high-performance liquid chromatography, with a C8 (nonpolar) stationary phase, methanol/0.1% phosphoric acid (polar) as the mobile phase, and separation is based on hydrophobic interaction.

Question 2
解释为何流动相中加入磷酸 | Question 2: Explain why phosphoric acid is added in the mobile phase

表中列出了常用流动相添加剂的pKa值。

The table lists pKa values of common mobile phase additives.

磷酸（phosphoric acid）的pKa1约为2.15。

The first pKa of phosphoric acid is about 2.15.

当需要使所有目标分析物（analyte）处于质子化状态时，低pH流动相很重要。

When it is important to render all target analytes protonated, a low-pH mobile phase is crucial.

加入0.1%（体积比）磷酸可以在pH 2处为液相-紫外检测提供合理的缓冲作用。

Adding 0.1% v/v phosphoric acid provides reasonable buffering at pH 2 for LC-UV applications.

这样设置后的流动相pH大约为2，远低于常见酸性药物的pKa。

With this, the mobile phase pH is about 2, which is much lower than the pKa values of common acidic drugs.

老师强调磷酸的加入是为了调节流动相pH至低值（约2.2），使药物分子保持未电离状态。

The instructor emphasized that the phosphoric acid is added to adjust the mobile phase pH to a low value (approx. 2.2) so drug molecules remain non-ionized.

药物示例包括对乙酰氨基酚（pKa=9.5）、2-乙酰氨基苯酚（pKa=8.8）、乙酰水杨酸（pKa=3.4）。

Examples include paracetamol (pKa=9.5), 2-acetamidophenol (pKa=8.8), and acetylsalicylic acid (pKa=3.4).

流动相pH=2，低于所有这些药物的pKa值。

The mobile phase pH=2, which is lower than the pKa of all these drugs.

因此，分析物以未电离形式存在，极性降低。

Thus, the analytes exist as non-ionized forms with reduced polarity.

未电离状态下，分析物在反相色谱中与固定相作用增强，更容易被保留。

In the non-ionized form, analytes interact more with the stationary phase in reversed-phase HPLC and are more retained.

老师特别说明，在分析药物样品时采用低pH能够防止酸性药物电离，提高方法的分离度和准确性。

The instructor specifically stated that for pharmaceutical analysis, using low pH prevents ionization of acidic drugs and improves separation and accuracy.

解题思路：

Solution approach:

首先查找流动相中添加剂的pKa，结合分析物的pKa。

First, check the pKa of additives in the mobile phase and compare with the analytes' pKa.

其次分析流动相实际pH值（磷酸控制pH约2），判断该条件下药物的电离状态。

Then, analyze the actual mobile phase pH (phosphoric acid controls pH at about 2) to determine the ionization state of the analyte.

第三，说明低pH使分析物保持未电离、极性降低，从而在反相色谱中加强与固定相的作用，延长保留。

Third, explain that low pH keeps analytes non-ionized and less polar, retaining them longer with the stationary phase in reversed-phase chromatography.

例题答案：

Sample answer:

流动相中加入磷酸是为了将pH降低至2左右，使目标分析物保持未电离状态、极性减小，在反相色谱中被更多保留，从而优化峰形和分离度。

Phosphoric acid is added to the mobile phase to lower the pH to around 2, ensuring that target analytes remain non-ionized and less polar, so they are more retained in reversed-phase chromatography, optimizing peak shape and separation.

Question 3
解释所研究药物的洗脱顺序 | Question 3: Explain the order of elution of the studied drugs

分配系数P定义为溶质在两种不混溶溶剂之间的浓度比值。

The distribution coefficient, P, is defined as the ratio of concentrations of a solute between the two immiscible solvents.

logP的计算公式为：

The equation for logP is:

logP值是分子疏水性的衡量指标。

The logP value is a measure of hydrophobic character.

疏水性由低到高的顺序为：

Hydrophobicity in increasing order is:

咖啡因 < 2-乙酰氨基苯酚 = 对乙酰氨基酚 < 乙酰苯胺 < 乙酰水杨酸 < 非那西丁

Caffeine < 2-acetamidophenol = Paracetamol < Acetanilide < Acetylsalicylic acid < Phenacetin

本实验所用的C8为疏水性固定相。

The stationary phase C8 used in this experiment is a bonded hydrophobic phase.

色谱保留时间顺序为：

The order of chromatographic retention times is:

对乙酰氨基酚 < 咖啡因 < 2-乙酰氨基苯酚 < 乙酰苯胺 < 乙酰水杨酸 < 非那西丁

老师强调，logP越高，分子越疏水，与C8固定相作用越强，保留时间越长。

The instructor emphasized that the higher the logP, the more hydrophobic the molecule, the stronger the interaction with the C8 stationary phase, and the longer the retention time.

咖啡因的logP最低，为最亲水，最先洗脱；非那西丁的logP最高，为最疏水，最后洗脱。

Caffeine has the lowest logP, is the most hydrophilic, and elutes the earliest; phenacetin has the highest logP, is the most hydrophobic, and elutes last.

实际实验测定中，部分同等logP的化合物（如2-乙酰氨基苯酚与对乙酰氨基酚）保留顺序接近。

In actual experimental measurements, compounds with similar logP (such as 2-acetamidophenol and paracetamol) have closely related retention orders.

解题思路：

Solution approach:

首先明确logP代表分子疏水性，值越高越疏水，在反相色谱中保留更久。

First, clarify that logP represents molecular hydrophobicity. The higher the value, the more hydrophobic the compound, and the longer it is retained in reversed-phase chromatography.

其次，排列出药物的logP数值，从低到高依次对应保留时间的增长。

Next, list the logP values of the drugs. With increasing logP, retention times increase correspondingly.

最后，根据色谱固定相的性质（C8为疏水）和药物的logP推导洗脱顺序。

Finally, deduce the elution order based on the hydrophobic nature of the stationary phase (C8) and the drugs’ logP.

例题答案：

Sample answer:

药物在反相C8色谱柱上的洗脱顺序受分子疏水性（logP）影响，logP值越大越晚洗脱。

The elution order of the drugs on the reversed-phase C8 column is determined by molecular hydrophobicity (logP); the higher the logP, the later the elution.

洗脱顺序为
对乙酰氨基酚 < 咖啡因 < 2-乙酰氨基苯酚 < 乙酰苯胺 < 乙酰水杨酸 < 非那西丁。

The order of elution is: Paracetamol < Caffeine < 2-acetamidophenol < Acetanilide < Acetylsalicylic acid < Phenacetin.

说明 Thomapyrin® 片中含有哪些药物 | Question 4: Indicate which drugs are present in Thomapyrin® tablets

图中显示了两种色谱图，a)为Thomapyrin®片，b)为标准品混合物。

The image shows two chromatograms, a) for Thomapyrin® tablets and b) for a standard mixture.

图例中标明了六个峰
1. 对乙酰氨基酚 2. 咖啡因 3. 2-乙酰氨基苯酚 4. 乙酰苯胺 5. 乙酰水杨酸 6. 非那西丁。

The legend lists six peaks: 1. Paracetamol 2. Caffeine 3. 2-Acetamidophen 4. Acetanilide 5. Acetylsalicylic acid 6. Phenacetin.

橙色色谱（a）对应Thomapyrin®片，绿色色谱（b）对应标准品混合物。

The orange chromatogram (a) corresponds to Thomapyrin® tablets, and the green chromatogram (b) is the standard mixture.

通过比较两种色谱中各峰的保留时间，可以确定样品中的药物成分。

By comparing the retention times of peaks in both chromatograms, the drug components in the sample can be identified.

老师讲解时强调，在Thomapyrin®片的色谱中明显出现了1号峰、2号峰和5号峰。

The instructor emphasized that peaks 1, 2, and 5 are clearly present in the chromatogram of Thomapyrin® tablets.

1号峰代表对乙酰氨基酚，2号峰代表咖啡因，5号峰代表乙酰水杨酸。

Peak 1 represents paracetamol, peak 2 represents caffeine, and peak 5 represents acetylsalicylic acid.

其余3号、4号和6号峰在Thomapyrin®片色谱中没有明显出现，说明这些成分未被检测到或含量低于检测限。

The other peaks, 3, 4, and 6, are not clearly present in the Thomapyrin® chromatogram, indicating these compounds are not detected or are below the detection limit.

因此，Thomapyrin®片只含有对乙酰氨基酚、咖啡因和乙酰水杨酸三种药物。

Therefore, Thomapyrin® tablets contain only paracetamol, caffeine, and acetylsalicylic acid.

老师补充说明
色谱图通过与标准对比，利用峰的位置和保留时间进行样品药物组分的确证，这是常规药品分析的重要方法。

The instructor added that using chromatogram comparison with standards, based on peak position and retention time, is an important and routine method for identifying drug components in samples.

解题思路：

Solution approach:

首先观察Thomapyrin®片的色谱图，记录其中出现的峰号。

First, observe the chromatogram of Thomapyrin® tablets and identify which peaks appear.

其次对照标准品混合物的色谱图，查找对应峰号代表的药物。

Second, refer to the standard mixture chromatogram to determine which drugs correspond to those peak numbers.

最后得出结论，Thomapyrin®片中对应的三个峰（1, 2, 5）为三种对应药物。

Finally, conclude that the three peaks (1, 2, 5) represent three corresponding drugs in Thomapyrin® tablets.

Question 5
计算色谱柱死体积（V_M）和死（空）时间（t_M），色谱柱空隙率为ε0.73 | Question 5: Calculate column dead volume (V_M) and dead (void) time (t_M), column porosity is ε=0.73

色谱柱长度L＝12.5cm。

The column length L = 12.5 cm.

内径i.d.＝0.4cm。

Internal diameter i.d. = 0.4 cm.

流速为1 mL/min。

The flow rate is 1 mL/min.

色谱柱总体积计算公式为V_C = L × A = L × π × (i.d./2)^2。

The total column volume is calculated as V_C = L × A = L × π × (i.d./2)^2.

死体积（V_M）= V_C × ε。

Dead volume (V_M) = V_C × ε.

V_M = L × π × (i.d./2)^2 × ε。

V_M = L × π × (i.d./2)^2 × ε.

将数据代入公式：

Insert the values into the formula:

V_M = 12.5 cm × 3.14 × (0.4 cm / 2)^2 × 0.73 = 1.146 cm^3 = 1.146 mL。

V_M = 12.5 cm × 3.14 × (0.4 cm / 2)^2 × 0.73 = 1.146 cm^3 = 1.146 mL.

死体积（V_M）即为1.146 mL。

The dead volume (V_M) is 1.146 mL.

死时间t_M的计算公式：

The formula for dead time t_M is:

V_M = t_M × F，整理得：t_M = V_M / F。

V_M = t_M × F, hence t_M = V_M / F.

代入已知数值：

Insert the known values:

t_M = 1.146 mL / 1 mL/min = 1.146 min。

t_M = 1.146 mL / 1 mL/min = 1.146 min.

因此，死时间t_M为1.146 min。

Thus, the dead time t_M is 1.146 min.

解题思路：

Solution approach:

首先根据柱长和内径计算色谱柱总体积。

First, calculate total column volume according to the column length and inner diameter.

其次使用色谱柱空隙率ε计算柱死体积。

Second, use column porosity ε to calculate the dead volume.

最后利用V_M和流速计算死时间t_M。

Finally, use V_M and flow rate to calculate dead time t_M.

例题答案：

Sample answer:

（1）死体积V_M = 1.146 mL。

(1) The dead volume V_M = 1.146 mL.

（2）死时间t_M = 1.146 min。

(2) The dead time t_M = 1.146 min.

Question 6a

死时间t_M=1.146 min。 Dead (void) time t_M=1.146 min。

The formula for k is:

k = (t_R - t_M) / t_M k = (t_R - t_M) / t_M

对乙酰氨基酚的k计算如下
The calculation for k_paracetamol is as follows:

k_paracetamol = (1.40 min - 1.146 min) / 1.146 min = 0.22 k_paracetamol = (1.40 min - 1.146 min) / 1.146 min = 0.22

咖啡因的k计算如下
The calculation for k_caffeine is as follows:

k_caffeine = (1.75 min - 1.146 min) / 1.146 min = 0.53 k_caffeine = (1.75 min - 1.146 min) / 1.146 min = 0.53

Question 6b
计算相邻色谱峰的选择性（α） | Question 6b: Calculate the selectivity (α) between consecutive peaks

选择性α是评价相邻两个色谱峰分离能力的重要参数。 Selectivity α is an important parameter for evaluating the separation ability between two consecutive chromatographic peaks.

α的计算公式为
α = k₂ / k₁，且α > 1。 The formula for α is: α = k₂ / k₁, and α > 1.

计算过程中k₂为流出较晚组分的保留因子，k₁为流出较早组分的保留因子。 In the calculation, k₂ is the retention factor of the later-eluting compound, and k₁ is of the earlier one.

PPT表格列出了每种药物的t_R（保留时间）、w₀․₅（峰宽）、k（保留因子）和α（选择性）。 The PPT table lists for each drug t_R (retention time), w₀․₅ (peak width), k (retention factor), and α (selectivity).

逐一计算相邻组分间的选择性α，公式如下： Calculate selectivity α between each pair of consecutive components as follows:

α_caffeine/paracetamol = k_caffeine / k_paracetamol = 0.53 / 0.22 = 2.38 α_caffeine/paracetamol = k_caffeine / k_paracetamol = 0.53 / 0.22 = 2.38

α_2-acetamidophenol/caffeine = k_2-acetamidophenol / k_caffeine = 0.92 / 0.53 = 1.75 α_2-acetamidophenol/caffeine = k_2-acetamidophenol / k_caffeine = 0.92 / 0.53 = 1.75

α_acetanilide/2-acetamidophenol = k_acetanilide / k_2-acetamidophenol = 1.77 / 0.92 = 1.92 α_acetanilide/2-acetamidophenol = k_acetanilide / k_2-acetamidophenol = 1.77 / 0.92 = 1.92

α_acetylsalicylic acid/acetanilide = k_acetylsalicylic acid / k_acetanilide = 2.34 / 1.77 = 1.33 α_acetylsalicylic acid/acetanilide = k_acetylsalicylic acid / k_acetanilide = 2.34 / 1.77 = 1.33

α_phenacetin/acetylsalicylic acid = k_phenacetin / k_acetylsalicylic acid = 3.16 / 2.34 = 1.35 α_phenacetin/acetylsalicylic acid = k_phenacetin / k_acetylsalicylic acid = 3.16 / 2.34 = 1.35

老师强调，选择性永远大于1，且选择性越高，理论上峰分离越容易。 The instructor emphasized that selectivity is always greater than 1, and the higher the selectivity, the easier the theoretical separation between peaks.

解题思路
Solution approach:

首先，查找相邻峰的保留因子k₁和k₂。 First, find the retention factors k₁ and k₂ for the adjacent peaks.

然后代入α = k₂ / k₁公式计算每一对相邻峰的选择性。 Then, substitute values into the formula α = k₂ / k₁ for each pair of adjacent peaks.

最后汇总每对峰的具体计算结果，并注意α均大于1。 Finally, summarize the calculation result for each peak pair, ensuring every α > 1.

例题答案
Sample answer:

对乙酰氨基酚和咖啡因的选择性α = 2.38 Selectivity α between paracetamol and caffeine = 2.38

咖啡因和2-乙酰氨基苯酚的选择性α = 1.75 Selectivity α between caffeine and 2-acetamidophenol = 1.75

2-乙酰氨基苯酚和乙酰苯胺的选择性α = 1.92 Selectivity α between 2-acetamidophenol and acetanilide = 1.92

乙酰苯胺和乙酰水杨酸的选择性α = 1.33 Selectivity α between acetanilide and acetylsalicylic acid = 1.33

乙酰水杨酸和非那西丁的选择性α = 1.35 Selectivity α between acetylsalicylic acid and phenacetin = 1.35

选择性相同，但分离度可以不同。 Same selectivity but different resolution is possible.

Question 7
根据第5个峰计算理论塔板数（N）和色谱柱塔板高度（H） | Question 7: Calculate from the peak (5) the number of theoretical plates (N) and the plate height (H) for the column

第5号峰（乙酰水杨酸）数据如下：t_R = 3.83 min，w_{0.5} = 0.116 min。

For peak 5 (acetylsalicylic acid): t_R = 3.83 min, w_{0.5} = 0.116 min.

理论塔板数N的计算公式为：

The number of theoretical plates N is calculated as:

N = 5.54 × ( t_R / w_{0.5} )²

N = 5.54 × ( t_R / w_{0.5} )²

将数据代入公式：

Insert the data into the formula:

N = 5.54 × (3.83 / 0.116 )² = 6039

N = 5.54 × (3.83 / 0.116 )² = 6039

色谱柱长度L = 12.5 cm = 125×10³ μm。

Column length L = 12.5 cm = 125×10³ μm.

塔板高度H的计算公式为：

The plate height H is calculated as:

H = L / N

H = L / N

H = 125×10³ μm / 6039 = 20.7 μm

H = 125×10³ μm / 6039 = 20.7 μm

老师强调，N 值越高表示柱效越高，H 值越低柱效越好。

The instructor emphasized: higher N indicates better column efficiency; lower H means better performance.

解题思路：

Solution approach:

首先查找给出的保留时间t_R和半高宽w_{0.5}，代入公式计算N。

First, identify the given retention time t_R and half-height peak width w_{0.5}, and substitute into the formula for N.

然后用色谱柱长度L除以N，得到塔板高度H。

Then, divide column length L by N to obtain the plate height H.

例题答案：

Sample answer:

理论塔板数N = 6039。

Number of theoretical plates N = 6039.

塔板高度H = 20.7 μm。

Plate height H = 20.7 μm.

流速增加对色谱参数的影响 | Effect of Increased Flow Rate on Chromatographic Parameters

流速从1 mL/min增加到1.5 mL/min。

The flow rate is increased from 1 mL/min to 1.5 mL/min.

t_M = L / u，因此死时间和保留时间都会减小。

t_M = L / u, thus void time and retention time both decrease.

即t_R和t_M均除以1.5。

Both t_R and t_M are divided by 1.5.

保留因子k的公式为
k = (t_R - t_M) / t_M

Retention factor k is defined as: k = (t_R - t_M) / t_M

由于t_R和t_M同时等比例变小，k值不变。

Since both t_R and t_M decrease proportionally, the k value does not change.

柱后压（ΔP）由Darcy方程计算：

Column backpressure (ΔP) is calculated via the Darcy equation:

流速u从1提升至1.5，ΔP成1.5倍上升。

Flow velocity u increases from 1 to 1.5, so ΔP increases by a factor of 1.5.

如ΔP原为80.4 bar，则新ΔP = 80.4 × 1.5 = 120.6 bar。

If ΔP was initially 80.4 bar, then new ΔP = 80.4 × 1.5 = 120.6 bar.

分离度Resolution略微降低或基本不变。

Resolution slightly decreases or does not change significantly.

老师强调：

The instructor emphasized:

t_M、t_R都除以1.5，保留因子k不变。

Both t_M and t_R are divided by 1.5, but k does not change.

分离度一般略微降低或几乎不变。

Resolution usually slightly decreases or remains almost unchanged.

柱后压ΔP增加为原先的1.5倍。

Backpressure ΔP increases by 1.5 times.

解题思路：

Solution approach:

首先，明确提升流速会导致t_M和t_R都按比例减小（皆除以1.5）。

First, recognize that increasing the flow rate makes both t_M and t_R decrease proportionally (by a factor of 1.5).

因k值为二者之比，所以k不变。

Since k is a ratio of the two, k remains unchanged.

分离度因峰展宽稍微增加，理论上会稍有下降或基本不变。

Resolution may slightly decrease or remain unchanged due to a minor increase in peak broadening.

柱后压用Darcy方程推算，增加倍数和流速倍数一致。

Use the Darcy equation to calculate backpressure, which increases proportionally to the flow rate multiplication.

例题答案：

Sample answer:

流速由1增至1.5 mL/min后，t_M和t_R均除以1.5，k值不变，分离度略降或基本不变，柱后压ΔP提升为原先的1.5倍。

After increasing the flow rate from 1 to 1.5 mL/min, both t_M and t_R are divided by 1.5, k remains unchanged, resolution slightly decreases or stays nearly the same, and column backpressure ΔP increases by a factor of 1.5.

降低甲醇体积分数对保留因子的影响 | Effect of Decreasing Methanol Fraction on Retention Factor (k)

有机溶剂体积分数φ由0.4降至0.3。

The volume fraction of organic solvent, φ, decreases from 0.4 to 0.3.

φ代表有机溶剂体积分数。

φ represents the volume fraction of the organic solvent.

用下式描述保留因子的变化：

The change of retention factor is described by the following equation:

logk_new = logk_w − Sφ_new

logk_new = logk_w − Sφ_new

logk = logk_w − Sφ

logk = logk_w − Sφ

两式相减，得：

Subtract the two equations to get:

log(k_new / k) = −S(φ_new − φ)

log(k_new / k) = −S(φ_new − φ)

代入S=3，φ=0.4，φ_new=0.3：

Substitute S=3, φ=0.4, φ_new=0.3:

log(k_new / k) = −3 × (0.3 − 0.4) = 0.3

log(k_new / k) = −3 × (0.3 − 0.4) = 0.3

解得：

This gives:

k_new / k = 10^0.3 ≈ 2

k_new / k = 10^0.3 ≈ 2

即k_new = 2k

So k_new = 2k

结论：降低甲醇体积分数后，保留因子k及保留时间均增加为原来的2倍。

Conclusion
After decreasing the methanol fraction, both the retention factor k and retention time double.

解题思路：

Solution approach:

首先，根据公式log(k_new / k) = −S(φ_new − φ)代入已知数据。

First, use the formula log(k_new / k) = −S(φ_new − φ) with the given data.

然后，计算log(k_new / k)并换算为倍率关系。

Next, calculate log(k_new / k) and convert to the ratio.

最后，得出k_new = 2k，总结影响。

Finally, conclude k_new = 2k and summarize the influence.

例题答案：

Sample answer:

降低甲醇体积分数（有机溶剂强度），所有药物的保留因子（k）和保留时间将变为原来的2倍。

By decreasing the methanol volume fraction (organic solvent strength), the retention factors (k) and retention times of all drugs will double.

改变溶剂选择性（不改变溶剂强度）：可用的两种流动相组合 | Change Solvent Selectivity (without Changing Solvent Strength): Two Usable Mobile Phase Compositions

溶剂选择性是指通过改变流动相的极性、氢键能力或其它特性，影响分离过程中各组分的分离效果，而不改变整体溶剂强度。 Solvent selectivity refers to changing the separation characteristics (e.g., polarity, hydrogen bonding, etc.) by modifying the composition of the mobile phase, but without changing its overall solvent strength.

要保持“溶剂强度”不变，可以通过等强度替换不同类型的有机溶剂，实现不同选择性。 To maintain the same "solvent strength," different types of organic solvents with equivalent strength can be substituted to alter selectivity.

例如，原有流动相为甲醇/0.1%磷酸（40/60 v/v）。 For example, the original mobile phase is methanol / 0.1% phosphoric acid (40/60 v/v).

根据PPT建议，可替换为下列两种等强度流动相，以改变选择性： According to the PPT, two alternative equivalent-strength mobile phases to change selectivity are:

1）乙腈/0.1%磷酸（30/70 v/v）

1
ACN / 0.1% phosphoric acid (30/70 v/v)

2）四氢呋喃/0.1%磷酸（23/77 v/v） 2) THF / 0.1% phosphoric acid (23/77 v/v)

这些组合能够有效改变色谱分离的选择性，但保持整体溶剂强度基本不变。 These combinations effectively alter chromatographic selectivity while keeping overall solvent strength nearly constant.

根据溶剂选择性三角图，不同有机溶剂（如醇类、腈类、醚类等）因氢键能力、酸碱性或极性不同，从而影响分离效果。 According to the solvent selectivity triangle, different organic solvents (such as alcohols, nitriles, ethers, etc.) impact separation due to variations in hydrogen bonding, acidity/basicity, or polarity.

老师强调
在保持溶剂强度的前提下，通过更换有机溶剂的种类（如甲醇→乙腈、甲醇→四氢呋喃）可以调节体系的选择性，实现更好的分离。 The instructor emphasized: under constant solvent strength, selectivity can be tuned—and thus separation improved—by switching the type of organic solvent (e.g., methanol to acetonitrile, or methanol to THF).
解题思路
Solution approach:

首先，识别现有流动相组成及其有机溶剂种类、配比。

First, identify the composition and ratio of the current mobile phase and the organic solvent used.

其次，查找等强度替换有机溶剂的推荐比值。

Second, find recommended ratios of alternative organic solvents that provide the same strength.

最后，列举出可选的两种组合，说明其能改变溶剂选择性但不影响分离强度。

Finally, list two alternative combinations and explain they alter selectivity without changing strength.

例题答案
Sample answer:

可用于改变选择性的两种流动相组合是：

Two mobile phase compositions that can be used to change selectivity are:

1）乙腈/0.1%磷酸（30/70 v/v）

1
ACN / 0.1% phosphoric acid (30/70 v/v)

2）四氢呋喃/0.1%磷酸（23/77 v/v）

2) THF / 0.1% phosphoric acid (23/77 v/v)

082025-12-08（第 2 讲）

气相色谱基本概念 Concept of Gas Chromatography

1
气相色谱（GC）的定义和用途

气相色谱（GC）是一种广泛应用的分析技术，用于分离和分析混合物中的化合物。

Gas chromatography (GC) is a widely used analytical technique for separating and analyzing compounds in a mixture.

2
GC仪器的基本组成
注射器（Injector）：将样品注入并蒸发。 Injector: Vaporize the sample.

柱子（Column）：用惰性载气将样品带入，分离各组分。 Column: Uses an inert gas mobile phase to carry the sample and separate the components.

检测器（Detector）：将每种组分的量转化为电信号。 Detector: Converts the amount of each component into an electrical signal.

数据处理单元（Data processing Unit）：用于数据分析和可视化。 Data processing Unit: Used for data processing and visualization.

3
分析流程简述

起始为液体/待测样品，通过注射口蒸发，成为蒸气态后随载气进入色谱柱。 The process starts with a liquid or test sample, vaporized through the injector, and then enters the column with the carrier gas.

气体分子在柱中被分离，根据各自在固定相和移动相间的分配系数不同而分离。 Gas molecules are separated in the column due to different partition coefficients between the stationary and mobile phases.

检测器将各组分的含量转为信号，并由数据处理单元进行分析和展示。 The detector converts the content of each component into a signal, which is then analyzed and displayed by the data processing unit.

4
课堂补充与要点说明

GC主要用于需要将样品蒸发为气态的分析，其中使用惰性气体作为载体，这些气体不与样品发生反应。 GC is mainly used for analyzing samples that need to be vaporized into gas, using inert carrier gases that do not react with the sample.

GC与高效液相色谱（HPLC）的一个主要区别是载体的不同：GC为气体且惰性，HPLC为液体且有时可与分析物发生作用。 A key distinction between GC and HPLC is the carrier: GC uses an inert gas, while HPLC uses a liquid that may interact with analytes.

为了分析，样品必须能够被蒸发（挥发性），且对温度有一定稳定性，否则无法完成分析。 For analysis, the sample must be vaporizable (volatile) and thermally stable; otherwise, analysis cannot be performed.

药物领域的应用 Applications in Pharmaceutical Field

1
质量控制与纯度检测 Quality control and purity testing 用于药物质量监控和纯度检测，确保产品符合标准。

Used for monitoring drug quality and purity testing to ensure the product meets standards.

2
药物制剂稳定性 Stability of drug formulation 监测药物活性成分（API）的降解与降解产物的识别。

Monitoring the degradation of active pharmaceutical ingredients (APIs) and identifying degradation products.

3
残留溶剂分析 Residual solvent analysis 分离和定量分析药物制剂中的残留溶剂，如甲醇、乙酸乙酯、二氯甲烷等。

Separation and quantification of residual solvents in drug formulations such as methanol, ethyl acetate, dichloromethane, etc.

4
法医分析 Forensic analysis 可用于滥用药物（如可卡因、海洛因、苯丙胺、大麻素等）的分析。

Used for analyzing drugs of abuse such as cocaine, heroin, amphetamines, cannabinoids, and others.

5
天然产物的表征 Characterization of natural products 鉴定和定量精油、萜烯等活性成分和其它挥发性化合物。

Identification and quantification of active components such as essential oils, terpenes, and other volatile compounds.

6
药物代谢与药代动力学 Drug metabolism and pharmacokinetics 了解药物在体内的吸收、分布、代谢和排泄过程，有助于治疗药物监测与药代动力学评估。

Understanding drug absorption, distribution, metabolism, and elimination in the body, aiding in therapeutic drug monitoring and pharmacokinetic assessment.

分离原理Principle of Separation

一、两种气相色谱类型及其基本区别Types of Gas Chromatography and Their Differences

气固色谱（GSC）：流动相为气体，固定相为固体，分离机制是吸附。 Gas-solid Chromatography (GSC): Mobile phase is gas, stationary phase is solid, and the mechanism is adsorption.

气液色谱（GLC）：流动相为气体，固定相为液体，分离机制是分配。 Gas-liquid Chromatography (GLC): Mobile phase is gas, stationary phase is liquid, and the mechanism is partitioning.

GLC更常用，因为其分辨率高、效率高，而GSC分辨率和效率较低。 GLC is more common because of its higher resolution and efficiency, while GSC has moderate resolution and lower efficiency.

二、表格对比Summary Table

项目 Aspect气固色谱GSC气液色谱GLC
固定相Stationary固体Solid液体Liquid
分离机制Mechanism吸附Adsorption分配Partitioning
灵敏度Sensitivity高High中等Moderate
分辨率Resolution中等Moderate高High
柱长Column Length短Shorter长Longer
效率Efficiency低Lower高Higher

三、分离产生的机理Mechanism of Separation

样品中不同化合物与固定相作用不同，造成分离。 Different compounds in the sample interact differently with the stationary phase, causing separation.

与固定相作用弱的分子更快通过色谱柱。 Compounds with weaker interactions move faster through the column.

与固定相作用强的分子停留时间更长。 Compounds with stronger interactions are retained longer.

组分在不同时间流出色谱柱，称为保留时间。 Components elute at different times from the column, called retention times.

四、分配作为分离基础Partition as the Basis of Separation

化合物在固定相和流动相之间分配，分配程度因溶解度和挥发性不同而异。 Compounds partition between the stationary and mobile phases, based on their solubility and volatility.

与固定相的溶解度越高，保留时间越长。 Higher solubility in the stationary phase leads to longer retention time.

挥发性越高的分子越易停留在气相中，流出越快。 More volatile compounds tend to stay in the gas phase and elute faster.

五、示意图及问题讲解Interpretation of Diagram and Discussion Questions

例题
A、B两种化合物，A峰先出现，B峰后出现。哪种被保留得更多？哪种在固定相中溶解度更高？ Example: For compounds A and B, A appears first, B appears later. Which is more retained? Which has higher solubility in the stationary phase?

答：B被保留得更多，B在固定相中溶解度更高。 Answer: B is more retained, B has higher solubility in the stationary phase.

课堂解释
如老师比喻，"B就像在街上不停地和人打招呼，‘话多’所以走得慢，被留得时间长。" As explained by the teacher: “B is like someone who talks to everyone on the street, so they move slower and are retained longer.”

六、课堂额外补充Extra Lecture Points

气相色谱适用于分析能蒸发、对温度有一定稳定性的化合物。 Gas chromatography is suitable for analyzing compounds that can vaporize and are thermally stable.

样品最终需以气态进柱，故高分子量如蛋白质难以用气相色谱分析。 The sample must ultimately enter the column in gaseous form, so high molecular weight compounds like proteins are rarely analyzed by GC.

总结Summary

气相色谱分离原理基于混合物组分与固定相、流动相之间物理化学相互作用的差异，通过对保留时间的分析实现组分的有效分离和定量。 The principle of separation in gas chromatography relies on the different physicochemical interactions of mixture components with the stationary and mobile phases, achieving effective separation and quantification by analyzing retention times.

GC仪器组成GC Instrumentation

1
气相色谱仪主要组分 Main Components of Gas Chromatograph

载气（Carrier Gas）：作为流动相，携带样品通过色谱柱，本身不与样品发生化学反应。 Carrier gas: Acts as the mobile phase, carries the sample through the column and does not chemically interact with analytes.

气体流量调节器（Gas Flow Regulator）：精确调控载气流速，保证分离效果稳定。 Gas flow regulator: Precisely controls the flow rate of the carrier gas to ensure stable separation.

进样口（Injection Port）：负责将液体样品汽化，并以受控方式送入色谱柱。 Injection port: Vaporizes the sample and introduces it into the column in a controlled manner.

色谱柱/固定相（Column/Stationary Phase）：根据各组分与固定相的相互作用、挥发性差异将样品分离。 Column/Stationary phase: Separates sample components based on their interaction with the stationary phase and their volatility.

烘箱（Oven）：提供温度控制，优化组分分离与流出；常采用线性升温，增强分离效率。 Oven: Maintains precise temperature control to optimize the separation and elution of components; usually uses programmed temperature ramps to improve separation.

检测器（Detector）：将色谱柱流出各组分的存在转化为可测信号，实现定性和定量分析。 Detector: Identifies and quantifies separated compounds as they exit the column by converting their presence into a measurable signal.

废液口（Waste）：多余未捕获组分排出，保护检测器和系统。 Waste: Disposes of unused or excess components to protect the detector and system.

计算机/工作站（Computer/Workstation）：数据采集、处理及分析显示色谱图，便于结果解读。 Computer/workstation: Collects, processes, and displays chromatograms for easy interpretation and analysis.

2
各部件功能详解 Functional Explanation of Components

载气一般选用惰性气体（如氦气、氮气、氩气），需高纯度且不含氧气，避免对样品和色谱柱造成损伤。 The carrier gas is usually an inert gas (e.g., helium, nitrogen, argon), requiring high purity and no oxygen to avoid damage to samples or the column.

进样口通常加热，使液体样品立即汽化为气体，保证样品的定量进样和瞬时混合。 The injection port is typically heated to instantly vaporize the liquid sample for accurate dosing and rapid mixing.

色谱柱是分离的核心，可为填充柱或毛细管柱，固定相材料根据分析目标和性质选择。 The column is the core part for separation and can be packed or capillary, with stationary phase materials selected according to analytical needs.

烘箱提供升温环境，使不同组分在不同温度下流出，实现更好的分离。 The oven provides temperature programming so that different components elute at various temperatures, enhancing separation.

检测器有多种类型，常用有火焰离子化检测器（FID）与热导检测器（TCD），前者灵敏度高、后者适用性强。 Detectors include various types; commonly used are Flame Ionization Detector (FID, high sensitivity) or Thermal Conductivity Detector (TCD, wide applicability).

最终由计算机进行数据处理，输出色谱图，每个峰代表一个组分。 Finally, the computer processes the data and outputs chromatograms, where each peak represents a component.

3
教师课堂补充重点 Teacher’s Key Supplementary Points

GC对样品的体积要求极小，注入量常为微升级别，极大节约样品、提高检测灵敏度。 GC requires very small sample volumes, usually microliters, which saves samples and increases detection sensitivity.

仪器优势包括分离速度快（秒至分钟级），高分辨率，高灵敏度，适用于复杂混合物分析。 Major advantages include rapid separation (from seconds to minutes), high resolution, and high sensitivity, suitable for analysis of complex mixtures.

仪器核心思想是各部分协作
载气承担转运，进样口实现气化，色谱柱执行分离，烘箱提供温控，检测器定性定量，计算机分析结果。 The essential idea is the collaboration of each component: the carrier gas transports, the injector vaporizes, the column separates, the oven controls temperature, the detector identifies/quantifies, and the computer analyzes results.

对于载气的选择，重点在于其惰性和纯度，以保证系统稳定和分离效果最佳。 The selection of carrier gas emphasizes its inertness and purity, ensuring system stability and optimal separation performance.

4
小结 Summary

气相色谱仪由载气、进样口、色谱柱、烘箱、检测器、废液出口和计算机构成，各功能环环相扣，共同完成高效分离与检测。 The GC instrument is composed of the carrier gas, injection port, column, oven, detector, waste outlet, and computer, with each function closely linked for efficient separation and detection.

载气Carrier Gas

1
载气的作用Role of carrier gas

载气作为流动相，将汽化后的样品输送通过整个气相色谱系统。 The carrier gas serves as the mobile phase, transporting the vaporized sample throughout the gas chromatography system.

它与样品不发生化学反应，主要目的是保证流动过程的一致性和惰性。 It does not chemically react with the sample; its primary purpose is to ensure consistent and inert movement.

2
常用载气种类Common carrier gases

氦气（He）：因其惰性和合适的黏度最常用。 Helium (He): The most commonly used due to its inertness and optimal viscosity.

氮气（N₂）：成本较低，但流速较慢。 Nitrogen (N₂): Cheaper, but has a slower flow rate.

氢气（H₂）：分离效率高且速度快，但可燃性强。 Hydrogen (H₂): Offers fast and efficient separation, but is highly flammable.

3
主要考量因素Key considerations

载气必须是惰性的，避免与样品或固定相发生反应，这是其与液相色谱的核心区别之一。 The carrier gas must be inert to avoid reactions with the sample or stationary phase—this is a key difference from liquid chromatography.

载气需具备高纯度，不能含氧气或水，避免对样品和色谱柱造成损害。 The carrier gas must be of high purity, containing no oxygen or water, to prevent damage to the sample and the column.

载气流速会影响分离效率和保留时间。 The flow rate of the carrier gas affects both separation efficiency and retention times.

4
课堂补充讲解Lecture-related key points

选择载气时必须考虑其惰性、纯度和实验需求，常用惰性气体如氦气、氮气和氢气。 In the lecture, it was emphasized that when choosing a carrier gas, its inertness, purity, and experimental requirements must be considered. Commonly used inert gases include He, N₂, and H₂411.

若载气中含有氧气或水分，会导致化学反应甚至色谱柱损伤，尤其是高温操作时风险更大。 If the carrier gas contains oxygen or water, it may cause chemical reactions or damage the column, especially during high-temperature operation153.

流速调控对分离有直接影响，过快或过慢都不利于最佳分辨率与效率。 The control of carrier gas flow rate directly affects the separation; too fast or too slow rates are not conducive to optimal resolution and efficiency3.

5
小结Summary

载气作为气相色谱的流动相，核心在于其惰性、高纯度以及恰当的流速调控，对保障样品安全和分离效果至关重要。 As the mobile phase in gas chromatography, the core requirements for the carrier gas are inertness, high purity, and appropriate flow rate regulation, which are critical for sample safety and effective separation.

载气
优化线速度以提高色谱柱效率
Carrier gas
optimizing linear velocity for high column efficiency
HETP
理论塔板高度 HETP: height equivalent to a theoretical plate

HETP是生成一个理论塔板所需的色谱柱长度，理论塔板代表分析物分子在流动相与固定相之间完成一次传递过程。 HETP is the length of column required to generate one theoretical plate, with a theoretical plate being one transfer process of an analyte molecule between the mobile phase and the stationary phase.

公式 Formula HETP = L / N = L / [16 × (tR / tW)²] HETP = L / N = L / [16 × (tR / tW)²]

其中L为柱长，N为理论塔板数，tR为保留时间，tW为峰宽。 Here, L is the column length, N is the number of theoretical plates, tR is the retention time, and tW is the peak width.

带扩散速率 Rate of band broadening

理论塔板越多，HETP越小，说明分离效率越高；峰越窄，说明柱效率越好。 The more theoretical plates, the smaller the HETP, indicating higher separation efficiency; narrower peaks indicate better column efficiency.

峰宽、保留时间相关的公式还可以写为
The formula relating peak width and retention time can also be expressed as: N = 16 × (tR / Wb)² = 5.545 × (tR / Wh)² N = 16 × (tR / Wb)² = 5.545 × (tR / Wh)²

HETP和N依赖多种参数 HETP and N depend on multiple parameters

影响因素包括色谱柱温度、载气流速、进出口压力、柱子的尺寸、以及载气的选择等。 Influencing factors include column temperature, carrier gas flow rate, inlet and outlet pressures, column dimensions, and the choice of carrier gas.

老师补充
峰越细越高效，因为薄峰对应更高理论塔板数，更好分离效果。精细控制温度和流速可有效减小HETP，提高分离度。 Instructor’s note: The thinner the peak, the higher the efficiency, because a narrow peak corresponds to a higher theoretical plate number and better separation. Precise control of temperature and flow rate can effectively reduce HETP and improve separation.

在实验设计中，应选取导致更低HETP的条件以最大化分离效率。同时，载气的选择通常优先惰性气体，并保证纯度足够高，避免反应或污染。 In experimental design, conditions that lead to lower HETP should be targeted to maximize separation efficiency. Also, inert carrier gases with high purity are preferred to avoid reactions or contamination.

问我们应追求更高还是更低的HETP？ Shall we target conditions of higher or lower HETP?
答

应追求更低的HETP，这样色谱柱效率高，组分分离清晰。 Answer: We should target lower HETP, resulting in higher column efficiency and clearer separation of components.

小结 Summary

HETP是反映色谱柱分离效率的重要参数，依赖多种操作与结构条件，实验中应力求其值越低越好。 HETP is a key parameter reflecting the column’s separation efficiency and depends on operational and structural factors; in practice, it’s best to achieve the lowest possible value.

载气流速的影响Carrier gas: effect of flowrate

HETP：理论塔板高度 HETP: Height Equivalent to a Theoretical Plate

HETP（理论塔板高度）是衡量色谱柱分离效率的重要指标，其值越小，说明效率越高。 HETP (height equivalent to a theoretical plate) is a key parameter for column efficiency; the smaller the value, the higher the efficiency.

其数学表达由Van Deemter方程给出： The mathematical expression is given by the Van Deemter equation: HETP = A + B/u + Cu HETP = A + B/u + Cu

其中： Where:

A为涡流扩散（多路径效应、色散） A is eddy diffusion (multipath effects, dispersion)

B为纵向分子扩散（沿流动方向的扩散） B is longitudinal molecular diffusion (diffusion along or against flow)

C为质量转移阻力（传质阻力） C is resistance against mass transfer (mass transfer resistance)

u为载气线速度 u is the linear velocity of the carrier gas

HETP与载气流速的关系 Relationship between HETP and Carrier Gas Flowrate

色谱柱效率由载气的线速度或流速决定，流速不同，HETP也不同。 Column efficiency depends on the linear velocity or flowrate of the carrier gas; different rates yield different HETP values.

随着线速度增加，B/u项（分子扩散）快速下降，而Cu项（质量转移）线性上升。 As linear velocity increases, the B/u term (molecular diffusion) decreases rapidly, while the Cu term (mass transfer) increases linearly.

总体曲线呈“U”型，存在最低点，对应最优流速。 The overall curve is “U”-shaped, with a minimum point representing the optimum flowrate.

最小HETP=最佳柱效 Smallest HETP = Best Efficiency

曲线最低点对应最小的HETP值，也是柱效最高的点。 The lowest point of the curve gives the smallest HETP, which means highest column efficiency.

实验时，需优化载气流速或线速度，使其达到该最小值。 In practice, optimizing the carrier gas flowrate/velocity to reach this minimum yields the best efficiency.

课堂讲解及补充 Lecture Explanation and Supplement

老师强调，载气需要足够纯净且不含氧、水，保证色谱分离安全与准确，同时相关参数如温度和进出口压力也会对效率产生影响。 The instructor emphasized that the carrier gas must be high-purity and free of oxygen or water, to ensure safe and accurate separation; temperature and inlet/outlet pressures also influence efficiency38.

在色谱法开发和应用中，通常需通过调整线速度实验，找到以Van Deemter方程为基础的最佳分离条件。 In chromatographic method development, adjusting linear velocity and finding the optimum using the Van Deemter equation is standard practice89.

小结 Summary

色谱柱效率受载气流速直接影响，依据Van Deemter方程，需选择使HETP最小的流速，以获得最佳分离性能。 Column efficiency is directly affected by carrier gas flowrate; according to the Van Deemter equation, selecting the velocity that minimizes HETP achieves optimal separation.

The smaller the HETP, the 载气类型对色谱柱效率和分辨率的影响Type of carrier gas effect on column efficiency and resolution

1
HETP与不同载气The relationship between HETP and different carrier gases

HETP（理论塔板高度）体现色谱柱效率，HETP越小，效率越高。 HETP (height equivalent to a theoretical plate) reflects column efficiency; the smaller the HETP, the higher the efficiency.

不同载气（氮气N₂、氦气He、氢气H₂）曲线表现不同：在相同线速度下，HETP依次递减，氢气最低，氦气次之，氮气最高。 Different carrier gases (nitrogen N₂, helium He, hydrogen H₂) show different curves: for the same linear velocity, HETP decreases in order: hydrogen is the lowest, helium is the next, nitrogen the highest.

2
HETP与分辨率的关系Relationship between HETP and resolution

分辨率（Rs）反映色谱峰分开的清晰度，分辨率越高，分析效果越好。 Resolution (Rs) reflects the clarity of peak separation; higher resolution means better analysis.

HETP越小，分辨率越高，因此三种载气的分辨率顺序正好与HETP相反：氢气最大，氦气居中，氮气最小。 The smaller the HETP, the higher the resolution; thus, the resolution order of the three carrier gases is the opposite: hydrogen is the best, helium is between, nitrogen the worst.

3
图示及实际效果Analysis of diagrams and actual effect

图中以58 cm/sec线速度对比，氢气峰最窄、分辨率最高（R=1.37），氦气略宽（R=1.17），氮气最宽且分开最差。 At 58 cm/sec linear velocity, the diagram shows hydrogen peaks are the narrowest and sharpest (R=1.37), helium is wider (R=1.17), and nitrogen is the widest with the poorest separation.

理论顺序总结：N₂ HETP > He HETP > H₂ HETP，分辨率则为Rs H₂ > Rs He > Rs N₂。 In summary, theoretical order is: N₂ HETP > He HETP > H₂ HETP, while for resolution: Rs H₂ > Rs He > Rs N₂.

4
课堂补充讲解Lecture explanations and highlights

老师强调，分辨率与柱效率紧密相关，载气类型不仅影响分离效果，还影响分析的速度与安全性，应综合考虑。 The instructor emphasized that resolution is tightly linked to efficiency; the type of carrier gas affects not only separation but analysis speed and operational safety, so all aspects need balancing416.

虽然氢气效率最高，但因其易燃性，在常规实验中多用氦气或氮气，高要求或特定场合可选择高效但需安全防护的氢气。 Although hydrogen has the highest efficiency, due to its flammability, helium or nitrogen is more commonly used in routine experiments; hydrogen is selected for special high-efficiency needs with appropriate safety measures1619.

选择载气时，还要结合实验目的、经费预算及设备条件等多重因素。 When selecting the carrier gas, other factors such as experimental purpose, budget, and device requirements must also be considered20.

5
小结Summary

HETP小、分辨率高是色谱分离的理想目标。在具体载气选择上需兼顾效率、安全与实际需求。 Smallest HETP and highest resolution are the ideal goals for chromatographic separation. The choice of carrier gas should balance efficiency, safety, and practical needs.

GC仪器组成GC Instrumentation

1
仪器结构总览 Overview of Instrument Structure

气相色谱仪主要由载气系统、进样口、色谱柱（固定相）、温控烘箱、检测器和数据处理工作站组成。 The gas chromatograph mainly consists of a carrier gas system, injection port, chromatographic column (stationary phase), temperature-controlled oven, detector, and data processing workstation.

2
载气系统 Carrier Gas System

载气作为流动相，从高压气瓶输出，经气体流量调节器精确调节后，驱动样品经进样口进入色谱系统。 The carrier gas serves as the mobile phase, delivered from a high-pressure cylinder, regulated precisely by a gas flow regulator, and drives the sample through the injection port into the chromatographic system.

要求惰性、高纯度、无水无氧，常用氦气、氮气或氢气，实现分析的稳定和安全。 It must be inert, highly pure, free from moisture and oxygen. Commonly used gases are helium, nitrogen, or hydrogen, ensuring analytical stability and safety.

3
进样口 Injection Port

进样口负责将少量液体样品瞬间汽化，并以受控方式引入色谱柱。 The injection port instantly vaporizes a small amount of liquid sample and introduces it into the column in a controlled manner.

一般加热以保证样品全部汽化且混合均匀，防止样品分布不均导致峰形异常。 Typically, it is heated to ensure complete vaporization and homogeneous mixing, preventing uneven distribution and peak shape anomalies1119.

4
色谱柱/固定相 Column/Stationary phase

色谱柱是仪器核心，由螺旋状管道组成，内部包覆或填充固定相材料。 The column is the core of the instrument, consisting of a coiled tube coated or packed with stationary phase material.

不同组分与固定相作用强弱和挥发性不同，导致它们被分离并在不同时间流出。 Different components interact with the stationary phase and have different volatilities, leading to their separation and elution at different times12.

5
温控烘箱 Oven

烘箱可精准控制柱温，通常支持程序升温，优化组分分离与流出效果。 The oven precisely controls the column temperature and usually allows programmed temperature ramps to optimize component separation and elution.

控温对分离度和分析速度有重要影响。 Temperature control significantly affects resolution and analysis speed119.

6
检测器 Detector

检测器将色谱柱流出组分转化为电子信号，实现对各组分的检测与定量。 The detector converts components eluting from the column into electronic signals, enabling identification and quantification.

常用有火焰离子化检测器（FID）、热导检测器（TCD）等，根据样品及灵敏度需求选择。 Common types are Flame Ionization Detector (FID) and Thermal Conductivity Detector (TCD), chosen according to sample and sensitivity needs11.

7
数据处理与输出系统 Data Processing and Output

信号经处理后以色谱图方式显示，每个峰对应样品中的一种组分。 After processing, signals are displayed as chromatograms, with each peak representing a sample component.

工作站软件便于峰识别、积分、定量及最终结果输出分析。 Workstation software facilitates peak identification, integration, quantification, and final result analysis11.

8
教师课堂补充与讲解 Instructor’s Additional Points

样品进样量一般为微升级别，有助于提高检测灵敏度且节约样品。 Sample injection volume is typically at the microliter level, improving detection sensitivity and conserving sample713.

气体高纯度和仪器各单元协调是获得高分辨率和高灵敏度的基础。 High gas purity and coordinated instrument components are key to achieving high resolution and sensitivity5.

若载气中含氧或水，将损伤柱或引发火灾（特别是用氢气时），故实验安全必须重视。 If the carrier gas contains oxygen or water, it can damage the column or cause fires (especially when using hydrogen), so laboratory safety is crucial320.

9
小结 Summary

气相色谱仪各组成环环相扣：载气保证输运和稳定，进样口实现样品气化，色谱柱核心分离，烘箱控制温度，检测器信号转换，数据系统结果输出，共同实现高效分析。 The core GC modules are closely linked: the carrier gas enables stable transport, the injection port vaporizes samples, the column performs separation, the oven controls temperature, the detector converts signals, and the data system provides output, together ensuring efficient analysis.

进样器Injector

1
进样器的作用Role of Injector

进样器负责将一小而可控体积的样品（液体或气体）注入到气相色谱系统中。 The injector is responsible for introducing a small and controlled volume of the sample (liquid or gas) into the gas chromatography system.

进样过程中需要将液体样品瞬间汽化为气体，保证样品全量汽化混匀后与载气融合。 During injection, the liquid sample must be vaporized instantly to ensure complete vaporization and even mixing with the carrier gas.

只有充分汽化并与载气混合，才能保证后续色谱分离的准确性和重现性。 Only with thorough vaporization and mixing with the carrier gas can subsequent chromatographic separation be accurate and reproducible.

老师特别强调：控制进样量的准确性和进样的瞬时性，否则会导致色谱峰变形、不对称或定量误差。 The instructor emphasized particularly: accuracy and immediacy of injection are crucial; otherwise, peak distortion, asymmetry, or quantification errors may occur.

2
常见进样器类型Common Types of Injectors

分流进样器Split Injector： 分流进样器把样品分成两部分，仅有一小部分随载气进入色谱柱，其余被排出。适合高浓度样品分析。 The split injector divides the sample such that only a fraction enters the column with the carrier gas, while the rest is vented out. Suitable for analysis of high-concentration samples.

不分流进样器Splitless Injector： 不分流进样器将全部样品直接送入色谱柱，适用于低浓度痕量分析。 The splitless injector introduces the entire sample into the column, ideal for trace analysis of low-concentration samples.

柱上进样器On-column Injector： 直接将样品注入色谱柱内，不经过汽化室，常用于热不稳定性样品。 The on-column injector introduces the sample directly into the column without prior vaporization, often used for thermally labile samples.

3
分流进样器的结构与原理Structure and Principle of Split Injector

操作过程包括三步： The operational process includes three steps: ①样品注入Sample Injection ②样品汽化Sample Vaporizing ③传输到色谱柱Transfer to Column

分流比（Split ratio）是表征进入色谱柱样品比例的重要参数，定义为： The split ratio is key for quantifying the sample portion entering the column, defined as: 分流比 = 排出气体体积 / 进入色谱柱气体体积 Split ratio = Gas to vent / Gas to column

举例：如进样口总载气流量105 ml/min，分流口排出气体100 ml/min，色谱柱2 ml/min，密封口3 ml/min，则分流比为： For example, if total carrier gas is 105 ml/min, split vent 100 ml/min, column 2 ml/min, purge 3 ml/min, then: 分流比 = 100 / 2 = 50:1 Split ratio = 100 / 2 = 50:1

分流比越高，进柱样品越少，适合浓度高的样品；分流比低或用不分流进样，则可全部进柱，适合痕量分析。 The higher the split ratio, the less sample enters the column, suitable for concentrated samples; a low split ratio or splitless mode allows all sample to enter, suitable for trace analysis.

4
分流进样器示意及典型流路Split Injector Diagram and Typical Flow

图示说明：进样器内样品汽化，部分随载气向分流口排出，只有小部分随载气流入色谱柱。 Diagram: sample vaporizes in the liner, some flows out via the split vent, only a small amount goes with the carrier gas into the column.

5
分流与不分流进样的区别Comparison of Split vs Splitless Injection

分流进样：分流比高，多数样品被排出，进入色谱柱较少，避免超载，峰宽较窄。 Split injection: High split ratio, most sample is vented, less enters the column, avoids overload, yields narrower peaks.

不分流进样：全部样品进入色谱柱，提高检出限，适合分析低浓度、痕量物质。 Splitless injection: Entire sample enters the column, increases sensitivity, suitable for analyzing low-concentration or trace compounds.

不分流进样峰形更高更尖锐，适合灵敏度要求高的分析场景。 Splitless injection results in higher, sharper peaks, ideal where high sensitivity is required.

6
教师课堂讲解补充 Instructor’s Additional Explanation

课堂提问和回答强调分流比的实际计算及其含义，如分流比50:1表示注入的样品50份中，仅1份真正进入色谱柱。 In-class Q&A emphasized the actual calculation and meaning of the split ratio, e.g., 50:1 means only 1 in 50 portions of sample enters the column.

若分析样品极其稀少或者高灵敏度分析必须全部进柱，此时应选择不分流模式。 If the sample is very dilute or high sensitivity is needed, all of the sample must enter the column, so the splitless mode should be used.

图示和实验现象说明，不分流峰面积更大，分析窗口更窄，能检测更低浓度。 Diagrams and experiment results show that splitless yields larger peak area and narrower peak window, better for low concentration detection.

7
小结Summary

进样器用于样品气化和快速注入，是样品高效、定量分析的关键。 The injector is critical for sample vaporization and rapid injection, enabling efficient and quantitative analysis.

分流与不分流进样应根据样品浓度和分析需求合理选择。 The choice between split and splitless injection should be made according to sample concentration and analytical requirements.

分流比计算、实际流路及仪器结构理解是掌握GC进样操作的基础。 Understanding split ratio calculation, flow paths, and instrument structure is fundamental for mastering GC injection operations.

分流与不分流进样器Split vs Splitless Injector

1
基本概念与工作原理 Fundamental Concepts and Working Principles

分流进样器（Split）：多数样品通过分流口排出，只有一小部分进入色谱柱。如分流比20:1时，仅5%样品进入色谱柱，适合高浓度样品分析。 Split injector: Most of the injected sample is vented, and only a small portion (e.g., 5% with a split ratio of 20:1) enters the column, suitable for high-concentration samples.

不分流进样器（Splitless）：几乎所有样品（>98%）都进入色谱柱，多用于低浓度、痕量组分的高灵敏度检测。 Splitless injector: Nearly all of the injected sample (>98%) enters the column, suitable for trace or low-concentration sample analysis.

2
实验条件下的表现 Comparison under Experimental Conditions

分流进样的优点：通过控制分流比，防止色谱柱过载；适合浓度较高样品，色谱峰形美观，峰分辨率高。 Advantages of Split Injection: By controlling the split ratio, column overloading is prevented; suitable for samples with high concentration, good peak shapes, and high resolution.

不分流进样的优点：最大化样品进柱量，提高灵敏度，检测窗口窄，更适合低浓度或痕量分析，如农残检测。 Advantages of Splitless Injection: Maximizes sample amount entering the column, higher sensitivity, narrower detection window, more suitable for low-concentration or trace analysis such as pesticide detection.

3
对比图谱分析 Chromatogram Comparison Analysis

分流进样所得色谱图：色谱峰分离清晰，分辨率高，适合常规样品分析。 Chromatogram from split injection: Clear and well-separated peaks, high resolution, suitable for routine sample analysis.

不分流进样所得色谱图：峰面积更大，适合灵敏度要求高时，但若样品过浓易峰拖尾或分辨下降。 Chromatogram from splitless injection: Larger peak areas, ideal for high sensitivity needs, but excessive sample may cause tailing or lower resolution.

4
灵敏度与适用性 Sensitivity and Suitability

在灵敏度需求高、样品浓度低时，应优先选择不分流进样。 For high sensitivity and low sample concentration, splitless injection is preferred.

当样品浓度较高且关注分辨率、峰对称性时，应采用分流进样，防止进样过量。 For high-concentration samples with focus on resolution and symmetric peaks, use split injection to avoid column overload.

5
溶剂选择对结果的影响 Effect of Solvent Choice on Results

从实验数据比较，使用正己烷（n-hexane）稀释样品时，峰形更好、分离更清晰。 Experimental results show that using n-hexane as dilution solvent yields better peak shape and clearer separation.

高挥发性的溶剂如正己烷便于样品汽化，提高进样效率。 Highly volatile solvents such as n-hexane facilitate sample vaporization, improving injection efficiency.

6
教师课堂讲解补充 Instructor’s Additional Explanation

老师强调不同进样方式的适用范围：分流进样理想用于“样品量充足，需防止过载”；不分流进样用于“分析目标低浓度，追求灵敏度”。 The instructor highlighted the application scope: split injection is ideal for “sufficient sample amount, need to prevent overload”, while splitless is for “analytes at low concentration, targeting high sensitivity”.

灵敏度对比：如果同等条件下比较，不分流进样峰面积和检出限明显优于分流进样。 Sensitivity comparison: Under equal conditions, splitless injection gives larger peak area and lower detection limit than split injection.

实验中分流进样分辨率优于不分流，特别是在复杂组分混合物分析时更为突出。 In experiments, split injection generally provides better peak resolution than splitless, especially when analyzing complex mixtures.

决定进样方式前需综合考虑样品浓度、期望灵敏度及分析复杂性。 When deciding on injection method, sample concentration, expected sensitivity, and complexity of analysis must all be considered.

7
总结 Summary

分流进样器适用于高浓度样品，可防止柱过载，分辨率好；不分流则适合痕量分析，灵敏度高但可能出现峰拖尾或分辨下降。 Split injection is suitable for high-concentration samples, prevents overloading, and yields good resolution; splitless is best for trace analysis, offers higher sensitivity but may cause peak tailing or lower resolution.

最佳稀释溶剂通常为正己烷，可以获得更佳峰形和分离效率。 The best dilution solvent is usually n-hexane, giving better peak shape and separation efficiency.

实验条件下的推荐结论 Recommended conclusion for experimental conditions

若目标是灵敏度和痕量检测，建议采用不分流进样法。若样品浓度高、分析目标为高分辨，选择分流进样法。稀释溶剂首选正己烷。 If the goal is high sensitivity and trace-level detection, splitless injection is recommended. For high-concentration samples and high resolution, use split injection. For solvent, n-hexane is optimal8.

On-column进样与其他进样方式On-column Injection and Other Injection Methods

On-column进样原理与特点 Principle and Features of On-column Injection

On-column进样是指样品直接注入色谱柱内，无需在进样器中先汽化。 On-column injection means the sample is introduced directly into the chromatographic column without prior vaporization in the injector.

微量注射器的针头插入毛细管柱前端，逐步升高进样口和色谱柱温度，使样品在柱内逐步被汽化并随载气进入色谱系统。 The tip of the microsyringe is inserted directly into the end of the capillary column; as the inlet and column temperature rises, the sample gradually vaporizes inside the column and is carried along by the carrier gas.

可使用中性预柱(一般为1至10米)以减少化学作用，保护主柱。 A chemically neutral precolumn (typically 1 to 10 meters) can be added to minimize interactions and protect the main column.

适用范围与优势 Application and Advantages

主要适用于热不稳定或沸点很高的分析物，这类化合物在高温进样器中容易分解，用on-column法能有效避免。 This method is mainly suitable for thermally sensitive or high-boiling analytes, as such compounds might decompose in a conventional hot injector; on-column avoids this problem.

优势在于无热分解，且能精确、无损地将全部样品引入色谱柱，保证分析的准确性和定量性。 The main advantages are no thermal degradation of analytes, and the ability to precisely introduce the entire sample quantitatively into the column, ensuring accuracy and quantitation.

主要不足与注意事项 Main Limitations and Cautions

需要小心进样，避免色谱柱过载，不然容易造成色谱峰展宽甚至失真。 Careful injection is required to avoid overloading the column; otherwise, peak broadening or distortion may occur.

必须充分考虑样品体积、浓度和复杂性，保证峰形与重现性。 Sample volume, concentration, and complexity must be carefully considered to ensure good peak shape and reproducibility.

其他常见进样方式与选择原则 Other Common Injection Methods and Selection Principles

常见进样方式还包括PTV程序升温进样、顶空进样、冷柱头进样等。 Other commonly used injection methods include Programmed Temperature Vaporization (PTV) injection, headspace injection, and cold-on-column injection.

选择进样方式不是“一刀切”，需根据样品热稳定性、挥发性、浓度、目标灵敏度等多因素判断。 Injector choice is not one-size-fits-all; it depends on thermal stability, volatility, analyte concentration, required sensitivity, and other analytical needs.

合理匹配进样方式才能实现最佳分析效果、准确性和效率。 Properly matching the injection method to the sample properties ensures optimal chromatographic performance, accuracy, and efficiency.

决策时关键问题 Key Questions for Method Choice

问样品类型？是液体还是气体？是易挥发还是非挥发化合物？ What is the sample type? Liquid or gas? Volatile or non-volatile compounds?
问分析目标？是做痕量检测、常规定量，还是追求高灵敏度？ What are the analytical goals? Trace-level quantitation, routine analysis, or high sensitivity?
问样品热稳定性？高温下是否会分解？是否需冷注射？ What is the thermal stability of the sample? Will analytes degrade at high temperature? Need a cold injection?
问浓度范围？是高浓度或低浓度（痕量）？ What is the expected concentration range? High or trace-level?
问基体复杂吗？基体干扰大时，是否需要顶空等特殊进样？ Is the sample matrix complex? Is headspace injection helpful for matrix interference?
答

课堂补充与讲解 Instructor's Supplement and Explanation

老师强调，On-column进样由于无热分解，对热敏性化合物极其有用，但进样精度要求高。所以只有在样品非常容易分解且浓度适当时才优先选择。 The instructor emphasized that on-column injection is essential for thermally sensitive compounds because it avoids thermal decomposition, but requires very precise injection skills. Thus, it's preferred when samples are easily decomposed and at appropriate concentration.

举例补充，常用PTV和顶空法应对多组分或易挥发组分、复杂基体，需根据具体分析目的调整方案。 For instance, PTV and headspace methods are frequently used for multicomponent or volatile analytes and complex matrices; method adaptation is key based onactual goals and sample nature.

小结 Summary

On-column进样避免了样品的热分解，尤其有利于特殊热敏分析物，但操作精准性要求高。不同进样方式需结合样品性质和分析目标科学选择，不能盲目照搬，更无法“一法通用”。 On-column injection prevents thermal decomposition and benefits thermally sensitive substances, but requires precise operation. The choice of injection mode must consider sample characteristics and analytical goals—there is no universal approach.

气相色谱仪的组成GC Instrumentation

1
气相色谱仪主要组成部分 Main Components of GC Instrument

仪器包括载气钢瓶、气流调节器、进样口、色谱柱（固定相）、柱温箱、检测器、废液口及计算机。 The instrument consists of a gas cylinder, gas flow regulator, injector port, column/stationary phase, oven, detector, waste, and computer.

2
各部分作用及原理 Function and Principle of Each Part

载气（Carrier Gas）：作为移动相，不与样品化学反应，用于将样品携带通过色谱柱，实现分离。 Carrier Gas: Acts as the mobile phase, does not chemically interact with the analytes, carries the sample through the column for separation810.

气流调节器（Gas Flow Regulator）：调节和保持载气的流速，保证色谱分析条件的稳定性与重复性。 Gas Flow Regulator: Adjusts and maintains carrier gas flow rate to ensure stable and repeatable chromatographic conditions.

进样口（Injection Port）：使样品瞬间汽化并定量、均匀地注入色谱柱内部，进样量需严格控制，保证后续分离准确性。 Injection Port: Rapidly vaporizes and introduces a controlled amount of sample into the column, the accuracy and uniformity of injection are critical for correct separation7.

色谱柱/固定相（Column/Stationary Phase）：根据样品与固定相的相互作用力及挥发性差异，实现成分分离。 Column/Stationary Phase: Separates sample components based on their interaction with the stationary phase and their volatility8.

柱温箱（Oven）：精确控制色谱柱温度，优化分离效率与洗脱时间。 Oven: Maintains precise temperature control to optimize separation and compound elution.

检测器（Detector）：对从色谱柱流出的各组分实时检测，将物质信号转换为可监测的电信号。 Detector: Detects compounds as they elute from the column and converts their signal into measurable output8.

废液口（Waste）：多余载气及未被检测的部分通过废液口排出。 Waste: Excess carrier gas and undetected material are vented out here.

计算机（Computer）：收集、显示并分析检测信号，输出色谱图和定量结果。 Computer: Collects, displays, and analyzes detection signals, outputs chromatograms and results8.

3
温度控制的关键作用 Key Role of Temperature Control

柱温箱（Oven）能稳定或程序升温，是影响分离效果最关键的参数之一。 The oven can operate isothermally or with programmed temperature, which is one of the most critical parameters affecting separation performance8.

稳定或优化温度有助于提升分辨率和缩短分析时间，特别适合复杂或多组分样品。 Stable or optimized temperature helps improve resolution and reduce analysis time, especially for complex or multi-component samples8.

4
色谱分离与检测原理 Separation and Detection Principle

样品经进样口汽化，随载气进入柱内，因各组分与固定相分配/吸附作用及挥发性差异分离。 The sample is vaporized at the inlet, carried by the carrier gas into the column, and separated according to their interaction with the stationary phase and differences in volatility819.

分离后的组分依序进入检测器，通过转化为可测信号，实现定性与定量检测。 Separated components elute into the detector in sequence, where they are converted into measurable signals for qualitative and quantitative analysis8.

5
教师补充与课堂讲解 Teacher’s Supplement and Lecture Key Points

载气必须为惰性气体（如氦、氮），不能参与反应，否则影响分离和检测结果。 The carrier gas must be inert (such as helium or nitrogen), to prevent interaction with analytes and ensure accurate separation and detection8.

进样体积非常小，常用微升量进样，有助于实现高效快速分离和检测，特别适合微量、痕量分析。 Injections are typically in microliter volumes, making GC suitable for high efficiency and rapid analysis, especially for trace analysis12.

教师强调：每个环节控制都直接影响分离效果，例如气流速率、柱温、进样量、样品气化效率等。 The lecturer emphasized that every operational step—like gas flow rate, oven temperature, injection amount, and vaporization efficiency—directly affects separation performance and reproducibility78.

色谱图（computer输出）是分析结果的最终表达，反映出每类化合物的保留时间和含量信息。 The chromatogram output is the final result, representing retention times and concentration of compounds in the sample8.

6
小结 Summary

气相色谱仪结构包含：载气、调节器、进样口、色谱柱及温控、检测器和数据处理单元，每部分协同实现高效定性定量分离检测。 GC structure includes: carrier gas system, regulator, injector, column with temp control, detector, and data processing, all working together for efficient qualitative and quantitative separation and detection.

各部分参数调整需紧密配合样品性质和分析需求，才能得到最优分离与可靠分析结果。 Each parameter must be adjusted according to sample properties and analytical needs for optimal separation and reliable results.

气相色谱仪的组成与色谱柱特性

GC Instrumentation and Column Characteristics

1
仪器主要组成与各自作用 Main Components of GC and Their Functions

载气（Carrier Gas）：作为流动相，负责将样品通过色谱柱，且本身不与样品发生化学反应。 Carrier Gas: Acts as the mobile phase, transporting the sample through the column without chemically interacting with the analytes.

气流调节器（Gas Flow Regulator）：调节和维持载气流速，确保分析过程的稳定性和重复性。 Gas Flow Regulator: Adjusts and maintains the flow rate of the carrier gas to ensure stability and reproducibility during analysis.

进样口（Injection Port）：迅速汽化样品并以受控方式注入柱内，准确控制进样量。 Injection Port: Rapidly vaporizes and introduces the sample into the column in a controlled manner, ensuring precise sample injection.

色谱柱/固定相（Column/Stationary Phase）：根据组分与固定相的相互作用和挥发性实现分离。 Column/Stationary Phase: Achieves separation of sample components based on their interaction with the stationary phase and their volatility.

柱温箱（Oven）：精确控制色谱柱温度，优化分离效率和洗脱速度。 Oven: Maintains precise temperature control for the column, optimizing separation efficiency and compound elution.

检测器（Detector）：检测分离后流出的各组分，将其转化为可测电信号，实现定性定量分析。 Detector: Identifies and quantifies separated compounds as they exit the column by converting their presence into measurable signals.

废气出口（Waste）：排出多余载气及未检测部分，避免系统污染。 Waste: Vents excess carrier gas and unmeasured components to avoid contamination.

计算机（Computer）：采集显示并分析信号，输出色谱图和分析结果。 Computer: Acquires, displays, and analyzes signals, generating chromatograms and analytical results.

2
教师课堂讲解补充 Instructor's Explanation Additions

必须选用惰性气体如氦气或氮气作载气，避免反应。 An inert gas such as helium or nitrogen must be chosen as the carrier gas to avoid reactions.

进样所用样品体积极小，一般为微升级，有助于实现高效微量、痕量分析。 Sample volume for injection is very small (microliter scale), facilitating high efficiency and trace analysis.

温度控制是分离效果的关键参数，需根据样品类型选择恒温或程序升温。 Temperature control is a critical parameter for separation performance and should be chosen as isothermal or programming mode depending on sample type.

每个环节的参数，包含流速、温度、进样量、及样品汽化效率，都会直接影响分辨率和结果的可靠性。 Every parameter—flow rate, oven temperature, injection amount, vaporization efficiency—directly affects resolution and analytical reliability.

色谱图是分析结果最终体现，反映了各化合物的保留时间和定量信号。 The chromatogram is the ultimate output of analysis, reflecting the retention time and quantitative signal of each compound.

3
色谱柱结构与类型 Column Structure and Types

色谱分离依赖样品在固定相与流动相间分配（气液色谱）或吸附（气固色谱）行为上的差异。 Chromatographic separation depends on differences in sample partitioning (gas-liquid chromatography) or adsorption (gas-solid chromatography).

两大类色谱柱：填充柱（Packed）和毛细管（Open/Capillary）： There are two main types of columns: packed and open/capillary columns:

填充柱较短（0.5–5 米）且内径较大（2–4 mm），适合大体积、简单样品。 Packed columns are shorter (0.5–5 m) and wider (2–4 mm), suited for larger volumes and simpler analyses.

毛细管柱内径小（如 0.25 mm），长度长（可达 60 米），可实现高分辨和高灵敏分离。 Open/capillary columns have small diameter (e.g. 0.25 mm), longer length (up to 60 m), offering higher resolution and better separation.

4
色谱柱材料与固定相特点 Column Materials and Stationary Phase Features

固定相常为低挥发高分解温度的惰性液体，化学惰性且与基体共价键合。 The stationary phase is typically a chemically inert, low volatility, high decomposition temperature liquid, covalently bonded to the column matrix.

开放管柱可分为壁涂开管柱和多孔层开管柱，二者可实现不同分离要求。 Open columns include wall coated open tubular columns and porous layer open tubular columns, each suitable for different separation needs.

5
主要参数对分离性能的影响 Effect of Key Parameters on Separation

列管内径越小，分辨率越高，但容量下降；大内径容量高但灵敏度受限。 The smaller the column internal diameter, the higher the resolution, but the lower the sample capacity; wider diameter increases capacity but lowers sensitivity.

色谱柱越长，理论塔板数越多，分离能力增强，但总分析时间增加。 Longer columns offer more theoretical plates and better separation, but increase total analysis time.

固定相膜厚影响分离，高沸点组分适合薄膜，相对挥发物适合厚膜以改善留存与分离。 Stationary phase film thickness affects separation: high-boiling compounds favor thin films; volatiles are better with thicker films for improved retention and separation.

6
教师讲课补充举例 Teachers’ Additional Key Examples

举例分析：毛细管柱可获得更窄、更高灵敏度色谱峰，便于微量组分检测；而填充柱更适合样品量大、复杂度低的常规分析。 Example: Capillary columns provide sharper, more sensitive peaks for trace analysis, while packed columns are better for larger samples with simpler matrices.

不同柱径和柱长的选择需根据样品复杂性、灵敏度和分离效率合理匹配。 Selection of column diameter and length should match sample complexity, sensitivity needs, and resolution requirements.

7
小结 Summary

气相色谱仪结构包含载气系统、调节器、进样、色谱柱（选择合适结构和固定相）、柱温控制、检测器和数据分析模块，各部分科学匹配，可实现高效、可靠的分离定量。 GC systems contain a carrier gas system, regulator, injector, column (with optimal structure and stationary phase), oven for temperature control, detector, and data analysis; all parts work together for efficient and reliable separation and quantification.

色谱柱性能由柱类型、尺寸和固定相材质决定，不同组合满足不同分析目标。 Column performance depends on column type, dimension, and stationary phase; different combinations fit varying analytical needs.

合理调整参数和选型才能达到所需分离效果和分析目标，保证结果的科学性与可靠性。 Proper adjustment of parameters and careful selection ensures the desired separation and analytical goals are scientifically and reliably achieved.

8
公式补充 Mathematical Expression for Efficiency

色谱理论塔板数 NN 与峰的保留时间 tRtR​ 和峰宽 ww 的关系： Theoretical plate number NN relates to retention time tRtR​ and peak width ww: N=16(tRw)2N=16(wtR​​)2

理论塔板高度（HETP，Height Equivalent to Theoretical Plate）表达： Height Equivalent to Theoretical Plate (HETP) expression: H=LNH=NL​ 其中 LL 为柱长，NN 为理论塔板数。 Where LL is column length and NN is number of plates.

GC仪器构成与检测器 GC instrumentation and detectors

一、GC仪器的基本组成 Overall components of a GC system

- 整体结构：GC仪器由载气系统、进样口、色谱柱/固定相、柱温箱（烘箱）、检测器以及数据处理系统（电脑）组成。

Overall structure: A GC system consists of the carrier-gas system, injection port, column/stationary phase, oven, detector, and data system (computer).**

- 载气（Carrier Gas）：由气瓶和流量调节器提供惰性气体，作为流动相，将样品带过色谱柱而不与分析物发生反应。

Carrier gas: Supplied from a gas cylinder and regulated by a flow controller; it is an inert mobile phase that carries the sample through the column without reacting with analytes.**

- 进样口（Injection port）：在受控条件下把液体或气体样品注入，并迅速汽化、与载气充分混合，再进入色谱柱。

Injection port: Introduces liquid or gaseous samples under controlled conditions, rapidly vaporizes them, mixes them with the carrier gas, and directs them into the column.**

- 色谱柱/固定相（Column / stationary phase）：根据分析物与固定相之间的相互作用强弱及挥发性差异，实现组分的分离。

Column / stationary phase: Separates components based on differences in their interaction with the stationary phase and their volatility.**

- 柱温箱（Oven）：精确控制色谱柱的温度，可采用恒温或程序升温，以优化分离度和分析时间。

Oven: Precisely controls column temperature, either isothermal or temperature-programmed, to optimize separation and analysis time.**

- 检测器（Detector）：在组分从色谱柱流出时识别并定量，把“浓度或质量变化”转变为可记录的电信号。

Detector: Identifies and quantifies components as they elute from the column by converting changes in concentration or mass into an electrical signal.**

- 计算机与废液/废气出口：计算机记录色谱图并进行定性定量计算，废气口排出未被检测利用的载气和残余样品。

Computer and waste outlet: The computer records chromatograms and performs qualitative/quantitative calculations, while the waste outlet vents unused carrier gas and remaining sample.**

二、检测器的核心作用与基本概念 Fundamental role and basic concepts of detectors

- 检测器的根本任务：把“随时间变化的被分离组分量”转成电信号峰，用峰高或峰面积反映分析物含量。

Fundamental task of detectors: Convert the time-varying amount of each separated component into signal peaks, with peak height or area reflecting analyte amount.**

- 灵敏度（Sensitivity）：能检测到的最小物质量或浓度，灵敏度越高，检出限越低，可以测到更微量的成分。

Sensitivity: The smallest mass or concentration that can be detected; higher sensitivity means a lower detection limit and the ability to measure trace levels.**

- 选择性/专一性（Selectivity/Specificity）：检测器对某些化合物或元素有响应、对其它几乎无响应的能力。

Selectivity/specificity: The ability of a detector to respond to certain compounds or elements while giving little or no response to others.**

- 通用型与选择型：并非所有检测器都能检测“一切”，能对几乎所有挥发组分响应的叫“通用检测器”，只对某一类或某几类物质响应的叫“选择性或专属性检测器”。

Universal vs selective: Not all detectors sense “everything”; those that respond to nearly all volatile components are called universal detectors, whereas those that respond mainly to certain families or elements are selective or specific detectors.**

- 为什么需要选择性检测器：在复杂样品中，如果所有组分都产生强信号，图谱会很杂、难以解析；选择性检测器只“看”特定组分或元素，可以提高信噪比和定性/定量的可靠性。

Why selective detectors are needed: In complex mixtures, if every component produces a strong signal, the chromatogram becomes crowded and hard to interpret; a selective detector “sees” only target components or elements, improving signal-to-noise and analytical reliability.**

- 检测限比较时要统一单位：不同检测器的检出限单位可能是pg/s、pg/mL、µg/L等，只有换算成相同单位后才能真正比较谁更灵敏；老师强调不要求死记具体数字，但要会比较。

Detection-limit comparison requires common units: Detection limits may be expressed in pg/s, pg/mL, µg/L, etc.; only after converting to the same unit can sensitivities be meaningfully compared; the instructor stressed understanding comparison rather than memorizing exact numbers.**

三、常见GC检测器类型与适用样品 Common GC detector types and applicable samples

1
质谱检测器（MS, Mass Spectrometer）

- 适用范围：几乎可对任何挥发且热稳定的化合物进行检测，是“可调谐”的高灵敏度检测器。

Applicable samples: Nearly any volatile and thermally stable compound; a tunable, highly sensitive detector.**

- 检测特点：不仅给出信号强度，还提供质谱信息（m/z），用于定性鉴定未知物和结构解析，特别适合成分复杂、未知物较多的样品。

Characteristics: Provides both signal intensity and mass spectra (m/z) for qualitative identification and structural elucidation, especially useful for complex samples with many unknowns.**

- 灵敏度：检出限在约0.25–100 pg量级，属高灵敏度检测器，常用于痕量分析和确认分析。

Sensitivity: Detection limits on the order of about 0.25–100 pg, making MS a highly sensitive detector for trace and confirmatory analysis.**

2
火焰离子化检测器（FID, Flame Ionization Detector）

- 适用样品：主要用于烃类及含碳有机物，对大多数有机化合物响应良好，对永久性气体和水几乎无响应。

Applicable samples: Primarily hydrocarbons and carbon-containing organics, giving strong response to most organic compounds but little to permanent gases and water.**

- 原理简述：样品在氢火焰中燃烧产生离子，电流信号与进入火焰的有机碳量近似成正比，因此非常适合有机物定量。

Principle in brief: Samples are burned in a hydrogen flame to generate ions; the resulting current is roughly proportional to the amount of organic carbon entering the flame, making FID excellent for quantifying organics.**

- 灵敏度：检出限约为1 pg/s，灵敏度高、线性范围宽，是常规有机分析中最常用的GC检测器之一。

Sensitivity: Detection limit around 1 pg/s, with high sensitivity and a wide linear range, making FID one of the most commonly used GC detectors for organic analysis.**

3
热导检测器（TCD, Thermal Conductivity Detector）

- 适用样品：理论上对所有能改变载气热导率的组分都有响应，因此被视为“通用型”检测器。

Applicable samples: In principle responds to any component that changes the thermal conductivity of the carrier gas, thus considered a universal detector.**

- 特点：可同时检测有机和无机气体，尤其适合配合不可燃载气（如氦）分析常规气体混合物。

Features: Can monitor both organic and inorganic gases, especially suitable for gas mixtures when used with nonflammable carriers like helium.**

- 灵敏度：检出限大约在500 pg/mL量级，灵敏度低于MS和FID，但结构简单、可非破坏性检测。

Sensitivity: Detection limit around 500 pg/mL, less sensitive than MS and FID, but structurally simple and often non-destructive.**

4
电子捕获检测器（ECD, Electron-Capture Detector）

- 适用样品：对卤素化合物（如含Cl、Br、F等的农药、卤代烃）高度敏感，几乎不响应不含强电子捕获基团的化合物。

Applicable samples: Extremely sensitive to halogenated compounds (such as chlorinated, brominated, fluorinated pesticides and haloalkanes), with little response to compounds lacking strong electron-capturing groups.**

- 灵敏度：检出限可达fg/s级别，属于超高灵敏度、强选择性的检测器，非常适合环境痕量污染物监测。

Sensitivity: Detection limits can reach the fg/s level, making ECD an ultra-sensitive and highly selective detector ideal for trace environmental pollutants.**

5
原子发射检测器（AED, Atomic Emission Detector）

- 适用样品：对特定元素（如C、H、N、S、P等）具有元素选择性，可同时监测多种元素发射谱线。

Applicable samples: Element-selective for certain elements (e.g., C, H, N, S, P), capable of monitoring multiple elemental emission lines simultaneously.**

- 灵敏度：典型检出限在1 pg量级，既保留一定灵敏度，又提供元素信息，用于含特定元素化合物的选择性检测。

Sensitivity: Typical detection limit around 1 pg, providing both reasonable sensitivity and elemental information for selective detection of compounds containing specific elements.**

6
化学发光检测器（CS, Chemiluminescence Detector）

- 适用样品：多用于含可氧化组分或特定元素（如含氮、含硫化合物），通过化学反应产生光信号。

Applicable samples: Often used for oxidizable components or specific elements (such as nitrogen- or sulfur-containing compounds), where chemical reactions produce light.**

- 特点：通过监测光电倍增管本底电流以上的化学发光信号，实现高选择性检测。

Features: Detects chemiluminescence above the dark current of a photomultiplier tube, offering high selectivity.**

7
光电离化检测器（PID, Photoionization Detector）

- 适用样品：对许多挥发性有机物（VOCs）以及部分无机气体敏感，尤其用于环境空气与职业卫生监测。

Applicable samples: Sensitive to many volatile organic compounds (VOCs) and some inorganic gases, widely used in ambient-air and occupational monitoring.**

- 灵敏度：检出限约为0.002–0.02 µg/L，比多数通用型检测器灵敏得多，适合痕量VOC检测。

Sensitivity: Detection limits around 0.002–0.02 µg/L, substantially more sensitive than most universal detectors, suitable for trace VOC analysis.**

四、检测器灵敏度与选择性的综合理解 Comprehensive understanding of sensitivity and selectivity

- 不同检测器灵敏度差异巨大：从TCD的“中等灵敏度”到ECD、MS等“极高灵敏度”，相差可达几个数量级。

Sensitivity varies widely among detectors: From moderate sensitivity in TCD to extremely high sensitivity in ECD and MS, spanning several orders of magnitude.**

- 有的检测器“家族选择性”：例如ECD主要对卤代化合物敏感，PID对易光电离的VOC更敏感，是“家族选择”的典型例子。

Some detectors are “family-selective”: For example, ECD is highly responsive to halogenated compounds, and PID to easily photoionized VOCs, both classic examples of family-selective detectors.**

- 有的检测器几乎“通用”：如TCD几乎对所有能改变热导率的组分响应，但选择性差，色谱图上会出现很多峰。

Some detectors are nearly “universal”: TCD responds to almost any component that changes thermal conductivity, but has poor selectivity, leading to many peaks in the chromatogram.**

- 老师强调：记住“谁更灵敏、谁更选择性”比死背具体检出限数值更重要，能根据数量级和单位做大致比较即可。

The instructor emphasized that knowing which detector is more sensitive or more selective is more important than memorizing exact detection-limit numbers; approximate comparison by order of magnitude and unit is sufficient.**

五、如何根据应用选择合适的检测器 Choosing an appropriate detector for a given application

问- 按“应用场景”选择：样品是简单还是复杂？是环境痕量监测、药物质量控制，还是未知混合物鉴定？
答

Choose by application scenario: Is the sample simple or complex? Is it for trace environmental monitoring, pharmaceutical quality control, or identification of unknown mixtures?**

- 按“分析物类型”选择：

Choose by analyte type:**

** • 有机烃类和大多数有机物：首选FID，线性范围广、响应稳定，适合常规定量。

• Organic hydrocarbons and most organics: FID is usually preferred due to its wide linear range and stable response, ideal for routine quantitation.**

** • 卤代化合物和含卤农药：优先考虑ECD，因其对卤素极高的灵敏度和选择性。

• Halogenated compounds and halogenated pesticides: ECD is favored because of its extremely high sensitivity and selectivity to halogens.**

** • 成分复杂、含未知组分：MS可同时提供高灵敏度和结构信息，用于确证分析。

• Complex mixtures with unknowns: MS offers both high sensitivity and structural information, making it suitable for confirmatory analysis.**

** • 一般气体混合物或需通用响应的场合：可用TCD，既可测有机也可测无机。

• General gas mixtures or when universal response is desired: TCD can be used to detect both organic and inorganic gases.**

- 按“灵敏度需求”选择：痕量分析（例如环境污染物）需用高灵敏度检测器，如MS、ECD、部分PID；常规较高浓度样品可用FID或TCD即可。

Choose by required sensitivity: Trace analyses (e.g., environmental pollutants) require highly sensitive detectors such as MS, ECD, or certain PIDs; routine higher-concentration samples can be handled by FID or TCD.**

- 老师补充：选择检测器没有“绝对最好”，要根据样品复杂度、目标分析物、所需检出限和是否需要元素/结构信息等多因素综合权衡。

Instructor’s note: There is no universally “best” detector; choice must weigh multiple factors including sample complexity, target analytes, required detection limit, and whether elemental or structural information is needed.**

六、小结 Summary

- GC仪器由载气、进样口、色谱柱、柱温箱、检测器和数据系统组成，其中检测器负责把分离后的组分转化为可定量的电信号。

A GC system consists of carrier gas, injection port, column, oven, detector, and data system, with the detector converting separated components into quantifiable electrical signals.**

- GC检测器在“灵敏度”和“选择性”上差别巨大：有通用型（如TCD），也有高度选择性的家族/元素专一型（如ECD、AED、PID等）。

GC detectors differ greatly in sensitivity and selectivity: some are universal (e.g., TCD), while others are highly family- or element-specific (e.g., ECD, AED, PID).**

- 选择检测器时，要结合应用目的、样品复杂程度、分析物类型以及所需灵敏度，理解“为什么用这个检测器”，而不是机械记忆表格。

When selecting a detector, one must consider the analytical goal, sample complexity, analyte type, and required sensitivity, focusing on understanding “why this detector” rather than mechanically memorizing tables.**

**A) Effect of % acetonitrile (pH = 7)**

When the acetonitrile content is decreased from 50% to 30%, the dead time remains almost constant (1.75–1.77 min).

However, the retention time and capacity factor of butylparaben increase strongly (tR: 4.92 → 11.6 → 32.35 min; k: 1.82 → 5.56 → 17.54).

The critical resolution also increases (Rs: 1.98 → 5.54 → 11.57), and the backpressure rises slightly (53 → 59 bar).

Thus, a weaker (more aqueous) mobile phase increases retention and improves separation.

**B) Effect of solvent type: methanol vs acetonitrile (40% organic, pH = 7)**

At 40% organic phase, replacing acetonitrile with methanol greatly increases retention (tR: 11.6 → 69.0 min) and k (5.56 → 38.55), and also improves Rs (5.54 → 12.86), while the dead time is unchanged.

The backpressure almost doubles (57 → 99 bar).

Methanol is more viscous and is a weaker eluent than acetonitrile, so analytes are more retained and better separated, but at the cost of much higher pressure.

**C) Effect of pH (40% acetonitrile)**

At 40% acetonitrile, increasing the pH from 7 to 9 leaves the dead time and pressure essentially unchanged (t0 ≈ 1.75 min; 57 bar), but slightly decreases retention and resolution (tR: 11.6 → 9.02 min; k: 5.56 → 4.17; Rs: 5.54 → 4.18).

A higher pH makes butylparaben more ionised and more polar, so it interacts less with the hydrophobic stationary phase and is less retained.

* pH 从 7 升到 9（40% ACN）→ 分析物电离增加 → tR、k、Rs 略降，t0 和压力不变 ✅

Conclusion:

a) Decrease of column length decreases dead and ratention times, number of plates and back pression(divided by 3) but retention factors remain the same. Resolution decreases as a consequence of Ndecrease.

b) Decrease of particle diameter did not change dead time, retention times and retention factors.Back pressure increases (Darcy equation), Efficiency (N) increases because plate height (H) decreases(rate theory),so resolution increases.

c) C4 and phenyl phases are less hydrophobic that C18, so retention times and retention factorsdecrease,N decreases (lower column performance, associated with column manufacturing and loweiretention)so resolution decreases.

We have to choose operating conditions that give chromatographic results in accordance with : 1<k<10and R>1,5 and time of analysis <15min.

The initial conditions provide results that fulfill these requirements (except k>1 for methyl paraben). wedecrease acetonitrile content to 35%, so k>0,5 for methyl paraben. Retention times increase but we candecrease the retention times with increasing the flow rate at 1,5 mL/min . The obtained chromatogramis given below.

At pH = 7 with 30% acetonitrile, co-elution is observed for the first three compounds, which are almost not retained (they elute close to the dead time).

Good separation is obtained when the mobile phase contains only 10% acetonitrile, but in this case the retention factors are much too high (k ≫ 20) and the analysis becomes too long.

At pH = 2.7, the acidic analytes are mainly non-ionised and therefore better retained.

Under these conditions, good separation is obtained with 35% acetonitrile: all acidic compounds are separated, but paracetamol is still not retained, and the retention factor of mefenamic acid is around k ≈ 20.

The elution order (for unionised compounds at pH = 2.7) is:

Paracetamol < Acetylsalicylic acid < Salicylic acid < Ketoprofen < Ibuprofen < Mefenamic acid.

This order is consistent with the LogP values of the unionised compounds.

At pH = 2.7 and 38% acetonitrile, the retention factor of mefenamic acid is > 20, while paracetamol is not retained. If we increase the percentage of acetonitrile, the critical resolution of the early-eluting compounds becomes too low.

With gradient elution, all compounds can be sufficiently retained and acceptable resolution values can be achieved within a reasonable analysis time.
